# 360Biznes ERP — Tam Sistem Audit Hesabatı

**Növ:** Statik kod auditi (read-only). Canlı app (auth+DB+data) işlədilməyib — hər iddia kod oxunaraq qiymətləndirilib; runtime təsdiq lazım olanlar `needsLiveVerification`-də işarələnib.

**XƏBƏRDARLIQ:** Adversarial verifikasiya mərhələsi session limit (7pm Baku) səbəbindən demək olar işləmədi — 248 tapıntının yalnız 2-si təsdiqləndi, qalanı YOXLANMAMIŞ auditor iddiasıdır (bəziləri false-positive ola bilər). `CROSS: Backend icazə` auditi tam alınmadı.

## ✅ Adversarial verifikasiyadan keçən (TƏSDİQLƏNMİŞ) tapıntılar

- 🟠 **[high] Stealth mode scales only 2 of ~10 monetary widgets — dashboard shows internally contradictory figures and leaks un-disguised real numbers**
  - The bug is real and reproducible purely from code; my attempt to refute it failed because no downstream scaling exists anywhere in the render path.

WHAT I VERIFIED (read-only):
1) features/dashboard/queries.ts — stealth scaling (s = stealth.aktiv ? stealth.scale : 1) is applied ONLY in getDashboardKpis (lines 85-91: scales todaySalesAmount, stockValue, customerDebt) and getCeoKpis (lines 300-306: scales todaySalesAmount, monthRevenue). Every other exported query returns RAW values: getMonthlyComparison (310-360), getRecentSalesByDay (96-143), getSalesVsExpense30 (678-729), getTopProducts (362-391), getTopSellers (393-428), getTopCustomers (438-485), getTopPlatforms (493-525), getRecentSales (527-561), getTodayCashFlow (742-770), and getDailyInsight (841-887). (The auditor's line refs were off by ~1 line in two spots, e.g. it said "86-91 and 301-305"; actual 85-91 / 300-306. Cosmetic only.)

2) lib/utils.ts formatMoney (lines 8-18) and formatNumber (20-28) are pure Intl.NumberFormat formatters with NO stealth awareness — so scaling cannot happen at format time and must come from the query, confirming the premise.

3) The render path confirms the leak/contradiction on the SAME screen. In features/dashboard/sections/hero-section.tsx: "Bu gün → Satış" uses formatMoney(ceo.todaySalesAmount) — SCALED (line 177); but on the SAME hero block "Bu ayın gəliri" uses formatMoney(monthly.current.revenue) — UNSCALED (line 142, from getMonthlyComparison), and "Net axın" uses formatMoney(cashflow.net) — UNSCALED (line 187). The 3 KPI cards (expense 229, profit 239, new-customers 248) and both sparklines/charts (revSpark line 156 + SalesChart line 476, SalesExpenseChart line 488) all consume unscaled getMonthlyComparison/getRecentSalesByDay/getSalesVsExpense30. The chart components (sales-chart.tsx, sales-expense-chart.tsx) merely spread the data props through — no scaling.

4) top-five-section.tsx formats raw mebleg/cemi (lines 117,130,144,157); recent-sales-activity.tsx formats raw cemiMebleg and son_mebleg (lines 71,121); top-debtors-section.tsx has its own inline getTopDebtors raw query and formats raw borc (lines 75,118) with no stealth import at all.

5) A repo-wide grep for getStealthState/applyStealth/stealth.scale shows the consistent project pattern: scaling is done INSIDE each query function at the data source; in the dashboard module it exists only at queries.ts:85 and :300. There is no middleware/layout/formatter-level transform that would catch the unscaled widgets.

6) Intent is documented: lib/stealth/server.ts (lines 14-16) says the function is ONLY for screen display so on-screen numbers show scale×value; components/layout/stealth-banner.tsx (line 19) tells the user "ekranda rəqəmlər X% göstərilir" (on-screen numbers are shown at X%). So ALL displayed money widgets are supposed to be scaled — the unscaled ones leak real figures and contradict the scaled "Bu gün satış" tile.

Minor nuance (does not refute): the getDailyInsight portion of the leak exposes sales COUNT and day-over-day PERCENTAGES, not absolute money; the claim only asserted "sales count/%", which is accurate. Counts are inherently not scaled by design (stealth scales only monetary values), so that is the weakest part of the leak but still consistent with the claim as written.

RUNTIME NOTE: I cannot run the live app, so the exact rendered figures with a real cookie (stealth_mode=1, stealth_scale=0.2) are not observed end-to-end; however the contradiction is deterministic from the code (scaled vs unscaled fields on the same component) and does not depend on data values, so a live test would only confirm the magnitude, not the existence, of the defect.
  - Sübut: features/dashboard/queries.ts: scaling only at 86-91 and 301-305; getMonthlyComparison (350-360), getRecentSalesByDay (115-143), getSalesVsExpense30 (719-729), getTopProducts (381-391), getTopSellers (418-428), getTopCustomers (475-485), getTopPlatforms (515-525), getRecentSales (551-561), getTodayCashFlow (760-770), getDailyInsight (841-887) and top-debtors-section.tsx have no scaling
- 🔴 **[critical] acceptReturn / createReturn / cancelReturn (classic return flow) have NO permission guard — direct server-action call bypasses all permissions**
  - The bug is real. features/qaytarma/actions.ts exports three "use server" mutations — createReturn (line 47), acceptReturn (line 143), cancelReturn (line 277) — none of which perform any permission check. The file's imports (lines 1-10) deliberately do NOT include requireTicaretActionPerm / ./access-guard, unlike every sibling file in features/ticaret/*. The only gate inside these actions is withTenant(), which (lib/db/with-tenant.ts:25-27) merely requires an authenticated session — it loads icazeler into context but never enforces any specific permission; requireTenant (lib/db/tenant-context.ts:38-46) only reads the AsyncLocalStorage store. acceptReturn (actions.ts:147-258) is a genuine financial+inventory mutation: it increments stock or calls safeStockDecrement, writes anbar_hereketleri, reduces the original sale's son_mebleg/odenilmis, and calls recalculateCustomerBalance/recalculateSupplierBalance — all reachable by any authenticated user invoking the action directly, regardless of qaytarma permissions. The page (app/(dashboard)/ticaret/qaytarma/page.tsx) gates only qaytarma.oxu via requireTicaretPerm, which is page-render UI gating. The central route gate (lib/auth/route-gate.ts gateRoute) runs only in the dashboard layout against the URL pathname and cannot guard server-action POSTs; there is no middleware.ts in the project at all, so nothing intercepts direct action calls. This is exactly the bypass the auditor described. The inconsistency contrast is also confirmed: features/ticaret/qaytarma-tez-actions.ts fastReturn (line 113) and returnFullSale (line 346) both start with `await requireTicaretActionPerm("qaytarma.yarat")` and bail if not ok. The auditor's only error was a path typo (they wrote features/qaytarma/qaytarma-tez-actions.ts; the real file is features/ticaret/qaytarma-tez-actions.ts) — but the line numbers and guard pattern match exactly, so this does not refute the claim.
  - Sübut: features/qaytarma/actions.ts:47-55 (createReturn no perm), :143 (acceptReturn no perm), :277 (cancelReturn no perm); contrast qaytarma-tez-actions.ts:113-114, :346-347

---

# Modul-modul detallı hesabat

## 1. Dashboard

**Xülasə:** The Dashboard module is read-only (no mutations, so no transaction/audit obligations apply) and is generally solid on the basics: server-side permission gating via dashboard.oxu redirect plus granular per-section codes, tenant scoping on every query, present null/empty guards, and all link targets resolve (no 404 risk). The substantive issues are data-consistency divergences, not crashes. Highest impact: stealth mode scales only 2 of ~10 monetary widgets (getDashboardKpis + getCeoKpis), so when stealth is active the dashboard shows internally contradictory numbers and leaks real, un-disguised figures through the monthly-revenue card, both charts, Top-5 lists, cash flow, debtors, recent sales, and the insight banner. Three medium divergences: (1) all dashboard sales aggregates omit deleted_at IS NULL, diverging from the canonical active-sales query and customer-balance SSOT — latent today only because cancel sets status='legv', but fragile given the generic soft-delete helper sets only deleted_at; (2) the 'Açıq tapşırıq' KPI counts the internal sahibkar_tapshiriq table while linking to /tapshiriqlar which renders the different tapshiriqlar table, so the count never matches the click-through (and disagrees with the 'Mənim işim' card on the same page); (3) the low-stock count uses per-warehouse rows including zero stock with '<=', whereas the product list it links to uses summed-across-warehouses, >0-only, so the headline number won't match. Plus minor issues: prioritet ordered as a string, and the misnamed 'stockValue' (a quantity sum). No critical/page-breaking bugs found.

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx; features/dashboard/queries.ts; features/dashboard/recent-activity-queries.ts; features/dashboard/sections/hero-section.tsx; features/dashboard/sections/low-stock-section.tsx; features/dashboard/sections/top-five-section.tsx; features/dashboard/sections/top-debtors-section.tsx; features/dashboard/sections/critical-alerts-section.tsx; features/dashboard/sections/recent-sales-activity.tsx; features/dashboard/sections/business-feed-section.tsx; features/dashboard/sections/webhook-orders-section.tsx; features/dashboard/sections/sync-health-section.tsx; features/dashboard/components/dashboard-quick-actions.tsx; features/dashboard/components/low-stock-panel.tsx; features/dashboard/components/dashboard-header.tsx; features/dashboard/components/demo-banner.tsx; lib/db/prisma.ts; lib/db/with-tenant.ts; lib/auth/get-permissions.ts; lib/balance/customer-balance.ts (header + calc); lib/soft-delete/actions.ts (delete/restore); features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts (cancel path); features/ticaret/satis-queries.ts (canonical active list); features/tapshiriqlar/queries.ts (task model/status); features/anbar/queries.ts (stok_status=az filter); prisma/schema.prisma (satis_sifarisleri, stok, kontragentler, tapshiriqlar, sahibkar_tapshiriq models); lib/permission-modules.ts / lib/permission-info.ts (dashboard.* codes)

**Əhatə boşluqları:** features/dashboard/components/sales-chart.impl.tsx, sales-expense-chart.impl.tsx, top-products-chart.impl.tsx, kpi-card.tsx, sparkline rendering — opened only at the data-input level (props), client-side chart rendering internals not deeply reviewed.; features/dashboard/components/birthday-widget.tsx and recent-activity-widget.tsx internals not opened (birthday-widget is rendered inside LowStockSection).; External query libs pulled into sync/webhook widgets (features/qiymet-kanal/*: api-stats, outbound-stats, outbound-retry, webhook-orders-queries) — treated as out-of-scope dependencies; their tenant scoping/soft-delete not audited here.; features/crm/dashboard-queries.ts and features/maliyye/queries.ts getDashboardKpis (same-named but different functions) — out of dashboard scope; only noted as consumers of the shared features/dashboard getDashboardKpis.; loading.tsx / error boundaries for the dashboard route not located/reviewed.

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ Backend permission gate on dashboard render: dashboard.oxu is checked server-side and redirects to /tapshiriqlar if missing; not just UI gating — _app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx:104-106 (redirect when !has(icazeler,'dashboard.oxu'))_
- ✅ Per-section UI gating wired to granular permission codes (kpi, cashflow, tapshiriq, aktivlik, stok, top5, alerts, sync, feed, charts, insight), all registered in permission-info.ts — _app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx:109-119; lib/permission-info.ts:114-119_
- ✅ All section queries run inside withTenant() and every prismaUnscoped call passes explicit sahibkar_id, so no cross-tenant data leak in dashboard reads — _features/dashboard/queries.ts:76-77,116-117 etc.; each prismaUnscoped where includes sahibkar_id (e.g. lines 43,48,52,59)_
- ✅ All dashboard link targets resolve to existing routes (no 404 risk): /pos (app/pos), /team, /marketplace, /xeberdarliqlar(+/[id]), /audit-log, /ticaret/satislar(+/[id]), /tapshiriqlar(+/[id]), /elaqe/musteriler/[id], /maliyye/debitor, /anbar/satinalma, /anbar/mehsullar, /hesabatlar(/pul), /marketplace/webhook-orders, /marketplace/sync-health — _verified via filesystem: app/pos exists; all (dashboard)/* targets and detail [id] routes present_
- ✅ Null/empty guards present on widgets: DemoBanner returns null when !abuneBitme; CriticalAlertsSection/WebhookOrdersSection/SyncHealthSection return null when empty; BusinessFeedSection catches errors; HeroSection conditionally renders only when ceo/monthly/cashflow non-null — _demo-banner.tsx:11; critical-alerts-section.tsx:10; webhook-orders-section.tsx:21; sync-health-section.tsx:33; business-feed-section.tsx:5 (.catch); hero-section.tsx:112,223,262,343_
- ✅ getMyPendingWork (Mənim işim card) queries the correct user-facing tapshiriqlar model with the same 'open' definition (status notIn [tamamlandi,legv]) used by the /tapshiriqlar page — _features/dashboard/queries.ts:794 vs features/tapshiriqlar/queries.ts:98,200_
- ✅ Customer debt KPI reading kontragentler.alacaq is an accepted cache read (SSOT doc designates it a perf cache); top-debtors widget recomputes from satis_sifarisleri with status<>'legv' and qaralama=false and nisye/borc filter, matching customer-balance SSOT semantics — _lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:10-21; features/dashboard/sections/top-debtors-section.tsx:41-44_
- ✅ Dashboard is read-only (KPIs/widgets); no mutations performed, so no audit-log-on-mutation or transaction-atomicity obligations apply to this scope — _features/dashboard/queries.ts and all sections only run findMany/aggregate/groupBy/$queryRaw reads_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Stealth mode scales only 2 of ~10 monetary widgets — dashboard shows internally contradictory figures and leaks un-disguised real numbers**
  - Detal: Stealth scaling (stealth.scale) is applied ONLY inside getDashboardKpis (lines 89-91) and getCeoKpis (lines 304-305). It is NOT applied to getMonthlyComparison (the big 'Bu ayın gəliri' card + the 3 KPI cards: expense/profit/new-customers), getRecentSalesByDay & getSalesVsExpense30 (sparklines + both charts), getTopProducts/getTopSellers/getTopCustomers/getTopPlatforms (Top-5 monetary values), getRecentSales (son_mebleg in 'Son 5 satış'), getTodayCashFlow (mədaxil/məxaric/net card), getTopDebtors (borc list), or getDailyInsight (sales count/% in the insight banner). Result: when stealth is active, the scaled 'Bu gün satış' tile (from getCeoKpis) will not reconcile with the unscaled 'Bu ayın gəliri' card, the unscaled sales chart, or the unscaled cash-flow card on the SAME screen, and the real (un-disguised) figures leak through all unscaled widgets — defeating the stealth feature's purpose and presenting contradictory numbers.
  - Sübut: features/dashboard/queries.ts: scaling only at 86-91 and 301-305; getMonthlyComparison (350-360), getRecentSalesByDay (115-143), getSalesVsExpense30 (719-729), getTopProducts (381-391), getTopSellers (418-428), getTopCustomers (475-485), getTopPlatforms (515-525), getRecentSales (551-561), getTodayCashFlow (760-770), getDailyInsight (841-887) and top-debtors-section.tsx have no scaling
  - Fayllar: features/dashboard/queries.ts, features/dashboard/sections/hero-section.tsx, features/dashboard/sections/top-five-section.tsx, features/dashboard/sections/recent-sales-activity.tsx, features/dashboard/sections/top-debtors-section.tsx

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Dashboard sales/finance KPIs omit deleted_at IS NULL filter — diverges from canonical active-sales query (soft-deleted sales can inflate dashboard totals)**
  - Detal: Every dashboard sales aggregate filters only on status != 'legv' and qaralama, but NOT on deleted_at IS NULL: fetchDashboardKpisRaw, fetchCeoKpisRaw, fetchMonthlyComparisonRaw, fetchRecentSalesByDayRaw, fetchSalesVsExpense30Raw, fetchTopProductsRaw, fetchTopSellersRaw, fetchTopCustomersRaw, fetchTopPlatformsRaw, fetchRecentSalesRaw. The canonical active-sales list (satis-queries.ts:103-105) requires BOTH status NOT IN ('legv','qaytarilib') AND deleted_at IS NULL, and the customer-balance SSOT requires deleted_at IS NULL (customer-balance.ts:69). The generic soft-delete helper (lib/soft-delete/actions.ts:50-55) sets ONLY deleted_at and explicitly does NOT change status (comment line 23). Today the sale-cancel action happens to set both status='legv' AND deleted_at (satis-actions.ts:291-296), so the omission is latent; but any soft-delete of a sale that keeps an active status (the documented contract of softDeleteRecord, which already accepts 'satis_sifarisleri') would silently count it in every dashboard total while the rest of the app excludes it. The tenant Prisma extension applies NO soft-delete filter (lib/db/prisma.ts only injects sahibkar_id), so nothing rescues this.
  - Sübut: features/dashboard/queries.ts:42-44,241-248,317-322 vs features/ticaret/satis-queries.ts:103-104 and lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:69; lib/soft-delete/actions.ts:23,50-55
  - Fayllar: features/dashboard/queries.ts, features/ticaret/satis-queries.ts, lib/soft-delete/actions.ts, lib/balance/customer-balance.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] 'Açıq tapşırıq' KPI tile queries the wrong table (sahibkar_tapshiriq) but links to /tapshiriqlar (tapshiriqlar) — count never matches what the user sees**
  - Detal: getCeoKpis().openTasks counts prismaUnscoped.sahibkar_tapshiriq with status IN ['acig','isleyir']. sahibkar_tapshiriq is the internal owner/system task table (used only by features/sahibkar/*, default status 'acig'). The dashboard 'Açıq tapşırıq' tile that displays this number links to /tapshiriqlar, which renders the DIFFERENT user-facing tapshiriqlar model (statuses yeni|icrada|gozlemede|tamamlandi|legv; 'open' = status notIn [tamamlandi,legv]). So the headline count is computed from one table while the click-through shows another, and the figures will not agree. (Note: the 'Mənim işim' card correctly uses tapshiriqlar, so the two task widgets on the same dashboard also disagree with each other.)
  - Sübut: features/dashboard/queries.ts:260-262 (sahibkar_tapshiriq, ['acig','isleyir']) and hero-section.tsx:190-201 (tile links to /tapshiriqlar); contrast features/dashboard/queries.ts:794-798 (getMyPendingWork uses tapshiriqlar) and features/tapshiriqlar/queries.ts:9,98
  - Fayllar: features/dashboard/queries.ts, features/dashboard/sections/hero-section.tsx

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Dashboard low-stock count diverges from the product list it links to (per-warehouse rows incl. zero stock vs. summed-across-warehouses, >0 only)**
  - Detal: Dashboard lowStockCount (fetchDashboardKpisRaw/fetchCeoKpisRaw) and getLowStockItems JOIN the stok table directly and count PER-WAREHOUSE rows where COALESCE(s.miqdar,0) <= m.kritik_stok, with NO lower bound (so out-of-stock miqdar=0 rows are included, and a product stocked in N warehouses can be counted up to N times / flagged as low even when its total across warehouses is plenty). The product list this tile links to (/anbar/mehsullar?stok_status=az) defines 'az' as SUM(stok.miqdar across warehouses) > 0 AND <= kritik_stok (features/anbar/queries.ts:656-657), i.e. excludes zero-stock (those are 'yox') and compares the aggregated total. So the 'Aşağı stok' headline number will generally not equal the count the user sees after clicking through.
  - Sübut: features/dashboard/queries.ts:55-63 (count) and 145-162 (items) — per-row JOIN stok, '<=' incl. zero; vs features/anbar/queries.ts:656-657 (SUM>0 && <=kritik) and link hero-section.tsx:203
  - Fayllar: features/dashboard/queries.ts, features/anbar/queries.ts

- ⚪ **[low · functional] getMyPendingWork orders next tasks by prioritet string DESC, which does not match priority semantics**
  - Detal: next_tasks orderBy uses { prioritet: 'desc' } where prioritet is a VARCHAR with values like 'tecili','yuksek','normal','asagi'. String DESC ordering sorts alphabetically (e.g. 'yuksek' > 'tecili' > 'normal'), so the most urgent task ('tecili') is NOT guaranteed first in the 'Mənim işim' next-tasks list. The UI even renders 'tecili' as the top red dot (hero-section.tsx:436), implying it should outrank 'yuksek', but the query ordering can put 'yuksek' first.
  - Sübut: features/dashboard/queries.ts:805-810 (orderBy prioritet desc) vs hero-section.tsx:436-438 (tecili treated as highest)
  - Fayllar: features/dashboard/queries.ts

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] stockValue KPI is a quantity sum mislabeled as a value; empty-state heuristic relies on it**
  - Detal: DashboardKpis.stockValue is computed as _sum of stok.miqdar (a unit quantity, Decimal 12,3), not a monetary value (no multiply by alish_qiymeti). The field name 'stockValue' is misleading. Within the dashboard scope it is only consumed by DashboardEmptyState as a 'has any data?' check (todaySalesCount===0 && stockValue===0), which still works as a heuristic; but the same getDashboardKpis is reused by other modules (crm/maliyye pages) where the misnamed field could be misread. Scope-limited impact here is low.
  - Sübut: features/dashboard/queries.ts:47-50,69 (sum of stok.miqdar) and app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx:57-58
  - Fayllar: features/dashboard/queries.ts, app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] Recent-activity feed and CEO active-customer count omit deleted_at IS NULL on kontragentler**
  - Detal: kontragentler has deleted_at (soft-delete) but: (a) fetchCeoKpisRaw activeCustomers counts kontragentler where nov='musteri' AND aktiv=true without deleted_at IS NULL (queries.ts:259); (b) getDashboardActivityFeed 'yeni müştəri' list and getDailyInsight today_cust count likewise omit deleted_at. Soft-deleted customers could be counted/shown. Lower severity because soft-delete of customers may also flip aktiv=false (not verified), but the filter is not defensive.
  - Sübut: features/dashboard/queries.ts:259, 861-863; features/dashboard/recent-activity-queries.ts:88-92
  - Fayllar: features/dashboard/queries.ts, features/dashboard/recent-activity-queries.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 dashboard.oxu master gate: Correctly enforced server-side (redirect), not bypassable by direct URL since the page itself redirects. No issue. — _app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx:104-106_
- 🔐 Per-section data exposure vs UI gating: Section permissions only gate UI rendering; the underlying query functions (getCeoKpis, getTopDebtors, etc.) are plain exported server functions guarded only by withTenant (auth+tenant), NOT by the dashboard.kpi/cashflow/etc. permission. They are not exposed as API routes within dashboard scope, so not directly invokable today; but if any of these query exports were reused by an API route or action without re-checking the granular code, a user lacking e.g. dashboard.cashflow could obtain cash-flow data. Within dashboard page render the gating is correct. — _features/dashboard/queries.ts getCeoKpis/getTodayCashFlow only call withTenant (no permission check); gating lives in app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx:109-119_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** features/dashboard/queries.ts, features/dashboard/sections/hero-section.tsx, features/dashboard/sections/top-five-section.tsx, features/dashboard/sections/recent-sales-activity.tsx, features/dashboard/sections/top-debtors-section.tsx, features/dashboard/recent-activity-queries.ts

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** Xeyr

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** Dashboard is a read aggregator over many modules, so its divergences are consistency risks rather than corruption risks. (1) Stealth scaling gap means dashboard figures contradict each other and leak real numbers — and other modules (crm/maliyye pages reuse getDashboardKpis) inherit the same partial-scaling behavior. (2) The deleted_at omission means dashboard sales totals can drift from the Sales module list, customer-balance SSOT, and finance reports if a sale is ever soft-deleted without status='legv' (the documented contract of lib/soft-delete/actions.ts, which already lists satis_sifarisleri as a valid table). (3) Low-stock count divergence from the Anbar product list, and (4) the Açıq-tapşırıq table mismatch with the Tapşırıqlar module, both undermine trust because click-through never reconciles with the headline. None of these corrupt data; they misreport it.

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) Stealth consistency (high): centralize stealth scaling so ALL monetary dashboard outputs are scaled uniformly — apply stealth.scale to getMonthlyComparison (revenue/expense/profit), getRecentSalesByDay (amount), getSalesVsExpense30 (satis/xerc), getTopProducts/Sellers/Customers/Platforms (mebleg/cemi), getRecentSales (son_mebleg), getTodayCashFlow (daxil/xaric/net), getTopDebtors (borc), and getDailyInsight (or suppress exact figures in insight when stealth active). Best done via a shared helper applied after unstable_cache (as the existing two do) so cached raw stays un-scaled. 2) Soft-delete (medium): add deleted_at IS NULL (or deleted_at: null for Prisma where) to every dashboard sales aggregate (the 10 fetch*Raw functions enumerated) to match satis-queries.ts and the customer-balance SSOT; likewise add deleted_at filter to the kontragentler activeCustomers count and the recent-activity customer list. 3) Task table mismatch (medium): change getCeoKpis().openTasks to query tapshiriqlar with status notIn ['tamamlandi','legv'] (matching getMyPendingWork and /tapshiriqlar), OR repoint the tile link to wherever sahibkar_tapshiriq is shown. 4) Low-stock (medium): change dashboard lowStockCount/getLowStockItems to aggregate SUM(stok.miqdar) per product across warehouses and compare to kritik_stok with the same >0 lower bound used by features/anbar/queries.ts (or extract a shared low-stock query and use it both places). 5) Priority ordering (low): map prioritet to a numeric rank for orderBy instead of string desc. 6) stockValue (low): rename to stockQty or compute an actual value; ensure crm/maliyye consumers are updated. All changes are read-only query/UI adjustments; no schema migration required.

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- Enable stealth mode with non-trivial real data, open the dashboard, and verify whether 'Bu gün satış' (scaled) reconciles with 'Bu ayın gəliri' card, the sales chart, and the cash-flow card — expect mismatch (scaled vs un-scaled) demonstrating the leak.
- Soft-delete a completed sale via any soft-delete path that leaves status != 'legv' (if available), then confirm today's/this-month's dashboard sales totals still include it while /ticaret/satislar excludes it.
- Compare the 'Aşağı stok' number on the dashboard tile against the row count on /anbar/mehsullar?stok_status=az for a tenant that has (a) a product out of stock (miqdar=0) and (b) a product low in one warehouse but abundant in another — expect the two counts to differ.
- Compare the 'Açıq tapşırıq' tile number against the open-task count shown on /tapshiriqlar — expect them to differ (different tables).
- Create tasks with prioritet 'tecili' and 'yuksek' both assigned to the current user with no/near deadlines, then check the 'Mənim işim' next-tasks ordering — verify whether 'tecili' actually appears first.

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Whether soft-deleting a sale via any path other than the cancel action (which sets status='legv') is actually reachable in the running app today — if softDeleteRecord('satis_sifarisleri',...) is never invoked, the deleted_at omission is purely latent. No current caller was found in static search.
- Whether customer soft-delete also sets aktiv=false (would mitigate the activeCustomers count omission) — not confirmed from schema/static read.
- Exact prioritet enum values and their intended rank order (tecili/yuksek/normal/asagi) to confirm the string-desc ordering actually misranks in practice.
- Runtime confirmation that stealth-active rendering shows the contradictory mix of scaled vs unscaled widgets described (requires toggling stealth with real data).

---

## 2. Xəbərdarlıqlar / Nəzarət Mərkəzi / Təsdiqlər

**Xülasə:** Audited the alerts (/xeberdarliqlar), Nəzarət Mərkəzi dashboard (/nezaret-merkezi), and Approval Center (/tesdiq) modules plus their generation triggers (stock helper, automation engine, maliyye threshold, finance-triggers cron, overdue-task cron) and the alert/bildiris/tesdiq server actions. The plumbing is largely sound: routes are gated, all server actions run under withTenant (auth-enforced, tenant-scoped), the approval flow enforces per-kind permission + 4-eyes and writes audit/log entries, alert-card and approval-action buttons are wired to real handlers, and stock alerts are idempotent. However there is one CRITICAL permission bug: bulkReject performs updateMany on the raw unfiltered id list instead of the permission/4-eyes-filtered validRejectIds, letting any user reject requests they cannot approve and even their own. Two HIGH data-integrity issues: approveRequest lacks a status='gozleyir' guard and re-approval double-applies side effects for stok_duzelis and product-update kinds; and the status update is not atomic with propagateDocumentApproval (which swallows errors), so a request can be marked approved while the underlying stock/sale/purchase/product is never activated. Another HIGH issue: the stock auto-clear writes status 'hell' instead of the canonical 'hell_olundu', leaving recovered alerts in a status no reader/report recognizes as resolved. Medium issues: overdue-task dedupe checks non-existent statuses ('resolved'/'dismissed'); the dashboard 'İşçi davamiyyət' KPI is dead due to alert category-code mismatch ('isci_gec/isci_devamiyyet' vs produced 'isci'); finance triggers (borc/payout/kassa/maya) only create notifications, never alerts, so they never surface on the risk panel; a pervasive 'yuxsek' vs 'yuksek' severity-spelling split breaks alert sorting, grouped critical-counts and escalation; and alert mutations have no authorization check and write no audit log. Low issues include approve/reject buttons shown to non-approvers (backend blocks it) and an inverted dashboard tone. All findings are code-grounded with file:line; cross-module/Prisma-extension scoping behavior and several tesdiq/automation subpages need live verification (listed in coverageGaps / needsLiveVerification).

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** app/(dashboard)/nezaret-merkezi/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/tesdiq/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/tesdiq/[id]/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/xeberdarliqlar/page.tsx; features/alerts/queries.ts; features/alerts/actions.ts; features/alerts/get-recent-alerts.ts; features/alerts/components/alert-card.tsx; features/alerts/components/mark-all-read.tsx; features/nezaret-merkezi/dashboard-queries.ts; features/nezaret-merkezi/counts.ts; features/tesdiq/actions.ts; features/tesdiq/create.ts; features/tesdiq/permissions.ts; features/tesdiq/components/approval-actions.tsx; features/bildirisler/actions.ts; features/bildirisler/get-my-notifications.ts; features/anbar/alert-helpers.ts; features/avtomatlasdirma/actions.ts (alert-creation block); features/maliyye/actions.ts (threshold approval+alert block); features/maliyye/triggers.ts (finance triggers); features/tapshiriqlar/actions.ts (_executeOverdueCheckForTenant); app/api/alerts/mark-all-read/route.ts; app/api/cron/check-overdue-tasks/route.ts; app/api/cron/finance-triggers/route.ts; lib/auth/route-gate.ts; lib/db/prisma.ts; lib/db/with-tenant.ts; lib/db/tenant-models.ts; prisma/schema.prisma (model alerts)

**Əhatə boşluqları:** features/tesdiq/settings.ts and settings-actions.ts (approval rule thresholds / approver lists) — read only via permissions.ts usage, not audited for save/validation correctness.; features/tesdiq/edit-actions.ts and components/edit-approval-form.tsx — the in-place edit-before-approve flow (yaradan_duzelis/tesdiq_eden_duzelis) not fully traced for permission/audit.; app/(dashboard)/tesdiq/statistika/page.tsx and app/(dashboard)/tesdiq/ayarlar/page.tsx — not opened.; app/(dashboard)/nezaret-merkezi/loglar and ayarlar subpages + features/nezaret-merkezi/log-queries.ts — not opened (audit/automation log views).; features/avtomatlasdirma full rule engine (findMatchedEntities, run-automation-rules cron, catalog.ts) — only the alert-creation block (actions.ts ~350-405) was reviewed; rule matching/scheduling correctness not audited (separate module scope).; features/maliyye/triggers.ts lines 180-348 (budget_warn branch, getFinanceRecipients, all-tenants loop) — partially read.; alert_status_history and alert_user_preferences tables — present in schema but their write/read paths not traced.; The xeberdarliqlar/[id] detail page and alert_comments/escalations rendering — not opened (only the list page and alert-card).; AlertFilters / view-toggle / grouped-view client components — not opened for filter/search/pagination wiring correctness.

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ Route-level gating exists for /tesdiq (tesdiq.view/tesdiq.tesdiq) and /nezaret-merkezi (nezaret.oxu/dashboard/audit.view); sahibkar/admin bypass; /xeberdarliqlar intentionally in BYPASS_PREFIXES (all tenant users) — _lib/auth/route-gate.ts:52,54,69-78; gateRoute() lib/auth/route-gate.ts:88-113; (dashboard)/layout.tsx:120 gateRoute_
- ✅ Nəzarət Mərkəzi dashboard page enforces a backend permission redirect (nezaret.oxu) in addition to route-gate — _app/(dashboard)/nezaret-merkezi/page.tsx:24-30 redirect('/tapshiriqlar')_
- ✅ All alert/tesdiq/bildiris server actions run inside withTenant(), which throws on unauthenticated session and binds sahibkar_id; alerts and tesdiq_telep are in TENANT_MODELS so bare update/findUnique by id is tenant-scoped by the Prisma extension — _lib/db/with-tenant.ts:24-26 throw; lib/db/prisma.ts:62-66 where-injection; lib/db/tenant-models.ts:13 alerts, :207 tesdiq_telep_
- ✅ Approval (approveRequest/rejectRequest/bulkApprove) enforces per-kind authorization via canApproveDocApproval AND 4-eyes (creator cannot approve own request) — _features/tesdiq/actions.ts:48-63 (single), :391-393 (bulkApprove uses validIds); features/tesdiq/permissions.ts:35-55_
- ✅ Approval propagation correctly routes per document kind and uses source-of-truth balance recalc (recalculateSupplierBalance) rather than manual increment; reject cancels the underlying document — _features/tesdiq/actions.ts:107-220 propagateDocumentApproval; :124-130 recalc; :280-342 propagateDocumentReject_
- ✅ Approval transitions are audited (audit('tesdiq'/'redd'/'legv', 'tesdiq_telep', ...)) and logged to tesdiq_log — _features/tesdiq/actions.ts:22-37 logTransition, :74-78 audit, :253-257 audit, :358-362 audit_
- ✅ Stock-critical alert helper is idempotent (24h dedupe by obyekt_id + open status) and refreshes last_seen_at instead of duplicating — _features/anbar/alert-helpers.ts:60-79_
- ✅ Per-user bildirisler (notifications) are correctly scoped by istifadeci_id + sahibkar_id on both read and mark-read — _features/bildirisler/actions.ts:15-18,33-36; get-my-notifications.ts:39-45_
- ✅ Alert-card action buttons (Tapşırıq, Təsdiqə göndər, Mənə, Qeyd, 4 saat snooze, Eskalə, Həll) are all wired to real server actions with disabled-during-pending and router.refresh — _features/alerts/components/alert-card.tsx:108-172, run() :42-52_
- ✅ createTaskFromAlert and sendAlertToApproval create real tapshiriqlar/tesdiq_telep rows and echo a comment back on the alert; revalidate /tesdiq — _features/alerts/actions.ts:144-190, :195-238_
- ✅ cron routes require Authorization: Bearer CRON_SECRET when the env var is set, and iterate active tenants with runWithTenant per tenant — _app/api/cron/check-overdue-tasks/route.ts:22-35,55-63; app/api/cron/finance-triggers/route.ts:22-27_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🔴 **[critical · permission] bulkReject rejects requests the user is NOT authorized for and even their OWN requests (permission + 4-eyes bypass)**
  - Detal: bulkApprove correctly limits the updateMany to validIds (permission-filtered + creator-excluded). bulkReject computes validRejectIds the same way but then calls updateMany with where:{ id:{ in: ids } } using the RAW, unfiltered ids array. Result: a user can reject any pending tesdiq_telep in the tenant regardless of canApproveDocApproval, and can reject their own requests (violating 4-eyes). The audit log records only validRejectIds while the DB updated all ids, so the audit trail is also wrong. Compare line 449 (uses ids) vs line 398-399 of bulkApprove (uses validIds).
  - Sübut: features/tesdiq/actions.ts:443-457 (validRejectIds computed but updateMany uses {id:{in:ids}} at :450); contrast :391-399
  - Fayllar: features/tesdiq/actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] approveRequest has no status guard — an already-approved request can be re-approved, re-running side effects (double-apply for stok_duzelis and product-update)**
  - Detal: cancelRequest guards `if (cur.status !== 'gozleyir')` but approveRequest does NOT. Re-calling approveRequest on an already-tesdiq row updates it again and re-invokes propagateDocumentApproval. For emeliyyat_nov 'stok_duzelis' this re-runs adjustStock with skipApprovalCheck:true (a second real stock movement); for 'mehsul_yaratma' update it re-applies detay.after to mehsullar. The alis_qaime / satis_qaime branches are protected by their own status guards (status==='tesdiq_gozleyir'), but stok_duzelis and mehsul branches are not, so a double-click / refresh / direct re-call double-applies the stock adjustment or product change. propagateDocumentApproval errors are swallowed (:217), so the second run can also silently no-op or partially apply.
  - Sübut: features/tesdiq/actions.ts:39-101 (no status check) vs :352 (cancelRequest guard); propagate stok_duzelis :187-216, mehsul :168-186; adjustStock skipApprovalCheck :199-210
  - Fayllar: features/tesdiq/actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Approval and its side effects are NOT atomic — status flips to 'tesdiq' even if document activation fails**
  - Detal: approveRequest updates tesdiq_telep.status='tesdiq' (line 64) and writes audit, THEN calls propagateDocumentApproval as a separate, non-transactional step (line 88). propagateDocumentApproval wraps its body in try/catch and only console.warns on failure (:217-219). So if activating the purchase/sale/stock-adjust/product fails (e.g. recalculateSupplierBalance throws, adjustStock returns !ok), the request is permanently marked 'tesdiq' but the underlying document is never activated / stock never moved — silent partial commit. There is no $transaction spanning the status update and the propagation, and no compensating rollback.
  - Sübut: features/tesdiq/actions.ts:64-89 (update then propagate, no $transaction); :217-219 swallow; adjustStock failure only warned :211-213
  - Fayllar: features/tesdiq/actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Stock-recovery auto-clear writes status='hell' but every reader treats resolved as 'hell_olundu' — auto-cleared alerts stay visible as open and corrupt counts**
  - Detal: checkAndCreateStockAlert auto-clears recovered stock alerts with data:{ status:'hell' } (a string never used anywhere else). The canonical resolved status is 'hell_olundu' (alerts actions resolveAlert, queries OPEN_STATUSES/STATUS_RANK, alert-card). Open-alert counts everywhere filter status:{ in:['yeni','baxilir'] }; 'hell' is NOT in that set, so technically it leaves the open list. BUT: getAlerts STATUS_RANK has no 'hell' key (rank 99, sorts last), the AlertCard !isOpen footer only labels 'hell_olundu'/'snoozed' (shows generic 'Bağlandı' for 'hell'), and resolved_today count filters status:'hell_olundu' so auto-cleared stock alerts are never counted as resolved_today. Net: inconsistent state machine — a stock alert resolved by recovery is in a status no UI/report recognizes as resolved.
  - Sübut: features/anbar/alert-helpers.ts:53-54 status:'hell'; canonical 'hell_olundu' in features/alerts/actions.ts:35, features/alerts/queries.ts:8,144; resolved_today filter dashboard-queries.ts:71 status:'hell_olundu'
  - Fayllar: features/anbar/alert-helpers.ts, features/alerts/queries.ts, features/nezaret-merkezi/dashboard-queries.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Overdue-task alert dedupe checks status notIn ['resolved','dismissed'] — statuses that are never written**
  - Detal: _executeOverdueCheckForTenant dedupe findFirst filters status:{ notIn:['resolved','dismissed'] }. Alerts are created with status 'yeni' and resolved as 'hell_olundu'/'baxilir'/'snoozed' — none of which are 'resolved' or 'dismissed'. So (a) the dedupe never excludes resolved alerts: if an overdue alert was resolved ('hell_olundu') and the task is still overdue, the findFirst still matches it (because hell_olundu is not in the notIn list) and skips creating a fresh alert, so a recurring overdue condition produces no new alert; (b) the literal status strings 'resolved'/'dismissed' are foreign to this codebase's alert state machine.
  - Sübut: features/tapshiriqlar/actions.ts:704-714 (status notIn ['resolved','dismissed'])
  - Fayllar: features/tapshiriqlar/actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Dashboard 'İşçi davamiyyət' KPI (late_employees_today) is always 0 — category-code mismatch between producer and consumer**
  - Detal: getRiskDashboard counts alerts with kateqoriya_kod in ['isci_gec','isci_devamiyyet']. No code path ever creates alerts with those codes: the only late/attendance alert producer is the avtomatlasdirma engine whose TRIGGER_TO_CAT maps isci_gec_geldi/isci_gelmedi -> 'isci' (singular, different code). So this tile always shows 0. (servis_stalled ['servis_gecikme','servis'] and mp_pending ['marketplace','mp_gecikme'] do match the engine's 'servis'/'marketplace' codes, so those work when a rule fires; only the isci codes are dead.)
  - Sübut: dashboard-queries.ts:96-102 (in:['isci_gec','isci_devamiyyet']); avtomatlasdirma TRIGGER_TO_CAT features/avtomatlasdirma/actions.ts:369 isci_gec_geldi/isci_gelmedi->'isci'
  - Fayllar: features/nezaret-merkezi/dashboard-queries.ts, features/avtomatlasdirma/actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Finance triggers (borc / payout / negative kassa / maya) create only bildirisler, never alerts rows — Nəzarət/Xəbərdarlıq panels miss these risks**
  - Detal: runFinanceTriggers (the finance-triggers cron) only writes prisma.bildirisler (in-app notifications) and tapshiriqlar for overdue debt/payout/negative-cash; it never inserts into the alerts table. As a result these high-value financial risks do NOT appear on the /xeberdarliqlar risk panel and are not reflected in alert-based dashboard KPIs (the dashboard's debtor/overdue_debt come from kontragentler/raw SQL, not from these triggers). The scope expectation 'alert generation triggers (stock/borc/kassa/payout)' is only met for stock (anbar alert-helpers); borc/kassa/payout produce notifications, not alerts.
  - Sübut: features/maliyye/triggers.ts:98 bildirisler.createMany, :174 tapshiriqlar.create, :212-231 payout via notify(); no alerts.create in file (grep)
  - Fayllar: features/maliyye/triggers.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Severity value 'yuxsek' vs 'yuksek' inconsistency breaks alert sorting, group critical-counts, and escalation**
  - Detal: The avtomatlasdirma engine and tapshiriqlar overdue check write seviyye:'yuxsek' (actions.ts:359, tapshiriqlar:722), but alert readers use 'yuksek': SEVERITY_RANK in queries.ts has only yuksek:3 so 'yuxsek' alerts get rank 0 and sort below info; getAlertsGrouped counts kritik_count only for 'kritik'||'yuksek' (:217) so 'yuxsek' alerts are not counted as critical in grouped view; escalateAlert SEV_NEXT has no 'yuxsek' key so escalating a 'yuxsek' alert resets it to 'yuksek' (:101-102 fallback). Same two spellings coexist across the codebase (yuxsek used by avtomatlasdirma/maliyye/dashboard tesdiq filter; yuksek used by alert queries/escalation/tasks).
  - Sübut: features/avtomatlasdirma/actions.ts:359 'yuxsek'; features/alerts/queries.ts:99-104 SEVERITY_RANK(yuksek only), :217 group kritik; features/alerts/actions.ts:101-102 SEV_NEXT(no yuxsek)
  - Fayllar: features/alerts/queries.ts, features/alerts/actions.ts, features/avtomatlasdirma/actions.ts, features/tapshiriqlar/actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] Alert mutations (acknowledge/resolve/snooze/assign/escalate/markAllRead) have no authorization check and write no audit log**
  - Detal: Unlike tesdiq actions (which gate on canApproveDocApproval), the alert server actions only call withTenant — any authenticated user in the tenant can resolve, snooze, reassign, escalate, or mark-all-read every alert. /xeberdarliqlar is in BYPASS_PREFIXES so no route-gate permission is required either. Additionally, none of these mutations call audit() (no @/lib/audit import in features/alerts/actions.ts), so there is no audit trail of who resolved/snoozed/reassigned an alert — only escalation gets an alert_escalations row and createTask/sendToApproval leave a comment. The mark-all-read API route (app/api/alerts/mark-all-read) checks only session presence, not any permission.
  - Sübut: features/alerts/actions.ts:18-93 (no permission check, no audit import); app/api/alerts/mark-all-read/route.ts:6-9 (only session check); lib/auth/route-gate.ts:77 BYPASS /xeberdarliqlar
  - Fayllar: features/alerts/actions.ts, app/api/alerts/mark-all-read/route.ts

- ⚪ **[low · ux] Approve/Reject buttons rendered to users without approval permission (UI not gated by canApproveDocApproval)**
  - Detal: Both tesdiq/page.tsx and tesdiq/[id]/page.tsx render <ApprovalActions> for every pending row unconditionally. The detail page even computes _canApprove (loadDetail :58) but only passes it to EditApprovalForm (:189), not to gate ApprovalActions (:123-127). The list page never computes it. A user without approval rights (or the creator) sees Təsdiq/Rədd buttons; clicking returns an error toast from the backend guard. Not a security hole (backend enforces), but confusing UX — buttons that always fail.
  - Sübut: app/(dashboard)/tesdiq/page.tsx:471-472 ApprovalActions unconditional; app/(dashboard)/tesdiq/[id]/page.tsx:123-127 ApprovalActions ignores _canApprove computed at :58
  - Fayllar: app/(dashboard)/tesdiq/page.tsx, app/(dashboard)/tesdiq/[id]/page.tsx

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] Priority typo 'yuxsek' in sendAlertToApproval / saveQuickOperation written to tesdiq_telep.prioritet**
  - Detal: sendAlertToApproval sets prioritet via a.seviyye==='yuksek'?'yuxsek' (alerts/actions.ts:204) and saveQuickOperation writes prioritet 'yuxsek' (maliyye/actions.ts:587). The tesdiq stats/dashboard filter on prioritet in ['yuxsek','kritik'] (tesdiq/page.tsx:135, dashboard-queries.ts:74) so these happen to match. But the canonical type union in create.ts is 'yuxsek' too (line 15) while the PRIORITY_CLS/select options also use 'yuxsek' — internally consistent on 'yuxsek' for tesdiq. The risk is the cross-domain confusion with alert seviyye 'yuksek'; flagging as low because the tesdiq side is self-consistent.
  - Sübut: features/alerts/actions.ts:204; features/maliyye/actions.ts:587; features/tesdiq/create.ts:15; app/(dashboard)/tesdiq/page.tsx:135
  - Fayllar: features/alerts/actions.ts, features/tesdiq/create.ts

- ⚪ **[low · ux] Dashboard 'Təsdiq gözləyir' tone is inverted (green when there ARE pending approvals)**
  - Detal: RiskCard for tesdiq_gozleyen uses tone={data.tesdiq_gozleyen > 0 ? 'emerald' : 'neutral'} — i.e. it shows the positive/green tone precisely when there are pending approvals needing attention, and neutral when there are none. Other risk cards use rose/amber for >0. Minor visual inconsistency, not a data bug.
  - Sübut: app/(dashboard)/nezaret-merkezi/page.tsx:88
  - Fayllar: app/(dashboard)/nezaret-merkezi/page.tsx

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] createTaskFromAlert sets no sahibkar context audit and uses unguarded mesul_id without verifying the user belongs to tenant**
  - Detal: createTaskFromAlert accepts an arbitrary mesul_id from the caller and writes it to tapshiriqlar.mesul_id with no validation that the user belongs to the current tenant. Since the alert UI calls it with no mesul_id (defaults to istifadeciId), this is not exploited by the shipped UI, but a direct server-action call could assign a task referencing a foreign user id. Low severity because tapshiriqlar is tenant-scoped on create and a bad mesul_id would just be an orphan reference.
  - Sübut: features/alerts/actions.ts:144-172 (mesul_id: mesul_id ?? istifadeciId, no validation)
  - Fayllar: features/alerts/actions.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 bulkReject backend authorization: updateMany uses unfiltered ids instead of permission/4-eyes-filtered validRejectIds, allowing rejection of unauthorized and own requests — _features/tesdiq/actions.ts:449-450_
- 🔐 alert mutation authorization: acknowledge/resolve/snooze/assign/escalate/markAllRead require only an authenticated tenant session — no role/permission check; /xeberdarliqlar bypasses route-gate — _features/alerts/actions.ts:18-93; lib/auth/route-gate.ts:77; app/api/alerts/mark-all-read/route.ts:6-9_
- 🔐 tesdiq approve UI gating: Approve/Reject buttons shown to users lacking canApproveDocApproval (backend blocks the call, so not a bypass, but UI leaks the capability) — _app/(dashboard)/tesdiq/page.tsx:471-472; app/(dashboard)/tesdiq/[id]/page.tsx:123-127_
- 🔐 cron route auth: CRON_SECRET check is conditional on the env var being set (if(secret)); if CRON_SECRET is unset the cron endpoints that generate alerts/tasks/notifications for ALL tenants are fully public — _app/api/cron/check-overdue-tasks/route.ts:22-23; app/api/cron/finance-triggers/route.ts:22-23_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** features/tesdiq/actions.ts, features/anbar/alert-helpers.ts, features/tapshiriqlar/actions.ts, features/alerts/queries.ts, features/alerts/actions.ts, features/avtomatlasdirma/actions.ts, features/maliyye/triggers.ts, features/nezaret-merkezi/dashboard-queries.ts, app/(dashboard)/tesdiq/page.tsx, app/(dashboard)/tesdiq/[id]/page.tsx

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** No schema change strictly required — alerts.status is a free-form VarChar(20) with no DB enum. Fixing requires normalizing status string literals in code ('hell' -> 'hell_olundu'; remove 'resolved'/'dismissed' usage). Optionally add a CHECK constraint / Prisma enum on alerts.status and alerts.seviyye to prevent future divergence (yuxsek vs yuksek).; Optional: add a unique/idempotency guard or a 'baxilib_tarix' on tesdiq_telep, or rely on a status='gozleyir' guard in code to prevent re-approval.

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** The Prisma tenant extension (lib/db/prisma.ts:62-66) injects `where = { ...where, sahibkar_id }` into update/delete/findUnique whose where is a unique-by-id selector. Whether Prisma accepts a non-unique field in update/delete where (vs requiring updateMany) is version-dependent; AGENTS.md warns this is a non-standard Next/Prisma build. If this silently does NOT scope (or throws), the alert/tesdiq update-by-id actions could be cross-tenant or error — needs runtime confirmation. The tesdiq approval propagation reaches deep into anbar (adjustStock), ticaret (alis/satis status), and balance services (recalculateSupplierBalance) — the non-atomic + non-idempotent approve path means a failure or double-approve there can leave stock, supplier balance, or product state inconsistent with the approval record, affecting the core ERP data integrity outside this module's own tables.

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) bulkReject (critical): change updateMany where to { id: { in: validRejectIds }, status: 'gozleyir' } and log/audit validRejectIds only (mirror bulkApprove). 2) approveRequest (high): add early guard `if (cur?.status !== 'gozleyir') return { ok:false, error:'Yalnız gözləyən sorğu təsdiqlənə bilər' }` before update; make stok_duzelis/mehsul propagation idempotent (re-check current document state before applying). 3) Atomicity (high): wrap the status update + propagateDocumentApproval inside a single prisma.$transaction, and on propagation failure either roll back the status flip or surface an error instead of swallowing; do the same for reject. 4) alert-helpers (high): change status:'hell' -> 'hell_olundu' and also set resolved_by where applicable so auto-cleared stock alerts count as resolved_today. 5) tapshiriqlar dedupe (medium): change status notIn ['resolved','dismissed'] -> status in ['yeni','baxilir'] (open only) so resolved overdue alerts can re-fire. 6) Severity normalization (medium): pick one spelling ('yuksek'), update SEVERITY_RANK / SEV_NEXT / group kritik_count and the avtomatlasdirma+tapshiriqlar writers to emit it; or add 'yuxsek' aliases everywhere. 7) Category-code alignment (medium): make late-employee alert producer emit 'isci_gec'/'isci_devamiyyet' OR change dashboard-queries to read 'isci'. 8) Finance triggers (medium): also insert alerts rows (borc/payout/kassa/maya) so they appear on the risk panel, with 24h idempotency like alert-helpers. 9) Authorization (medium): add a permission check (e.g. nezaret.idare or alert.idare) to alert mutation actions and write audit() entries on resolve/snooze/assign/escalate/markAllRead. 10) UI gating (low): pass canApprove into the list/detail pages and hide ApprovalActions when false. 11) cron (low): require CRON_SECRET unconditionally (fail closed if unset) for the alert/notification-generating routes.

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- As a non-approver (or as the request creator), select several pending requests and click bulk Rədd — confirm whether they get rejected (they should NOT; current code rejects them).
- Approve a 'Stok düzəliş' (stok_duzelis) request, then trigger approveRequest again (double-click / refresh / re-open) — check the warehouse movement log for a duplicate adjustment.
- Configure a stok_duzelis approval, approve it while making adjustStock fail (e.g. invalid anbar) — confirm the request still shows 'Təsdiq' even though no stock moved (silent partial commit).
- Let a product drop below min_stok to create a stok_kritik alert, then restock above min — verify the alert disappears from the open list AND appears in 'Bu gün həll olunan'/resolved counts (currently it won't count as resolved due to status 'hell').
- On the dashboard, create employee-lateness conditions and run automation — confirm 'İşçi davamiyyət' tile stays 0 (category mismatch).
- Let a customer debt pass 60/90 days and run the finance-triggers cron — confirm the risk appears (or not) on /xeberdarliqlar (currently only a bell notification, not an alert).
- Create an alert via automation (severity 'yuxsek'), open /xeberdarliqlar list — verify it does NOT sort above 'info' alerts and is not counted as critical in grouped view.
- As a user without tesdiq.tesdiq permission but with access to the page, confirm Təsdiq/Rədd buttons appear and clicking shows an error toast.

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Confirm Prisma version accepts the injected sahibkar_id in update/delete/findUnique where (unique selector + extra field) and that it actually tenant-scopes vs throwing — affects whether alert/tesdiq update-by-id is cross-tenant safe.
- Confirm whether any avto_qayda rule with action 'xeberdarliq' is actually configured per tenant; without a rule, dashboard service_stalled/mp_pending KPIs are 0 even though their codes match.
- Confirm CRON_SECRET is set in production env (otherwise cron alert-generation endpoints are public).
- Reproduce double-approval: call approveRequest twice on a stok_duzelis request and verify a second stock movement is created (no idempotency guard).
- Reproduce bulkReject permission bypass: as a non-approver, call bulkReject with ids of others' pending requests and confirm they flip to 'red'.
- Verify whether escalateAlert on a 'yuxsek' alert actually downgrades the displayed severity ranking in the list view.

---

## 3. POS / İsti satış

**Xülasə:** POS core happy-path is solid: createSale is properly atomic (single $transaction covering stock decrement with FOR UPDATE + atomic guard, sale header/lines, warehouse movement, cash op, finance op, and customer-balance recalc via the shared source-of-truth), backend permission-gated on pos.satis, audited, and uses race-safe document numbering. However there are several high/critical data-integrity defects. CRITICAL: (1) 'kreditle'/'taksit' payments are silently downgraded to cash (pos-client.tsx:884-891) producing phantom cash + a lost receivable; (2) the offline queue has no server-side idempotency, so a lost response on drain creates duplicate sales. HIGH: the F9/F10/F11 keyboard path bypasses the only double-submit guard (the button disabled state) because onComplete and createSale have no completing/idempotency check; the nisyə credit-limit guard reads kontragentler.borc which POS never updates (real debt lives in alacaq) so the limit is effectively unenforced; the cross-module cancelSale neither reverses finance_operations for cancelled POS sales nor handles POS nisyə correctly (writes a spurious negative cash op because it checks 'borc' not 'nisye'); and openKassa/closeKassa/extras server actions have no permission guard (bypassable by any authenticated tenant user). MEDIUM: session summary cari_bank/cari_borc always read 0 and bank (kecirme) sales vanish from the per-method breakdown due to a key mismatch; all card/bank sales post to the single session cash account instead of a matching account; and a finance_operations write failure is swallowed leaving paid sales missing from reports. Most findings need a live run to confirm exact runtime impact (timing of the double-submit race, behavior of next_sened_nomre PG function, real values of borc vs alacaq in production data).

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** features/pos/sale-action.ts; features/pos/session-actions.ts; features/pos/sale-queries.ts; features/pos/session-queries.ts; features/pos/extras-actions.ts; features/pos/components/pos-client.tsx (payment mapping, onComplete, F9/F10/F11 keyboard handlers, complete button); features/pos/components/offline-banner.tsx; features/pos/components/open-session-card.tsx; features/pos/components/session-bar.tsx; lib/pos/offline-queue.ts; app/pos/page.tsx; app/api/debug/pos/route.ts; lib/db/stock-guards.ts (safeStockDecrement); lib/db/sened-nomre.ts (nextDocNumber); lib/db/with-tenant.ts + lib/db/tenant-context.ts; lib/balance/customer-balance.ts; features/ticaret/access-guard.ts (requireTicaretActionPerm); features/ticaret/customer-tier.ts (checkCustomerCreditLimit); features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts (cancelSale cross-module flow); prisma/schema.prisma (kassa_emeliyyatlari, kassalar models; odenis_nov VarChar — no DB enum constraint); lib/permission-info.ts (pos.* keys)

**Əhatə boşluqları:** quick-products-queries.ts and search-actions.ts (product/search helpers) not opened in detail; close-session-dialog.tsx, quick-create-cash-dialog.tsx, pos-header.tsx, hooks/use-online-status.ts not read line-by-line; lib/stock-change-emitter.ts (emitStockChange side-effects / channel sync) not audited; features/ticaret/discount-approval.ts requestDiscountApproval internals not fully read; lib/balance/account-balance.ts recalculateAccountBalance internals not read; applyBonusToSale (kampaniyalar) loyalty side-effect not audited; vergi-cek API route (app/api/ticaret/vergi-cek) called by POS not opened; market-pos route (app/market-pos) — separate marketplace POS, not part of this scope, not audited

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ createSale wraps stock decrement + sale header/lines + warehouse movement (anbar_hereketleri) + cash op + finance_operations + customer-balance recalc in ONE prisma.$transaction with 20s timeout — atomic, partial failure rolls back — _features/pos/sale-action.ts:106 ($transaction) through :323_
- ✅ Stock decrement is race-safe: FOR UPDATE row lock + availability check (sale-action.ts:121-138) then atomic UPDATE...WHERE miqdar>=qty via safeStockDecrement — _lib/db/stock-guards.ts:30-37; features/pos/sale-action.ts:219-224_
- ✅ createSale backend permission guard enforced: requireTicaretActionPerm(['pos.satis']) before any work; privileged roles bypass — _features/pos/sale-action.ts:55-57; features/ticaret/access-guard.ts:40-49_
- ✅ POS page frontend-gates on pos.access and redirects unauthorized non-owner/admin users — _app/pos/page.tsx:29-37_
- ✅ Nisyə (credit) sale creates NO cash op and NO finance inflow, sets odenilmis=0; cash/card/bank sale sets odenilmis=sonMebleg — _features/pos/sale-action.ts:193, :242 (if odenis_nov !== 'nisye')_
- ✅ Customer balance is recalculated via shared lib/balance/customer-balance.ts source-of-truth (recalculateCustomerBalance) inside the transaction, not ad-hoc per page — _features/pos/sale-action.ts:317-319; lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:118-148_
- ✅ Account balance recalculated from source-of-truth after finance_operations write — _features/pos/sale-action.ts:304-306; recalculateAccountBalance import_
- ✅ Audit log written on every POS sale (audit 'yarat' 'pos_satis') and on kassa open/close (audit 'yarat'/'yenile' 'kassa') — _features/pos/sale-action.ts:335-348; features/pos/session-actions.ts:66-73, :133-142_
- ✅ Sale document number generated race-safe inside tx via nextDocNumber (PG function / counter upsert) — _features/pos/sale-action.ts:160; lib/db/sened-nomre.ts:41-108_
- ✅ Complete button (TAMAMLA / F10 / park / print buttons) disabled while completing transition pending — guards mouse double-click — _features/pos/components/pos-client.tsx:1972, :1962, :1943, :1954_
- ✅ openKassa prevents a second simultaneously-open session for same user — _features/pos/session-actions.ts:37-42_
- ✅ closeKassa expected-cash math correctly nets only odenis_nov='negd' across all op types; refund/cancel writes negative mebleg so it nets out — _features/pos/session-actions.ts:113-119; features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts:278 (mebleg: -sonMebleg)_
- ✅ Live stok table is decremented directly (not derived from sales), so soft-deleted/legv sales never inflate or corrupt on-hand stock — _lib/db/stock-guards.ts:30-37 operates on stok table; customer-balance.ts:69-72 excludes deleted_at/legv_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🔴 **[critical · data-flow] Kreditlə and Taksit payments are silently recorded as CASH — phantom cash + lost receivable**
  - Detal: In the POS client, paymentMethod values 'kreditle' (bank-financed) and 'taksit' (installment) fall through the serverOdenis mapping to 'negd' (cash). createSale then treats them as a paid cash sale: it creates a kassa_emeliyyatlari cash op for the full amount, posts a finance_operations 'daxil' to the cash account, sets odenilmis=sonMebleg, and creates NO customer debt. Result: the cash drawer and finance reports show money that was never received in cash, and any amount the bank/customer still owes (installment) is invisible. The qeyd note records 'Kreditlə/Taksit' text but the financial classification is cash.
  - Sübut: features/pos/components/pos-client.tsx:884-891 (else branch -> 'negd'); consumed at sale-action.ts:242, :248-254, :284-302
  - Fayllar: features/pos/components/pos-client.tsx, features/pos/sale-action.ts

- 🔴 **[critical · data-flow] Offline-queue drain can create DUPLICATE sales — no server-side idempotency**
  - Detal: Offline sales are enqueued in localStorage with a client-side id, but that id is NEVER sent to createSale and createSale has no idempotency/dedupe check (comment in offline-queue.ts:13 says server-side idempotency is a future feature). drainQueue removes an item only when res.ok !== false. If the server commits the sale but the response is lost (network drop, timeout, tab closed mid-await, transaction commit then connection reset), the item stays queued and is re-sent on the next drain -> a second full sale: double stock decrement, double cash/finance entries, duplicate POS receipt number. The 5s auto-drain timer is also independent of the manual Sinxronlaşdır button, so two concurrent drains over the same queue snapshot can each submit the same items.
  - Sübut: lib/pos/offline-queue.ts:13,55-66; features/pos/components/offline-banner.tsx:41-75 (drain loop, removeFromQueue only on success); features/pos/sale-action.ts:50 (no idempotency key param)
  - Fayllar: features/pos/components/offline-banner.tsx, lib/pos/offline-queue.ts, features/pos/sale-action.ts

- 🟠 **[high · functional] F9/F10/F11 keyboard shortcuts bypass the double-submit guard**
  - Detal: onComplete() has no internal early-return on the `completing` transition flag; the only double-submit protection is the disabled attribute on the on-screen buttons. The keyboard handlers for F9 (Tamamla), F10 (çap+növbəti) and F11 (park) only check `cart.length>0 && !insufficient && !success` — they do NOT check `completing`. Pressing F9 twice quickly (common at a fast POS) fires createSale twice before the first transaction resolves; with no server idempotency this produces two sales. success is set only after the awaited createSale returns, so it does not block the second keypress in the race window.
  - Sübut: features/pos/components/pos-client.tsx:815 (onComplete has no completing guard), :1095/:1098/:1101 (keyboard handlers omit completing); :941-952 (success set only post-await)
  - Fayllar: features/pos/components/pos-client.tsx

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Nisyə credit-limit check reads the wrong field (kontragentler.borc) — limit effectively not enforced**
  - Detal: checkCustomerCreditLimit computes current debt from kontragentler.borc, but customer debt is tracked in kontragentler.alacaq (the documented single source of truth; customer-balance.ts only writes alacaq, and a project-wide grep shows borc is written only by supplier-balance.ts for suppliers). For a pure customer, borc is typically 0/stale, so `current` reads ~0 and the limit check almost always passes regardless of real outstanding debt. The POS nisyə credit-limit guard is therefore largely ineffective. (UI debt badge is correct because searchCustomers uses max(alacaq, borc, open-sales), but the guard does not.)
  - Sübut: features/ticaret/customer-tier.ts:77-82 (selects/uses borc); lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:135-141 (writes only alacaq); grep: kontragentler.borc updated only in lib/balance/supplier-balance.ts:91
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/customer-tier.ts, features/pos/sale-action.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] cancelSale does NOT reverse finance_operations for a cancelled POS sale**
  - Detal: A POS cash/card/kecirme sale writes a finance_operations 'daxil' record (the documented source for P&L / cashflow reports). The ticaret cancelSale flow (the cross-module path that voids sales) restores stock, writes a negative kassa_emeliyyatlari, soft-deletes the sale, and recalculates customer balance — but never deletes/reverses the finance_operations row keyed by satis_id. After cancelling a POS sale the cash-drawer view is corrected but finance reports keep counting the revenue and inflow, diverging the two views.
  - Sübut: features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts:246-305 (stock restore + neg cash op + soft-delete + balance recalc; no finance_operations reversal); sale-action.ts:284-302 created the finance row; grep found no finance_operations.delete/update by satis_id in cancel path
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts, features/pos/sale-action.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] cancelSale creates a spurious negative cash reversal for cancelled POS nisyə sales**
  - Detal: cancelSale reverses cash only when sale.odenis_nov !== 'borc'. POS nisyə sales store odenis_nov='nisye' (not 'borc'), so the guard is TRUE and a negative kassa_emeliyyatlari (mebleg=-sonMebleg, odenis_nov='nisye') is written — even though the original nisyə sale never created any cash op (sale-action.ts:242 skips kassa op for nisye). The cash-flow/session aggregates can then be pushed negative by a phantom refund for cash that never entered the drawer.
  - Sübut: features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts:271 (condition uses 'borc' not 'nisye'); features/pos/sale-action.ts:189,:242 (POS uses 'nisye' and creates no cash op for it)
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts, features/pos/sale-action.ts

- 🟠 **[high · permission] openKassa, closeKassa and extras server actions have NO permission guard — bypassable by any authenticated tenant user**
  - Detal: Only createSale calls requireTicaretActionPerm. openKassa/closeKassa (session-actions.ts) and createQuickCustomer/sendVergiCekAction/ensureWarrantyForSale/getQuickProductsAction (extras-actions.ts) only call withTenant/requireTenant, which require an authenticated session but NOT any pos permission. The page redirects users lacking pos.access, but a user without POS permissions can invoke these server actions directly (they are POSTable server actions): open/close a cash session, fire mock tax-check (vergi_cekleri) writes, mint warranty records (zemanetler), and create kontragentler. closeKassa additionally has no role check beyond acan_id ownership for who may close, but anyone can call it for any open kassa they did not open (it returns an error only if acan_id mismatches — so it cannot close others', but the missing pos permission gate still lets non-POS users close their own/created sessions and create the above records).
  - Sübut: features/pos/session-actions.ts:25-93 (openKassa) & :95-146 (closeKassa) — no permission import/check; features/pos/extras-actions.ts:31-201 — no permission check; contrast features/pos/sale-action.ts:55-57
  - Fayllar: features/pos/session-actions.ts, features/pos/extras-actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Session summary cari_bank and cari_borc always show 0; bank-transfer (kecirme) sales vanish from the per-method breakdown**
  - Detal: getActiveKassa aggregates kassa_emeliyyatlari by odenis_nov and maps to cari_negd/cari_kart from keys 'negd'/'kart', but cari_bank from key 'bank' and cari_borc from key 'borc'. createSale never writes 'bank' (it writes 'kecirme' for bank transfer) and never writes 'borc' (nisyə creates no cash op). Therefore the displayed cari_bank and cari_borc are always 0, and kecirme sales are excluded from both cari_kart and cari_bank — the bank amount disappears from the breakdown shown in the POS session stats, even though cemi_satis (which sums all rows) still includes it. Misleading totals for cashier/manager reconciliation.
  - Sübut: features/pos/session-queries.ts:73-76 (reads byPayment.bank / byPayment.borc); features/pos/sale-action.ts:248 (writes odenis_nov='kecirme', never 'bank'); displayed at features/pos/components/pos-client.tsx:2403,:2406-2410
  - Fayllar: features/pos/session-queries.ts, features/pos/sale-action.ts, features/pos/components/pos-client.tsx

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] All non-nisyə payments post to the single session account regardless of cash/card/bank — card and bank sales credit the cash register account**
  - Detal: A session is opened with one maliye_hesab_id (typically the cash account). createSale posts the finance_operations for every non-nisyə sale to kassa.maliye_hesab_id, regardless of whether the customer paid by kart or kecirme. So card and bank-transfer revenue inflate the cash-register account balance instead of a card/POS-acquirer or bank account. This violates the expectation that a card/bank sale credits the matching account, and corrupts per-account balances and bank reconciliation.
  - Sübut: features/pos/sale-action.ts:275-307 (hesabIdForOp = kassa.maliye_hesab_id for all non-nisye); open-session-card.tsx:126-147 (single account chosen at open)
  - Fayllar: features/pos/sale-action.ts, features/pos/components/open-session-card.tsx

- 🟡 **[medium · error] finance_operations failure on a paid POS sale is swallowed — sale closes but revenue missing from reports**
  - Detal: The finance_operations + account recalc block is wrapped in try/catch that only console.warns and records financeOpSkipped; the sale still commits successfully. By design this avoids blocking the sale, but the consequence is a completed cash/card sale whose revenue and cash inflow never reach finance reports (P&L, cashflow) — a silent under-reporting that depends on someone manually reviewing the 'finance_operation_skip' audit entry. The cash-drawer kassa_emeliyyatlari is still written, so kassa close and finance reports will disagree.
  - Sübut: features/pos/sale-action.ts:260-313 (try/catch swallow), :352-363 (audit-only skip record)
  - Fayllar: features/pos/sale-action.ts

- ⚪ **[low · permission] createSale does not verify the kassa belongs to the calling user — can post to a colleague's open drawer**
  - Detal: Inside the transaction the kassa is loaded by id + status='acig' only (tenant-scoped by sahibkar), without checking acan_id === istifadeciId. The page passes the user's own kassaId, but the kassa_id is part of the client payload, so a user with pos.satis could submit a sale against another open session in the same tenant, attributing cash/sales to a colleague's drawer.
  - Sübut: features/pos/sale-action.ts:108-111 (where {id, status:'acig'} — no acan_id check)
  - Fayllar: features/pos/sale-action.ts

- ⚪ **[low · ux] Over-limit discount creates an orphan 'pending-sale' approval request and refuses the sale**
  - Detal: When a line/overall discount exceeds the role cap and override is not set, createSale calls requestDiscountApproval with the literal ref 'pending-sale' and returns an error, so no sale exists. The approval request is created with a placeholder reference that links to no real sale; even if a manager approves it later, there is no mechanism shown that re-applies it to a subsequent sale, leaving dangling approval records and an unclear cashier workflow.
  - Sübut: features/pos/sale-action.ts:88-103 (requestDiscountApproval('pending-sale', ...) then returns ok:false)
  - Fayllar: features/pos/sale-action.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 openKassa / closeKassa permission guard: No pos permission check; only withTenant (auth-only). Any authenticated tenant user can open/close cash sessions regardless of POS permissions. — _features/pos/session-actions.ts:25-146 — no requireTicaretActionPerm / route-gate import_
- 🔐 extras-actions guards (createQuickCustomer, sendVergiCekAction, ensureWarrantyForSale, getQuickProductsAction): No permission check; bypassable by direct server-action invocation by any authenticated user — can create customers, mint tax-check (vergi_cekleri) and warranty (zemanetler) records. — _features/pos/extras-actions.ts:31-201 — only withTenant/requireTenant_
- 🔐 createSale kassa ownership: Loads kassa by id+status='acig' only, no acan_id===istifadeciId check; client-supplied kassa_id lets a user post a sale to another user's open drawer in the same tenant. — _features/pos/sale-action.ts:108-111_
- 🔐 api/debug/pos route exposure: Debug endpoint returns tenant/session internals (sahibkar_id, kassa counts, sahibkar name) to any authenticated user with no permission gate; informational/debug route, low risk but should not ship without gating. — _app/api/debug/pos/route.ts:28-80 — auth() only, no permission check_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** features/pos/components/pos-client.tsx, features/pos/sale-action.ts, features/pos/session-actions.ts, features/pos/extras-actions.ts, features/pos/session-queries.ts, features/pos/components/offline-banner.tsx, lib/pos/offline-queue.ts, features/ticaret/customer-tier.ts, features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** Add an idempotency/dedupe key column on satis_sifarisleri (e.g. unique (sahibkar_id, client_sale_id)) so offline-queue drain and double-submit are deduped at the DB level; Optionally add DB check-constraint or align odenis_nov vocabulary so kassa_emeliyyatlari/session aggregation keys ('bank' vs 'kecirme', 'borc' vs 'nisye') match the values actually written

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** POS feeds satis_sifarisleri, stok, anbar_hereketleri, kassa_emeliyyatlari, finance_operations and kontragentler.alacaq, which are consumed by Maliyyə (kassalar/finance reports), Ticarət (sale detail, cancel/return), Anbar (stock/movements), Hesabatlar (P&L/cashflow built on finance_operations), and customer/debitor balances. The most consequential cross-module risks: (1) finance_operations is the reporting source-of-truth but POS card/bank sales all credit the single session cash account, and cancelSale never reverses finance_operations — so finance reports diverge from the cash drawer and from reality; (2) POS nisyə sales use odenis_nov='nisye' while ticaret cancelSale branches on 'borc', producing a spurious negative cash reversal cross-module; (3) credit-limit enforcement reads kontragentler.borc (supplier field) instead of the alacaq source-of-truth that POS/customer-balance maintains, so the limit guard is decoupled from actual customer debt; (4) kreditle/taksit cash-misclassification means receivables that should appear in debitor/finance modules never get created. Stock itself is consistent across modules because all paths decrement the live stok table via the shared safeStockDecrement guard.

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) Payment classification: in pos-client onComplete, map 'kreditle'/'taksit' to a dedicated server odenis_nov (e.g. 'kredit'/'taksit' or 'nisye' with a financing flag) instead of falling back to 'negd'; extend createSale to treat financed/installment sales as a receivable (no cash op, odenilmis=0, create customer debt / kredit_satis record) rather than cash. 2) Idempotency: add an optional client_sale_id to CreateSaleInput, pass the offline-queue item id (and a fresh uuid for online F9), and short-circuit in createSale if a sale with that key already exists (DB unique constraint); have drainQueue send the id. 3) Double-submit: add `if (completing) return;` at the top of onComplete and guard F9/F10/F11 handlers with `!completing`. 4) Credit limit: change checkCustomerCreditLimit to compute current debt from calculateCustomerBalance (alacaq source-of-truth), not kontragentler.borc. 5) cancelSale: reverse finance_operations for the cancelled sale (delete/negate by satis_id) inside the same transaction, and change the cash-reversal guard from `!== 'borc'` to also exclude 'nisye' (only reverse cash when an actual cash op existed). 6) Permissions: add requireTicaretActionPerm gates to openKassa/closeKassa (e.g. 'pos.access') and to extras-actions mutations; add acan_id ownership check in createSale's kassa lookup. 7) Session breakdown: in getActiveKassa, read the 'kecirme' key (or fold it into a bank bucket) and drop the dead 'bank'/'borc' keys, or compute cari_borc from open nisyə sales; align UI labels. 8) finance_operations failure: surface skipped finance op more loudly (e.g. mark sale needs-review) instead of silent under-reporting.

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- Ring up a sale with payment 'Kreditlə' (and separately 'Taksit'); then check Maliyyə kassa + finance reports — verify it is NOT counted as cash and that a receivable/financing record exists (currently it appears as cash).
- Go offline (devtools), complete 2-3 sales (they queue), come back online; while drain runs, kill the network just as the first response would return, then re-drain — verify NO duplicate sale/receipt is created.
- At the TAMAMLA step, mash the F9 key twice very fast — verify only ONE sale and one receipt number are created.
- Set a customer borc_limiti, give them existing nisyə debt, then attempt a new nisyə POS sale exceeding the limit — verify it is blocked (currently the limit reads borc=0 and likely allows it).
- Complete a cash POS sale, then cancel it from Ticarət; verify finance reports (P&L/cashflow) no longer count its revenue/inflow and that the cash drawer and finance views agree.
- Complete a nisyə POS sale, then cancel it; verify no negative cash entry is created for a sale that never put cash in the drawer.
