- As a user WITHOUT pos.access, attempt to invoke openKassa/closeKassa/sendVergiCek/ensureWarranty directly (e.g. via a crafted request) — verify they are rejected (currently they succeed).
- Do a 'Bank' (kecirme) POS sale and open the POS session stats — verify the bank amount shows in the per-method breakdown (currently cari_bank shows 0 and the amount disappears).
- Do card and bank-transfer POS sales in a cash-account session — verify each credits the correct account, not the cash register (currently both hit the single session account).

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Exact double-submit race window: confirm whether rapid F9 presses actually fire two createSale calls before `completing` re-renders (React transition timing) and before `success` is set
- Whether next_sened_nomre PG function and sened_nomre_counter table exist in the deployed DB (otherwise nextDocNumber falls back to non-race-safe max+1, risking duplicate sale numbers under concurrency)
- Production values of kontragentler.borc vs alacaq for customers — confirm borc is effectively 0/stale so the credit-limit guard is bypassed in practice
- End-to-end cancel of a POS cash sale to confirm finance_operations row persists in reports after cancellation (finance report divergence)
- Whether 'kreditle'/'taksit' are actually reachable in production POS config (PAYMENT_OPTIONS rendering) — if exposed, confirm the cash misclassification occurs
- Offline drain duplicate scenario: simulate server-commit-then-lost-response to confirm a duplicate sale is created on re-drain
- Confirm whether any middleware/route-gate intercepts server-action POSTs to openKassa/closeKassa/extras before they run (could mitigate the missing in-action guards)

---

## 4. Ticarət

**Xülasə:** The Ticarət sale-CREATION paths (createOrUpdateSatisYeni, createMarketSatis, createPurchase, convertTeklifToSale) and cancelPurchase are well-engineered: atomic $transactions, race-safe stock guards, FOR UPDATE locks for idempotency, source-of-truth balance recalcs, audit logs, and proper permission + soft-delete handling. The serious problems are concentrated in the PAYMENT and CANCEL/RETURN lifecycle, all sharing one root cause: account balances and finance/cash reports derive solely from finance_operations, but recordSalePayment, applySplitPayment, acceptKreditPayment write only to kassa_emeliyyatlari (or nothing), and cancelSale never reverses the sale's finance_operations row. The result is silent, ongoing divergence between Ticarət and Maliyyə/cash reports after any post-creation payment or any sale cancellation (critical). On top of that: the classic return flow (features/qaytarma/actions.ts) is entirely unguarded permission-wise and never links original_id (so accepted returns restock goods without clearing customer debt); the three export API routes skip the module read-permission check; satis lines don't persist per-line anbar_id, so multi-warehouse cancels/returns restock to the wrong warehouse; recordKreditPayment manually increments account qaliq causing drift; and the operation-detail payments panel is effectively always empty due to a type_kod mismatch. Customer balances themselves are robust (always recalculated from the sale source-of-truth), which limits debitor-side damage except for the original_id return gap.

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts; features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts; features/ticaret/satis-queries.ts; features/ticaret/teklif-actions.ts; features/ticaret/market-satis-action.ts; features/ticaret/split-payment-actions.ts; features/ticaret/kredit-actions.ts; features/ticaret/alis-actions.ts; features/ticaret/alis-payment-action.ts; features/ticaret/operation-detail-action.ts; features/ticaret/access-guard.ts; features/ticaret/qaytarma-tez-actions.ts; features/qaytarma/actions.ts; lib/balance/customer-balance.ts; lib/balance/account-balance.ts (partial); features/maliyye/kassa-queries.ts (partial); app/(dashboard)/ticaret/satislar/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/ticaret/qaytarma/page.tsx (partial); app/api/ticaret/satis-export/route.ts; app/api/ticaret/qaytarma-export/route.ts (guard lines); app/api/ticaret/alis-export/route.ts (guard lines); prisma/schema.prisma (satis_sifarisleri, satis_sifaris_satirlari, qaytarma_sifarisleri models); features/ticaret/extra-actions.ts (bulkCancelSales + survey); features/ticaret/components/quick-return-scanner.tsx (wiring); features/qaytarma/components/* (wiring)

**Əhatə boşluqları:** features/ticaret/extra-actions.ts beyond bulkCancelSales (emergencyFreezeSales, exportSalesCsv, analytics/forecast queries) not deeply read; features/ticaret/kredit-yeni-actions.ts (createKreditSatis full flow) not opened; features/ticaret/teklif-bron-actions.ts and stok_bron reservation lifecycle not audited; features/satinalma/* (proposal-actions, recompute-action, auto-order) not opened — purchase-planning side of scope; features/ticaret/emeliyyat-queries.ts and operations-table delete/bulk wiring not fully traced; satis-yeni client component validation/disabled-state (double-submit protection on the UI) not inspected; lib/db/stock-guards.ts (safeStockDecrement/stockIncrement) internals assumed correct, not read; commission-actions.ts, discount-approval.ts internals not opened; API route app/api/v1/marketplace/orders/[kanal]/route.ts (inbound order ingestion) auth not reviewed

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ Sale creation (createOrUpdateSatisYeni) is atomic: stock decrement (race-safe safeStockDecrement) + anbar_hereketleri + kassa_emeliyyatlari + finance_operations + account-balance recalc + customer-balance recalc all inside one $transaction; partial failure rolls back. — _satis-yeni-actions.ts:184-491 ($transaction), :318-341 (stock), :427-452 (finance_operations + recalculateAccountBalance), :463-466 (recalculateCustomerBalance)_
- ✅ Credit (nisye) sale creates NO cash inflow and increases debitor balance; cash/card/bank credits the matching account. odenilen=0 default for nisye, finalOdenisNov forced to nisye for partial/credit so customer-balance picks it up. — _satis-yeni-actions.ts:351-372 (defaultOdenilen nisye=0, kassa op only when odenilen>0), :463-466_
- ✅ Sale list excludes drafts and soft-deleted by default; stats exclude legv/qaytarilib/deleted. — _satis-queries.ts:151 (qaralama not true), :157-161 (deleted_at null default), :103-105 (stats exclude legv/qaytarilib/deleted)_
- ✅ Customer balance is a single source-of-truth derived from satis_sifarisleri (excludes legv/qaytarilib/deleted/draft, only nisye/borc) plus servis; all mutation paths call recalculateCustomerBalance instead of manual increments. — _lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:54-108; called from satis-actions.ts:124, satis-yeni-actions.ts:465, qaytarma-tez-actions.ts:259/535_
- ✅ cancelPurchase is the gold-standard reversal: reverses stock with negative-stock guards/blockers, sets linked finance_operations to status=legv and recalculates account balance from source-of-truth, soft-deletes, recalcs supplier balance, FOR UPDATE lock for idempotency. — _alis-actions.ts:417-536 (esp. :493-515 finance reversal + recalc, :420-422 FOR UPDATE)_
- ✅ createPurchase atomic: pay_now checks account sufficiency before paying, creates finance_operations mexaric + recalcs account; otherwise creates supplier debt; receive_now stocks with real_maya_eded COGS. Permission guarded (alis.yarat). — _alis-actions.ts:44-46, :60-234, :177-216 (sufficiency + finance + recalc)_
- ✅ receivePurchase uses FOR UPDATE lock to prevent double-medaxil on parallel/double-click; status guard gozlemede only. — _alis-actions.ts:310-317_
- ✅ createMarketSatis locks stok rows FOR UPDATE, verifies availability, single $transaction, dedupes external order code, creates pending marketplace payout (not immediate cash). Permission guarded. — _market-satis-action.ts:74-75, :127-152 (FOR UPDATE), :89-104 (dedupe), :241-255 (pending payout)_
- ✅ Quick-return paths (fastReturn, returnFullSale) are permission-guarded (qaytarma.yarat), set original_id, restock, write anbar_hereketleri, reverse marketplace finance proportionally, and recalc customer balance — all in $transaction. — _qaytarma-tez-actions.ts:113-114, :166/415 (original_id), :456-504 (proportional marketplace reverse)_
- ✅ Teklif (offer) creation does not touch stock/cash/debt; status transitions are validated via a transition map; convertTeklifToSale guards against double-convert (t.satish_id) and decrements stock atomically. — _teklif-actions.ts:28-44 (transition map), :84-86 (perm), :263 (double-convert guard), :300-336 (stock decrement)_
- ✅ recordSalePayment uses FOR UPDATE lock and rejects overpayment (mebleg > qaliq), preventing concurrent over-payment. — _satis-actions.ts:44-50 (FOR UPDATE), :70-72 (overpay guard)_
- ✅ Sale list page enforces satis.oxu and gates pay/cancel UI by permission; soft-delete record-status filter gated by canSeeDeleted. — _satislar/page.tsx:110-113, :118-121_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🔴 **[critical · data-flow] cancelSale does NOT reverse the sale's finance_operations row → account/bank balance keeps the cancelled sale's inflow**
  - Detal: On finalize, createOrUpdateSatisYeni writes a finance_operations 'qaime' daxil row (with satis_id) and recalculates the account balance from that ledger. cancelSale only writes a reversing kassa_emeliyyatlari row and recalculates the CUSTOMER balance — it never queries/legv-marks the finance_operations row tied to satis_id, and never calls recalculateAccountBalance. Since maliye_hesablari.qaliq is derived ONLY from finance_operations (account-balance.ts has zero kassa_emeliyyatlari references), the cash/bank account balance and all finance/cash reports still include the cancelled sale's money. Contrast cancelPurchase which does this correctly. bulkCancelSales inherits the bug.
  - Sübut: satis-actions.ts:203-371 (no finance_operations query/reversal, no recalculateAccountBalance); cf. satis-yeni-actions.ts:427-452 (creates the op + recalc); account-balance.ts:50-80 (balance from finance_operations only)
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts, lib/balance/account-balance.ts, features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts

- 🔴 **[critical · data-flow] recordSalePayment writes only kassa_emeliyyatlari, never finance_operations → later sale payments invisible to account balance & finance/cash reports**
  - Detal: When a customer later pays down a sale, recordSalePayment creates a kassa_emeliyyatlari row and bumps satis.odenilmis (customer balance recalc is correct), but creates NO finance_operations row and never calls recalculateAccountBalance. Account balances and finance ledger are derived from finance_operations only, so the incoming cash is never reflected in the cash/bank account or finance reports. Money physically received goes untracked in the accounting ledger.
  - Sübut: satis-actions.ts:90-127 (only kassa_emeliyyatlari.create + odenilmis increment; grep finance_operations in satis-actions.ts = 0 hits); account-balance.ts has no kassa_emeliyyatlari reference
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts, lib/balance/account-balance.ts

- 🔴 **[critical · permission] acceptReturn / createReturn / cancelReturn (classic return flow) have NO permission guard — direct server-action call bypasses all permissions**
  - Detal: features/qaytarma/actions.ts exports createReturn, acceptReturn, cancelReturn with no requireTicaretActionPerm / route-gate. acceptReturn applies stock movement (restock or decrement via safeStockDecrement), reduces original sale son_mebleg/odenilmis and recalculates customer balance — i.e. real financial + inventory mutation. Any authenticated user (even without any qaytarma permission) can invoke these directly. The page (qaytarma/page.tsx) gates qaytarma.oxu but the server actions themselves are unguarded. The quick-return actions (qaytarma-tez-actions.ts) DO guard, so this is inconsistent.
  - Sübut: features/qaytarma/actions.ts:47-55 (createReturn no perm), :143 (acceptReturn no perm), :277 (cancelReturn no perm); contrast qaytarma-tez-actions.ts:113-114, :346-347
  - Fayllar: features/qaytarma/actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] acceptKreditPayment records a credit-sale payment with ZERO finance/cash entry**
  - Detal: acceptKreditPayment only increments satis.odenilmis. It writes no kassa_emeliyyatlari and no finance_operations, and calls no account-balance recalc. Customer debt drops correctly but the cash/bank account that received the payment is never credited anywhere in the ledger — the received money is entirely untracked in finance. (kredit-actions.ts finance_operations references are all inside recordKreditPayment, not this function.)
  - Sübut: kredit-actions.ts:18-64 (only odenilmis update; no kassa/finance write)
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/kredit-actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Multi-warehouse sale cancel restocks to wrong warehouse (line anbar_id not persisted)**
  - Detal: satis_sifaris_satirlari has no anbar_id column (schema:5147-5164). createOrUpdateSatisYeni decrements stock per line.anbar_id (multi-warehouse) but stores only data.lines[0].anbar_id as the sale header anbar_id. cancelSale restores ALL lines to sale.anbar_id (the primary), so a line that was deducted from warehouse B is restored to warehouse A on cancel → permanent per-warehouse stock corruption for any multi-warehouse sale. returnFullSale has the same single-anbar assumption.
  - Sübut: schema.prisma:5147-5164 (line model lacks anbar_id); satis-yeni-actions.ts:248 (primaryAnbar=lines[0]), :318-322 (decrement per line.anbar_id); satis-actions.ts:250-253 (restore to sale.anbar_id for every line)
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts, features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts, prisma/schema.prisma

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Classic createReturn never sets original_id → acceptReturn never corrects the original sale or customer debt**
  - Detal: createReturn (features/qaytarma/actions.ts) builds the qaytarma row without original_id. acceptReturn's sale-correction and recalculateCustomerBalance blocks are guarded by `if (ret.nov === 'satis_qaytarma' && ret.original_id)`. Since original_id is always null for returns made via the wired new-return dialog, accepting a customer return restocks goods but does NOT reduce the customer's outstanding debt or adjust the original sale's son_mebleg — the debt persists even though goods came back.
  - Sübut: features/qaytarma/actions.ts:62-84 (no original_id on create), :208 (correction gated on ret.original_id), :237 (supplier side same); new-return-dialog.tsx:98 calls createReturn
  - Fayllar: features/qaytarma/actions.ts, features/qaytarma/components/new-return-dialog.tsx

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] cancelSale reverses full son_mebleg to the cash drawer regardless of how much was actually paid**
  - Detal: The kassa_emeliyyatlari reversal uses mebleg = -sonMebleg, but for a partially-paid sale only odenilmis was ever taken in. Reversing the full son_mebleg over-credits the drawer reversal by (son_mebleg - odenilmis). Also the condition `sale.odenis_nov !== 'borc'` will MISS credit sales whose odenis_nov was set to 'nisye' on finalize (finalize uses 'nisye', not 'borc'), so a partially-paid nisye sale that did take cash gets no kassa reversal at all. Combined with the finance_operations gap above, cash records after cancel are unreliable.
  - Sübut: satis-actions.ts:271-285 (mebleg=-sonMebleg; guard on odenis_nov !== 'borc'); satis-yeni-actions.ts:358 (finalOdenisNov='nisye' for non-full-paid)
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] applySplitPayment writes only kassa_emeliyyatlari, never finance_operations → split cash/card payments missing from account balance**
  - Detal: Same root cause as recordSalePayment: non-nisye split parts create kassa_emeliyyatlari rows and bump odenilmis, but create no finance_operations and no account recalc, so the cash/card account balance and finance reports never see the split payment.
  - Sübut: split-payment-actions.ts:73-96 (only kassa_emeliyyatlari + odenilmis; grep finance_operations = 0)
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/split-payment-actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] Sale/return/purchase export API routes only check session, not module read permission**
  - Detal: GET /api/ticaret/satis-export, qaytarma-export, alis-export gate on session?.user only; they do not verify satis.oxu / qaytarma.oxu / alis.oxu. They use withTenant (no cross-tenant leak) but a low-privilege user within the tenant (e.g. warehouse-only) can export full sales/returns/purchase financials (up to 5000 rows incl. customer + amounts), bypassing the UI page permission gate.
  - Sübut: satis-export/route.ts:15-39 (only 401 on no session); qaytarma-export/route.ts:21-22; alis-export/route.ts:11-12
  - Fayllar: app/api/ticaret/satis-export/route.ts, app/api/ticaret/qaytarma-export/route.ts, app/api/ticaret/alis-export/route.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] recordKreditPayment manually increments maliye_hesablari.qaliq instead of source-of-truth recalc (double-count / drift risk)**
  - Detal: recordKreditPayment creates a finance_operations daxil row AND ALSO does maliye_hesablari.updateMany({ qaliq: { increment: tutulan } }). Account balance is supposed to be derived solely from finance_operations via recalculateAccountBalance. This manual increment means: (a) the qaliq is double-applied relative to the canonical formula, and (b) the next time anything calls recalculateAccountBalance on that account, the manual increment is overwritten/reconciled — producing a balance jump/drift. The codebase elsewhere explicitly warns against manual increments.
  - Sübut: kredit-actions.ts:137-161 (finance_operations.create THEN maliye_hesablari.updateMany increment); cf. account-balance.ts derive-only model
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/kredit-actions.ts, lib/balance/account-balance.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · ux] Operation detail payments panel never shows sale payments (type_kod mismatch)**
  - Detal: getOperationDetailAction lists sale 'payments' from finance_operations where type_kod IN ('satis_odenis','musteri_odenis','nisye_odenis'). But the only finance_operations a sale ever creates is type_kod='qaime' (from createOrUpdateSatisYeni / recordKreditPayment); recordSalePayment & applySplitPayment write to kassa_emeliyyatlari (not finance_operations at all). So the payments panel on the sale detail page will be empty for virtually all sales, hiding payment history from the operator.
  - Sübut: operation-detail-action.ts:86-106 (type_kod filter); satis-yeni-actions.ts:400/427 (creates type_kod='qaime'); satis-actions.ts recordSalePayment writes kassa only
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/operation-detail-action.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Customer cash refund on accepted/full returns is not recorded as cash out**
  - Detal: For a fully-paid CASH sale, acceptReturn (classic) caps odenilmis down to the new son_mebleg with no offsetting kassa/finance refund entry (comment even references a 'separate kassa op' that is never created). returnFullSale only creates a cash refund for marketplace sales (komisyon path); a normal cash sale fully returned restocks goods and flips status to 'qaytarilib' but issues no cash-out record, so the drawer/account overstates cash by the refunded amount.
  - Sübut: features/qaytarma/actions.ts:218-227 (odenilmis capped, no cash-out); qaytarma-tez-actions.ts:506-510 (fullReturn just sets status, cash refund only in marketplace block :461-504)
  - Fayllar: features/qaytarma/actions.ts, features/ticaret/qaytarma-tez-actions.ts

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] convertTeklifToSale deducts stock but records no payment and no debt**
  - Detal: Converting an offer to a sale creates the sale with odenis_nov='negd', odenilmis=0 and decrements stock. Because customer-balance only counts nisye/borc sales, this produces no debt; and no finance_operations/kassa entry is created, so no cash inflow either. Net effect: goods leave inventory with zero financial record until a separate payment is made. It is an intentional design (comment acknowledges it) but creates a window where stock is gone yet revenue/debt is unrecorded.
  - Sübut: teklif-actions.ts:277-336 (status='yeni', odenilmis=0, stock decrement), :343-349 (recalc only)
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/teklif-actions.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 qaytarma.* on classic return server actions: createReturn / acceptReturn / cancelReturn have no requireTicaretActionPerm guard; direct invocation mutates stock + customer balance with no permission — _features/qaytarma/actions.ts:47, :143, :277_
- 🔐 satis.oxu / qaytarma.oxu / alis.oxu on export endpoints: Export API routes only verify a logged-in session, not the module read permission, so any tenant user can export full financial lists — _app/api/ticaret/satis-export/route.ts:16-17; qaytarma-export/route.ts:21-22; alis-export/route.ts:11-12_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts, features/qaytarma/actions.ts, features/ticaret/split-payment-actions.ts, features/ticaret/kredit-actions.ts, features/ticaret/operation-detail-action.ts, app/api/ticaret/satis-export/route.ts, app/api/ticaret/qaytarma-export/route.ts, app/api/ticaret/alis-export/route.ts, features/ticaret/qaytarma-tez-actions.ts, prisma/schema.prisma

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** Add anbar_id (Int) to satis_sifaris_satirlari so per-line warehouse is persisted; cancelSale/returnFullSale must restock to the line's actual warehouse, not the header anbar_id

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** Ticarət writes into Maliyyə (finance_operations / maliye_hesablari), Anbar (stok / anbar_hereketleri), Kassa (kassa_emeliyyatlari), Elaqe (kontragentler.alacaq/borc), Tesdiq (approval requests) and marketplace payouts. The most damaging cross-module risk is ledger divergence: account balances and cash/finance reports are derived ONLY from finance_operations, but several ticaret payment/cancel paths write to kassa_emeliyyatlari (or nothing) instead — so Maliyyə and cash reports silently drift from physical reality after any sale payment, split payment, credit payment, or sale cancellation. Customer balances are safer because every path recalculates from the satis source-of-truth, but the classic return flow's missing original_id leaves debitor balances stale after returns. Multi-warehouse cancel/return corrupts Anbar per-warehouse stock totals (used by POS/marketplace/reorder).

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) cancelSale: inside the transaction, query finance_operations where satis_id=sale.id AND status='aktiv', set them to status='legv', collect hesab_ids, and call recalculateAccountBalance(hid, tx) for each (mirror cancelPurchase:493-515). Reverse cash to the drawer only for the amount actually received (odenilmis) and fix the odenis_nov guard to include 'nisye'. 2) recordSalePayment, applySplitPayment, acceptKreditPayment: in addition to (or instead of) the kassa_emeliyyatlari row, create a finance_operations daxil row tied to the resolved hesab_id and call recalculateAccountBalance so the account/finance ledger reflects incoming cash — matching the creation path in satis-yeni-actions.ts:427-452. 3) features/qaytarma/actions.ts: add requireTicaretActionPerm guards — createReturn -> 'qaytarma.yarat'; acceptReturn -> 'qaytarma.tesdiq'/'qaytarma.idare'; cancelReturn -> 'qaytarma.legv'/'qaytarma.idare'. Also persist original_id in createReturn (resolve the source sale) so acceptReturn corrects the sale and customer debt, and add a cash-out entry for paid cash sales. 4) Export routes: add a getRequestPermissions() check for the corresponding *.oxu (or export) permission, returning 403 when missing. 5) recordKreditPayment: drop the manual maliye_hesablari.qaliq increment; rely on recalculateAccountBalance after creating the finance_operations row. 6) operation-detail-action.ts: include type_kod='qaime' (and/or read kassa_emeliyyatlari) so the payments panel is populated. 7) Persist per-line anbar_id (schema migration) and update cancelSale/returnFullSale restock to use it.

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- Cash sale 100 AZN fully paid -> note cash account qaliq -> cancel sale -> verify cash account qaliq returns to pre-sale value (currently expected to FAIL: stays inflated).
- Nisye sale 100 AZN, pay 30 later via recordSalePayment -> check /maliyye and cash report show the 30 AZN inflow (expected FAIL).
- Two-warehouse sale (line1 anbar A, line2 anbar B) -> cancel -> verify stock A and B each restored correctly (expected FAIL: both restored to A).
- As a user with only anbar permissions, open /api/ticaret/satis-export -> verify access is denied (expected FAIL: file downloads).
- As a user without qaytarma permission, call acceptReturn directly -> verify rejected (expected FAIL: succeeds, restocks).
- Create classic return via new-return dialog for a nisye sale, accept it -> verify customer debt drops (expected FAIL: debt unchanged because original_id null).
- 2 AZN + 100 AZN open nisye invoices, customer pays 10 AZN -> verify allocation: per-sale payment model requires picking a sale; confirm there is no customer-level FIFO auto-allocation that would mis-route surplus to advance.
- Credit sale, record payment via acceptKreditPayment -> verify the receiving bank/cash account is credited somewhere in finance (expected FAIL: no finance/kassa entry).

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Confirm at runtime that after cancelSale the cash/bank account qaliq still shows the (uncancelled) inflow — reproduce: cash sale -> cancel -> check maliye_hesablari.qaliq / cash report.
- Confirm recordSalePayment / applySplitPayment / acceptKreditPayment do not appear in /maliyye/emeliyyat or hesabatlar/pul (cash report) after a payment.
- Confirm a non-permission user can POST to /api/ticaret/satis-export and receive the xlsx.
- Confirm acceptReturn can be invoked by a user lacking qaytarma perms (server-action POST) and that it restocks without reducing customer debt (because original_id is null).
- Reproduce multi-warehouse sale (two lines, two anbars) -> cancel -> verify both quantities land back in the header's primary anbar, not their originals.
- Verify recordKreditPayment account qaliq value vs recalculateAccountBalance result (expect a discrepancy/double-count).

---

## 5. Anbar

**Xülasə:** Anbar's core stock engine is fundamentally sound: a signed anbar_hereketleri ledger as source-of-truth, race-safe atomic decrement/increment guards, $transaction-wrapped stock mutations, correct product soft-delete with a stock blocker and audit, a DB trigger keeping stok_cemi in sync, and proper bron release on the normal draft->sale conversion. However I found systemic data-integrity and permission defects. The most serious are: (1) a pervasive permission-catalog vs enforcement mismatch — defekt, bron, inventory, konsiqnasiya, bulk-product and price actions check permission strings (anbar.idare/stok.idare/bron.idare/inventar.idare/konsiqnasiya.idare/mehsul.idare/qiymet.idare/stok.duzelt) that are not in the assignable catalog, so staff delegation is broken and only owners/admins can run them; (2) divergent available-stock math where the product list and POS/new-sale context subtract reserves WITHOUT excluding expired brons, unlike every canonical query; (3) bulk product 'sil' is a fake soft-delete that also skips the stock-remaining blocker; (4) defekts can be recorded without removing stock, leaving damaged goods sellable; (5) cancelSale mis-restores multi-warehouse stock to the header warehouse; (6) acceptTransfer can double-decrement a partially-received transfer. Plus medium/low issues: no available-stock check on bron creation (over-reservation), inventory completion lost-update against a stale snapshot, missing audit logs on transfer/bron/konsiqnasiya, an unguarded /anbar/defekt read page, two conflicting expired-bron status strings, and inconsistent CURRENT_DATE vs now() reserve windows. All findings are code-grounded; trigger application, changeSaleStatus draft exploitability, and tenant-scoping of id-based updates need live confirmation.

**1. **Əhatə boşluqları:** features/anbar/hesabat/queries.ts and /anbar/hesabat page (warehouse reports) — not opened; report-level stock/value consistency vs stok-queries not verified.; features/anbar/qiymet/* (price rules/bulk adjust actions) — not deeply reviewed beyond quick-fix price actions.; features/anbar/import/import-actions.ts and /anbar/mehsul-yukle — stock-on-import behavior (does Excel import create medaxil movements / set stok atomically?) not reviewed.; features/anbar/transfer/stock-lookup-action.ts — not opened.; features/anbar/performance-queries.ts, product-history(-actions).ts, attention-queries.ts, alert-helpers.ts — not reviewed.; Servis (service) stock interaction: servis_freeze/servis_mexaric/servis_iade movement types appear in product-stock.ts CASE but the servis module actions that emit them are out of this scope and not verified.; etiket-cap, kateqoriyalar, markalar pages and their actions — only brand list touched; full review not done.; Concurrency of completeInventar vs adjustStock/transfer at row level (true lost-update reproduction) requires runtime/DB-level verification.

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ Single source-of-truth ledger for stock drift: calculateProductStock + scanStockDrift compute SUM over anbar_hereketleri with consistent signed nov sets, and the canonical raw-SQL available-stock queries all exclude expired brons (status='aktiv' AND (bitme_tarixi IS NULL OR bitme_tarixi >= CURRENT_DATE)). — _lib/balance/product-stock.ts:48-91, stok-queries.ts:149-155, queries.ts:259-269, problem-stats-queries.ts:52-57_
- ✅ Race-safe stock decrement: safeStockDecrement uses atomic UPDATE ... WHERE miqdar >= n and refuses on 0 rows, preventing negative stock from concurrent sales/transfers; stockIncrement uses upsert (additive, race-safe). — _lib/db/stock-guards.ts:21-83_
- ✅ adjustStock / transferStock wrap stock change + anbar_hereketleri in a single $transaction, write audit logs, and emit stock-change events. — _features/anbar/stock-actions.ts:125-198 (audit:179), :225-289 (audit:281)_
- ✅ Transfer accept and partial accept use safeStockDecrement at source + upsert at destination + paired transfer_cixis/transfer_giris movements inside one $transaction. — _features/anbar/transfer/actions.ts:98-176, :220-324_
- ✅ Product soft-delete is correct: deleteProduct blocks when stock>0 (unless force), sets aktiv=false + deleted_at + deleted_by + delete_reason and audits; stock queries filter m.aktiv=TRUE so soft-deleted products are excluded from active stock lists/value KPIs; restoreProduct re-sets aktiv=true + deleted_at=null. — _features/anbar/actions.ts:347-401, :406-443; stok-queries.ts:199; queries.ts:391-397_
- ✅ stok_cemi denormalized column is maintained by a DB trigger (AFTER INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE on stok), keeping getProducts sort (stok_cemi) consistent with displayed SUM(stok.miqdar). — _scripts/migrations/2026-05-26-stok-cemi-denormalize.sql:31-54_
- ✅ Draft sale with reserve (stok_bron) is correctly released on conversion: createOrUpdateSatisYeni deletes the draft's bron and then decrements stock, so no double subtraction in the normal draft->sale flow. — _features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts:222-224, :314-341_
- ✅ completeInventar wraps stock force-set + inventar_artim/azalma movement + variance expense (xercl_r) write in one $transaction, guards inv.status==='aktiv', requires variance reasons, and audits. — _features/anbar/inventar/actions.ts:242-347, status guard :248, audit :371_
- ✅ Negative/zero-stock anomalies are surfaced for reconciliation on the anomali page (defensive detection of integrity errors). — _app/(dashboard)/anbar/anomali/page.tsx:33-50_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🟠 **[high · permission] Permission catalog vs enforcement mismatch blocks all staff delegation for defekt/bron/inventory/konsiqnasiya/bulk-product/price actions**
  - Detal: Server actions check permission strings that do NOT exist in the assignable permission catalog (lib/permission-info.ts) nor the anbar permission seed. recordDefekt/resolveDefekt require ['stok.duzelt','anbar.idare']; createBron/cancelBron require ['bron.idare','stok.idare','anbar.idare']; createInventar/completeInventar/updateInventarRow require ['inventar.idare','stok.idare','anbar.idare']; konsiqnasiya actions require ['konsiqnasiya.idare','anbar.idare']; bulkUpdateProducts requires ['mehsul.idare']; setProductCost/setProductSalePrice require ['qiymet.idare','mehsul.idare']. None of stok.duzelt / anbar.idare / bron.idare / stok.idare / inventar.idare / konsiqnasiya.idare / mehsul.idare / qiymet.idare are defined/seeded (catalog has stok.duzelis, stok.bron, sayim.yarat, konsiqnasiya.oxu, mehsul.duzelt, qiymet.duzelt). Net effect: only sahibkar/admin/owner (who bypass via isAnbarPrivileged) can run these mutations; a staff member granted the matching page-read perm (e.g. stok.bron opens /anbar/bron) gets 'icazə lazımdır' on every action. Delegation is effectively broken and the granular perms are dead.
  - Sübut: bron/actions.ts:29,76; inventar/actions.ts:52,143,164,215,390; defekt-actions.ts:41,163; konsiqnasiya/actions.ts:21,119,196,238; actions.ts:495 (mehsul.idare); quick-fix-actions.ts:56,88 (qiymet.idare); vs lib/permission-info.ts:235-276 and scripts/migrations/2026-06-01-anbar-permissions.sql perm list
  - Fayllar: features/anbar/bron/actions.ts, features/anbar/inventar/actions.ts, features/anbar/defekt-actions.ts, features/anbar/konsiqnasiya/actions.ts, features/anbar/actions.ts, features/anbar/quick-fix-actions.ts, lib/permission-info.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Divergent available-stock math: product list and new-sale/POS context do NOT exclude expired brons**
  - Detal: The canonical raw-SQL stock queries subtract only active, non-expired reserves (status='aktiv' AND (bitme_tarixi IS NULL OR bitme_tarixi >= CURRENT_DATE)). But two Prisma-aggregate paths that feed the user-facing 'available to sell' number filter ONLY status='aktiv' and drop the expiry condition. getProducts (main /anbar/mehsullar list) computes satis_acig_miqdari = stok - bronMap where bronMap groupBy filters only status:'aktiv'. getProductContext (used in new-sale/POS product context) computes satilabilen = toplam - rezerv where rezerv aggregate filters only status:'aktiv'. So a bron that is past bitme_tarixi but still status='aktiv' (it only flips to expired lazily when the bron page is opened) over-subtracts and understates available stock in the product list and at point-of-sale, diverging from /anbar/stok, KPIs, and product-stock.ts.
  - Sübut: features/anbar/queries.ts:558-567,628 vs stok-queries.ts:149-155; features/anbar/product-context-action.ts:91-99,166 vs lib/balance/product-stock.ts:83-90
  - Fayllar: features/anbar/queries.ts, features/anbar/product-context-action.ts, lib/balance/product-stock.ts, features/anbar/stok-queries.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Bulk 'sil' on products is not a real soft-delete and skips the stock blocker**
  - Detal: bulkUpdateProducts op='sil' only runs updateMany({ aktiv:false }) — it does NOT set deleted_at/deleted_by/delete_reason (unlike single deleteProduct), so it is identical to 'passiv'. Consequences: (1) bulk-deleted products never appear under recordStatus='silinmis' (which filters deleted_at IS NOT NULL) and cannot be restored via restoreProduct (which requires deleted_at set), (2) NO stock-remaining blocker check runs (single deleteProduct blocks when stock>0 unless force), so products with live stock get deactivated and their stock silently drops out of active lists/value KPIs (which filter aktiv=TRUE) with no warning and no record of the lost stock value.
  - Sübut: features/anbar/actions.ts:502-507 vs deleteProduct stock-blocker :361-376 and soft-delete fields :379-387
  - Fayllar: features/anbar/actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Defekt can be recorded without reducing physical stock (defective units stay sellable)**
  - Detal: recordDefekt only decrements source stock and writes movements when menbe_anbar_id is provided AND differs from defekt_anbar_id. The defekt dialog makes 'Mənbə anbarı' optional (no required attr; passed as undefined when blank). When omitted, a defekt_qeydleri row is created but stok is unchanged and no anbar_hereketleri entry exists — the defective quantity remains counted in physical stock and in satis_acig_miqdari/satilabilen, so damaged goods can still be sold and never leave inventory accounting.
  - Sübut: features/anbar/defekt-actions.ts:65 (if menbe_anbar_id && != defekt) wraps the only stock decrement; defekt record created unconditionally :113-125; dialog optional field components/defekt-dialog.tsx:79,133-148 (no required)
  - Fayllar: features/anbar/defekt-actions.ts, features/anbar/components/defekt-dialog.tsx

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] cancelSale restores stock to the sale header warehouse, not each line's source warehouse (multi-warehouse mis-restore)**
  - Detal: New sales are explicitly multi-warehouse: each line decrements safeStockDecrement at line.anbar_id. But cancelSale restores every line's quantity to sale.anbar_id (the header/primary warehouse) and logs the medaxil reversal against that single warehouse, ignoring each line's true source. If a sale drew lines from warehouses other than the header primary, cancellation puts the stock back in the WRONG warehouse — per-warehouse stok totals and the anbar_hereketleri ledger become inconsistent (global total preserved, location wrong). This corrupts /anbar/stok per-warehouse balances and transfer/inventory baselines.
  - Sübut: decrement per line.anbar_id: features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts:318-321; restore to sale.anbar_id only: features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts:247-268
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts, features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] acceptTransfer does not guard 'qebul_qismi' or 'legv' status → double decrement of already-received quantity**
  - Detal: acceptTransfer only refuses when status==='tesdiqlendi'. It does NOT check 'qebul_qismi' (partially received) or 'legv' (cancelled). A transfer already partially accepted via partialAcceptTransfer (status qebul_qismi, with qebul_edilen recorded on lines) can then be fully accepted by acceptTransfer, which ignores qebul_edilen and decrements the FULL planned line.miqdar from source again — double-removing the quantity already received, and a cancelled (legv) transfer can still be accepted. partialAcceptTransfer correctly guards both states, making acceptTransfer the inconsistent/unsafe path.
  - Sübut: features/anbar/transfer/actions.ts:104-105 (only tesdiqlendi guard) and full-miqdar decrement :108-112; compare partialAcceptTransfer guards :226-227
  - Fayllar: features/anbar/transfer/actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] createBron has no available-stock validation → over-reservation drives available stock to 0/negative**
  - Detal: createBron inserts a stok_bron row with arbitrary sayi and no check against physical/available stock in the chosen warehouse. Available-stock formulas clamp with Math.max(0, stok - rezerv), so an over-reservation silently zeroes out satis_acig_miqdari/satilabilen for that product even when physical stock exists, blocking legitimate sales. (POS/new-sale do their own stock guard at decrement, but the displayed available number is corrupted.)
  - Sübut: features/anbar/bron/actions.ts:41-56 (no stock check); clamp at queries.ts:628 and product-context-action.ts:166
  - Fayllar: features/anbar/bron/actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] completeInventar uses the snapshot 'sistemde_olan' and force-sets stok=fakti → lost update of concurrent stock movements**
  - Detal: On completion, stok.miqdar is force-set to fakti (the counted value entered earlier), and the ledger delta written is fakti - sistemde_olan where sistemde_olan was snapshotted at inventory CREATE time. Any sale/purchase/transfer that changed stock between snapshot and completion is silently overwritten (no re-read of current stock), so legitimate movements during the count window are lost. It can also widen ledger-vs-cache drift because the recorded movement (abs(fakti - snapshot)) need not reconcile the cache to SUM(ledger).
  - Sübut: features/anbar/inventar/actions.ts:110 (snapshot sistemde_olan at create), :252-269 (force-set miqdar=fakti, no current re-read), :274-287 (movement = abs(fakti - snapshot))
  - Fayllar: features/anbar/inventar/actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Stock-affecting mutations in transfer/bron/konsiqnasiya write NO audit log**
  - Detal: createTransfer, acceptTransfer, partialAcceptTransfer (transfer/actions.ts), createBron/cancelBron (bron/actions.ts), and createKons/acceptKonsReturn/sellKonsignedItem/completeKons (konsiqnasiya/actions.ts) contain zero audit() calls despite all moving or reserving stock. This violates the 'audit log written on mutations' expectation; only stock-actions.ts adjustStock/transferStock and inventar/defekt actions audit. Stock movements via these paths are untraceable in audit_log (anbar_hereketleri exists for transfer/kons but not for bron create/cancel).
  - Sübut: grep audit( count = 0 in transfer/actions.ts, bron/actions.ts, konsiqnasiya/actions.ts
  - Fayllar: features/anbar/transfer/actions.ts, features/anbar/bron/actions.ts, features/anbar/konsiqnasiya/actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] /anbar/defekt page has no permission guard (read exposure)**
  - Detal: Every other anbar subpage calls requireAnbarPerm(...) (stok.oxu, mehsul.oxu, stok.transfer, sayim.oxu, stok.bron, konsiqnasiya.oxu, anbar.hesabat, anbar.anomali). defekt/page.tsx calls only withTenant/requireTenant in getData with no requireAnbarPerm — any authenticated tenant user (e.g. a cashier without anbar.oxu) can view all defekt records including product, warehouse, quantities, reasons and recorder. Mutations are still server-guarded, so this is a UI read-gate gap, not a write bypass.
  - Sübut: app/(dashboard)/anbar/defekt/page.tsx:35-71 (no requireAnbarPerm) vs guarded pages stok/page.tsx:229-230, bron/page.tsx:24-25
  - Fayllar: app/(dashboard)/anbar/defekt/page.tsx

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] changeSaleStatus can move a draft (qaralama) to active without decrementing stock or releasing its reserve**
  - Detal: changeSaleStatus only updates satis_sifarisleri.status (and audits) — it never touches stok, anbar_hereketleri, or stok_bron. The intended draft->sale conversion goes through createOrUpdateSatisYeni (which releases the bron and decrements stock), but changeSaleStatus has no guard preventing a qaralama row from being flipped to 'yeni'/'tamamlandi'. If any UI/path invokes it on a draft, the sale becomes active while stock was never decremented and the 48h reserve remains, double-counting reserved stock and overstating physical stock.
  - Sübut: features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts:154-197 (status-only, no stock/bron), vs proper finalize satis-yeni-actions.ts:222-224,314-341
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] Two different status strings for expired brons ('muddet_kecdi' vs 'vaxti_bitdi')**
  - Detal: bron/expire-stale.ts expireStaleBronReservations sets expired brons to status 'muddet_kecdi', while bron/queries.ts expireOverdueBrons sets them to 'vaxti_bitdi'. The bron list status filter (BronStatus) recognizes 'vaxti_bitdi'. Brons expired by the expire-stale helper (muddet_kecdi) will not match the 'vaxti_bitdi' UI filter and the two helpers also use different time bases (lt today midnight vs lt now), producing inconsistent expiry classification.
  - Sübut: features/anbar/bron/expire-stale.ts:20-28 ('muddet_kecdi') vs bron/queries.ts:170-176 ('vaxti_bitdi') and BronStatus :45
  - Fayllar: features/anbar/bron/expire-stale.ts, features/anbar/bron/queries.ts

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] Expired-bron exclusion uses inconsistent time granularity (CURRENT_DATE vs new Date())**
  - Detal: Live available-stock queries treat a bron as active while bitme_tarixi >= CURRENT_DATE (date-only, so it still counts all of its expiry day), whereas KPI counts (queries.ts) and the value KPI use bitme_tarixi >= new Date() (full timestamp). On the expiry day these disagree, so a reserve can be counted by /anbar/stok but excluded from KPI counts during the same day. Minor reconciliation noise on top of bug #2.
  - Sübut: stok-queries.ts:154 (>= CURRENT_DATE) and product-stock.ts:89 vs queries.ts:264,82 (gte: new Date())
  - Fayllar: features/anbar/stok-queries.ts, features/anbar/queries.ts, lib/balance/product-stock.ts

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] Konsiqnasiya return uses 'medaxil' while issue uses 'konsiqnasiya_mexaric' — asymmetric ledger nov, and qaliq logic ignores satilan_say**
  - Detal: createKons 'verilen' logs konsiqnasiya_mexaric; acceptKonsReturn logs generic 'medaxil' (rather than a konsiqnasiya-specific giris nov). Both are handled by the drift CASE so totals reconcile, but the movement vocabulary is asymmetric and makes consignment returns indistinguishable from purchases in the ledger. Also qaliq is read as qaliq_say ?? sayi without subtracting satilan_say, so returning the full remaining can be allowed even when some units were already marked sold (sellKonsignedItem decrements qaliq_say, so consistent only if qaliq_say is always maintained).
  - Sübut: features/anbar/konsiqnasiya/actions.ts:78-91 (konsiqnasiya_mexaric) vs :146-158 (medaxil); qaliq read :134,210
  - Fayllar: features/anbar/konsiqnasiya/actions.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 recordDefekt / resolveDefekt require ['stok.duzelt','anbar.idare']: Neither permission exists in the catalog/seed (it is stok.duzelis, and there is no anbar.idare). No non-privileged staff can ever be granted defekt rights; only sahibkar/admin/owner pass via isAnbarPrivileged. — _features/anbar/defekt-actions.ts:41,163; lib/permission-info.ts:238 (stok.duzelis); anbar-permissions.sql perm list_
- 🔐 createBron / cancelBron require ['bron.idare','stok.idare','anbar.idare']: Assignable bron perm is stok.bron (opens the page) but none of the three checked strings exist, so a user granted stok.bron sees the page yet cannot create/cancel a bron. — _features/anbar/bron/actions.ts:29,76; lib/permission-info.ts:244 (stok.bron)_
- 🔐 createInventar/completeInventar/updateInventarRow/bulkUpdateInventarRows/cancelInventar require ['inventar.idare','stok.idare','anbar.idare']: Catalog perms are sayim.oxu/sayim.yarat; inventar.idare/stok.idare/anbar.idare are undefined, so a user granted sayim.yarat cannot create or complete an inventory. — _features/anbar/inventar/actions.ts:52,143,164,196(no perm),215,390; lib/permission-info.ts:253-258 (sayim.*)_
- 🔐 konsiqnasiya actions require ['konsiqnasiya.idare','anbar.idare']: Only konsiqnasiya.oxu exists; konsiqnasiya.idare/anbar.idare undefined → no staff can manage consignments. — _features/anbar/konsiqnasiya/actions.ts:21,119,196,238; lib/permission-info.ts:268 (konsiqnasiya.oxu)_
- 🔐 bulkUpdateProducts op='sil' requires mehsul.idare (not mehsul.sil): Single deleteProduct requires mehsul.sil, but bulk delete (a true deactivation that drops stock from active lists) only needs mehsul.idare — and mehsul.idare is not even a defined perm, so effectively the bulk delete gate diverges from / is looser than the single-delete gate it should match. — _features/anbar/actions.ts:495 (mehsul.idare) vs deleteProduct :348 (mehsul.sil); catalog has mehsul.sil/mehsul.duzelt, no mehsul.idare_
- 🔐 setProductCost / setProductSalePrice require ['qiymet.idare','mehsul.idare']: Catalog price perm is qiymet.duzelt; qiymet.idare and mehsul.idare are undefined, so quick-fix maya/satış price edits are gated only for sahibkar/admin despite a granular price perm existing. — _features/anbar/quick-fix-actions.ts:56,88; lib/permission-info.ts:262 (qiymet.duzelt)_
- 🔐 setInventarRowReasons and updateInventarRow scoping: setInventarRowReasons has no requireAnbarActionPerm call at all (only withTenant); inventar row reason writes are unguarded beyond tenant scope. updateInventarRow uses prisma.inventar_satirlari.update by satir_id with no explicit sahibkar check beyond the tenant extension — verify tenant filter applies to inventar_satirlari updates. — _features/anbar/inventar/actions.ts:190-210 (no perm check), :148-159_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** features/anbar/queries.ts, features/anbar/product-context-action.ts, features/anbar/actions.ts, features/anbar/defekt-actions.ts, features/anbar/components/defekt-dialog.tsx, features/anbar/bron/actions.ts, features/anbar/inventar/actions.ts, features/anbar/transfer/actions.ts, features/anbar/konsiqnasiya/actions.ts, features/anbar/bron/expire-stale.ts, features/anbar/bron/queries.ts, features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts, app/(dashboard)/anbar/defekt/page.tsx, lib/permission-info.ts

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** No schema change required for the bugs; the stok_cemi trigger (scripts/migrations/2026-05-26-stok-cemi-denormalize.sql) must actually be applied in prod since it is a manual SQL script, not a prisma/migrations migration — verify it ran (otherwise getProducts stok_cemi sort/filter and KPIs silently drift).; Reconcile permission catalog: either add anbar.idare/stok.idare/bron.idare/inventar.idare/konsiqnasiya.idare/mehsul.idare/qiymet.idare to permission-info.ts + seed, or change the action checks to use the existing granular perms (stok.bron, sayim.yarat, konsiqnasiya.oxu, stok.duzelis, mehsul.sil, qiymet.duzelt). This is config/code, not schema.

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** High. Anbar stock integrity is tightly coupled to Ticaret (sales): (1) cancelSale restores stock to the wrong warehouse for multi-warehouse sales, corrupting per-warehouse stok and the anbar_hereketleri ledger that product-stock.ts/drift scanning rely on. (2) changeSaleStatus can desync stok_bron reserves and physical stock if it flips a draft to active. (3) The shared available-stock formula diverges between Anbar product list/POS and the canonical raw-SQL services, so POS, new-sale, marketplace push (emitStockChange -> autoPushAfterSale) and warehouse views can publish/show different sellable quantities. (4) Marketplace product/orders routes also read stok_cemi (app/api/v1/marketplace/*), so any stok_cemi trigger drift propagates to external channels. (5) Inventory completion writing xercl_r couples Anbar to the Maliyye/expense module; a lost-update during count window mis-values the write-off."

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) Permissions (highest impact): align action permission checks with the seeded catalog OR add the missing perms. Recommended — change checks to existing granular perms: bron->stok.bron, inventar->sayim.yarat, konsiqnasiya->konsiqnasiya.oxu (or add konsiqnasiya.idare), defekt->stok.duzelis, quick-fix price->qiymet.duzelt, bulkUpdateProducts 'sil'->mehsul.sil, replace all 'anbar.idare'/'stok.idare'/'mehsul.idare' with real keys. 2) Centralize available-stock math: replace the ad-hoc bron aggregates in getProducts (queries.ts:558-567) and getProductContext (product-context-action.ts:91-99) with the canonical filter including (bitme_tarixi IS NULL OR bitme_tarixi >= CURRENT_DATE), ideally calling a single shared helper. 3) bulkUpdateProducts op='sil': route through the same soft-delete (set deleted_at/deleted_by/delete_reason) and apply the stock-remaining blocker (or require force). 4) recordDefekt: require menbe_anbar_id (make dialog field required) or refuse to create a defekt note without a stock movement so defective units leave sellable stock. 5) cancelSale: restore each line to its own source warehouse (join satis_sifaris_satirlari.anbar_id) instead of sale.anbar_id; write the reversal movement against the correct warehouse. 6) acceptTransfer: guard against status 'qebul_qismi' and 'legv', and account for already-received qebul_edilen (or block full-accept once partial accept has occurred). 7) completeInventar: re-read current stok inside the transaction and compute delta against live stock (or warn on changes since snapshot) to avoid lost updates. 8) Add audit() calls to transfer/bron/konsiqnasiya mutations. 9) Add requireAnbarPerm('anbar.hesabat' or a defekt-specific read perm) to /anbar/defekt page. 10) Unify expired-bron status string ('vaxti_bitdi') and time basis across expire-stale.ts and expireOverdueBrons. 11) changeSaleStatus: refuse transitioning a qaralama row to active (force conversion through createOrUpdateSatisYeni).

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- Create a draft sale with reserve for product X (qaralama + reserve_stock), let the 48h bron exist; open /anbar/mehsullar and /anbar/stok and the POS/new-sale product context for X — confirm whether 'available' shows the same number in all (bug #2 predicts list/POS subtract the reserve even after bitme_tarixi passes, /anbar/stok does not).
- As a staff user, grant only stok.bron and open /anbar/bron; try to create a bron — expect it to FAIL with 'icazə lazımdır' (confirms permission mismatch bug).
- Record a defekt for product X WITHOUT selecting a source warehouse; then check /anbar/stok and product available — stock should be unchanged (confirms defective units stay sellable).
- Make a multi-warehouse sale (line A from warehouse 1, line B from warehouse 2), then cancel it; check per-warehouse stok — all quantity returns to the header warehouse, not the original sources (confirms mis-restore).
- Create a transfer of 10 units; partial-accept 6 (status->qebul_qismi); then call full accept — verify whether source warehouse is decremented by another 10 (double decrement).
- Bulk-select products that still have stock and run bulk 'sil'; check that they get aktiv=false with no stock blocker, do not appear under recordStatus=silinmis, and cannot be restored (confirms not-a-real-soft-delete + no blocker).
- Start a full inventory count for warehouse 1, enter counted values, then sell some of the same product before completing; complete the inventory — verify the sale's decrement is overwritten (lost update).
- As a low-privilege authenticated user with no anbar.oxu, navigate directly to /anbar/defekt — page should load and show all defekt records (confirms missing read guard).

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Whether the stok_cemi trigger migration (manual SQL) is actually applied in the running DB — if not, getProducts stok sort/filter and several KPIs drift from SUM(stok.miqdar).
- Whether changeSaleStatus is ever invoked on a qaralama-status sale from the UI (the bug's exploitability depends on a code path flipping a draft to active without going through createOrUpdateSatisYeni).
- Whether status 'legv' / 'qebul_qismi' transfers are reachable to the same acceptTransfer call (UI flow) to trigger the double-decrement; static code shows partialAcceptTransfer can produce qebul_qismi and acceptTransfer does not block it.
- Whether bron-dialog disables the submit button while pending (double-submit dedupe) — onSubmit uses useTransition pending; confirm the Button passes disabled={pending} (not fully read).
- Whether the Prisma tenant $extends filter actually scopes inventar_satirlari.update / transfer_satirlari.update / stok.update by satir_id and stok.id alone (id-based updates) so cross-tenant id guessing is blocked.
- Runtime confirmation that a past-expiry but still status='aktiv' bron exists long enough (before the lazy expiry on the bron page runs) to actually understate available stock in the product list / POS — i.e. how often lazy expiry runs.

---

## 6. Maliyyə

**Xülasə:** "Audited the Maliyyə module: balance SoT services, all finance server actions, the page guards, and the read queries. The transactional core is solid — payment allocation (FIFO cascade with surplus-only advance), atomic $transactions, and cancel/reverse-with-recalc are implemented correctly, and the operations/expense LIST views exclude soft-deleted by default. However I found one CRITICAL data-integrity defect and several high-severity ones, all stemming from inconsistencies between writers and readers of finance_operations: (1) account-balance.ts counts only yön='mexaric' as outflow, but saveQuickOperation persists salary/expense/dividend ops as yön='xaric' (QUICK_META), so those never reduce the cash/bank balance; (2) getOperationsSummary is the mirror — counts only 'xaric', misses 'mexaric', and also ignores soft-delete; (3) dashboard/finance KPIs read customer debt from the wrong column (borc instead of alacaq) and supplier debt from negative-borc-only, diverging from the debitor/kreditor pages; (4) dashboard/P&L/daily-flow/cashflow expense aggregates don't exclude legv_de expenses; (5) expense EDIT/DELETE don't reverse the linked finance_operations row or recalc the account; (6) supplier overpayment drains cash with no offsetting credit. Permission gaps: the marketplace page and the bank-reconciliation server actions are ungated, and route-gate uses legacy maliye.* keys that don't match the maliyye.*/specific catalog keys the pages enforce. Live verification is needed to size the 'xaric' blast radius (depends on seeded finance_operation_types.yön) and the role→permission seed."

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** lib/balance/account-balance.ts; lib/balance/customer-balance.ts; lib/balance/supplier-balance.ts; features/maliyye/access-guard.ts; features/maliyye/actions.ts (saveExpense, deleteExpense, createExpenseCategory, createAccount, saveQuickOperation, importBankStatement, startMarketplaceReconciliation, approveOperation, rejectOperation, cancelOperation, closeGunSonu, receivePartialPayment, markPayoutReceived, payAllOpenInvoices, paySupplierAllOpen, paySupplierInvoice, applyAdvanceToInvoice, saveExpenseWithInvoiceLink, applyExpenseToInvoice, QUICK_META, threshold helpers); features/maliyye/cancel-operation-action.ts; features/maliyye/queries.ts (fetchFinanceKpisRaw, fetchMaliyyeDashboardKpisRaw, getDailyFlow, getExpenses, getPLReport, getDebtors, getCreditors, getOpenSalesForCustomer); features/maliyye/operations-queries.ts (getOperations, getOperationsSummary); features/maliyye/account-queries.ts (getAccounts, getAccountDetail); features/maliyye/marketplace-queries.ts; features/maliyye/recurring-rules-actions.ts; features/maliyye/bank-reconcile-actions.ts (matchBankItem etc.); features/maliyye/marketplace-commission-actions.ts; features/maliyye/debt-detail-actions.ts; features/maliyye/extra-actions.ts (getCashflowForecast, agingReport); app/(dashboard)/maliyye/page.tsx (guard + KPI cards); app/(dashboard)/maliyye/debitor/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/maliyye/kreditor/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/maliyye/xercler/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/maliyye/emeliyyat/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/maliyye/recurring/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/maliyye/edv/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/maliyye/marketplace/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/maliyye/yol-vergisi/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx (route gate); lib/auth/route-gate.ts; lib/auth/finance-permissions.ts; lib/permission-info.ts (finance keys); prisma/schema.prisma (finance_operations.yön, kontragentler.borc/alacaq, satis_sifarisleri.status/deleted_at)

**Əhatə boşluqları:** features/maliyye/kassa-queries.ts and gun-sonu-queries.ts (kassa balance computation from hesab_emeliyyatlari) — only skimmed; kassa vs finance_operations balance reconciliation not deeply audited; features/maliyye/cashflow-queries.ts (cashflow chart) not opened; features/maliyye/customer-statement.ts and supplier-statement.ts (statement PDFs) not fully audited beyond yön usage; features/maliyye/triggers.ts not read; app/api/maliyye/{balance,bank,operation}/route.ts API route handlers not opened — direct-API permission guard status unverified; features/maliyye component .tsx files (button/handler wiring, disabled-state for double-click) not individually verified; app/(dashboard)/maliyye/{bank,hesab,kassalar,pl-balans,pul-axini,gun-sonu,hesab,hesabat} pages not opened; getReceivableAging, getPaymentMethodBreakdown, getMonthlyFlow, getTopDebtors/Creditors not fully verified for soft-delete/field correctness

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ Payment allocation (nisyə) applies to open invoices first via FIFO cascade; only true surplus becomes avans — _features/maliyye/actions.ts:1098-1156 receivePartialPayment — builds processOrder (selected invoice first, then oldest-first), Math.min(remain, qalig) per invoice, toAdvance = max(0, remain) only after all invoices closed; matches the 2+100/10 spec_
- ✅ Sale/payment/cancel mutations are wrapped in prisma.$transaction (atomic) — _receivePartialPayment actions.ts:1159; payAllOpenInvoices:1523; paySupplierAllOpen:1690; paySupplierInvoice:1866; applyAdvanceToInvoice:2043; saveExpenseWithInvoiceLink:2165; cancelFinanceOperation cancel-operation-action.ts:47_
- ✅ Cancel of a finance operation reverses allocations, restores satis/alis odenilmis, and recalcs all touched account + customer + supplier balances atomically — _cancel-operation-action.ts:71-149 — sets status=legv + deleted_at, deletes allocations with decrement, recalculateAccountBalance/Customer/Supplier inside the same tx_
- ✅ Customer/supplier balances are derived by the shared SoT services from sales/purchases, not ad-hoc per page — _lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:50-108 (alacaq from satis_sifarisleri), supplier-balance.ts:33-69 (borc from alis_sifarisleri); callers use recalculate*Balance after every mutation_
- ✅ Idempotency guard prevents duplicate nisyə payments within 5 min — _receivePartialPayment actions.ts:1078-1093 checks finance_operations for [IDEM:key] in last 5 min and returns existing id_
- ✅ Operations list excludes soft-deleted by default and supports a record-status filter — _operations-queries.ts:65-67 getOperations defaults recordStatus='aktiv' → where.deleted_at = null_
- ✅ Expenses list excludes soft-deleted (legv_de) by default — _queries.ts:370-374 getExpenses mode='aktiv' → where.legv_de = null; deleteExpense soft-deletes via legv_de (actions.ts:238-245)_
- ✅ Negative-balance guard on outgoing cash uses real SoT balance (not cache) — _lib/balance/account-balance.ts:202-216 checkAccountSufficient; used in saveExpense:134, paySupplierAllOpen:1718, paySupplierInvoice:1891, saveExpenseWithInvoiceLink:2169_
- ✅ Most mutating server actions are permission-gated and tenant-scoped — _requireMaliyyeActionPerm at saveExpense:80, saveQuickOperation:436, receivePartialPayment:1062, payAllOpenInvoices:1462, paySupplier*:1635/1807, cancelFinanceOperation:35; all wrap withTenant/requireTenant_
- ✅ Mutations write audit log entries (safeAuditLog/audit) — _saveQuickOperation:658, receivePartialPayment:1262, cancelFinanceOperation audit at :151, saveExpense:201, payAll/paySupplier audit blocks_
- ✅ Transfer operations are correctly double-sided in the balance service (no double count) — _account-balance.ts:76-93 — hesab_id2 credited transfer_daxil, hesab_id with yön='transfer' debited transfer_mexaric_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🔴 **[critical · data-flow] Account balance ignores outflow operations created with y_n='xaric' (salary/expense/dividend via Quick-Op) — cash/bank balance stays too high**
  - Detal: calculateAccountBalance only treats yön='mexaric' as an outflow (account-balance.ts:67-73) and yön='daxil' as inflow. But saveQuickOperation persists the operation type's y_n = meta.yon, and QUICK_META marks xercler/maas/avans/bonus/dividend/azaltma as yon:'xaric' (actions.ts:407-419). The find-or-create at actions.ts:489-498 creates finance_operation_types with y_n:'xaric' for these kods, and the op row gets y_n:'xaric' (actions.ts:543). recalculateAccountBalance therefore does NOT subtract these amounts — paying salary/expense/dividend through the Quick-Op dialog does not reduce the source account qaliq. Inflows are safe (all use 'daxil'); supplier payments and saveExpense use 'mexaric' so they are correct. The two outflow tokens 'xaric' and 'mexaric' are both in use across the codebase with no normalization.
  - Sübut: lib/balance/account-balance.ts:67-73 (only 'mexaric'); features/maliyye/actions.ts:407-419 (yon:'xaric'), :495 (y_n: meta.yon), :543 (y_n: type.y_n); schema finance_operations.y_n @map("yön")
  - Fayllar: lib/balance/account-balance.ts, features/maliyye/actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Operations summary counts only 'xaric' outflows and misses 'mexaric'; also includes soft-deleted (legv) operations**
  - Detal: getOperationsSummary sums outflow only when y_n==='xaric' (operations-queries.ts:223) and never matches 'mexaric', so supplier payments (paySupplier*, y_n:'mexaric') and hesab-linked expenses (saveExpense, 'mexaric') are excluded from the xaric/net totals shown above the operations table. This is the mirror image of the balance-service bug (which counts only 'mexaric'). Additionally the function receives filter.recordStatus but never applies a deleted_at/status filter (no where.deleted_at), so cancelled/legv operations are counted in daxil/xaric/net even though the list (getOperations) hides them — summary and list disagree.
  - Sübut: features/maliyye/operations-queries.ts:218-226 (only 'xaric', net=daxil-xaric), :177-209 (where built without recordStatus/deleted_at); page passes recordStatus at app/(dashboard)/maliyye/emeliyyat/page.tsx:49,55
  - Fayllar: features/maliyye/operations-queries.ts, app/(dashboard)/maliyye/emeliyyat/page.tsx

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Dashboard KPI reads wrong balance fields: debitor from kontragentler.borc (should be alacaq) and kreditor from negative borc only**
  - Detal: fetchMaliyyeDashboardKpisRaw (feeds /maliyye KPI cards debitor_cem/kreditor_cem) computes customer receivables from kontragentler.borc>0 (queries.ts:135-139) and supplier payables from ABS(SUM(LEAST(borc,0))) (queries.ts:140-144). But the SoT services store customer receivable in kontragentler.alacaq (customer-balance.ts:135-141 writes alacaq) and supplier payable in kontragentler.borc as a POSITIVE value (supplier-balance.ts:88-93). So debitor_cem reads the wrong column and kreditor_cem (only negative borc) is ~0 for normal positive supplier debt. The kreditor page getCreditors correctly treats positive borc as debt (queries.ts:831-835) and agingReport correctly uses alacaq/borc (extra-actions.ts:234) — so the dashboard cards diverge from the dedicated pages. fetchFinanceKpisRaw (getFinanceKpis) has the identical mistake (queries.ts:38-49).
  - Sübut: features/maliyye/queries.ts:135-144 and :38-49; SoT: customer-balance.ts:138 writes alacaq, supplier-balance.ts:91 writes positive borc; correct usage at queries.ts:831-835 and extra-actions.ts:234
  - Fayllar: features/maliyye/queries.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Dashboard/P&L/daily-flow/cashflow expense and outflow aggregates do not exclude soft-deleted/cancelled records**
  - Detal: Multiple finance aggregates omit the soft-delete filter: (a) monthly/daily expense sums use xercl_r without legv_de IS NULL — fetchMaliyyeDashboardKpisRaw (queries.ts:123-134), fetchFinanceKpisRaw (queries.ts:30-33), getDailyFlow (queries.ts:222-224), getPLReport expense subquery (queries.ts:458-465). Cancelled expenses (deleteExpense sets legv_de) therefore still reduce profit/menfeet and inflate xerc totals on the dashboard, KPIs, daily-flow chart and P&L. The list view (getExpenses) correctly excludes them, so the numbers are internally inconsistent.
  - Sübut: features/maliyye/queries.ts:123-134, :30-33, :222-224, :458-465 (no legv_de filter); contrast getExpenses queries.ts:373 and deleteExpense actions.ts:238-245
  - Fayllar: features/maliyye/queries.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Editing or deleting a cash-linked expense does not reverse the finance_operations row or recalc the account balance**
  - Detal: When an expense is created with hesab_id, saveExpense creates a matching finance_operations mexaric row and recalcs the account (actions.ts:130-167). But on EDIT (d.id branch, actions.ts:110-123) only xercl_r is updated — the linked finance_operations row keeps the old amount and the account qaliq is not recalculated. On DELETE, deleteExpense only soft-deletes xercl_r via legv_de (actions.ts:238-245) and never soft-deletes/cancels the linked finance_operations row nor recalcs the account, so the cash account stays reduced after the expense is 'deleted' and the expense still appears as an active mexaric in the operations journal/account balance.
  - Sübut: features/maliyye/actions.ts:110-123 (edit path, xercl_r only), :224-265 (deleteExpense, no finance_operations reversal / no recalculateAccountBalance)
  - Fayllar: features/maliyye/actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Supplier overpayment drains the cash account but is not credited anywhere (no advance/credit recorded)**
  - Detal: In paySupplierInvoice, the cash outflow finance_operations is created with the FULL meblegh: d.mebleg (actions.ts:1907) and checkAccountSufficient is checked against d.mebleg, but if d.mebleg exceeds total open purchase qaliq, only totalApplied is allocated and the overpay just produces a UI message 'artıq qaldı' (actions.ts:1984-1985) with no supplier advance/credit row. Net effect: account loses the full amount while supplier balance only drops by the allocated part, so the overpaid cash disappears from the books. Customer-side receivePartialPayment handles surplus→avans correctly; supplier-side does not.
  - Sübut: features/maliyye/actions.ts:1863-1864 (overpay computed), :1907 (meblegh: d.mebleg full), :1984-1986 (overpay only messaged, no credit); compare receivePartialPayment toAdvance handling actions.ts:1237-1251
  - Fayllar: features/maliyye/actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] Marketplace finance page has no permission guard**
  - Detal: app/(dashboard)/maliyye/marketplace/page.tsx never calls requireMaliyyePerm or auth() (no guard at all), unlike debitor/kreditor/xercler/emeliyyat/edv/recurring pages which gate with requireMaliyyePerm. It relies solely on the dashboard layout route-gate, which for /maliyye requires maliye.view/maliye.gor/maliye.idare (see separate route-gate key-mismatch finding). Any user who passes the layout gate can view all marketplace payout amounts and the page exposes the reconcile/commission editors (their actions: markPayoutReceived is gated, setMarketplaceCommission has an inline role check, but listing is ungated).
  - Sübut: app/(dashboard)/maliyye/marketplace/page.tsx:26-40 (no requireMaliyyePerm); contrast debitor/page.tsx:29-31, edv/page.tsx:42-43
  - Fayllar: app/(dashboard)/maliyye/marketplace/page.tsx

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] Bank reconciliation server actions have no permission/role guard**
  - Detal: matchBankItem, unmatchBankItem and autoMatchStatement mutate finance_bank_statement_items (match/unmatch state) with only requireTenant() and no requireMaliyyeActionPerm or role check. Any authenticated user in the tenant (e.g. a cashier with only pos.view) could invoke these server actions directly to manipulate bank-statement matching, bypassing the UI gating. Tenant scoping prevents cross-tenant access but not intra-tenant privilege escalation.
  - Sübut: features/maliyye/bank-reconcile-actions.ts:21-69 matchBankItem (no perm check), :72+ unmatchBankItem, :105+ autoMatchStatement; contrast permission-gated actions in actions.ts
  - Fayllar: features/maliyye/bank-reconcile-actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] Route-gate uses permission keys (maliye.*) that do not match the granted catalog keys (maliyye.oxu / *.oxu)**
  - Detal: ROUTE_RULES gates /maliyye on anyOf maliye.view/maliye.gor/maliye.idare (route-gate.ts:31), but the user-grantable catalog (permission-info.ts) defines maliyye.oxu, debitor.oxu, xerc.oxu etc. (two y's / specific keys) and the page guards check those (access-guard.ts:23). Only maliye.view is defined as a constant (lib/auth/permissions.ts:21) — there is a legacy maliye.* vs current maliyye.*/specific naming split. The route-gate bypasses only sahibkar/admin/owner (route-gate.ts:94-96), NOT muhasib/direktor, yet access-guard treats muhasib/direktor as privileged (access-guard.ts:31-40). So a non-owner finance user (e.g. muhasib without sahibkar/admin in rol_ad, or a custom finance role) granted maliyye.oxu but not maliye.view would be redirected to /icaze-yox by the layout before any maliyye page renders; conversely maliye.view without maliyye.oxu passes the gate but fails the page guard. Exact impact depends on the role→permission seed (needs live verification).
  - Sübut: lib/auth/route-gate.ts:31 ('maliye.view','maliye.gor','maliye.idare') and :94-96 (only sahibkar/admin/owner bypass); lib/permission-info.ts:327,358,361,343 (maliyye.oxu/debitor.oxu/kreditor.oxu/xerc.oxu); features/maliyye/access-guard.ts:23,31-40; lib/auth/permissions.ts:21
  - Fayllar: lib/auth/route-gate.ts, lib/permission-info.ts, features/maliyye/access-guard.ts

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] getDebtors raw query omits deleted_at filter (relies on status=legv coupling)**
  - Detal: getDebtors computes open_total/open sales filtering only status NOT IN ('legv','qaytarilib') and qaralama, without s.deleted_at IS NULL (queries.ts:674-678), whereas the SoT customer-balance.ts:69 includes deleted_at IS NULL. Currently safe because the satis soft-delete path always sets status='legv' together with deleted_at (satis-actions.ts:288-298), so they coincide; but any code path that sets deleted_at without status='legv' would leak a soft-deleted sale into debitor balances. Defense-in-depth gap, not an active leak.
  - Sübut: features/maliyye/queries.ts:674-678 (no deleted_at); customer-balance.ts:69 (has deleted_at IS NULL); satis-actions.ts:288-298 (sets both)
  - Fayllar: features/maliyye/queries.ts

- ⚪ **[low · ux] getAccountDetail mislabels cash accounts (is_kassa) due to enum typo 'nagd' vs 'negd'**
  - Detal: getAccountDetail returns is_kassa: h.nov === 'nagd' (account-queries.ts:233), but the account nov enum value is 'negd' (createAccount AccountSchema actions.ts:310; closeGunSonu queries nov:'negd' actions.ts:986). So is_kassa is always false for cash accounts on the detail view — a display/labeling defect, no balance impact.
  - Sübut: features/maliyye/account-queries.ts:233 ('nagd'); features/maliyye/actions.ts:310 (enum 'negd'), :986 (nov:'negd')
  - Fayllar: features/maliyye/account-queries.ts

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] markPayoutReceived adjusts account qaliq by manual increment without recalculateAccountBalance**
  - Detal: markPayoutReceived increments maliye_hesablari.qaliq directly (actions.ts:1388-1391) AND creates a finance_operations daxil row (actions.ts:1398-1414) but does not call recalculateAccountBalance, unlike every other finance action. Because the SoT recalc fully recomputes qaliq from finance_operations, the cached value matches now, but it diverges from the established pattern and would drift if the manual increment and the op amount ever differ; not an active double-count.
  - Sübut: features/maliyye/actions.ts:1387-1414 (manual increment + op create, no recalc); contrast recalculateAccountBalance usage in receivePartialPayment:1257, paySupplierInvoice:1937
  - Fayllar: features/maliyye/actions.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 Route-gate vs page-guard permission key naming: /maliyye route-gate requires maliye.view/maliye.gor/maliye.idare (legacy keys) while pages require maliyye.oxu/debitor.oxu/etc. (catalog keys); only sahibkar/admin/owner bypass the gate (not muhasib/direktor who are privileged at page level). A finance user with catalog keys but not maliye.view is blocked at the layout. — _lib/auth/route-gate.ts:31,94-96 vs lib/permission-info.ts:327,358,361 and features/maliyye/access-guard.ts:23,31-40_
- 🔐 Direct server-action invocation guard on bank reconciliation: matchBankItem/unmatchBankItem/autoMatchStatement mutate reconciliation state with only requireTenant() — no requireMaliyyeActionPerm/role check, so any authenticated tenant user can call them directly. — _features/maliyye/bank-reconcile-actions.ts:21-69,72,105_
- 🔐 Marketplace page UI gating: app/(dashboard)/maliyye/marketplace/page.tsx renders payout amounts and editors with no requireMaliyyePerm/auth() call; relies solely on the (key-mismatched) layout route-gate. — _app/(dashboard)/maliyye/marketplace/page.tsx:26-40 (no guard)_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** lib/balance/account-balance.ts, features/maliyye/actions.ts, features/maliyye/operations-queries.ts, features/maliyye/queries.ts, features/maliyye/extra-actions.ts, features/maliyye/account-queries.ts, features/maliyye/bank-reconcile-actions.ts, app/(dashboard)/maliyye/marketplace/page.tsx, lib/auth/route-gate.ts

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** No schema change strictly required for the core bug; the 'xaric'/'mexaric' split should be resolved by normalizing finance_operations.yön values (data migration to a single outflow token, e.g. set yön='mexaric' where yön='xaric') OR by making calculateAccountBalance and all consumers accept both tokens. A one-time recalculateAllAccountBalances run is needed after the fix to correct drifted qaliq cache.; Optional: backfill kontragentler.alacaq (customers) / borc (suppliers) consistency before relying on dashboard KPIs.

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** "The 'xaric' vs 'mexaric' direction split is the highest cross-module risk: lib/balance/account-balance.ts is the shared SoT consumed by POS, ticaret (sales/purchase payments), maas/payroll, and anbar cost flows. Because account-balance counts only 'mexaric', any module that writes 'xaric' (Quick-Op salary/expense/dividend) silently fails to reduce account balances, while modules reading 'xaric' (cashflow forecast, P&L, ops summary, pul-axini in extended-queries) miss 'mexaric' supplier/expense outflows — so cash position, P&L profit, and forecasting are all skewed in opposite directions depending on which token the source used. The customer receivable field confusion (alacaq vs borc) also spans dashboard, nezaret-merkezi, and hesabatlar which consume getDashboardKpis/getFinanceKpis. Soft-deleted-expense leakage into KPIs/P&L affects dashboard and reporting modules. Recommend a single normalization migration plus a global recalculateAllAccountBalances run, coordinated with the ticaret and hesabatlar audits."

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** "1) Resolve the outflow-token split: pick a single canonical value (recommend 'mexaric' since balance service and supplier payments use it). Either (a) make calculateAccountBalance count both 'mexaric' and 'xaric' as outflow AND make getOperationsSummary/getCashflowForecast/getPLReport/extended pul-axini count both; OR (b) change QUICK_META yon to 'mexaric' and the find-or-create type creation in saveQuickOperation to persist 'mexaric', plus a data migration for existing 'xaric' rows.
