Then run recalculateAllAccountBalances. 2) Fix dashboard KPIs: in fetchMaliyyeDashboardKpisRaw and fetchFinanceKpisRaw read customer receivable from kontragentler.alacaq (nov musteri/her_ikisi, alacaq>0) and supplier payable from positive kontragentler.borc (nov techizatci/her_ikisi, borc>0); align with getDebtors/getCreditors/agingReport. 3) Add legv_de IS NULL to all xercl_r aggregates in fetchMaliyyeDashboardKpisRaw, fetchFinanceKpisRaw, getDailyFlow, getPLReport. 4) Make getOperationsSummary apply recordStatus (default deleted_at=null) like getOperations. 5) Expense edit/delete: on EDIT update the linked finance_operations row amount + recalc account; on deleteExpense, soft-delete/cancel the linked finance_operations row and recalc the source account (find the op by [XERC:<id>] tag or expense link). 6) paySupplierInvoice/paySupplierAllOpen: clamp the cash outflow + finance_operations meblegh to totalApplied, or record the overpay as a supplier advance/credit. 7) Add requireMaliyyeActionPerm to matchBankItem/unmatchBankItem/autoMatchStatement; add requireMaliyyePerm to marketplace page. 8) Reconcile route-gate keys with permission-info catalog (use maliyye.oxu/maliye.view consistently and ensure muhasib/direktor either bypass or are granted the gate key). 9) Fix is_kassa typo 'nagd'→'negd' in getAccountDetail. 10) Optionally call recalculateAccountBalance in markPayoutReceived for pattern consistency."

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- Create a salary (maas) or expense via the Quick-Op dialog selecting a cash/bank account; verify the account qaliq decreases by the amount (expected to FAIL — qaliq unchanged after recalc due to y_n='xaric').
- On the operations page (/maliyye/emeliyyat), pay a supplier invoice (creates 'mexaric' op) and a quick salary (creates 'xaric' op); verify the xaric/net summary boxes include BOTH (expected to FAIL — only 'xaric' counted).
- Cancel a finance operation; verify summary totals drop and the operation disappears from both list and summary (list updates, summary may not exclude legv).
- Create an expense linked to a cash account, then delete it; verify the cash account balance is restored and the operation no longer appears in the journal (expected to FAIL).
- Edit an existing cash-linked expense amount; verify the account balance and the linked operation amount update (expected to FAIL).
- Pay a supplier more than they are owed; verify the surplus is recorded as a supplier advance/credit rather than vanishing (expected to FAIL).
- Compare /maliyye dashboard debitor_cem/kreditor_cem cards against the /maliyye/debitor and /maliyye/kreditor page totals — they should match (expected to MISMATCH).
- Create then soft-delete an expense; verify it no longer affects the P&L report, daily-flow chart, and monthly KPI xerc (expected to still be counted).
- As a non-owner finance user (muhasib/custom role) with maliyye.oxu but without maliye.view, open /maliyye — verify access (may be redirected to /icaze-yox by route-gate).
- As a low-privilege user, attempt to call matchBankItem/setMarketplaceCommission server actions directly — verify they are rejected (matchBankItem expected to succeed = bug).

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Whether finance_operation_types rows for kods maas/avans/bonus/dividend/xercler/azaltma actually carry y_n='xaric' in production (vs pre-seeded with 'mexaric') — determines real impact radius of the balance bug. Inspect: SELECT kod,"yön" FROM finance_operation_types.
- Whether any existing finance_operations rows have yön='xaric' and the resulting qaliq drift — run calculateAccountBalance vs cached maliye_hesablari.qaliq.
- Actual role→permission seed: does any non-owner finance role get maliye.view AND maliyye.oxu, or only one? Determines if the route-gate key mismatch blocks real users.
- Confirm dashboard KPI cards (debitor_cem/kreditor_cem) display 0/wrong values against real data — requires running /maliyye with seeded customer alacaq and supplier borc.
- Confirm deleting a hesab-linked expense leaves the account qaliq reduced and the op visible in the journal (manual repro).
- Confirm supplier overpay scenario (pay 100 to supplier owing 30) leaves account -100 with no 70 credit anywhere.

---

## 7. Əlaqələr / Müştəri / Təchizatçı / Kontragent 360

**Xülasə:** Customer/Supplier 360 (detail page reused for both novs), the debt timeline/journey, statements (hesab-çıxarış) and CSV exports were audited end-to-end against the data-integrity checklist. Strong points: tenant isolation is enforced via withTenant + the prisma sahibkar_id extension on all relevant models; FIFO payment allocation (surplus-only-to-avans) is correctly implemented in both payment actions; cancelled sales/purchases set status='legv' so status-only filters reliably exclude them; statement running-balance math and avans dedupe are correct; list/detail/borclar pages enforce CRM permissions. Three high-severity issues: (1) payments taken through the elaqe PaymentDialog (recordContactPayment, type_kod='musteri_odenis') are invisible in the customer statement, which only reads type_kod IN ('qaime','borc_silinme') — son_qaliq is overstated; (2) that same action writes the finance_operation with hesab_id NULL, so the cash never credits any account balance (asset side lost, books unbalanced); (3) both statement pages and both CSV API routes lack a CRM permission check, and /elaqe is in the route-gate bypass list, so any authenticated tenant user can read a customer/supplier financial ledger. Medium issues: the 360 debt-timeline/journey counts unconfirmed/cancelled returns at full umumi_mebleg (distorting the displayed running balance); three different borc figures can show for one customer (card getContactStats vs statement ledger vs source-of-truth calculateCustomerBalance) because their filters diverge; recordContactPayment lacks the idempotency guard that receivePartialPayment has. Recommended root fix: consolidate the two customer-payment paths onto receivePartialPayment (correct type_kod, hesab crediting, idempotency) and add explicit permission guards to the statement pages and CSV routes.

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** app/(dashboard)/elaqe/musteriler/[id]/page.tsx (full, 1560 lines — customer/supplier 360 detail; reused by techizatcilar/[id]/page.tsx); app/(dashboard)/elaqe/techizatcilar/[id]/page.tsx (re-exports musteriler detail page); app/(dashboard)/elaqe/musteriler/[id]/hesab-cixaris/page.tsx (customer statement UI); app/(dashboard)/elaqe/techizatcilar/[id]/hesab-cixaris/page.tsx (supplier statement UI); app/(dashboard)/elaqe/borclar/page.tsx (debitor/kreditor lists); features/elaqe/detail-queries.ts (full — 360 KPIs, health, journey, debt timeline, sales stats, documents); features/elaqe/queries.ts (getContactDetail, getContactFinanceOps, getContactTags partial); features/elaqe/actions.ts (recordContactPayment full; function index); features/elaqe/access-guard.ts (requireElaqePerm / requireElaqeActionPerm); features/elaqe/components/payment-dialog.tsx; features/maliyye/customer-statement.ts (full); features/maliyye/supplier-statement.ts (full); features/maliyye/queries.ts (getOpenSalesForCustomer; receivePartialPayment range); features/maliyye/actions.ts (receivePartialPayment partial 1061-1230); app/api/musteri/[id]/hesab-cixaris.csv/route.ts; app/api/techizatci/[id]/hesab-cixaris.csv/route.ts; lib/balance/customer-balance.ts (full — source of truth); lib/db/with-tenant.ts, lib/db/tenant-context.ts, lib/db/tenant-models.ts (tenant scoping); lib/auth/route-gate.ts (layout gate + BYPASS_PREFIXES); lib/soft-delete/actions.ts (softDeleteRecord); features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts (sale cancel 270-306); features/ticaret/alis-actions.ts (purchase cancel 505-535); features/qaytarma/actions.ts (return create/accept/cancel status lifecycle); features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts (odenilmis on cash vs nisye sales 340-379)

**Əhatə boşluqları:** features/elaqe/components/customer-journey-client.tsx (client renderer for Səfər tab) — not opened; only the server data path getCustomerJourney was reviewed.; features/elaqe/components/timeline-peek-drawer.tsx + timeline-peek-action.ts (the 'Bax' peek used in timeline) — not reviewed for permission/data leakage.; features/elaqe/components/sened-tab.tsx and the document upload action (writes [DOC:...] tags into kontragentler.qeyd) — parse/build helpers reviewed in detail-queries.ts but the mutation/upload action and its permission guard were not opened.; features/elaqe/components/notes-tab.tsx + addContactNote/note mutations — not opened (only that NotesTab is rendered with server notes).; features/elaqe/components/tags-manager.tsx and addContactTag/removeContactTag/bulkAddTag actions — guards not verified.; features/elaqe/contact-context-action.ts, drawer-actions.ts, debt-reminder-actions.ts, birthday-reminder-action.ts, auto-tag-action.ts, timeline-peek-action.ts, sms-limit.ts — server actions not opened for permission/transaction/audit.; features/elaqe/quick-create-customer.ts / quick-create-supplier.ts and mergeContacts / importContacts / resetContactDebt actions — only function signatures seen, bodies not audited (mergeContacts and resetContactDebt are balance-affecting and deserve their own review).; features/servis/queries.getServisHistoryForCustomer and features/kampaniyalar customer-loyalty-panel — consumed by the detail page but not opened.; ObjectTasksTab (tapshiriqlar) integration on the detail page — not reviewed.; The /elaqe/dogum-gunu, /dublikat, /followup, /hesabat, /inaktiv sub-pages — out of the core 360/statement focus, not opened.; Whether revalidatePath after payment actually refreshes the statement route (no revalidate of the hesab-cixaris path is issued by recordContactPayment) — not verified.

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ Tenant isolation on all 360 / statement queries — _All read paths go through withTenant() (lib/db/with-tenant.ts) and the prisma $extends injects sahibkar_id for TENANT_MODELS incl kontragentler/satis_sifarisleri/finance_operations/qaytarma_sifarisleri/alis_sifarisleri (lib/db/tenant-models.ts:93,184,67,150,15; lib/db/prisma.ts:65,87). customer-statement.ts:56 and supplier-statement.ts:55 also add explicit sahibkar_id WHERE. Cross-tenant data access is blocked._
- ✅ FIFO payment allocation — surplus only becomes avans (the 2+100 / 10 AZN scenario) — _features/elaqe/actions.ts:382-421 (recordContactPayment) and features/maliyye/actions.ts:1098-1153 (receivePartialPayment) both fetch open nisyə/borc sales orderBy tarix asc, apply Math.min(remain, qalig) per invoice, and only push leftover to avans (actions.ts:421 toAdvance = max(0,remain)). Correctly closes oldest invoices first._
- ✅ Payment recorded atomically with balance recalc + audit — _recordContactPayment wraps invoice updates + avans + finance_operations.create in prisma.$transaction (features/elaqe/actions.ts:424-493), calls recalculateCustomerBalance inside tx (455-456), writes audit('yenile','kontragent_borc',...) (496-507)._
- ✅ Cancelled (legv) sales/purchases excluded from 360 + statements — _Sale cancel sets BOTH status='legv' AND deleted_at (features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts:291-296); purchase same (alis-actions.ts:522-527). No generic softDeleteRecord caller exists for sales (grep returned none), so status:{not:'legv'} filters in detail-queries.ts and statements reliably drop cancelled docs._
- ✅ Statement debet/kredit running balance math — _customer-statement.ts:218-226 computes qaliq += debet-kredit per row after sorting (satış before ödəniş same-day, line 213); supplier-statement.ts:180-188 mirrors with qaliq += kredit-debet. baslangic_qaliq computed from pre-period UNION ALL aggregate (customer 63-93)._
- ✅ Statement avans double-count avoided — _customer-statement.ts:162-164 builds advanceIds Set and skips those ops in the payments loop, so a [AVANS]-tagged finance_op appears once as nov='avans' not twice._
- ✅ Open-invoices list / payment dialog exclude soft-deleted + drafts — _getOpenSalesForCustomer (features/maliyye/queries.ts:893-913) filters status notIn ['legv','qaytarilib'], qaralama:{not:true}, deleted_at:null, odenis_nov in nisye/borc — and .filter(qalig>0.001)._
- ✅ List & detail pages enforce CRM permission — _musteriler/page.tsx:166 requireElaqePerm('musteri.oxu'); techizatcilar/page.tsx:40 requireElaqePerm('techizatci.oxu'); borclar/page.tsx:30 requireElaqePerm('elaqe.borc'); detail page.tsx:93-102 requires elaqe.oxu + (musteri.oxu OR techizatci.oxu)._
- ✅ Cash vs nisyə sale paid-amount consistency — _satis-yeni-actions.ts:351-366 sets odenilmis=son_mebleg for nağd/kart/köçürmə (status tamamlandi), 0 for nisyə; partial forces odenis_nov='nisye'. So getContactStats unpaid (son_mebleg-odenilmis) nets cash sales to 0._

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] PaymentDialog (recordContactPayment) payments are INVISIBLE in the customer hesab-çıxarış statement**
  - Detal: recordContactPayment writes the finance_operation with type_kod='musteri_odenis' (features/elaqe/actions.ts:459, 475-491). But getCustomerStatement only reads payments where type_kod IN ('qaime','borc_silinme') (features/maliyye/customer-statement.ts:79 and 115). Therefore every payment taken via the elaqe PaymentDialog (rendered on the customer card page.tsx:299 and on the borclar debitor list borclar/page.tsx:347) never appears as a kredit row in the statement, and is also excluded from baslangic_qaliq. Result: the statement's Dövr kredit, son_qaliq and the running Qalıq column are OVERSTATED — the customer looks like they still owe money they have already paid. The same payment DOES correctly increment satis.odenilmis and reduce the source-of-truth alacaq, so the statement contradicts the customer card balance. (NisyePaymentQuick→receivePartialPayment uses type_kod='qaime' and is shown correctly — only the PaymentDialog path is broken.)
  - Sübut: features/elaqe/actions.ts:459 typeKod='musteri_odenis' vs features/maliyye/customer-statement.ts:115 type_kod:{in:['qaime','borc_silinme']}
  - Fayllar: features/elaqe/actions.ts, features/maliyye/customer-statement.ts, features/elaqe/components/payment-dialog.tsx, app/(dashboard)/elaqe/musteriler/[id]/hesab-cixaris/page.tsx

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] PaymentDialog payment credits NO cash/bank account — money vanishes from account balances**
  - Detal: recordContactPayment's PaymentSchema has no hesab_id field (features/elaqe/actions.ts:358-363) and the finance_operations.create it runs sets kontragent_id + satis_id but leaves hesab_id NULL (features/elaqe/actions.ts:472-491). Account balance is computed as SUM over finance_operations filtered by hesab_id (lib/balance/account-balance.ts:59-60). With hesab_id NULL the inflow is counted by NO kassa/bank account, so the cash the customer paid never lands in any account balance. The debt is reduced but the asset side is lost — books no longer balance. Contrast receivePartialPayment which writes hesab_id and recalculates the account (features/maliyye/actions.ts:~1187). This also violates the audit expectation that a cash/card/bank payment credits the matching account.
  - Sübut: features/elaqe/actions.ts:472-491 (no hesab_id) + PaymentSchema 358-363; vs lib/balance/account-balance.ts:59-60 hesab_id filter; vs features/maliyye/actions.ts hesab_id:d.hesab_id||null
  - Fayllar: features/elaqe/actions.ts, lib/balance/account-balance.ts

- 🟠 **[high · permission] Statement & CSV export reachable without any CRM permission**
  - Detal: The /elaqe prefix is in BYPASS_PREFIXES (lib/auth/route-gate.ts:73) so the dashboard layout gate allows all /elaqe/* for any authenticated user, relying on per-page guards. The list/detail pages call requireElaqePerm, but the two statement pages do NOT: customer-statement page.tsx has no requireElaqePerm/auth-permission check (only the underlying query's tenant scope), and supplier-statement page.tsx likewise. The CSV API routes only check session (auth() at musteri .../route.ts:15-16 and techizatci .../route.ts:15-16) with no musteri.oxu/techizatci.oxu/elaqe.oxu check. So a logged-in employee with zero CRM permissions can open /elaqe/musteriler/{id}/hesab-cixaris and /api/musteri/{id}/hesab-cixaris.csv and read a customer's full financial ledger (it is still tenant-scoped, but the intra-tenant permission gate is bypassed).
  - Sübut: lib/auth/route-gate.ts:73 ('/elaqe' bypass); app/(dashboard)/elaqe/musteriler/[id]/hesab-cixaris/page.tsx:29-42 (no requireElaqePerm); app/api/musteri/[id]/hesab-cixaris.csv/route.ts:14-24 (only auth())
  - Fayllar: app/(dashboard)/elaqe/musteriler/[id]/hesab-cixaris/page.tsx, app/(dashboard)/elaqe/techizatcilar/[id]/hesab-cixaris/page.tsx, app/api/musteri/[id]/hesab-cixaris.csv/route.ts, app/api/techizatci/[id]/hesab-cixaris.csv/route.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Debt timeline counts unconfirmed/cancelled returns at full umumi_mebleg, distorting the running balance shown on the customer card**
  - Detal: getContactDebtTimeline fetches qaytarma with where:{kontragent_id:id} and NO status filter, then uses mebleg=umumi_mebleg and delta=-umumi_mebleg (features/elaqe/detail-queries.ts:64-71,113-124). Returns are created in status 'tesdiqlenmemis' with geri_qaytarildi=0 and only get geri_qaytarildi populated + stock/cash effects on acceptReturn (features/qaytarma/actions.ts:71,199-200); cancelled returns become status 'legv' (293). So a draft (tesdiqlenmemis) or cancelled (legv) return — which moved no stock/money — still subtracts its full umumi_mebleg from the timeline's running Qalıq shown in MaliyyeTabSection (page.tsx:1069-1110). getCustomerJourney has the same unfiltered returns query (detail-queries.ts:317-324). Statement is NOT affected (it uses geri_qaytarildi and excludes legv).
  - Sübut: features/elaqe/detail-queries.ts:64-71 (returns where {kontragent_id} only) + 95/123 use umumi_mebleg; features/qaytarma/actions.ts:71 status 'tesdiqlenmemis', 199-200 geri_qaytarildi set only on accept
  - Fayllar: features/elaqe/detail-queries.ts, app/(dashboard)/elaqe/musteriler/[id]/page.tsx

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Three different 'borc' numbers can show for the same customer (card vs statement vs source-of-truth)**
  - Detal: Detail card borc = stats.sales_total - stats.sales_paid (page.tsx:118-126) where getContactStats aggregates son_mebleg/odenilmis over status:{not:'legv'} WITHOUT filtering odenis_nov, qaytarilib, or deleted_at (detail-queries.ts:135-139). Source-of-truth calculateCustomerBalance filters odenis_nov IN(nisye,borc) AND excludes 'qaytarilib' AND deleted_at AND adds servis borc (lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:62-99). Statement son_qaliq is a full ledger that additionally subtracts returns and standalone payments. So for a customer with a fully-returned sale (status 'qaytarilib') or with servis debt, the card borc, the statement son_qaliq, and kontragentler.alacaq diverge. The card's own comment (page.tsx:121-124) claims it reads real data to avoid drift, but getContactStats does not match the documented source-of-truth filters.
  - Sübut: features/elaqe/detail-queries.ts:135-139 (getContactStats — only status:{not:legv}); lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:68-72 (SoT filters deleted_at, qaytarilib, odenis_nov + servis); app/(dashboard)/elaqe/musteriler/[id]/page.tsx:118-126
  - Fayllar: features/elaqe/detail-queries.ts, lib/balance/customer-balance.ts, app/(dashboard)/elaqe/musteriler/[id]/page.tsx

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] recordContactPayment has no idempotency guard — double-submit / refresh can create duplicate payments**
  - Detal: receivePartialPayment implements an [IDEM:key] dedupe window (features/maliyye/actions.ts:1081-1093) but recordContactPayment (the elaqe PaymentDialog action) has no such guard: it always re-fetches open invoices and increments odenilmis + creates a new finance_operation. The PaymentDialog disables the button only via useTransition local state (payment-dialog.tsx:34-42), which does not protect against a second tab, a retried request, or navigation. A duplicate submission would over-pay invoices (push more to avans) and create a second finance_op.
  - Sübut: features/elaqe/actions.ts:365-522 (no idempotency key / dedupe) vs features/maliyye/actions.ts:1081-1093 (idemKey dup check)
  - Fayllar: features/elaqe/actions.ts, features/elaqe/components/payment-dialog.tsx

- ⚪ **[low · functional] Statement 'to' date mutates the shared Date and excludes intra-day rows inconsistently**
  - Detal: getCustomerStatement does to.setHours(23,59,59) on the Date built from the 'to' param (customer-statement.ts:53-54; supplier 52-53). setHours mutates in place and sets ms=current-clock-ms (not 999), so the upper bound is e.g. 23:59:59.xxx; an operation timestamped 23:59:59.5 could be filtered out depending on the random ms. Minor edge case but non-deterministic. Also baslangic uses tarix < from while period uses gte from — a row exactly at 'from' midnight is in-period (correct), but finance_operations tarix is cast ::timestamp in the raw before-aggregate (line 73) while period query compares Date objects, a representation mismatch worth runtime confirmation.
  - Sübut: features/maliyye/customer-statement.ts:53-54 to.setHours(23,59,59); raw cast tarix::timestamp line 73 vs Prisma Date compare line 104-107
  - Fayllar: features/maliyye/customer-statement.ts, features/maliyye/supplier-statement.ts

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] Supplier statement omits avans/borc-silinmə rows that the customer statement includes**
  - Detal: Supplier payments are read only for type_kod='alis_odenis' (supplier-statement.ts:109). Any supplier-side debt write-off (borc_silinme) or supplier advance is not represented as a statement row, so a written-off supplier balance would leave a stale qaliq. Needs confirmation that supplier write-offs/advances are a real flow in this ERP; if they are, the supplier statement under-represents kredit reductions.
  - Sübut: features/maliyye/supplier-statement.ts:103-114 (only type_kod 'alis_odenis'); customer statement by contrast handles 'borc_silinme' + [AVANS]
  - Fayllar: features/maliyye/supplier-statement.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 Customer/Supplier hesab-çıxarış page-level permission: Statement pages do not call requireElaqePerm / requireElaqeActionPerm; /elaqe is in route-gate BYPASS_PREFIXES so any authenticated tenant user can read full financial ledgers regardless of musteri.oxu/techizatci.oxu. — _lib/auth/route-gate.ts:73 + app/(dashboard)/elaqe/musteriler/[id]/hesab-cixaris/page.tsx:29-42 (no guard)_
- 🔐 Statement CSV export API guards: GET handlers only verify auth() session, no CRM permission check — direct API hit returns the customer/supplier statement to any logged-in user. — _app/api/musteri/[id]/hesab-cixaris.csv/route.ts:15-16; app/api/techizatci/[id]/hesab-cixaris.csv/route.ts:15-16_
- 🔐 recordContactPayment server-action permission: Guard exists (requireElaqeActionPerm('musteri.duzelt'), features/elaqe/actions.ts:367) but it gates a financial mutation behind a contact-edit permission rather than a finance/payment permission; a user who can edit contacts but should not take payments can still record (and mis-book) money. Lower priority than the unguarded statement routes but worth noting. — _features/elaqe/actions.ts:366-368 (comment says 'odenis.qebul' but code checks 'musteri.duzelt')_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** features/maliyye/customer-statement.ts, features/elaqe/actions.ts, app/(dashboard)/elaqe/musteriler/[id]/hesab-cixaris/page.tsx, app/(dashboard)/elaqe/techizatcilar/[id]/hesab-cixaris/page.tsx, app/api/musteri/[id]/hesab-cixaris.csv/route.ts, app/api/techizatci/[id]/hesab-cixaris.csv/route.ts, features/elaqe/detail-queries.ts, features/maliyye/supplier-statement.ts, features/elaqe/components/payment-dialog.tsx

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** Xeyr

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** The 360 module is a read aggregator over ticaret (satis_sifarisleri), maliyyə (finance_operations + account balances), qaytarma, servis, and CRM (leads/communications). The two high-severity payment bugs originate in the elaqe module's own recordContactPayment but corrupt maliyyə account balances (hesab_id NULL inflow) and the maliyyə statement service output — so a fix touches maliyyə account-balance integrity. The payment type_kod mismatch means two parallel customer-payment code paths exist (elaqe.recordContactPayment vs maliyye.receivePartialPayment) writing different type_kod; consolidating them is the safest cross-module fix. The 'available balance/borc computed identically everywhere' invariant is violated: card uses getContactStats, statement uses a ledger, source-of-truth uses calculateCustomerBalance — three formulas. Returns lifecycle (qaytarma status tesdiqlenmemis→tamamlandi→legv) is interpreted correctly by the statement but not by the 360 timeline/journey.

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) Statement payment visibility + account crediting: Make recordContactPayment write the same type_kod as receivePartialPayment ('qaime') AND require/accept a hesab_id so the inflow credits a cash/bank account and triggers recalculateAccountBalance — OR better, retire recordContactPayment in favour of receivePartialPayment (which already does FIFO + hesab + idempotency) and point PaymentDialog at it. If type_kod must stay 'musteri_odenis', add it to the customer-statement payments filter (customer-statement.ts:79 and 115) so it shows; but that still leaves the missing-account-credit bug, so the hesab_id fix is mandatory regardless.\n2) Permission: add requireElaqePerm('musteri.oxu') to the customer statement page and ('techizatci.oxu') to the supplier statement page; add the same permission check (after auth) to both CSV API route handlers.\n3) Debt timeline / journey returns: add status NOT IN ('legv') and (for amount) prefer geri_qaytarildi over umumi_mebleg, or filter status='tamamlandi', in getContactDebtTimeline and getCustomerJourney (detail-queries.ts qaytarma queries).\n4) Balance consistency: replace the card's getContactStats-derived borc with the shared source-of-truth (calculateCustomerBalance / kontragentler.alacaq) so card, statement, and SoT agree; align getContactStats filters (odenis_nov, qaytarilib, deleted_at) if it must remain.\n5) Idempotency: add an [IDEM:] dedupe window to recordContactPayment (mirroring receivePartialPayment) — moot if step 1 consolidates onto receivePartialPayment.\n6) Minor: use a deterministic end-of-day (setHours(23,59,59,999)) in both statement services; consider adding supplier write-off/advance handling if those flows exist.

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- Create a nisyə sale for a customer (e.g. 100 AZN). Open the customer card → take a payment of 40 AZN via the 'Ödəniş al' PaymentDialog (NOT the Nisyə Quick). Then open Hesab çıxarışı: the 40 AZN should appear as a kredit and son_qaliq should be 60 — verify whether it is missing (expected bug: payment absent, son_qaliq still 100).
- After the above payment, open Maliyyə → the cash/bank account that should have received the 40 AZN: verify the account balance did NOT increase (expected bug: hesab_id NULL).
- Two open nisyə invoices: 2 AZN (older) and 100 AZN (newer). Take a 10 AZN payment via Nisyə Quick with no invoice selected: expect 2 invoice fully closed + 8 applied to the 100 invoice + 0 avans (verify via Açıq qaimələr tab).
- As a user whose role has NO CRM permission (no musteri.oxu/elaqe.oxu), navigate directly to /elaqe/musteriler/{id}/hesab-cixaris and to /api/musteri/{id}/hesab-cixaris.csv: confirm whether the statement and CSV are served (expected: served — permission bypass).
- Create a return for a customer and leave it in 'tesdiqlenmemis' (do not accept). Open the customer card Borc/Ödəniş tab → check the 'Borc / Ödəniş xronologiyası' running Qalıq: confirm the unaccepted return wrongly reduced the displayed balance by its full amount.
- For a customer with a sale fully returned (status 'qaytarilib') or with open servis debt, compare the borc shown on the card header KPI vs son_qaliq on the statement vs the debitor-list borc: confirm whether all three agree.
- Double-click the 'Ödənişi qeyd et' button in the PaymentDialog (or submit, then refresh and submit again quickly): confirm whether two finance_operations / double odenilmis increments are created (expected: no idempotency guard).
- Open a supplier card and its Hesab çıxarışı; verify alış / ödəniş / qaytarma rows and son_qaliq sign (positive = biz borcluyuq) match the supplier balance shown on the card and the kreditor list.

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Confirm at runtime that a payment taken via the elaqe PaymentDialog (recordContactPayment) does NOT appear on the customer hesab-çıxarış and does NOT change any kassa/bank account balance (expected from code: type_kod='musteri_odenis' + hesab_id NULL).
- Confirm a non-CRM employee (no musteri.oxu/elaqe.oxu) can actually open /elaqe/musteriler/{id}/hesab-cixaris and download the CSV (expected: yes, only session checked).
- Verify whether any production flow ever leaves a satis with deleted_at set but status != 'legv' (would slip past statement/detail status-only filters); code review found no such path but a data-level check is advised.
- Verify whether supplier-side borc_silinme / supplier advances exist as finance_operations in this tenant's data (determines severity of supplier-statement omission).
- Confirm the exact ms behaviour of to.setHours(23,59,59) on boundary-timestamped operations.
- Confirm getContactStats vs calculateCustomerBalance produce different borc for a customer that has a fully-returned sale (status 'qaytarilib') or open servis debt.

---

## 8. Servis

**Xülasə:** The Servis module is generally well-engineered for atomicity and tenant safety: status changes, payments, spare-part consumption, quotes and reversals are wrapped in $transaction with explicit sahibkar_id guards, audit logging, and a shared customer-balance source-of-truth (GREATEST(temir_xerci - musteriden_alinan,0)). Cash/card payments correctly credit the finance account; credit (borc) payments correctly create no cash and only raise customer debt; public customer endpoints are HMAC-token-gated and rate-limited. However there are real cross-module data-integrity and functional defects. The most serious (HIGH): (1) every cash/card service payment ALSO writes a parallel satis_sifarisleri 'sale' line keyed to the repaired customer-owned device, which double-records the income in sales reports and pollutes the margin report with phantom COGS/units for that device; (2) the detail-page 'Rədd edildi' dropdown action always fails because it sends no reject reason, which the backend requires. MEDIUM issues cluster around stock cache vs source-of-truth drift in the spare-part add/no-stock/reversal paths, a permission vocabulary split (route-gate uses servis.view while the module enforces servis.oxu), the complete absence of any soft-delete/cancel path for service records (so the record-status filter is dead and a mistaken paid service cannot be reversed), and cash payments accepted with no account (orphan income). LOW issues: a non-functional emergency-freeze kill-switch, report queries missing the deleted_at filter, and qaytarildi services still counted in customer debt. Several findings need live verification (actual report double-count magnitude, drift accumulation) but are all grounded in the code paths cited.

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** app/(dashboard)/servis/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/servis/[id]/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/servis/[id]/print/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/servis/sla/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/servis/hesabat/page.tsx; features/servis/actions.ts; features/servis/extra-actions.ts; features/servis/zemanet-actions.ts; features/servis/access-guard.ts; features/servis/queries.ts; features/servis/types.ts; features/servis/components/payment-form.tsx; features/servis/components/servis-status-actions.tsx; features/servis/components/reject-reason-dialog.tsx; features/servis/components/quick-status-bar.tsx; app/api/servis/[id]/detail/route.ts; app/api/servis/[id]/upload/route.ts; lib/balance/customer-balance.ts; lib/balance/account-balance.ts; lib/balance/product-stock.ts; lib/db/prisma.ts; lib/db/tenant-models.ts; lib/auth/route-gate.ts; app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx; lib/soft-delete/record-filter.ts; lib/soft-delete/actions.ts; lib/blockers/find-contact-blockers.ts; lib/permission-modules.ts; features/hesabatlar/satis-queries.ts; features/hesabatlar/marja-queries.ts; features/ticaret/satis-queries.ts; prisma/schema.prisma (servis_qeydleri, servis_status_tarixce, satis_sifaris_satirlari, satis_sifarisleri models)

**Əhatə boşluqları:** features/servis/components/servis-table.tsx and servis-detail-modal.tsx (filter/sort/column/export wiring and bulk-status UI) were not fully opened — only confirmed they import RejectReasonDialog; their filter/pagination correctness not audited.; features/servis/components/servis-kanban.tsx drag-drop status transitions not reviewed for permission/transaction correctness.; features/servis/components/akt-print-button.tsx, print-trigger.tsx, musteri-share-button.tsx, notify-tab.tsx handler wiring only partially traced.; app/servis-track/[token] public tracking page and /api/zemanet/[id]/talon.pdf, /api/servis/[id]/qebz.pdf print endpoints not opened (printQebzPdf/printZemanetPdf return mock URLs).; features/servis/actions-problemli.ts not reviewed.; zemanet zemanet/page.tsx UI and zemanet-table/zemanet-dialog wiring not opened in detail.; Exact contents of the seeded permission catalog (which of servis.oxu/servis.view/servis.yarat etc. actually exist as DB rows) could not be located via grep — permission-key existence is inferred from usage, not from a definitive seed file.

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ Spare-part stock decrement is atomic and race-safe in the happy path: stok cache decrement + anbar_hereketleri journal + servis cost update + customer-balance recalc all in one $transaction with a guarded conditional decrement (updated.count===0 throws) — _features/servis/actions.ts:440-528 (addEhtiyatHisse $transaction, updateMany with miqdar:{gte} guard at 476-487)_
- ✅ Cash/card/bank service payment credits the matching account: creates finance_operations with y_n='daxil', azn_meblegh, hesab_id; account-balance sums these correctly — _features/servis/actions.ts:752-778; lib/balance/account-balance.ts:60-72_
- ✅ Credit (borc) service payment creates NO finance op and NO sale; only triggers customer-balance recalc (debt source-of-truth = temir_xerci - musteriden_alinan) — _features/servis/actions.ts:723-733; lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:79-87_
- ✅ Service unpaid balance is included in the shared customer-balance source-of-truth (GREATEST(temir_xerci - musteriden_alinan,0)), not recomputed per page; redd_edildi excluded — _lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:75-99,178-198_
- ✅ Status change is atomic (status update + history row in one $transaction) with explicit sahibkar_id guard; redd_edildi requires a reason at the action level — _features/servis/actions.ts:237-282_
- ✅ All mutating server actions enforce backend permission via requireServisActionPerm (OR-list) and audit-log on success via safeAuditLog/writeServisAudit — _features/servis/actions.ts:99-100,233-234,425-426,672-674; access-guard.ts:45-54; writeServisAudit 19-34_
- ✅ servis_qeydleri is a tenant model; reads/writes auto-scoped by extended prisma client; cross-tenant detail access returns null -> notFound — _lib/db/tenant-models.ts:192; lib/db/prisma.ts:62-66; app/(dashboard)/servis/[id]/page.tsx:54_
- ✅ Public customer endpoints (review, quote approval) are token-gated (HMAC timing-safe) + rate-limited; daxili_qeyd excluded from public track query — _features/servis/actions.ts:945-1078; access-guard.ts:79-134; queries.ts:464_
- ✅ Spare-part reversal source-of-truth is internally consistent: servis_iade recognized as positive, servis_mexaric/_stoxsuz as negative in product-stock — _lib/balance/product-stock.ts:52-72_
- ✅ List query applies soft-delete default filter (deleted_at IS NULL) and excludes returned items only via recordStatus — _features/servis/queries.ts:54-56_
- ✅ Customer-card service-history tab is wired to getServisHistoryForCustomer — _app/(dashboard)/elaqe/musteriler/[id]/page.tsx:39,875_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Cash/card service payment also writes a parallel satis_sifarisleri 'sale' that double-counts revenue and distorts margin reports**
  - Detal: recordPayment with satis_kimi_qeyd_et (default ON) creates a real satis_sifarisleri row (status='tamamlandi', qaralama=false, deleted_at null, son_mebleg=meblegh) AND a satis_sifaris_satirlari line with mehsul_id = the repaired device, miqdar=1, vahid_qiymet=meblegh. This sale is in addition to the finance_operations cash row. (1) Sales revenue dashboards/reports sum son_mebleg from satis_sifarisleri (hesabatlar/satis-queries.ts), while cash/finance reports sum the finance_operations row — the same service income is recorded in two income systems. (2) The margin report (marja-queries) joins satis_sifaris_satirlari->mehsullar and computes cogs = miqdar * alish_qiymeti of the repaired device and revenue = sls.cemi(=meblegh). Because the repaired customer-owned device was NOT actually sold, its purchase cost is subtracted as phantom COGS and the device shows inflated units-sold and a distorted margin. The auto-sale also has no stock line/decrement, which is correct for a service but means the line is a pure accounting artifact polluting product analytics.
  - Sübut: features/servis/actions.ts:780-833 (auto-sale + line with mehsul_id=servis.mehsul_id); features/hesabatlar/marja-queries.ts:23-30,85-92 (cogs = sls.miqdar*m.alish_qiymeti); features/hesabatlar/satis-queries.ts:57-115 (son_mebleg aggregation)
  - Fayllar: features/servis/actions.ts, features/hesabatlar/marja-queries.ts, features/hesabatlar/satis-queries.ts

- 🟠 **[high · functional] Detail-page 'Rədd edildi' action always fails — no reason collected**
  - Detal: ServisStatusActions (the 'Növbəti mərhələ' dropdown on /servis/[id]) lists 'redd_edildi' as a next stage for qebul_edildi/teklif_gozleyir and calls go(s) -> changeServisStatus(id,'redd_edildi',undefined). But changeServisStatus rejects redd_edildi when qeyd is missing/<3 chars, returning 'Rədd səbəbi tələb olunur'. So this menu item always shows an error toast and never rejects the service. The proper RejectReasonDialog (which passes a reason) is wired only into servis-table.tsx and servis-detail-modal.tsx, not into the detail page header.
  - Sübut: features/servis/components/servis-status-actions.tsx:25,27,43-52,67-73 (go('redd_edildi') with undefined qeyd); features/servis/actions.ts:237-239 (requires qeyd>=3)
  - Fayllar: features/servis/components/servis-status-actions.tsx, features/servis/actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] deleteEhtiyatHisse reversal does not restore the stok cache table (cache vs source-of-truth drift)**
  - Detal: addEhtiyatHisse (with-stock branch) decrements BOTH the stok cache (stok.updateMany decrement) AND writes a servis_mexaric journal row. deleteEhtiyatHisse only writes a positive servis_iade journal row and lowers temir_xerci — it never re-increments the stok cache. So the anbar_hereketleri source-of-truth nets back to the original quantity, but the stok cache stays permanently under-counted. Any code path reading stok.miqdar directly (POS / fast paths) will show the part as still consumed, diverging from the source-of-truth and being flagged as drift.
  - Sübut: features/servis/actions.ts:474-484 (cache decrement on add) vs 1107-1142 (reversal: only servis_iade journal + temir_xerci, no stok increment); lib/balance/product-stock.ts:43-47,144-146 (cache vs hereket drift)
  - Fayllar: features/servis/actions.ts, lib/balance/product-stock.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] servis_mexaric_stoxsuz path decrements the source-of-truth without touching cache, and can drive computed stock negative**
  - Detal: When stock is insufficient, addEhtiyatHisse writes a servis_mexaric_stoxsuz journal row but does NOT decrement the stok cache (by design, to avoid negative cache). However product-stock.ts counts servis_mexaric_stoxsuz as NEGATIVE in the hereket-based source-of-truth, so the computed available quantity goes negative — exactly the 'mənfi stok' the code comment claims to prevent. This also guarantees stok-cache vs hereket drift for that quantity.
  - Sübut: features/servis/actions.ts:503-527 (stoxsuz branch: journal only, no stok update; comment at 462-464 about avoiding negative); lib/balance/product-stock.ts:63-72 (servis_mexaric_stoxsuz => -miqdar)
  - Fayllar: features/servis/actions.ts, lib/balance/product-stock.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] Permission key mismatch: route-gate requires servis.view/servis.idare while the module's own guards require servis.oxu**
  - Detal: The dashboard layout route-gate gates the whole /servis prefix on anyOf ['servis.view','servis.idare']. But every module guard (requireServisPerm, requireServisActionPerm) treats servis.oxu as the master read permission and the page subroutes have no per-page guard, relying solely on the gate. Net effect: a role granted servis.oxu + action perms (e.g. servis.yarat) but NOT servis.view/servis.idare is redirected away from /servis by the gate even though the module was designed for servis.oxu; conversely a role granted only servis.view passes the gate but extra-actions.ts subqueries that call requireServisPerm('hesabat.oxu'/'zemanet.oxu') will deny. The two permission vocabularies are not aligned, making correct least-privilege provisioning effectively impossible.
  - Sübut: lib/auth/route-gate.ts:45 ({prefix:'/servis', anyOf:['servis.view','servis.idare']}); features/servis/access-guard.ts:23 (servis.oxu); grep: servis.view only appears in route-gate.ts + features/hesabatlar, servis.oxu only in features/servis/*
  - Fayllar: lib/auth/route-gate.ts, features/servis/access-guard.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · functional] No way to delete/cancel a service record — soft-delete columns and the record-status filter are dead for servis**
  - Detal: servis_qeydleri has deleted_at/deleted_by/delete_reason columns and the /servis page exposes a RecordStatusFilter (Aktiv/Silinmiş/Hamısı), but NO server action ever sets deleted_at (no entry in lib/soft-delete/actions.ts, no servis soft-delete action anywhere). So the 'Silinmiş'/'Hamısı' filter always returns 0 and there is no path to cancel/reverse a wrongly-created service. A mistaken service that already received a cash payment (finance_operations + auto-sale) therefore cannot be deleted or reversed; status can only move to redd_edildi/qaytarildi, which does NOT reverse the cash op or the parallel sale.
  - Sübut: grep: no deleted_at write on servis_qeydleri except queries.ts:55-56 filter; lib/soft-delete/actions.ts has no servis case; features/servis/actions.ts:227-335 redd path does not reverse finance_operations/satis
  - Fayllar: features/servis/queries.ts, lib/soft-delete/actions.ts, features/servis/actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Cash payment can be recorded with no account (hesab_id null) — orphan income not in any cash drawer**
  - Detal: PaymentForm.submit() only validates meblegh>0; it does not require an account selection. With kassaya_elave_et=true (default) and the '— Seçin —' option left selected, hesab_id is omitted, so recordPayment creates finance_operations with hesab_id:null. account-balance.ts only credits operations matching a specific hesab_id, so this income exists in finance totals but credits no cash register, breaking cash reconciliation. createServisRequest's auto-initial-payment mitigates this with a default-kassa fallback, but recordPayment itself has no such fallback or required-account validation.
  - Sübut: features/servis/components/payment-form.tsx:33-41 (only meblegh guard; hesab_id appended only if truthy); features/servis/actions.ts:753-766 (hesab_id: d.hesab_id || null); contrast createServisRequest fallback at actions.ts:155-163
  - Fayllar: features/servis/components/payment-form.tsx, features/servis/actions.ts

- ⚪ **[low · functional] emergencyFreezeServis kill-switch is never enforced (dead control)**
  - Detal: emergencyFreezeServis writes an ayarlar 'servis/freeze' flag and isServisFrozen reads it, but createServisRequest (and createServisFromZemanet) never call isServisFrozen. The freeze is the only reference to itself. Toggling 'freeze' has no effect — new services can still be accepted, so the emergency-stop is non-functional.
  - Sübut: grep: isServisFrozen referenced only at its own definition (extra-actions.ts:464); createServisRequest (actions.ts:98-223) never checks freeze
  - Fayllar: features/servis/extra-actions.ts, features/servis/actions.ts

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] Analytics/report queries omit the soft-delete (deleted_at IS NULL) filter that the list query applies**
  - Detal: getServisRequests filters deleted_at by recordStatus, but getServisStats, getServisHesabat, getServisHistoryForProduct, getServisHistoryForCustomer, getProblemMehsullar, getSLAOverview, getServisDashboardWidgets and the extra-actions aggregates do NOT exclude deleted_at. This is currently latent because no path ever sets deleted_at on servis (see separate finding), but if a soft-delete is ever added, deleted service rows would silently inflate every report/stat while disappearing from the list — an inconsistency that should be closed alongside any soft-delete implementation.
  - Sübut: features/servis/queries.ts:162-235 (stats, no deleted_at), 955-1063 (hesabat), 1402-1445 (customer history); contrast 54-56 (list filter)
  - Fayllar: features/servis/queries.ts, features/servis/extra-actions.ts

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] Customer-balance includes qaytarildi (returned) services in debt**
  - Detal: calculateCustomerBalance excludes only status='redd_edildi' from the servis-debt sum, not 'qaytarildi'. A device returned to the customer without completed repair but with a non-zero temir_xerci (e.g. diagnostic fee left, or cost set then returned) keeps adding GREATEST(temir_xerci - musteriden_alinan,0) to the customer's alacaq. If qaytarildi is meant as a terminal no-charge return, this over-states customer debt; if it is meant to be billable, this is intended — needs a product decision but is worth flagging as an inconsistency vs redd_edildi.
  - Sübut: lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:86 (status NOT IN ('redd_edildi') — qaytarildi not excluded); status list features/servis/actions.ts:36-47
  - Fayllar: lib/balance/customer-balance.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 /servis route access vs module guards: Route-gate gates /servis on servis.view/servis.idare, but all module server actions and subqueries use servis.oxu as the master read permission. The two vocabularies are not aligned, so a least-privilege role with servis.oxu is locked out by the gate and a role with only servis.view passes the gate but is denied by requireServisPerm in subpages/actions. — _lib/auth/route-gate.ts:45; features/servis/access-guard.ts:23,45-54; features/servis/extra-actions.ts:43,154,487_
- 🔐 Page-level guard on /servis, /servis/[id], /servis/sla, /servis/hesabat: These server-component pages call query functions (getServisRequests/getServisDetail/getSLAOverview/getServisHesabat) that perform NO permission check (only withTenant). They rely entirely on the layout route-gate. If the gate is bypassed (e.g. x-pathname header missing — the layout swallows that error) the pages would still render for any authenticated tenant user. Backend mutations are independently guarded, so this is read-exposure only, but page-level requireServisPerm is absent unlike the extra-actions functions. — _app/(dashboard)/servis/[id]/page.tsx:44-54 (no requireServisPerm); app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx:115-128 (gate swallows header error)_
- 🔐 Soft-deleted record visibility gate: readRecordStatusFromSearch correctly downgrades non-privileged users to 'aktiv', so deleted-record visibility is gated — but it is moot for servis because nothing ever soft-deletes a servis record. No bypass, just dead functionality. — _lib/soft-delete/record-filter.ts:26-41; no deleted_at writer for servis_qeydleri_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** features/servis/actions.ts, features/servis/components/payment-form.tsx, features/servis/components/servis-status-actions.tsx, lib/auth/route-gate.ts, features/servis/queries.ts, lib/soft-delete/actions.ts, features/hesabatlar/marja-queries.ts

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** Xeyr

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** High cross-module impact. (a) recordPayment writes into ticaret (satis_sifarisleri/satis_sifaris_satirlari) and maliyye (finance_operations) simultaneously, so the double-count affects the Ticarət sales list, Hesabatlar sales/marja reports, and any dashboard summing son_mebleg — service income appears both as a 'sale' and as a finance inflow. (b) The repaired device's mehsul_id flowing into satis_sifaris_satirlari corrupts product margin/units analytics in features/hesabatlar/marja-queries.ts and product-detail sales stats. (c) Spare-part consumption feeds Anbar stock (stok cache + anbar_hereketleri) and the shared product-stock source-of-truth used by POS/new-sale/marketplace; the cache-vs-source-of-truth drift from the no-stock and reversal branches can mislead availability across all selling surfaces and trigger drift alerts. (d) Service debt feeds the shared customer-balance used by debitor lists, customer 360, and ticaret reports, so qaytarildi inclusion and the inability to reverse a rejected paid service propagate to receivables.

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) Service-sale double-count/margin pollution (HIGH): decide one source of truth for service income. Either stop creating satis_sifarisleri from recordPayment (rely only on finance_operations xidmet_geliri) and remove satis_kimi_qeyd_et, OR tag/segregate these sales (e.g. a service flag / synthetic 'xidmet' product with zero alish_qiymeti) and exclude them from marja-queries and product analytics by excluding the [XIDMET] sales or NULLing their mehsul_id so cogs is not attributed to a real catalog product. At minimum, set the line's mehsul_id to null (or a non-stock service SKU) so marja COGS is not charged against the repaired device. 2) Detail-page reject (HIGH): replace the direct go('redd_edildi') in servis-status-actions.tsx with the existing RejectReasonDialog (as servis-table already does) so a reason is collected. 3) Stock drift (MEDIUM): make deleteEhtiyatHisse re-increment the stok cache for parts that originally decremented it (mirror the add path), and reconsider whether servis_mexaric_stoxsuz should count negatively in product-stock or be neutral; align the cache and source-of-truth in all three branches. 4) Permission mismatch (MEDIUM): unify on one key set — either add servis.oxu to the route-gate anyOf or rename module guards to servis.view; ensure the seeded permission catalog contains exactly the keys used. 5) Soft-delete/cancel (MEDIUM): add a servis soft-delete/cancel action that, inside one $transaction, sets deleted_at, reverses the finance_operations cash op + the auto-created sale, restores any spare-part stock, and recalcs customer balance; then add deleted_at IS NULL to all report/stat queries. 6) Cash account (MEDIUM): require an account in PaymentForm when kassaya_elave_et is on, or apply the same default-kassa fallback recordPayment's caller uses. 7) Freeze (LOW): call isServisFrozen() at the top of createServisRequest. 8) qaytarildi (LOW): confirm intended billing semantics and exclude from customer-balance if non-billable.

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- Accept a new service with an initial cash amount and no account selected -> verify the initial payment lands in the default cash register (createServisRequest fallback) and that a finance op + sale are created exactly once.
- On /servis/[id], open 'Növbəti mərhələ' and choose 'Rədd edildi' -> observe it fails with 'Rədd səbəbi tələb olunur' (bug); then reject via the table/modal RejectReasonDialog and confirm it succeeds.
- Record a cash service payment with satis_kimi_qeyd_et ON, then open Ticarət sales list and Hesabatlar margin report -> check whether the service income appears as a sale and whether the repaired product's margin/units are distorted.
- Add a spare part when product has stock, then delete that spare part -> check the product's available quantity on the product card / POS vs the warehouse source-of-truth for drift.
- Add a spare part when the product has zero stock (servis_mexaric_stoxsuz) -> check whether computed available goes negative.
- Record a service payment with the account dropdown left on '— Seçin —' and kassaya_elave_et ON -> check whether any cash register balance changes (expected: none, orphan income).
- Set a service to redd_edildi after a cash payment was recorded -> verify whether the cash op and auto-sale are reversed (expected: not reversed).
- Toggle Emergency Freeze ON, then accept a new service -> verify whether creation is actually blocked (expected: not blocked).
- As a user granted servis.oxu + servis.yarat but not servis.view/servis.idare, navigate to /servis -> verify whether the route-gate redirects to /icaze-yox.

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Confirm at runtime that the [XIDMET] auto-sale actually appears in /ticaret/satislar and in Hesabatlar sales totals and double-counts vs the finance_operations row (i.e. that no downstream filter excludes [XIDMET]-prefixed sales).
- Confirm the marja report charges the repaired device's alish_qiymeti as COGS for the service sale line and shows it as a sold unit (run with a service payment where servis.mehsul_id is a catalog product with non-zero alish_qiymeti).
- Verify Prisma 6 accepts the tenant extension injecting sahibkar_id into findUnique where for getServisDetail/getServisFullDetail without throwing (expected supported in v6 as an additional filter, but confirm no PrismaClientValidationError at runtime).
- Confirm clicking 'Rədd edildi' in the detail-page ServisStatusActions dropdown produces the 'Rədd səbəbi tələb olunur' error and never transitions status.
- Measure actual stok-cache vs hereket source-of-truth drift after an addEhtiyatHisse + deleteEhtiyatHisse cycle, and after a no-stock (servis_mexaric_stoxsuz) consumption.
- Verify recordPayment with kassaya_elave_et=true and no account selected creates a finance_operations row with hesab_id null that is absent from every account balance.

---

## 9. Əməkdaşlar

**Xülasə:** The Əməkdaşlar module is structurally sound on the write path: every mutation server action enforces requireHrActionPerm before touching the DB, tenant isolation is applied via the Prisma extension for all HR models, payroll payment is wrapped in a $transaction (bordro status + isci_odenisleri + finance_operations + account recalc), audit logging is comprehensive, and login blocks deactivated staff. The serious problems are READ-side permission gaps: salary/FİN/bank PII is exposed to any isci.view user on the employee list, /iscilier/maas, and /iscilier/kpi (none apply the maas.view/canViewSalary check that the detail page correctly uses), and two export API routes are under-guarded — /api/iscilier/kpi-export has no auth/permission check at all and /api/iscilier/davamiyyet/export checks only auth. Data-integrity concerns: payBordro has a TOCTOU double-pay window (status checked outside the transaction, no conditional update / idempotency key), there is no reversal/unpay path for a paid bordro (so a mistaken payment can't be atomically rolled back from finance), the sales-commission (payroll) and bonus-pool calculations attribute sales to different fields (satis_meneceri_id vs yaradan_id), negative net pay can create a negative isci_odenisleri row with no finance leg, and bustHrCache misses the hr: tag so the headcount strip goes stale. Soft-delete exclusion from active payroll/attendance/lists is correctly implemented, but there is no employee restore action. Several items (mezuniyyet, bonus bands, training, vacancy guards, analitika raw counts) were not fully audited — see coverageGaps. Concurrency/double-pay and the low-permission read exposure should be confirmed with a live test.

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** app/(dashboard)/iscilier/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/iscilier/[id]/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/iscilier/[id]/bordro-print/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/iscilier/maas/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/iscilier/davamiyyet/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/iscilier/kpi/page.tsx; app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts; app/api/iscilier/davamiyyet/export/route.ts; features/iscilier/actions.ts; features/iscilier/hr-actions.ts; features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts; features/iscilier/davamiyyet-actions.ts; features/iscilier/vezife-actions.ts; features/iscilier/access-guard.ts; features/iscilier/queries.ts; features/iscilier/maas-queries.ts; features/iscilier/detail-queries.ts; features/iscilier/payroll-account-action.ts; features/iscilier/payroll-context-action.ts; features/iscilier/bonus-calc.ts; features/iscilier/hr-queries.ts (partial); features/iscilier/components/bordro-pay-dialog.tsx; features/iscilier/components/employees-table.tsx (salary/PII columns + delete wiring); lib/db/prisma.ts (tenant extension); lib/db/with-tenant.ts; lib/db/tenant-models.ts; lib/auth/route-gate.ts; lib/soft-delete/actions.ts (registry); lib/soft-delete/record-filter.ts (grep); prisma/schema.prisma (istifadeciler, maas_hesablamalar, isci_odenisleri models)

**Əhatə boşluqları:** features/iscilier/mezuniyyet-actions.ts and mezuniyyet page (leave request/approve flow) — not opened in detail; leave affects payroll prorata via mezuniyyet_gun.; features/iscilier/bonus-profile-actions.ts, bonus-profile.ts, maas-band-actions.ts/queries (salary bands) — not read; hr.bonus_idare / maas.skala gating unverified.; features/iscilier/treninq-actions.ts, vakansiya-actions.ts, documents-actions.ts, budce-actions.ts — out of core scope, not audited for guards.; features/iscilier/components/* beyond employees-table and bordro-pay-dialog (employee-dialog form fields, kpi-dashboard-table, davamiyyet-panel client logic) — only grepped, not fully read.; features/iscilier/analitika-queries.ts and /iscilier/analitika page (uses getEmployeeStats raw counts) — getEmployeeStats at queries.ts:157-188 uses prisma.count() without deleted_at filter; analitika page consumes it but I did not fully trace whether soft-deleted are double-counted there.; kpi-dashboard-queries.ts read only via grep for permission gate; full bonus/score math not line-audited.; auth.ts session shape (rol_ad presence for isHrPrivileged) confirmed only partially.

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ Tenant scoping covers all HR models — istifadeciler, davamiyyet, maas_hesablamalar, isci_odenisleri, isci_mezuniyyet, vezifeler are in TENANT_MODELS, and the prisma extension injects sahibkar_id into where for findUnique/findFirst/update/delete and into create data; payBordro's findUnique({where:{id}}) is therefore tenant-scoped at runtime. — _lib/db/tenant-models.ts:50,80,81,87,125 + lib/db/prisma.ts:62-66_
- ✅ Route-gate protects /iscilier prefix at the layout level requiring isci.view/isci.idare/hr.view; sahibkar/admin/owner bypass. — _lib/auth/route-gate.ts:34; app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx:120 gateRoute()_
- ✅ Mutation server actions enforce backend permission via requireHrActionPerm before any DB write (saveEmployee, deactivateEmployee, calculateBordro, payBordro, bulkPayBordro, adjustBordro, saveBonusOrPenalty, terminateEmployee, addDisciplinaryAction, saveAttendance, markAllPresentToday, addWarning, createVezife). — _features/iscilier/actions.ts:34,113; maas-actions.ts:109,368,442,514,256; hr-actions.ts:130,352,465; davamiyyet-actions.ts:43,191,303; vezife-actions.ts:20,67,104_
- ✅ Payroll payment writes are atomic: bordro status flip + isci_odenisleri row + finance_operations mexaric + account balance recalc all inside one prisma.$transaction with the tenant-extended tx client. — _features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts:387-415 (payBordro), 457-482 (bulkPayBordro)_
- ✅ Maaş finance leg credits a real cash/bank account (default nagd, fallback bank/kart) and uses checkAccountSufficient to prevent negative balance, then recalculateAccountBalance from source-of-truth. — _features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts:36-92 (recordMaasFinanceLeg)_
- ✅ Audit log written on every mutation (yarat/yenile/sil/hesabla/isden_cixar/intizam/xebardarliq/perf_review). — _actions.ts:78,94,133; maas-actions.ts:229,343,416,489,548; hr-actions.ts:95,194,280,437,511; davamiyyet-actions.ts:105,176,223,336_
- ✅ Employee detail page and bordro-print correctly redact salary/FİN/bank via canViewSalary and gate edit dialog behind isci.idare/privileged. — _app/(dashboard)/iscilier/[id]/page.tsx:68,85,90,93,150; bordro-print/page.tsx:35-38_
- ✅ Login blocks deactivated/terminated employees (auth filters aktiv:true), and deactivateEmployee/terminateEmployee set aktiv=false + isden_cixdi (+deleted_at on deactivate). — _auth.ts:67; actions.ts:123-131; hr-actions.ts:499-507_
- ✅ Active payroll/attendance queries exclude deactivated employees (aktiv:true, isden_cixdi:null) and active employee list excludes soft-deleted by default (deleted_at:null). — _maas-queries.ts:45; maas-actions.ts:126; davamiyyet-actions.ts:197; queries.ts:42_
- ✅ Self-cannot-deactivate / self-cannot-terminate guards present. — _actions.ts:117; hr-actions.ts:475-477_
- ✅ Pay dialog disables the confirm button during the pending transition, reducing single-user double-click. — _features/iscilier/components/bordro-pay-dialog.tsx:33,213 (disabled={pending})_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🟠 **[high · permission] Salary/FİN/bank PII leaked to any isci.view user on employee LIST page (no maas.view gate)**
  - Detal: getEmployees() returns raw aylik_maas, fin_kod, bank_ad, bank_hesab for every employee with NO redaction, and the list page passes items straight to EmployeesTable, which renders the maas/fin_kod/bank columns unconditionally (column visibility is just a UI toggle, not a permission gate). The route-gate for /iscilier only requires isci.view. So any user holding isci.view (e.g. a regular sales rep) can read every colleague's monthly salary, FİN code, and bank/IBAN by opening /iscilier (and toggling the column). The detail page DOES redact via canViewSalary, so the protection is inconsistent and was clearly intended.
  - Sübut: features/iscilier/queries.ts:129,134-137 (returns unredacted) + app/(dashboard)/iscilier/page.tsx:83-93 (no redaction, no canViewSalary) + components/employees-table.tsx:289,302,307-316
  - Fayllar: features/iscilier/queries.ts, app/(dashboard)/iscilier/page.tsx, features/iscilier/components/employees-table.tsx

- 🟠 **[high · permission] KPI export API route has NO auth() and NO permission check**
  - Detal: GET /api/iscilier/kpi-export builds a CSV containing every employee's salary, bonus pool, cerime, net pay and sales for the tenant, but the handler never calls auth() and never checks isci.view/maas.view. It only calls getKpiDashboard which uses withTenant (which internally calls auth() and would throw for a fully unauthenticated request → 500), so it is not anonymous-readable, but ANY authenticated user of the tenant — regardless of HR permissions — can hit this URL directly and download the full salary roster. The davamiyyet export route at least checks session?.user; this one does not even do that.
  - Sübut: app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts:16-30 (no auth/perm) ; kpi-dashboard-queries.ts has no maas.view/canViewSalary gate (grep returned none) and selects aylik_maas at line 40
  - Fayllar: app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts, features/iscilier/kpi-dashboard-queries.ts

- 🟠 **[high · permission] /iscilier/maas and /iscilier/kpi pages expose all salaries with only isci.view (no maas.view page gate)**
  - Detal: The maas (bordro) and kpi pages render every employee's esas_maas / net / bonus / cerime but have NO page-level guard beyond the layout route-gate, which only requires isci.view. getMaasTable and getKpiDashboard apply no maas.view / canViewSalary check. A user with isci.view but without maas.view sees the entire payroll. access-guard.ts even defines requireHrPagePerm('maas.view') and canViewSalary for exactly this purpose, but these pages don't call them.
  - Sübut: app/(dashboard)/iscilier/maas/page.tsx (no requireHrPagePerm/canViewSalary) ; app/(dashboard)/iscilier/kpi/page.tsx:45 (no gate) ; maas-queries.ts:74-122 returns esas/net unconditionally
  - Fayllar: app/(dashboard)/iscilier/maas/page.tsx, app/(dashboard)/iscilier/kpi/page.tsx

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] Davamiyyet export API route checks only auth, not davamiyyet.view**
  - Detal: GET /api/iscilier/davamiyyet/export verifies session?.user but does not require davamiyyet.view or isci.view. Any logged-in tenant user can download the full attendance roster (entry/exit times, lateness, hours) for all employees, bypassing the davamiyyet.view permission that gates the corresponding UI logic.
  - Sübut: app/api/iscilier/davamiyyet/export/route.ts:6-9 (only auth, then getDavamiyyetData)
  - Fayllar: app/api/iscilier/davamiyyet/export/route.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] payBordro double-pay race: status check is outside the $transaction (TOCTOU)**
  - Detal: payBordro reads b.status and rejects if 'odenilib' BEFORE opening the $transaction; inside the transaction it updates status without re-checking and without a conditional updateMany guard. Two concurrent requests (double-click that beats the disabled-state, browser retry, or two tabs) can both pass the pre-check and each create an isci_odenisleri row + a finance_operations mexaric, double-deducting the account. bulkPayBordro is safer (it filters status:{not:'odenilib'} but still without a per-row optimistic guard). No DB-level idempotency key on isci_odenisleri (sened_nomresi MAAS-{bordroId} is on finance_operations but not enforced unique).
  - Sübut: features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts:378-415 (findUnique+status check at 380, transaction opens at 387, update at 388 has no status-conditional)
  - Fayllar: features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Paid payroll has no reversal/unpay path — finance leg cannot be atomically reversed**
  - Detal: There is no action to un-pay / cancel a paid bordro. Once payBordro runs (status=odenilib, isci_odenisleri created, finance_operations mexaric posted, account balance reduced), a mistaken payment cannot be reversed through the module: there is no reverse function, and adjustBordro/saveBonusOrPenalty/addDisciplinaryAction all refuse when status==='odenilib'. Per the data-integrity expectation that cancelling a payment must reverse the finance movement + restore balance, this flow is missing entirely for payroll.
  - Sübut: features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts — grep for unpay/reverse/legv returned only the sales status filters; payBordro:367-439 has no inverse; adjustBordro:527 and saveBonusOrPenalty:278 block edits on paid rows
  - Fayllar: features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Sales commission (payroll) and bonus pool use DIFFERENT attribution fields — divergent salesperson math**
  - Detal: calculateBordro/getMaasTable compute the 3% sales commission from satis_sifarisleri grouped by satis_meneceri_id, and getEmployeeSales also uses satis_meneceri_id — consistent with each other. BUT bonus-calc.ts (calculateMonthlyBonus, used on the employee detail bonus panel and via KPI) computes the bonus pool from yaradan_id. Since sales set satis_meneceri_id = data.satis_meneceri_id ?? istifadeciId (the assigned manager) while yaradan_id is the row creator, when a sale is created by user A on behalf of salesperson B, the 3% payroll commission goes to B but the bonus pool credits A. Two different sources-of-truth for 'who made the sale' across the same module, risking mis/double-attribution.
  - Sübut: maas-actions.ts:132-138 & maas-queries.ts:52-58 (satis_meneceri_id) vs bonus-calc.ts:91-93,290-298 (yaradan_id) ; satis set at features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts:237,266 and features/pos/sale-action.ts:195 (satis_meneceri_id ?? istifadeciId)
  - Fayllar: features/iscilier/bonus-calc.ts, features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts, features/iscilier/maas-queries.ts

- ⚪ **[low · ux] Headcount KPI strip cache not invalidated by HR mutations (tag mismatch)**
  - Detal: getHeadcountStats caches under tag hr:${sahibkarId} (unstable_cache, revalidate 120). bustHrCache() (called after every HR mutation) revalidates dashboard:, nezaret:, iscilier:, ref:${id}:iscilier — but NOT hr:${id}. So after creating/deactivating an employee, the top headcount strip (Cəmi/Aktiv/Bu ay yeni/İşdən çıxmış) stays stale for up to 120s.
  - Sübut: features/iscilier/hr-queries.ts:74 (tag hr:${sahibkarId}) vs access-guard.ts:117-120 (bustHrCache revalidates iscilier/dashboard/nezaret/ref, not hr)
  - Fayllar: features/iscilier/access-guard.ts, features/iscilier/hr-queries.ts

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] Negative net payroll still creates an isci_odenisleri row with a negative amount**
  - Detal: son_meblegh = gross - cerime - avans - vergi - sosial can go negative (e.g. large cerime/avans on a small prorata). payBordro creates isci_odenisleri.meblegh = b.son_meblegh (negative) but recordMaasFinanceLeg early-returns ok when meblegh<=0, so the finance/account side is skipped. Result: an employee-payment record showing a negative payout with no matching finance movement, plus the bordro flips to 'odenilib' while nothing was actually paid. Net is never clamped to 0.
  - Sübut: maas-actions.ts:195 (son = gross - vergi - sosial), 317, 543 (son = gross - cerime - avans - ...), 392-401 (isci_odenisleri uses son_meblegh raw), 35 (recordMaasFinanceLeg returns ok on meblegh<=0)
  - Fayllar: features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts

- ⚪ **[low · ux] No restore action for soft-deleted employees (silinmis filter shows them but they can't be restored in-module)**
  - Detal: deactivateEmployee sets deleted_at/deleted_by (soft delete), and the list supports recordStatus=silinmis to view them. But istifadeciler is NOT in the generic softDeleteRecord allowed-table union, and there is no restoreEmployee action; EmployeesTable only renders the deactivate (Trash) button when e.aktiv is true and has no restore button. A soft-deleted employee shown under 'silinmis' has no way to be reactivated from this module's UI.
  - Sübut: lib/soft-delete/actions.ts:33-38 (table union excludes istifadeciler) ; features/iscilier/components/employees-table.tsx:332 (delete only when aktiv, no restore) ; features/iscilier/actions.ts has no restore function
  - Fayllar: features/iscilier/actions.ts, features/iscilier/components/employees-table.tsx, lib/soft-delete/actions.ts

- ⚪ **[low · permission] quickMarkAttendance allows self-marking attendance (potential time-fraud) without davamiyyet.idare**
  - Detal: quickMarkAttendance lets the current user mark their OWN attendance (qaydasinda/gecikib/qaib) without davamiyyet.idare; only marking someone else requires the permission. It is audited (self flag recorded) but this is by design a self-service punch-in. Worth confirming intent: a user can set status=qaydasinda for themselves, which then feeds prorata payroll (ish_faktiki) and the davamiyyet bonus category, so self-attendance can inflate own pay/bonus.
  - Sübut: features/iscilier/davamiyyet-actions.ts:129-139 (self bypasses requireHrActionPerm) ; feeds maas-actions.ts:167-188 prorata and bonus-calc.ts:196-209
  - Fayllar: features/iscilier/davamiyyet-actions.ts

- ⚪ **[low · permission] saveEmployeeExtras / self-service profile not gated by a HR page; PerfReview reviewer trust**
  - Detal: saveEmployeeExtras allows editing if d.istifadeci_id === current user (self) OR isci.idare — correct. updateMyProfile only updates the caller's own row (id: istifadeciId) — correct. No backend bypass found here; flagged only to note these were verified as the safe path. (Included for completeness, not an exploit.)
  - Sübut: features/iscilier/hr-actions.ts:71-76 (self-or-perm), 535-552 (self only)
  - Fayllar: features/iscilier/hr-actions.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 Can a non-HR user read all employee salaries/FİN/bank?: YES via the list page (/iscilier) and /iscilier/maas, /iscilier/kpi — these require only isci.view (route-gate) and apply no maas.view/canViewSalary redaction. getEmployees returns raw PII; EmployeesTable renders maas/fin_kod/bank columns unconditionally. — _lib/auth/route-gate.ts:34; features/iscilier/queries.ts:129-137; app/(dashboard)/iscilier/page.tsx:83-93; components/employees-table.tsx:289,302,307-316_
- 🔐 Can the API be hit directly to bypass UI gating?: YES — /api/iscilier/kpi-export has no auth() and no permission check (full salary CSV to any authenticated tenant user); /api/iscilier/davamiyyet/export checks only auth, not davamiyyet.view (full attendance to any logged-in user). — _app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts:16; app/api/iscilier/davamiyyet/export/route.ts:6-9_
- 🔐 Are mutation server actions guarded server-side (not just UI)?: No bypass found — all HR write actions call requireHrActionPerm before DB writes; tenant scope enforced via prisma extension on findUnique/update. This area is solid. — _features/iscilier/actions.ts:34,113; maas-actions.ts:109,368; davamiyyet-actions.ts:43,137_
- 🔐 Can a user self-mark attendance to inflate own payroll/bonus?: Possible — quickMarkAttendance self-path skips davamiyyet.idare, and attendance feeds prorata maaş and davamiyyet bonus. Audited but not blocked. Confirm whether self punch-in is intended. — _features/iscilier/davamiyyet-actions.ts:129-139_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** features/iscilier/queries.ts (redact PII in getEmployees based on canViewSalary), app/(dashboard)/iscilier/page.tsx (pass canSeeSalary and redact, or gate columns), features/iscilier/components/employees-table.tsx (hide maas/fin/bank columns when not permitted), app/(dashboard)/iscilier/maas/page.tsx (add requireHrPagePerm('maas.view')), app/(dashboard)/iscilier/kpi/page.tsx (add maas.view gate), app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts (add auth + maas.view check), app/api/iscilier/davamiyyet/export/route.ts (add davamiyyet.view check), features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts (status-conditional update inside tx for payBordro idempotency; clamp negative net; add reverse/unpay action), features/iscilier/bonus-calc.ts (align attribution field with payroll commission — satis_meneceri_id vs yaradan_id), features/iscilier/access-guard.ts (add hr: tag to bustHrCache, or change headcount cache tag)

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** Consider a partial unique index / idempotency guard for payroll payments (e.g. unique on finance_operations.sened_nomresi = MAAS-{bordroId}, or a guard column) to make payBordro double-submit safe at the DB layer.; No schema change strictly required for the permission fixes (handled in app code); maas_hesablamalar.son_meblegh DB-generated default omits vergi/sosial but app always supplies son_meblegh explicitly, so it's harmless as long as code keeps doing so.

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** Payroll payment writes into finance_operations (cash/bank mexaric) and triggers recalculateAccountBalance from lib/balance/account-balance — so any payBordro double-submit or missing reversal directly corrupts the shared cash/bank balance and the maliyye module's figures (revalidatePath('/maliyye') is called). The bonus/commission attribution split (satis_meneceri_id vs yaradan_id) couples Əməkdaşlar to the Ticarət/POS sale-creation logic; changing one side affects payroll commission, KPI dashboard, and the employee sales panel. Salary/PII exposure also surfaces in /iscilier/kpi and the analitika page which both pull aylik_maas. Headcount cache tag mismatch affects the dashboard/nezaret strips that read the same hr: cache.

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) PERMISSION (highest priority): (a) In app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts add auth() + getRequestPermissions() and require isci.view AND maas.view (or privileged). (b) In davamiyyet/export route require davamiyyet.view/isci.view. (c) Gate /iscilier/maas and /iscilier/kpi pages with requireHrPagePerm('maas.view') (already defined in access-guard). (d) In getEmployees/list page, apply redactPii (already exists in access-guard) when canViewSalary is false, and have EmployeesTable suppress maas/fin_kod/bank columns when a canSeeSalary flag is false. 2) PAYROLL ATOMICITY: make payBordro update conditional — inside the tx use updateMany({where:{id, status:{not:'odenilib'}}}) and abort if affected===0; clamp son_meblegh negative to 0 (or block payment of negative net); add a reverseBordroPayment action that, in one $transaction, deletes/voids the isci_odenisleri row, posts a reversing finance_operations (or soft-cancels it), recalculates the account balance, and flips status back to cernovik. 3) ATTRIBUTION: pick one source-of-truth (satis_meneceri_id recommended, since payroll commission and getEmployeeSales already use it) and make bonus-calc.ts use the same field, or document the intended distinction. 4) CACHE: add revalidateTag(`hr:${sahibkarId}`) to bustHrCache (or move headcount cache to the iscilier: tag). 5) Optionally add restoreEmployee action + restore button for the silinmis filter.

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- As a user with ONLY isci.view (no maas.view): open /iscilier, enable the 'Maaş' / 'FİN' / 'Bank' columns — confirm whether salaries are visible (expected after fix: redacted/hidden).
- As that same low-permission user: open /iscilier/maas and /iscilier/kpi directly — confirm whether full payroll is shown.
- As that user: GET /api/iscilier/kpi-export?month=2026-06 and /api/iscilier/davamiyyet/export?month=2026-06 directly in the browser — confirm whether a CSV/XLSX with salaries/attendance downloads.
- Pay one bordro, then rapidly double-click 'Ödə və qeyd et' (or open two tabs on /iscilier/maas and pay the same row) — check isci_odenisleri and finance_operations for duplicate rows and whether the account balance was deducted twice.
- Create a bordro where cerime+avans exceed prorata (negative net), then pay it — verify isci_odenisleri shows a negative meblegh and the account balance is untouched while status flips to odenilib.
- Create a sale for salesperson B while logged in as user A (set satis_meneceri_id=B), then check B's payroll commission (maas) vs B's and A's bonus pool on the detail bonus panel to expose the attribution mismatch.
- Soft-delete (deactivate) an employee, then open /iscilier?recordStatus=silinmis — confirm there is no way to restore them.
- Create then immediately deactivate an employee and watch the headcount strip — confirm it stays stale (~up to 120s) due to the cache tag mismatch.

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- payBordro concurrency: confirm via a live double-click / two-tab test that two isci_odenisleri rows + two finance_operations can be created for one bordro (static read shows the TOCTOU window but Postgres transaction isolation behavior should be observed).
- Confirm at runtime that a low-permission user holding only isci.view (not maas.view) actually reaches /iscilier and sees the salary column, and can download /api/iscilier/kpi-export (the withTenant inside getKpiDashboard requires a session but not a specific permission).
- Verify Prisma runtime accepts the extension injecting sahibkar_id into a findUnique where clause for these models (it relies on the extension; if Prisma rejects non-unique fields in findUnique where at runtime, payBordro/calculateMonthlyBonus findUnique calls would throw — needs a live query to confirm it filters rather than errors).
