- Confirm whether self punch-in via quickMarkAttendance is the intended product behavior (business decision).

---

## 10. KPI / Bonus / Cərimə

**Xülasə:** The KPI/Bonus/Cərimə area has TWO disconnected 'KPI bonus' systems that do not reconcile. (1) features/iscilier/bonus-calc.calculateMonthlyBonus computes a rich, configurable bonus (qazanilan_cemi from attendance/tasks/errors/collections/sales) and is displayed on the employee profile and the KPI dashboard, but it is NEVER written to maas_hesablamalar.kpi_bonus — it is purely cosmetic. (2) features/ticaret/commission-actions.calculateCommission writes a completely different tiered sales commission into kpi_bonus, and when it updates an existing draft row it fails to recompute son_meblegh, leaving stale net pay. On top of that, calculateBordro already injects a flat 3% commission, so running both yields double-paid commission. The manual bonus/cerime path (saveBonusOrPenalty) and the disciplinary cerime path (addDisciplinaryAction) are correctly guarded, transactional-enough, audited, and do recompute son_meblegh; payBordro/bulkPayBordro are properly atomic with finance legs. The most serious issues are PERMISSION leaks: the KPI CSV export API route (app/api/iscilier/kpi-export) has no role/permission check and returns RAW, unmasked payroll for the whole tenant to any logged-in user; and both /iscilier/kpi and /iscilier/maas pages display full salaries without the defined maas.view guard, relying only on the broad isci.view route gate. The ayarlar/satis-hedef saveTarget action is unguarded (ayar.view can rewrite the company target, no audit). Secondary correctness issues: BonusOverview edit dialog silently wipes musteri_filter/excluded blacklist; attendance % uses two different bases (fixed 22 vs weekday count) across bonus-calc and dashboard; sales attribution is split between satis_meneceri_id (payroll/commission) and yaradan_id (bonus/KPI dashboard) so different people are rewarded for the same sale. Soft-deleted sales are excluded today only because cancelSale couples status='legv' with deleted_at; the bonus/commission queries filter status only, which is fragile. All findings are static-code-grounded; live verification is still needed to confirm runtime payout amounts and the export route's reachability under each role.

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** features/iscilier/kpi-queries.ts; features/iscilier/kpi-dashboard-queries.ts; features/iscilier/bonus-calc.ts; features/iscilier/bonus-profile.ts; features/iscilier/bonus-profile-actions.ts; features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts; features/iscilier/maas-queries.ts; features/iscilier/maas-band-actions.ts; features/iscilier/maas-band-queries.ts; features/iscilier/hr-actions.ts (addDisciplinaryAction); features/iscilier/access-guard.ts; features/iscilier/analitika-queries.ts; features/iscilier/components/bonus-overview.tsx; features/iscilier/components/bonus-form.tsx; features/iscilier/components/disciplinary-form.tsx; features/iscilier/components/maas-table.tsx; features/ticaret/commission-actions.ts; features/ticaret/commission-queries.ts; features/ticaret/satis-hedef.ts; features/crm/kpi-queries.ts; app/(dashboard)/crm/kpi/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/iscilier/kpi/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/iscilier/maas/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/satis-hedef/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/maas-band/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/layout.tsx; app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts; app/(dashboard)/iscilier/[id]/page.tsx; lib/auth/route-gate.ts; lib/db/prisma.ts (tenant extension); lib/db/tenant-context.ts; lib/soft-delete/actions.ts; features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts (cancelSale lines 280-305); prisma/schema.prisma (model maas_hesablamalar, satis_sifarisleri)

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🔴 **[critical · permission] KPI CSV export API route has no permission/role check — raw payroll leak**
  - Detal: app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts GET runs getKpiDashboard({ rawData: true }) and streams ad_soyad, aylik_maas, bonus_qazanilan, bonus_pool, cerime_toplam, net (full salary/bonus/cerime per employee) with stealth masking explicitly disabled. The route performs NO permission check; proxy.ts auth() only ensures a logged-in session of the tenant, not maas.view/isci HR privilege. Any authenticated low-privilege user of the tenant (e.g. cashier) can GET this URL and exfiltrate the entire payroll, bypassing the stealth/scale masking that the on-screen page applies.
  - Sübut: app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts:16-27 (rawData:true, no auth/perm guard) ; kpi-dashboard-queries.ts:419 (stealth skipped when rawData) ; proxy.ts (auth only, no perm)
  - Fayllar: app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts, features/iscilier/kpi-dashboard-queries.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] KPI-profile bonus (calculateMonthlyBonus) is display-only and never reaches payroll net**
  - Detal: calculateMonthlyBonus computes qazanilan_cemi from davamiyyet/tapsiriq/sehv/borc_yigim/satis_hedef and is shown on the employee detail (bonus-overview) and KPI dashboard (bonus_qazanilan, net_maas = maas + bonusQaz - cerime). But this result is NEVER written to maas_hesablamalar.kpi_bonus. The bordro/payroll kpi_bonus is populated only by features/ticaret/commission-actions.calculateCommission (tiered sales commission), a completely different formula. So the 'Bonus' big number an employee sees on their profile and on the KPI dashboard net column does not match what payBordro actually pays. Two divergent 'KPI bonus' definitions coexist.
  - Sübut: features/iscilier/bonus-calc.ts:168-180 (returns qazanilan_cemi, no DB write) ; kpi-dashboard-queries.ts:237-242,314,344 ; vs features/ticaret/commission-actions.ts:120-137 (only writer of kpi_bonus)
  - Fayllar: features/iscilier/bonus-calc.ts, features/iscilier/kpi-dashboard-queries.ts, features/ticaret/commission-actions.ts, app/(dashboard)/iscilier/[id]/page.tsx

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] calculateCommission overwrites kpi_bonus on existing draft rows without recomputing son_meblegh — stale net pay**
  - Detal: For an existing cernovik bordro row, calculateCommission does update({ data: { kpi_bonus: totalBonus } }) and does NOT set son_meblegh. son_meblegh is only a Postgres DEFAULT expression (not GENERATED ALWAYS) and there is no trigger maintaining it (verified: no trigger in scripts/). getMaasTable reads the stored son_meblegh for existing rows and payBordro pays the stored son_meblegh. Result: the bonus column in the maas table updates (it sums kpi_bonus+manual_bonus+komisyon live) but the NET column and the actual paid amount stay stale and inconsistent with the bonus shown in the same row.
  - Sübut: features/ticaret/commission-actions.ts:122-125 (update sets kpi_bonus only) ; features/iscilier/maas-queries.ts:96-98 (net=stored son_meblegh) ; features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts:385 (pays stored son_meblegh) ; maas-table.tsx:213-215 vs 230+
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/commission-actions.ts, features/iscilier/maas-queries.ts, features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Double-counted sales commission: flat 3% in calculateBordro AND tiered commission in calculateCommission for the same month**
  - Detal: calculateBordro adds komisyon = sales * COMMISSION_RATE(0.03) into detal.satis_komisyon and gross. Separately, calculateCommission computes tiered commission on the same sales and writes it into kpi_bonus. getMaasTable's gross/bonus then includes BOTH satis_komisyon (3%) and kpi_bonus (tiered) for the same sales, so an employee can be paid commission twice. There is no guard preventing both from being applied to one bordro/month.
  - Sübut: features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts:191-192,215-220 (satis_komisyon) ; features/ticaret/commission-actions.ts:120-124 (kpi_bonus) ; features/iscilier/maas-queries.ts:86-89 (gross includes both)
  - Fayllar: features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts, features/ticaret/commission-actions.ts, features/iscilier/maas-queries.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] BonusOverview EditDialog silently resets musteri_filter and excluded_kontragent_ids to defaults on every save**
  - Detal: saveBonusProfil's zod schema defaults musteri_filter='hamisi' and excluded_kontragent_ids=[]. The EditDialog.onSave only passes istifadeciId, metod, fixed_mebleg, percent, paylanma — it omits musteri_filter and excluded_kontragent_ids. So whenever a manager edits bonus rules from this dialog, any previously configured customer filter / blacklist is wiped back to 'all customers / no exclusions', changing the bonus pool computation (bonus-calc buildSalesContactFilter) without the user intending it.
  - Sübut: features/iscilier/components/bonus-overview.tsx:271-277 (onSave omits filter+excluded) ; features/iscilier/bonus-profile-actions.ts:94-95,120-127 (defaults applied) ; bonus-calc.ts:79-83 uses these fields
  - Fayllar: features/iscilier/components/bonus-overview.tsx, features/iscilier/bonus-profile-actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · permission] KPI dashboard and Maaş bordro pages lack maas.view page guard — visible to any isci.view user**
  - Detal: app/(dashboard)/iscilier/kpi/page.tsx and .../iscilier/maas/page.tsx render full salary, bonus, cerime and net for all employees but call no requireHrPagePerm('maas.view') and no canViewSalary; they rely solely on route-gate which only requires isci.view/isci.idare/hr.view for the whole /iscilier prefix. The codebase defines a dedicated maas.view permission (access-guard.ts) but it is never enforced at these salary-display pages, so a user with only the broad employee-list permission sees all salaries/bonuses. No per-viewer redactPii is applied either.
  - Sübut: app/(dashboard)/iscilier/kpi/page.tsx (no requireHrPagePerm/auth) ; app/(dashboard)/iscilier/maas/page.tsx:11-13 (no guard) ; lib/auth/route-gate.ts:33 (/iscilier anyOf isci.view/isci.idare/hr.view) ; access-guard.ts:101-109 (maas.view exists but unused here)
  - Fayllar: app/(dashboard)/iscilier/kpi/page.tsx, app/(dashboard)/iscilier/maas/page.tsx, lib/auth/route-gate.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] satis-hedef saveTarget server action has no permission check — ayar.view user can rewrite company sales target**
  - Detal: The inline 'use server' saveTarget in ayarlar/satis-hedef/page.tsx calls setMonthlyTarget with no permission guard. setMonthlyTarget (features/ticaret/satis-hedef.ts:39-55) only does withTenant + upsert, no perm check, no audit. The /ayarlar route-gate allows entry with read-only ayar.view, so a user with only ayar.view can POST this action and change the tenant-wide monthly sales target, which directly feeds commission bonus_on_target (hedefe_catdi) and dashboard progress. Mutation has no audit log either.
  - Sübut: app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/satis-hedef/page.tsx:15-25 (saveTarget, no perm) ; features/ticaret/satis-hedef.ts:39-55 (setMonthlyTarget, no guard/audit) ; lib/auth/route-gate.ts:56 (/ayarlar anyOf ayar.view/ayar.idare)
  - Fayllar: app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/satis-hedef/page.tsx, features/ticaret/satis-hedef.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · functional] calculateMonthlyBonus 'davamiyyet' achievement formula double-divides — silently caps/skews bonus**
  - Detal: For davamiyyet, faktikiFaiz is already min(100,(days/22)*100), then nailiyyet_faiz = (faktikiFaiz / max(1,hedef)) * 100. With the UI default hedef=90 (%), a perfect-attendance employee (faktikiFaiz=100) gets nailiyyet=100/90*100=111% (capped to pay), but partial attendance is divided by 90 again, so the 'percent of target' is non-linear and inconsistent with how the same metric is computed in kpi-dashboard (davFaiz = dav/expectedWorkDays). Two different attendance bases (fixed 22 in bonus-calc vs countWeekdays excluding Sundays in dashboard) produce different attendance % for the same employee/month.
  - Sübut: features/iscilier/bonus-calc.ts:206-209 (expectedDays=22, double divide by hedef) ; kpi-dashboard-queries.ts:95,303,442-451 (countWeekdays, no /hedef)
  - Fayllar: features/iscilier/bonus-calc.ts, features/iscilier/kpi-dashboard-queries.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Sales attribution field is inconsistent across KPI/bonus/payroll (satis_meneceri_id vs yaradan_id)**
  - Detal: getEmployeeKpiTable, calculateBordro (3% komisyon), getMaasTable and getSalespersonCommissions attribute sales by satis_meneceri_id. But calculateMonthlyBonus and the KPI dashboard satis columns / satis_hedef category attribute by yaradan_id. The same sale can have different menecer vs creator, so the bonus pool (yaradan_id) and the payroll commission (satis_meneceri_id) reward different people for the same sale, and the satis figures on the KPI dashboard (yaradan_id) won't match the maas commission (satis_meneceri_id).
  - Sübut: features/iscilier/kpi-queries.ts:44-49 + maas-actions.ts:133 + commission-queries.ts:106 (satis_meneceri_id) vs bonus-calc.ts:93,290-296 + kpi-dashboard-queries.ts:189 (yaradan_id)
  - Fayllar: features/iscilier/bonus-calc.ts, features/iscilier/kpi-queries.ts, features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts, features/ticaret/commission-queries.ts, features/iscilier/kpi-dashboard-queries.ts

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] Bonus/KPI sales aggregations filter only on status, not deleted_at — fragile against any non-cancel soft-delete**
  - Detal: The tenant Prisma extension injects only sahibkar_id, not a soft-delete filter (verified lib/db/prisma.ts). bonus-calc and kpi-dashboard exclude sales only by status notIn [legv,...]; getSalespersonCommissions uses status not 'legv' with no deleted_at filter. Today this is safe because cancelSale sets status='legv' alongside deleted_at, but every other sales query in the codebase (e.g. ticaret/dashboard-queries) defensively also filters deleted_at IS NULL. If a sale is ever soft-deleted without status=legv (e.g. via the generic softDeleteRecord helper), these bonus/commission figures would silently include a deleted sale.
  - Sübut: lib/db/prisma.ts:42-72 (no deleted_at injection) ; bonus-calc.ts:95,296 / kpi-dashboard-queries.ts:194 / commission-queries.ts:104-106 (no deleted_at) vs features/ticaret/dashboard-queries.ts:73,187 (deleted_at IS NULL)
  - Fayllar: features/iscilier/bonus-calc.ts, features/iscilier/kpi-dashboard-queries.ts, features/ticaret/commission-queries.ts

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] adjustBordro recomputes gross EXCLUDING the just-set value when editing cerime/avans, but that's correct; however son_meblegh formula diverges from DB-default formula (excludes komisyon & taxes)**
  - Detal: App code computes son = prorata + kpi + manual + komisyon - cerime - avans - vergi - sosial (subtracts taxes, adds komisyon). The DB DEFAULT for son_meblegh is prorata + kpi_bonus + manual_bonus - cerime - avans (no komisyon, no taxes). So rows created with explicit son_meblegh (calculateBordro/saveBonusOrPenalty/adjustBordro) use the tax-net formula, while rows created by calculateCommission (omitting son_meblegh) use the DB formula. Mixed-origin rows are computed by two different net formulas, so totals.cemi can be internally inconsistent.
  - Sübut: features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts:314-317,540-543 (app formula) vs prisma/schema.prisma maas_hesablamalar son_meblegh dbgenerated (no komisyon/no taxes) ; commission-actions.ts:127-136 omits son_meblegh
  - Fayllar: features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts, features/ticaret/commission-actions.ts

- ⚪ **[low · functional] adjustBordro is missing operation-date / audit-of-old-value robustness and has no explicit tenant ownership recheck before update**
  - Detal: adjustBordro fetches the row with findUnique({ where: { id } }) — the tenant extension injects sahibkar_id into the where for findUnique so cross-tenant read is blocked, which is fine. It does block paid rows. But unlike saveBonusOrPenalty it does not validate the operation date and does not pass through a per-tenant ownership assertion beyond the extension; acceptable but inconsistent with sibling actions. Low risk.
  - Sübut: features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts:513-555 (no date guard; relies on extension where-injection at line 525)
  - Fayllar: features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts

- ⚪ **[low · functional] Dead/duplicate KPI query: getEmployeeKpiTable is never imported or rendered**
  - Detal: features/iscilier/kpi-queries.ts getEmployeeKpiTable (hedef = aylik_maas*10 hardcoded heuristic) is defined but has zero call sites in app/ or features/. The actual KPI dashboard uses getKpiDashboard. Dead code that implies an unused/abandoned KPI target heuristic (10x salary) which contradicts the configurable satis_hedef path.
  - Sübut: grep getEmployeeKpiTable shows only its definition at features/iscilier/kpi-queries.ts:17 with no importers
  - Fayllar: features/iscilier/kpi-queries.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts GET: No HR permission / role check. Returns raw (rawData:true) salaries, bonuses, cerime, net for all employees to ANY authenticated tenant user; stealth masking explicitly bypassed. — _app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts:16-27 ; kpi-dashboard-queries.ts:419_
- 🔐 app/(dashboard)/iscilier/kpi/page.tsx: No requireHrPagePerm('maas.view') / canViewSalary. Full salary+bonus+cerime KPI dashboard visible to any user with broad isci.view via route-gate. — _app/(dashboard)/iscilier/kpi/page.tsx (no auth/perm import) ; lib/auth/route-gate.ts:33_
- 🔐 app/(dashboard)/iscilier/maas/page.tsx: No maas.view page guard; renders full bordro (all salaries) for any isci.view user. — _app/(dashboard)/iscilier/maas/page.tsx:11-13_
- 🔐 ayarlar/satis-hedef saveTarget + features/ticaret/satis-hedef.setMonthlyTarget: Mutation has no permission check and no audit; ayar.view (read-only) user can rewrite the tenant monthly sales target feeding commission bonus_on_target. — _app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/satis-hedef/page.tsx:15-25 ; features/ticaret/satis-hedef.ts:39-55_
- 🔐 app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/maas-band/page.tsx: Page renders salary-band current_avg per position with no page-level guard beyond ayar.view; salary statistics exposed to read-only settings viewers (save/delete actions themselves are correctly guarded by maas.skala). — _app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/maas-band/page.tsx (no guard) ; maas-band-queries.ts:38-49_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts (add auth + maas.view/isci HR privilege guard), app/(dashboard)/iscilier/kpi/page.tsx (add requireHrPagePerm('maas.view')), app/(dashboard)/iscilier/maas/page.tsx (add maas.view guard / redactPii), features/ticaret/commission-actions.ts (recompute son_meblegh+detal on update; reconcile with calculateBordro 3% to avoid double commission), features/iscilier/components/bonus-overview.tsx (pass musteri_filter + excluded_kontragent_ids in onSave), features/ticaret/satis-hedef.ts + app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/satis-hedef/page.tsx (add ayar.idare guard + audit to setMonthlyTarget), features/iscilier/bonus-calc.ts (decide whether KPI bonus should write to kpi_bonus; align attendance base + sales attribution field with payroll)

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** Consider converting maas_hesablamalar.son_meblegh to a single authoritative computation (either a GENERATED ALWAYS column including the agreed formula, or always recompute in app) so commission updates cannot leave it stale — currently it is only a DEFAULT expression with no trigger.

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** "High cross-module coupling and divergence. Bonus/KPI in features/iscilier reads sales (satis_sifarisleri by yaradan_id), tasks (tapshiriqlar), approvals (tesdiq_telep), attendance (davamiyyet), and finance (finance_operations for borc_yigim). Payroll commission lives in a DIFFERENT module (features/ticaret/commission-actions) and writes the SAME kpi_bonus field by a DIFFERENT formula and a DIFFERENT attribution field (satis_meneceri_id), so the two modules silently fight over maas_hesablamalar.kpi_bonus and over what counts as a 'sale' for a person. satis-hedef (features/ticaret) is the company target consumed by commission bonus_on_target and by the (dead) getEmployeeKpiTable 10x-salary heuristic. The bonus→payroll→finance chain only completes through payBordro (which is sound), but the KPI-profile bonus never enters that chain, so reports (analitika-queries yearly_costs.bonus sums kpi_bonus+manual_bonus from paid rows) reflect commission bonuses only, not KPI-profile bonuses. Soft-delete correctness depends on the ticaret cancelSale convention (status=legv) holding for every future delete path."

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** "1) Lock down salary exposure first (critical/high): add a server-side auth+permission guard (session present AND HR-privileged OR maas.view) to app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts before calling getKpiDashboard, and never serve rawData:true to non-privileged callers; add requireHrPagePerm('maas.view') (or canViewSalary + redactPii) to /iscilier/kpi and /iscilier/maas pages. 2) Reconcile the two KPI bonus systems: decide one source of truth for maas_hesablamalar.kpi_bonus. If commission is authoritative, make calculateMonthlyBonus clearly labelled as an advisory KPI score (not 'bonus payable') in the UI; if the profile bonus is authoritative, have it write kpi_bonus inside a guarded action and remove/limit calculateCommission. Either way, in calculateCommission's update branch recompute son_meblegh and detal (gross/vergi/sosial) exactly like adjustBordro, and remove the double-commission by not adding the flat 3% in calculateBordro when tiered commission is used (or vice versa). 3) Fix BonusOverview.onSave to include musteri_filter and excluded_kontragent_ids (and surface them in the dialog) so saving cannot silently reset them. 4) Add ayar.idare permission check + audit to setMonthlyTarget / the satis-hedef inline action. 5) Unify the sales attribution field (pick satis_meneceri_id or yaradan_id) and the attendance base (one helper) across bonus-calc, kpi-dashboard, maas-actions, commission-queries. 6) Add deleted_at: null to bonus/commission sales filters for defense-in-depth. 7) Remove dead getEmployeeKpiTable or wire it to the configurable target. After each change, run the user test scenarios above to confirm the profile bonus, KPI dashboard, bordro and actual payout all reconcile."

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- As owner: configure an employee bonus profile (e.g. percent_satis 5% with satis_hedef + davamiyyet categories), then open the employee profile and the KPI dashboard, then open the Maaş bordro for the same month — verify the 'Bonus' number is the SAME in all three (expected: it is NOT, profile/KPI show calculateMonthlyBonus, bordro shows commission kpi_bonus).
- As owner: run 'Komissiya hesabla' (calculateCommission) for a month that already has a cernovik bordro, then look at the Maaş bordro — verify the NET column increased by the commission (expected: bonus column changes but NET/son_meblegh stays stale).
- Run payroll calculateBordro (adds 3% komisyon) AND calculateCommission (tiered) for the same month for a salesperson — verify the person is not paid commission twice.
- As a user holding ONLY isci.view (not maas.view): open /iscilier/maas and /iscilier/kpi and call /api/iscilier/kpi-export — verify whether full salaries are visible (expected: yes, which is the leak).
- As a user holding ONLY ayar.view: submit the satis-hedef form — verify whether the company target changes (expected: yes, no guard).
- Edit an employee bonus rule via the profile 'Qaydaları düzəlt' dialog after having set a customer blacklist — verify the blacklist survives the save (expected: it is wiped).

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Confirm that hitting /api/iscilier/kpi-export directly as a low-privilege (e.g. kassir) tenant user actually returns the raw payroll CSV (route reachability + whether withTenant gets a valid session-derived tenant on /api routes).
- Confirm a real payment: run calculateCommission on an existing cernovik bordro and verify the paid son_meblegh does NOT include the new kpi_bonus (stale-net bug), and verify whether running calculateBordro + calculateCommission for the same month double-counts commission.
- Confirm BonusOverview save actually clears a previously-set customer blacklist/filter in the persisted ayarlar JSON.
- Confirm the displayed bonus_qazanilan on the KPI dashboard / employee profile vs the kpi_bonus actually written to the bordro diverge for a configured employee.

---

## 11. Tapşırıqlar

**Xülasə:** The Tapşırıqlar module is functionally solid on the core mutation paths: createTask and setTaskReminder are atomic ($transaction), all write actions enforce owner/mesul/icrachi access via assertTaskAccess, audit logging is comprehensive, the overdue cron is CRON_SECRET-guarded and per-tenant with alert deduplication, and tenant scoping is correct via TENANT_MODELS + withTenant. The deadline->alert/escalation path works and a completion->payroll-KPI path exists in iscilier. However there are two real permission gaps: (1) the /tapshiriqlar/baglanti page has NO guard at all while route-gate bypasses /tapshiriqlar, so users lacking tapshiriq.oxu can read aggregate task data (high); (2) the detail page (getTaskDetail) lets any tapshiriq.oxu holder read ANY task including other employees' private tasks, contradicting the membership model enforced for writes (medium); and (3) cross-module createTaskFor creates and assigns tasks without the tapshiriq.yarat/atayir checks (permission gap). Data-flow issues: obyekt_nov strings are inconsistent across writers/readers (satis vs satis_sifaris vs satish; alis_sifarisi vs alis_sifaris; finance_operations unlisted), breaking the global Modul-bağlantı aggregation labels/counts; tapshiriq_status_tarixce and tapshiriq_performance_log are never written so there is no status/performance history trail; KPI bonus (calculateTaskBonus) is preview-only and never persisted, and the in-module KPI (mesul + icrachi) diverges from the iscilier payroll KPI (mesul-only); and the scope's 'overdue->cerime' is only a soft bonus-loss, no actual penalty record is created.

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** features/tapshiriqlar/actions.ts; features/tapshiriqlar/queries.ts; features/tapshiriqlar/detail-queries.ts; features/tapshiriqlar/cross-module.ts; features/tapshiriqlar/cross-module-constants.ts; features/tapshiriqlar/kpi-actions.ts; features/tapshiriqlar/stats-queries.ts; features/tapshiriqlar/reminder-ack.ts; features/tapshiriqlar/template-actions.ts; features/tapshiriqlar/ai-actions.ts; app/api/cron/check-overdue-tasks/route.ts; app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/[id]/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/statistika/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/ai-analiz/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/layihe/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/baglanti/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/sablonlar/page.tsx; features/tapshiriqlar/components/quick-status-buttons.tsx; features/tapshiriqlar/components/new-task-dialog.tsx; lib/auth/route-gate.ts; lib/db/tenant-models.ts; prisma/schema.prisma (tapshiriqlar, tapshiriq_obyektleri, tapshiriq_status_tarixce, tapshiriq_performance_log); features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts (createTaskForSale); features/ticaret/alis-actions.ts (createTaskForPurchase/FinanceOp); features/iscilier/bonus-calc.ts (tapsiriq KPI); vercel.json (cron schedule)

**Əhatə boşluqları:** features/tapshiriqlar/templates.ts content (template definitions) not fully read — only template-actions.ts and the sablonlar page were examined.; Kanban drag-and-drop status change wiring (kanban-board.tsx) not opened — could be another changeTaskStatus call site with its own idempotency behavior.; calendar-view.tsx, filters.tsx, pomodoro-timer.tsx, multi-user-select.tsx, color-picker.tsx, deadline-countdown.tsx, reminder-dialog.tsx internals not deeply reviewed (UI-only; server actions they call were reviewed).; tapshiriq_tekrar (recurring tasks) model exists but the recurring-task generation path (if any cron/action creates repeats) was not located/verified.; tapshiriq_fayllari (attachments) upload/delete server action and its permission guard not examined.; app/(dashboard)/sahibkar/tapshiriq/* (separate sahibkar-area task UI) and features/sahibkar/components/task-dialog.tsx were not audited (out of the core 11 scope but share the model).; Exact SQL emitted by bonus-calc.ts field-reference comparison and any NULL-deadline edge in kpi-actions gecikmis SUM not runtime-verified.

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ Task creation (createTask) is atomic: tapshiriqlar + tapshiriq_iscilier + tapshiriq_obyektleri + tapshiriq_xatirlatmalar + bildirisler all inside one $transaction; partial failure rolls back. — _features/tapshiriqlar/actions.ts:188-272 (prisma.$transaction)_
- ✅ createTask enforces permissions both at entry (tapshiriq.yarat) and for assignment (tapshiriq.atayir): without atayir, mesul_id is forced to self, icracilar filtered to self, broadcast_all rejected. — _features/tapshiriqlar/actions.ts:132-160_
- ✅ All mutating server actions (changeTaskStatus, addComment, toggleChecklist, setTaskColor, setTaskReminder) call assertTaskAccess() which restricts to owner/mesul/icrachi/privileged within the tenant. — _features/tapshiriqlar/actions.ts:26-57, 359, 442, 481, 516, 578_
- ✅ Task completion/cancel is blocked when open checklist items remain (unless force), via findTaskBlockers — prevents premature completion. — _features/tapshiriqlar/actions.ts:367-380; lib/blockers/find-task-blockers.ts:19-33_
- ✅ Audit log is written on every mutation: createTask(yarat), changeTaskStatus(status_deyisdi), addComment, toggleChecklist, setTaskColor, setTaskReminder(xatirlatma_qoy), dismiss/snooze reminder, runOverdueCheck(overdue_yoxla), bonus(kpi_hesab), AI(ai_analiz). — _features/tapshiriqlar/actions.ts:294,421,456,495,534,653,780; reminder-ack.ts:61,110,146; kpi-actions.ts:98; ai-actions.ts:70_
- ✅ Overdue cron is authenticated with Bearer CRON_SECRET and runs per-tenant via runWithTenant with a system user context; deduplicates alerts (skips if open task_overdue_auto alert exists). — _app/api/cron/check-overdue-tasks/route.ts:21-28,41-64; actions.ts:704-714_
- ✅ Deadline->alert/escalation works: _executeOverdueCheckForTenant finds deadline<now, status notIn [tamamlandi,legv], escalation_enabled, creates alerts row + notifies escalation_to. — _features/tapshiriqlar/actions.ts:692-752_
- ✅ Completion->KPI flow exists: iscilier bonus-calc reads on-time task completion (tamamlandi_de <= deadline) for mesul, feeding payroll KPI. — _features/iscilier/bonus-calc.ts:212-231_
- ✅ All tapshiriq tables (tapshiriqlar, tapshiriq_obyektleri, checklist, kommentleri, xatirlatmalar, performance_log) are in TENANT_MODELS so $extends auto-scopes by sahibkar_id under withTenant. — _lib/db/tenant-models.ts:196-204_
- ✅ Overdue filter applied server-side in getTasks (where.deadline<now + status notIn) so pagination/total are correct, not client-trimmed. — _features/tapshiriqlar/queries.ts:96-99; app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/page.tsx:95-96_
- ✅ QuickStatusButtons disables the button while pending and short-circuits when status already equals current — mitigates double-click duplicate status changes. — _features/tapshiriqlar/components/quick-status-buttons.tsx:35,52_
- ✅ List/detail/statistika/layihe/ai-analiz pages all enforce tapshiriq.* permission with owner/admin bypass and redirect otherwise. — _app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/page.tsx:54-62; [id]/page.tsx:27-33; statistika/page.tsx:28-35; layihe/page.tsx:46-53; ai-analiz/page.tsx:18-25_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🟠 **[high · permission] Modul-bağlantı page (/tapshiriqlar/baglanti) has NO permission guard — tenant task data leaks to users without tapshiriq.oxu**
  - Detal: /tapshiriqlar is a BYPASS prefix in route-gate (middleware lets everyone through), so each page must self-guard. Every other task page checks tapshiriq.oxu (or specific perm) and redirects, but ModulBaglantiPage is `export default async function ModulBaglantiPage()` with NO auth()/getRequestPermissions()/redirect call. It directly runs getModuleTaskCounts() and getRecentByModule() and renders per-module task counts + recent task titles. A user explicitly lacking tapshiriq.oxu (who is redirected away from the list AND detail pages) can still open /tapshiriqlar/baglanti and read aggregate task data and recent task titles for their tenant. Tenant isolation still holds (withTenant), but the intra-tenant permission gate is bypassable.
  - Sübut: app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/baglanti/page.tsx:68 (no guard, compare app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/page.tsx:58); lib/auth/route-gate.ts:74 (BYPASS_PREFIXES includes /tapshiriqlar)
  - Fayllar: app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/baglanti/page.tsx, lib/auth/route-gate.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] Task detail page (/tapshiriqlar/[id]) lets any user with tapshiriq.oxu read ANY task in the tenant — not gated to owner/mesul/icrachi**
  - Detal: Mutations are correctly gated by assertTaskAccess (owner/mesul/icrachi/privileged), but the READ detail page only checks tapshiriq.oxu then calls getTaskDetail(id), which is a plain findUnique with no membership filter (only tenant scope via withTenant). So a non-privileged employee holding tapshiriq.oxu can open the URL of a private task (gorunurluk='sexsi') belonging to a different employee and read its description, checklist, participants and all comments. The list query (getTasks scope='menim') restricts visibility, and gorunurluk exists on the model, but the detail page never enforces it. This contradicts the membership model the actions enforce for writes.
  - Sübut: app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/[id]/page.tsx:31-37 (only tapshiriq.oxu check); features/tapshiriqlar/detail-queries.ts:5-21 (findUnique, no mesul/icrachi/gorunurluk filter)
  - Fayllar: app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/[id]/page.tsx, features/tapshiriqlar/detail-queries.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] obyekt_nov values are inconsistent across writers/readers — cross-module 'Modul bağlantı' aggregation mislabels and miscounts linked tasks**
  - Detal: The canonical LINKABLE_MODULES keys are 'satis_sifaris' and 'alis_sifaris', and the baglanti page MODULE_META keys on those. But the actual writers store divergent strings: createTaskForSale writes obyekt_nov='satis'; createTaskForPurchase writes 'alis_sifarisi'; createTaskForFinanceOp writes 'finance_operations'; NewTaskDialog ERP_OBJEKTLER offers 'satish'/'alish'/'servis'. Intra-module linking still works (each module reads with the same string it wrote — e.g. linked-tasks-panel reads 'alis_sifarisi'). But the aggregate /tapshiriqlar/baglanti page groups by raw obyekt_nov, so 'satis','alis_sifarisi','satish','finance_operations' rows appear with no LINKABLE_MODULES label and no MODULE_META icon, and the 'satis_sifaris'/'alis_sifaris' tiles always show zero. Counts are fragmented across spelling variants.
  - Sübut: cross-module-constants.ts:6-7 (satis_sifaris/alis_sifaris); ticaret/satis-actions.ts:460 (obyekt_nov:'satis'); ticaret/alis-actions.ts:41 (obyekt_nov:'alis_sifarisi'); new-task-dialog.tsx:18-26 (satish/alish/servis); baglanti/page.tsx:19-35 (MODULE_META keys)
  - Fayllar: features/tapshiriqlar/cross-module-constants.ts, features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts, features/ticaret/alis-actions.ts, features/tapshiriqlar/components/new-task-dialog.tsx, app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/baglanti/page.tsx

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Status history (tapshiriq_status_tarixce) and performance log (tapshiriq_performance_log) are never written — no completion/overdue history trail**
  - Detal: Scope explicitly requires task 'history' and 'completion->KPI'/'overdue->cerime'. The schema has tapshiriq_status_tarixce (old_status/new_status/changed_by) and tapshiriq_performance_log (hadise, vaxtinda, gecikmə_dq). A grep across the whole repo (excluding .next) shows ZERO write sites for either table — changeTaskStatus updates only tapshiriqlar.status and writes an audit row + bildiris, but never inserts a status-history or performance-log record. Consequently there is no per-task status timeline and no structured on-time/late performance record; all KPI/bonus math is recomputed ad-hoc from tamamlandi_de/deadline at query time. No detail/statistics page surfaces a status timeline.
  - Sübut: grep 'tapshiriq_status_tarixce' and 'tapshiriq_performance_log' over repo => only schema + tenant-models, no writes; features/tapshiriqlar/actions.ts:386 (changeTaskStatus updates status only)
  - Fayllar: features/tapshiriqlar/actions.ts, prisma/schema.prisma

- ⚪ **[low · functional] No 'cerime' (penalty) is created or applied when a task is overdue — overdue only produces an alert/notification**
  - Detal: Scope lists 'overdue->cerime'. The only overdue side effects are: an alerts row (task_overdue_auto) and a bildiris to escalation_to (_executeOverdueCheckForTenant). There is no cerime/penalty/deduction record anywhere in the tapshiriqlar feature (grep for cerime/penalty/cərimə in features/tapshiriqlar returns nothing). Lateness influences pay only indirectly and softly: kpi-actions.calculateTaskBonus applies a 1.2 coefficient only when gecikmis==0 (a lost bonus, not a penalty), and iscilier/bonus-calc lowers the KPI %. No actual cerime entity is generated.
  - Sübut: features/tapshiriqlar/actions.ts:716-747 (only alerts+bildiris); grep cerime/penalty in features/tapshiriqlar => no hits; kpi-actions.ts:82 (koef 1.2 if gecikmis===0)
  - Fayllar: features/tapshiriqlar/actions.ts, features/tapshiriqlar/kpi-actions.ts

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] calculateTaskBonus 'gecikmis' (and divergent KPI definitions) — KPI bonus is preview-only and never persists; module-vs-iscilier KPI use different population**
  - Detal: calculateTaskBonus produces a preview table only — it never writes to payroll/maas/bordro or any bonus table; nothing consumes its result besides the on-screen BonusCalculator. Separately, the tapshiriqlar module KPI (kpi-actions + getTaskPerformanceAnalytics) counts BOTH mesul_id and tapshiriq_iscilier links, while the iscilier payroll KPI (bonus-calc.ts k==='tapsiriq') counts ONLY mesul_id. So an employee who is an additional icrachi (not the mesul) earns task credit in the Tapşırıqlar module analytics but zero in actual payroll KPI — two divergent completion populations for the same concept.
  - Sübut: features/tapshiriqlar/kpi-actions.ts:53-73 (mesul OR tapshiriq_iscilier); features/iscilier/bonus-calc.ts:213-228 (mesul_id only); no persistence of bonus_mebleg anywhere (grep shows only preview UI consumer)
  - Fayllar: features/tapshiriqlar/kpi-actions.ts, features/iscilier/bonus-calc.ts

- ⚪ **[low · permission] Sablonlar page (/tapshiriqlar/sablonlar) has no auth guard**
  - Detal: TaskTemplatesPage is a non-async `export default function` with no auth()/permission check. It only renders static TASK_TEMPLATES (no tenant DB data), so there is no data leak; risk is limited to an unauthenticated/under-permissioned user seeing the static template catalog and the create button. The actual create action (createTaskFromTemplate) is itself guarded by requireTapshiriqPerm('tapshiriq.yarat'), so the action cannot be abused. Cosmetic/consistency issue rather than a data breach.
  - Sübut: app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/sablonlar/page.tsx (export default function TaskTemplatesPage, no guard); template-actions.ts:16-17 (action is guarded)
  - Fayllar: app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/sablonlar/page.tsx

- ⚪ **[low · functional] createTask not fully idempotent against rapid double-submit (no dedupe key); NewTaskDialog relies only on useTransition pending**
  - Detal: NewTaskDialog uses startTransition/pending and closes on success, which mitigates accidental double-submit in normal UI flow. However createTask has no server-side idempotency key or dedupe — two concurrent submissions (e.g. fast double Enter before the transition flips, or a retried request) would create two distinct tasks. Unlike sale/payment flows, duplicate tasks are low-impact, but the guarantee is purely client-side.
  - Sübut: features/tapshiriqlar/actions.ts:130-310 (no idempotency key); new-task-dialog.tsx:64-79 (client pending only)
  - Fayllar: features/tapshiriqlar/actions.ts, features/tapshiriqlar/components/new-task-dialog.tsx

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 /tapshiriqlar/baglanti page-level tapshiriq.oxu enforcement: No guard at all on ModulBaglantiPage; route-gate bypasses /tapshiriqlar so middleware won't stop it either. User without tapshiriq.oxu can read tenant task counts + recent titles. — _app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/baglanti/page.tsx:68; lib/auth/route-gate.ts:74_
- 🔐 Detail page membership/visibility enforcement: getTaskDetail has no mesul/icrachi/gorunurluk filter; any tapshiriq.oxu holder can read any task in tenant including private (sexsi) tasks of other employees. — _features/tapshiriqlar/detail-queries.ts:7-19; app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/[id]/page.tsx:31-37_
- 🔐 Server action direct-call guards: OK — createTask/changeTaskStatus/addComment/toggleChecklist/setTaskColor/setTaskReminder/createTaskFromTemplate all guard via requireTapshiriqPerm and/or assertTaskAccess; cron route guarded by CRON_SECRET; runOverdueCheck restricted to admin/sahibkar. No unguarded mutation found. — _features/tapshiriqlar/actions.ts:132,359,442,481,516,578,771-775; template-actions.ts:16; cross-module.ts:42 (createTaskFor relies on withTenant/requireTenant but has NO tapshiriq.yarat perm check)_
- 🔐 cross-module createTaskFor permission: createTaskFor (used by quick-add-bar and create-task-for-button) does NOT call requireTapshiriqPerm('tapshiriq.yarat') — it only requires a valid tenant context. Any authenticated tenant user can create a task (and assign it to another user via mesul_id) by invoking this server action directly, bypassing the tapshiriq.yarat/atayir gate that the main createTask enforces. — _features/tapshiriqlar/cross-module.ts:37-67 (no permission check; mesul_id = d.mesul_id ?? istifadeciId, assignable to others)_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/baglanti/page.tsx, app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/[id]/page.tsx, features/tapshiriqlar/detail-queries.ts, features/tapshiriqlar/cross-module.ts, features/tapshiriqlar/cross-module-constants.ts, features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts, features/ticaret/alis-actions.ts, features/tapshiriqlar/components/new-task-dialog.tsx, app/(dashboard)/tapshiriqlar/sablonlar/page.tsx

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** No schema change strictly required for the permission/visibility fixes. Optional: if status timeline + performance trail are desired, begin writing tapshiriq_status_tarixce (on changeTaskStatus) and tapshiriq_performance_log (on completion/overdue) — both tables already exist. Note tapshiriq_status_tarixce is NOT in TENANT_MODELS (lib/db/tenant-models.ts), so any code writing/reading it must pass sahibkar_id explicitly or it won't be tenant-scoped.

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** Tasks link to ERP entities via tapshiriq_obyektleri, but the obyekt_nov vocabulary is fragmented (satis vs satis_sifaris vs satish; alis_sifarisi vs alis_sifaris; finance_operations not in LINKABLE_MODULES). Intra-module 'linked tasks' panels still work because writer and reader use the same string, but the global /tapshiriqlar/baglanti aggregation and the LINKABLE label set are out of sync — counts/labels are unreliable there. Completion feeds two DIFFERENT KPI consumers with DIFFERENT populations: features/iscilier/bonus-calc.ts (mesul_id only) drives real payroll KPI, while features/tapshiriqlar/kpi-actions.ts + getTaskPerformanceAnalytics (mesul + icrachi) drive the in-module bonus preview/AI analytics — same metric, divergent math. createTaskFor (called from product/customer/sale/purchase/finance pages) is a permission gap: it spawns tasks and assigns to other users without the tapshiriq.yarat/atayir checks the canonical createTask enforces, so other modules can create/assign tasks bypassing the task module's own gate.

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) baglanti page: add the same guard block used by the list page (auth(), getRequestPermissions(), owner/admin bypass, redirect if !tapshiriq.oxu). 2) Detail page/getTaskDetail: enforce membership — for non-privileged users require mesul_id/yaradan_id/tapshiriq_iscilier membership (or honor gorunurluk umumi/secilmish), else notFound/redirect. 3) cross-module createTaskFor: add requireTapshiriqPerm('tapshiriq.yarat') and, when mesul_id != self, require 'tapshiriq.atayir' (mirror createTask). 4) obyekt_nov normalization: pick the canonical LINKABLE_MODULES keys (satis_sifaris/alis_sifaris) and make createTaskForSale, NewTaskDialog ERP_OBJEKTLER, and any readers use them consistently; add 'finance_operations' to LINKABLE_MODULES/MODULE_META or remap; consider a data migration for already-stored variants. 5) (optional/feature) Write tapshiriq_status_tarixce on changeTaskStatus and tapshiriq_performance_log on completion to provide history + an authoritative on-time/late trail; decide a single KPI population (mesul + icrachi vs mesul-only) shared between kpi-actions and iscilier/bonus-calc. 6) sablonlar page: add a lightweight auth/tapshiriq.oxu guard for consistency.

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- As a user WITHOUT tapshiriq.oxu, navigate directly to /tapshiriqlar/baglanti — expect: should be redirected but currently will see module task counts + recent task titles (bug).
- As employee A with tapshiriq.oxu but not a member of task X (private/sexsi, owned by employee B), open /tapshiriqlar/[X] — expect not-found/redirect; currently full detail + comments are shown (bug).
- From a product/customer/sale page, use 'Bu obyektə tapşırıq' / quick-add to call createTaskFor and assign mesul to another user while lacking tapshiriq.atayir — expect rejection; currently succeeds (permission gap).
- Create a task from the New Task dialog with object-link 'Satış', then open /tapshiriqlar/baglanti — expect it to count under the Satış tile; currently it lands under an unlabeled 'satish'/'satis' group while the 'Satış sifarişi' tile shows zero.
- Set a task deadline in the past with escalation_enabled, run /api/cron/check-overdue-tasks (with CRON_SECRET) — expect exactly one alert + one escalation_to notification, and a second run creates no duplicate alert.
- Complete a task on time vs late, then check iscilier KPI/bonus for the mesul vs an icrachi-only participant — expect both credited consistently; currently icrachi-only gets payroll credit only in the in-module preview, not in bonus-calc.
- Try to complete a task that still has open checklist items via QuickStatusButtons — expect blocker message listing open punkt; verify force path.

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Whether any production role string fails the substring rol matching (requireTapshiriqPerm/route-gate treat rol_ad containing 'admin'/'owner'/'sahibkar' as superuser) — a custom role named e.g. 'admin-köməkçi' would silently gain full task superuser rights. Verify actual roles.ad values in DB.
- Confirm at runtime that a user with NO tapshiriq.oxu can actually load /tapshiriqlar/baglanti and see data (static analysis says yes since there is no guard and route-gate bypasses /tapshiriqlar).
- Confirm a non-privileged employee can open another employee's private (gorunurluk='sexsi') task at /tapshiriqlar/[id] given only tapshiriq.oxu (detail-queries has no membership filter).
- Verify the cron 'system user' selection (orderBy rol_id asc, first active user) is acceptable for attribution of auto-created alerts; with empty icazeler this user is non-privileged but _executeOverdueCheckForTenant does not check perms, which is intended.
- Behavior of bonus-calc.ts on-time filter using prisma.tapshiriqlar.fields.deadline (field reference comparison tamamlandi_de <= deadline) — confirm Prisma generates correct SQL and that tasks with NULL deadline are excluded as intended.

---

## 12. CRM / Mesaj mərkəzi / Cavab generatoru / Toplu mesaj

**Xülasə:** The CRM module is feature-rich and its WRITE security is solid: every mutation/AI/broadcast server action calls requireCrmActionPerm before touching data, multi-tenant isolation is enforced through the tenant-scoped Prisma client + withTenant on every query, and audit logging is consistently wired. However I found two critical/high permission problems and several data-integrity gaps. CRITICAL: lib/auth/route-gate.ts:43 gates /crm on permission codes crm.view/mesaj.gor that do not exist anywhere (the real codes are crm.oxu/mesaj.cevab/crm.idare per the seed migration), so every non-privileged role the migration deliberately grants CRM access to is redirected to /icaze-yox and cannot use the module at all. HIGH: read queries (getChats/getThread/getLeads*) ignore the module's own getCrmScopeFilter, so any CRM-read user sees the whole tenant's conversations and leads and can open any thread by URL (within-tenant IDOR on reads); soft-deleted leads (status='silinib') leak into the list view, dashboard KPIs, funnel, source analytics, recent activity and per-user KPI because almost no read path excludes that status (only the Kanban board does); and the broadcast 'send' flow creates a draft that no cron/worker ever processes while the UI toasts that N customers were messaged. MEDIUM: lead->customer/sale conversions do multi-row writes without a $transaction (orphan/duplicate risk on partial failure), and segment-targeted broadcasts silently drop the ?seq= selection so custom/AI segments cannot be targeted. LOW: a lost-lead status guard checks the wrong string ('itirildi' vs 'itirdi'), the segment CSV export button is permanently disabled, and AI quota is checkable-but-not-strictly-enforced under concurrency. The route-gate and soft-delete-leak fixes are the highest priority.

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** app/(dashboard)/crm/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/crm/inbox/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/crm/inbox/[id]/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/crm/leadler/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/crm/broadcast/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/crm/segment/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/crm/sablonlar/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/crm/funnel/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/crm/kpi/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/crm/ai-cavab/page.tsx; features/crm/access-guard.ts; features/crm/leadler-actions.ts; features/crm/inbox-actions.ts; features/crm/ai-cavab-action.ts; features/crm/broadcast-action.ts; features/crm/sablon-actions.ts; features/crm/segment-actions.ts; features/crm/leadler-queries.ts; features/crm/inbox-queries.ts; features/crm/dashboard-queries.ts; features/crm/funnel-queries.ts; features/crm/kpi-queries.ts; features/crm/segment-queries.ts; features/crm/queries.ts; features/crm/quota.ts; features/crm/components/thread-view.tsx; features/crm/components/lead-row-actions.tsx; features/crm/components/inbox-list.tsx; features/crm/components/broadcast-dialog.tsx; lib/auth/route-gate.ts; lib/auth/get-permissions.ts; app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx (gate section); lib/db/tenant-models.ts; lib/audit/log.ts; scripts/migrations/2026-06-02-crm-permissions.sql; prisma/schema.prisma (leads, inbox_sohbetler, inbox_mesajlari)

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ All CRM server actions enforce backend permission checks via requireCrmActionPerm before any mutation; no unguarded mutation found — _leadler-actions.ts saveLead:33, changeLeadStage:99, deleteLead:169; inbox-actions.ts sendReply:20; broadcast-action.ts saveBroadcast:24; sablon-actions.ts saveTemplate:30; ai-cavab-action.ts suggestAiReply:36_
- ✅ Multi-tenant isolation correct: all CRM models (leads, inbox_sohbetler, inbox_mesajlari, broadcast_kampaniyalari, mesaj_sablonlari, musteri_seqment, ai_sohbet_loq, kontragentler) are in TENANT_MODELS and accessed via tenant-scoped prisma + withTenant — _lib/db/tenant-models.ts:9,39,74,75,93,124,141,143; dashboard-queries.ts uses prismaUnscoped but manually adds sahibkar_id to every where (lines 27-41)_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🔴 **[critical · permission] Route-gate for /crm requires permission codes that do not exist (crm.view, mesaj.gor) — locks out all non-privileged CRM users**
  - Detal: lib/auth/route-gate.ts:43 gates the /crm prefix with anyOf: ['crm.view','crm.idare','mesaj.gor']. But the actual seeded CRM permission codes (scripts/migrations/2026-06-02-crm-permissions.sql) are crm.oxu, crm.idare, mesaj.cevab, mesaj.idare, lead.* etc. The codes 'crm.view' and 'mesaj.gor' appear ONLY in route-gate.ts and nowhere else in the codebase. getRequestPermissions returns the real codes (crm.oxu, mesaj.cevab, ...). Consequence: any non-privileged role that the migration grants CRM access to (satici, kassir, satis_meneceri, menecer, team_lead) does NOT receive crm.idare, so gateRoute returns allowed:false and layout.tsx:121 redirects them to /icaze-yox. Only sahibkar/admin/owner (role bypass) and roles holding crm.idare (marketing_menecer) can open the CRM module at all. A satici with crm.oxu+mesaj.cevab+lead.yarat is fully blocked from /crm despite being granted those permissions.
  - Sübut: lib/auth/route-gate.ts:43 vs scripts/migrations/2026-06-02-crm-permissions.sql (crm.oxu/mesaj.cevab); grep confirms crm.view/mesaj.gor exist only in route-gate.ts; app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx:120-121
  - Fayllar: lib/auth/route-gate.ts, scripts/migrations/2026-06-02-crm-permissions.sql, app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Soft-deleted leads (status='silinib') leak into list view, dashboard KPIs, funnel, source analytics, recent activity and CRM stats**
  - Detal: deleteLead sets status='silinib' (no dedicated soft-delete column on the leads model). Many read paths do not exclude this status: (1) getLeadsListRows (leadler-queries.ts:54-74) has no status<>silinib filter, so archived leads still appear in the table view. (2) getDashboardKpis active_leads counts status NOT IN ['qazandi','itirdi'] (dashboard-queries.ts:27) which INCLUDES 'silinib', inflating the active-leads KPI; this_month_leads (line 30) and conversion denominator also count silinib. (3) getCrmStats (queries.ts:36) same notIn pattern. (4) getLeadSourceBreakdown (71), getLeadSourceAnalytics (102), getDailyLeadsLast30 (170), getRecentActivity (201), getFunnelLeadsForStage (funnel-queries.ts:25), getFunnelFilters (61), getUserKpi (kpi-queries.ts:40) and getCrmKpiSummary (101) all aggregate leads without excluding silinib. Only the Kanban board (getLeadsByStage) excludes it incidentally because 'silinib' is not a stage key. Net: deleting a lead does not remove it from active lists/reports as required.
  - Sübut: leadler-actions.ts:179-182 (status='silinib'); leadler-queries.ts:54-74 (no filter); dashboard-queries.ts:27,30; queries.ts:36; funnel-queries.ts:25
  - Fayllar: features/crm/leadler-actions.ts, features/crm/leadler-queries.ts, features/crm/dashboard-queries.ts, features/crm/queries.ts, features/crm/funnel-queries.ts, features/crm/kpi-queries.ts

- 🟠 **[high · permission] Inbox conversations and leads are NOT scoped per-user on read paths — any CRM-read user sees the whole tenant's data**
  - Detal: access-guard.ts documents (lines 58-73) that an ordinary user should see only chats/leads assigned to them, and provides getCrmScopeFilter() for this. But the read queries never call it: getChats (inbox-queries.ts:27-58) and getThread/getChatById build their where with no cavabdeh_id filter; getLeadsByStage / getLeadsListRows (leadler-queries.ts) only filter menecer_id if the caller explicitly passes ?menecer=. The inbox page (app/(dashboard)/crm/inbox/page.tsx) and leads page pass no per-user scope. So a non-privileged satici/kassir (once they can reach /crm) can read every conversation and every lead of the tenant, including reading any thread by visiting /crm/inbox/<id> directly (IDOR within tenant) and marking any chat read via markChatRead (inbox-actions.ts:79, no scope check). Write paths (sendReply, saveLead edit) do enforce scope, but confidentiality of reads is broken vs the documented model.
  - Sübut: inbox-queries.ts getChats:27-58 (no cavabdeh_id); inbox-actions.ts markChatRead:79-96 (no scope); leadler-queries.ts:54-74; access-guard.ts:58-73 (intended model); contrast searchInboxMessages:227-233 which DOES apply scope
  - Fayllar: features/crm/inbox-queries.ts, features/crm/leadler-queries.ts, features/crm/inbox-actions.ts, app/(dashboard)/crm/inbox/page.tsx, app/(dashboard)/crm/leadler/page.tsx

- 🟠 **[high · functional] Broadcast 'Təsdiqlə və göndər' creates a draft that nothing ever sends; success toast claims N customers were messaged**
  - Detal: saveBroadcast with test_send=false and no zamanlama sets status='taslak' with the comment 'Cron işləyəcək (avtomatik göndərmə)' (broadcast-action.ts:46), and zamanlama -> 'gozlemede'. There is NO broadcast-sending worker/cron anywhere: vercel.json crons list only daily-email-summary, run-automation-rules, compute-reorder-recommendations, check-overdue-tasks; grep for broadcast_kampaniyalari finds no API route or worker that transitions taslak/gozlemede -> gonderilir -> tamamlandi or increments gonderilen_say/ugurlu_say. So real broadcasts (and all drip-campaign rows from startDripCampaign) sit forever as taslak/gozlemede. Meanwhile broadcast-dialog.tsx:77 shows toast.success(`${res.sayi} müştəriyə göndərildi`) where res.sayi is the estimated recipient count — implying delivery that never happens. A confirmation alert does warn 'Real göndəriş üçün kanal inteqrasiyası tələb olunur', so it is partly disclosed, but the success message and status model are misleading and no delivery pipeline exists.
  - Sübut: broadcast-action.ts:42-62 (status logic, gonderilen_say:0); vercel.json crons (no broadcast); grep broadcast_kampaniyalari has no worker; broadcast-dialog.tsx:77 toast; segment-actions.ts startDripCampaign:267 creates gozlemede rows
  - Fayllar: features/crm/broadcast-action.ts, features/crm/segment-actions.ts, features/crm/components/broadcast-dialog.tsx, vercel.json

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Multi-write lead conversions are not wrapped in a Prisma $transaction — partial failure leaves orphaned data**
  - Detal: convertLeadToSale (leadler-actions.ts:283-359) performs up to three separate writes: create kontragentler, create satis_sifarisleri, update leads (+ safeAuditLog). convertLeadToMusteri (232-275) does create kontragentler then update leads as two separate awaits. No $transaction is used anywhere in features/crm (grep confirmed). If the sale create succeeds but the lead update fails (or vice versa), you get an orphaned customer/sale that is not linked back to the lead, and a retry would create a second customer (the idempotency guard relies on lead.kontragent_id/satish_id being set, which is the very write that may have failed). Should be wrapped in prisma.$transaction for atomicity.
  - Sübut: leadler-actions.ts convertLeadToSale:294-341 (create kontragent, create sale, update lead — separate awaits); convertLeadToMusteri:244-260; grep '\$transaction' in features/crm returns nothing
  - Fayllar: features/crm/leadler-actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · functional] Segment-targeted broadcast loses the segment selection — broadcast page/dialog ignore ?seq= and only support a fixed 6-value hedef enum**
  - Detal: Segment page links 'Broadcast göndər' to /crm/broadcast?seq=<kod> (segment/page.tsx:74) including custom/AI segments (kod custom_<id> / ai_<...>). But app/(dashboard)/crm/broadcast/page.tsx never reads searchParams at all, and BroadcastDialog has no seq prop; its hedef select is hardcoded to all_musteri/topdan/vip/borclu/passiv_30d/son_alish_30d (broadcast-dialog.tsx:16-23). BroadcastSchema.hedef is a z.enum of exactly those 6 values (broadcast-action.ts:18). So a user who builds a custom AI segment and clicks 'Broadcast göndər' cannot actually target that segment — the selection is silently dropped and they must re-pick one of the 6 built-ins. estimateRecipientCount also has no branch for custom segments.
  - Sübut: app/(dashboard)/crm/segment/page.tsx:74 (?seq link); app/(dashboard)/crm/broadcast/page.tsx:47 (no searchParams); broadcast-dialog.tsx:16-23 fixed HEDEF_OPTIONS; broadcast-action.ts:18 hedef enum; estimateRecipientCount:92-114 default returns 0
  - Fayllar: app/(dashboard)/crm/segment/page.tsx, app/(dashboard)/crm/broadcast/page.tsx, features/crm/components/broadcast-dialog.tsx, features/crm/broadcast-action.ts

- ⚪ **[low · ux] Lead 'İtirildi' status badge/guard checks the wrong string ('itirildi' vs actual 'itirdi')**
  - Detal: lead-row-actions.tsx:55 computes isLost = lead.status === 'itirildi', but the real status enum value used everywhere (LeadSchema status enum, loseLead, changeLeadStage, getLeadFunnel stages) is 'itirdi' (no second 'l'). Consequently the 'İtirildi' badge never shows for lost leads and the menu never hides the 'İtirildi olaraq qeyd et' / convert items for an already-lost lead — a user can mark an already-lost lead as lost again. Purely cosmetic/UX, no data corruption (loseLead is idempotent enough), but the guard is dead code.
  - Sübut: lead-row-actions.tsx:55 (isLost === 'itirildi'); leadler-actions.ts:97,213 use 'itirdi'; dashboard-queries.ts:153 stage 'itirdi'
  - Fayllar: features/crm/components/lead-row-actions.tsx

- ⚪ **[low · ux] Segment page 'CSV ixrac' button is permanently disabled (dead control)**
  - Detal: The segment customer list shows a 'CSV ixrac' button hardcoded disabled with no onClick (segment/page.tsx:78-80), so the advertised CSV export of a segment's customers does nothing. (Leads do have a working bulkExportLeads action, but segment customer export is unimplemented.)
  - Sübut: app/(dashboard)/crm/segment/page.tsx:78-80 (<Button ... disabled> with no handler)
  - Fayllar: app/(dashboard)/crm/segment/page.tsx

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] AI quota is reserved-but-not-decremented and ai_sohbet_loq writes are best-effort, so per-user AI limits can be slightly exceeded under concurrency**
  - Detal: checkAiCavabQuota counts existing ai_sohbet_loq rows for the user in the window (quota.ts:108-115) and compares perHour+needed. suggestAiReplyVariants makes 3 sequential AI calls and logs each (ai-cavab-action.ts:121-149); the log writes are wrapped in try/catch that swallows errors (line 135-148), so a failed log write means the request still consumed an AI call but is not counted toward quota. Two concurrent requests can both pass the same pre-check before either logs. Result: the saatlik/gunluk AI cost ceiling can be exceeded by a few calls. Low severity (caps are 50/300) but the limit is not strictly enforced.
  - Sübut: quota.ts:108-126 (count-then-compare, no reservation row); ai-cavab-action.ts:135-148 (best-effort log inside try/catch)
  - Fayllar: features/crm/quota.ts, features/crm/ai-cavab-action.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 Route-gate /crm requires crm.view OR crm.idare OR mesaj.gor: crm.view and mesaj.gor are not real permission codes (never seeded, never referenced elsewhere). Non-privileged roles granted CRM access (satici/kassir/satis_meneceri/menecer) only have crm.oxu/mesaj.cevab/lead.yarat and are therefore redirected to /icaze-yox — the entire module is unreachable for them. — _lib/auth/route-gate.ts:43 vs scripts/migrations/2026-06-02-crm-permissions.sql; grep shows crm.view/mesaj.gor only in route-gate.ts; layout.tsx:120-121_
- 🔐 Inbox/lead read queries should restrict ordinary users to their own assigned records: getChats/getThread/getChatById/getLeadsByStage/getLeadsListRows ignore getCrmScopeFilter; any user reaching /crm with crm.oxu sees ALL tenant conversations and leads, and can open any thread by URL (within-tenant IDOR on reads). markChatRead also has no scope check. — _features/crm/inbox-queries.ts:27-58,95-99; features/crm/leadler-queries.ts:54-74; features/crm/inbox-actions.ts:79_
- 🔐 Server actions guard mutations directly (cannot bypass UI gating by calling the action): No bypass found — every mutation/AI/broadcast action calls requireCrmActionPerm first; this part is correct. — _leadler-actions.ts:33,99,169; inbox-actions.ts:20,173,269; broadcast-action.ts:24,127; ai-cavab-action.ts:36,107,168,216; sablon-actions.ts:30,80; segment-actions.ts:51,177,239_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** lib/auth/route-gate.ts, features/crm/leadler-queries.ts, features/crm/inbox-queries.ts, features/crm/inbox-actions.ts, features/crm/dashboard-queries.ts, features/crm/queries.ts, features/crm/funnel-queries.ts, features/crm/kpi-queries.ts, features/crm/leadler-actions.ts, features/crm/broadcast-action.ts, features/crm/components/broadcast-dialog.tsx, app/(dashboard)/crm/broadcast/page.tsx, features/crm/components/lead-row-actions.tsx, app/(dashboard)/crm/segment/page.tsx

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** No schema change strictly required for soft-delete (status='silinib' is the existing mechanism), but optionally add a dedicated leads.silindi boolean / silinme_tarixi column + index for clean exclusion; alternatively rely on excluding status='silinib' in all read queries.; Seed the missing permission codes crm.view / mesaj.gor (or, preferably, fix route-gate to use the existing crm.oxu / mesaj.cevab codes) so the route-gate and the migration agree.

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** Lead -> customer/sale conversion writes into kontragentler and satis_sifarisleri without a transaction; a partial failure can create an orphaned customer or draft sale that other modules (Ticarət, Elaqe/Müştərilər, customer balance) will pick up while the lead is not linked, and a retry can duplicate the customer. The created sale is qaralama=true with umumi_mebleg=budce and no lines/stock/finance impact, so it correctly does NOT touch stock or cash until finalized — good — but it does surface a draft order in the sales module derived from an estimate. Soft-deleted (silinib) leads inflating CRM dashboard/KPI numbers is contained within CRM reports (does not touch finance/stock). Broadcast recipient targeting correctly excludes qara_siyahi/inactive customers, but since no sender exists there is no real outbound channel risk yet.

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) route-gate.ts:43 — change anyOf for /crm to the codes that actually exist: ['crm.oxu','crm.idare','mesaj.cevab','mesaj.idare'] (and/or seed crm.view/mesaj.gor). This unblocks all granted roles. 2) Exclude soft-deleted leads everywhere: add status: { not: 'silinib' } (or notIn including 'silinib') to getLeadsListRows, getDashboardKpis (all lead counts), getCrmStats, getLeadSourceBreakdown, getLeadSourceAnalytics, getDailyLeadsLast30, getRecentActivity, getFunnelLeadsForStage, getFunnelFilters, getUserKpi, getCrmKpiSummary; centralize an activeLeadsWhere helper to avoid divergence. 3) Apply per-user scope on reads: in inbox-queries/leadler-queries call getCrmScopeFilter and add cavabdeh_id / menecer_id filter when !privileged; add scope check to markChatRead and to the inbox/[id] page (404 if chat not owned). 4) Either implement a broadcast-sending cron (transition taslak/gozlemede rows, increment counters, honor qara_siyahi) or relabel the dialog flow as 'queued/draft' and remove the 'göndərildi' success wording; fix the count semantics. 5) Wrap convertLeadToSale and convertLeadToMusteri write sequences in prisma.$transaction. 6) Make broadcast read ?seq= and support custom/AI segments (extend BroadcastSchema.hedef + estimateRecipientCount + dialog). 7) Fix lead-row-actions isLost to compare 'itirdi'. 8) Implement segment CSV export or remove the dead disabled button.

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- Log in as a satici/kassir role (granted crm.oxu, mesaj.cevab, lead.yarat) and open /crm — verify you are NOT redirected to /icaze-yox (currently expected to be blocked).
- As an ordinary salesperson, open /crm/leadler and /crm/inbox — verify you see ONLY leads/conversations assigned to you (currently you will see the whole tenant's).
- Create a lead, then Sil (soft-delete) it, then check /crm/leadler list, the CRM dashboard 'Aktiv leadlər' KPI, the funnel, and /crm/kpi — the deleted lead must disappear from all of them (currently it remains in list/KPIs/funnel/analytics).
- Create a non-test broadcast via 'Təsdiqlə və göndər' and watch the campaign status and gonderilen_say over time — confirm whether anything actually transitions it from taslak (currently nothing does).
- From /crm/segment build a custom AI segment, click 'Broadcast göndər', and confirm the broadcast actually targets that segment (currently the seq is dropped and only the 6 built-in targets are selectable).
- Double-click 'Satışa çevir' on a lead and verify only one customer + one draft sale are created and the lead links to them (test the missing-transaction retry path).
- Mark a lead as 'İtirildi', reopen its row menu — confirm the 'İtirildi' badge shows and the lose/convert items are hidden (currently broken due to 'itirildi' vs 'itirdi').

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Confirm at runtime that a satici/kassir role (crm.oxu+mesaj.cevab, no crm.idare) is actually redirected to /icaze-yox when opening /crm — this is the predicted effect of the route-gate code mismatch but depends on the live x-pathname header being set and the role's seeded permissions.
- Confirm no broadcast-sending mechanism exists outside the repo (e.g., an external worker, queue consumer, or Supabase function) that processes broadcast_kampaniyalari rows; the static audit found none in-repo.
- Verify whether any background job or external integration sets leads.status back from 'silinib' or whether the leads model relies solely on status (no DB trigger), confirming silinib is the only soft-delete path.
- Confirm getRequestPermissions/loadPermissionsForRole returns plain codes like 'crm.oxu' (not namespaced/prefixed) in production data, validating the route-gate mismatch.

---

## 13. Kampaniyalar

**Xülasə:** The Kampaniyalar module is well-built at the unit level — mutations are permission-guarded server-side with correct codes, audited, soft-deleted via status='archived', and use crypto-safe codes plus an atomic conditional decrement for bonus spend. However, the module's whole reason for existing — affecting pricing on real sales — is broken at the integration seam. THREE critical data-flow defects: (1) the matcher's auto-apply engine and usage-commit functions (findApplicableCampaigns, commitCampaignApplications) are dead code with zero callers, so campaign_usage is never written and campaigns/coupons current_uses never increment — making all usage limits unenforceable and all ROI/analytics permanently zero, and loyalty accrual never fires; (2) the POS coupon discount is shown to the cashier and reduces the displayed total but is NOT included in the createSale payload, so the recorded sale and collected cash are overstated; (3) loyalty bonus is decremented from the card after the sale but the same reduction is omitted from the recorded total, double-charging the bonus. Plus a high-severity permission misconfig: the /kampaniyalar route-gate requires kampaniya.view/marketing.view codes that are never seeded (real code is kampaniya.oxu), locking legitimate read users out. Additional issues: gift cards can be sold but never spent (no qaliq decrement path), the expire-campaigns cron is not registered in vercel.json, getCampaigns/stats include archived records by default, and the list page's status filter links are no-ops. The financial-integrity bugs (1-3) are blocking and corrupt cash, customer balance, loyalty balance, and reporting downstream."

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** features/kampaniyalar/types.ts; features/kampaniyalar/actions.ts; features/kampaniyalar/matcher.ts; features/kampaniyalar/queries.ts; features/kampaniyalar/access-guard.ts; app/api/cron/expire-campaigns/route.ts; app/(dashboard)/kampaniyalar/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/kampaniyalar/[id]/page.tsx; features/pos/components/pos-client.tsx (lines 318-447, 880-998, 1660-1760); features/pos/sale-action.ts (lines 1-199); lib/auth/route-gate.ts; app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx (gate enforcement); prisma/schema.prisma (campaigns, coupons, campaign_usage, loyalty_cards, loyalty_tx, gift_cards models); scripts/migrations/2026-06-01-kampaniyalar.sql; scripts/migrations/2026-06-02-kampaniya-permissions.sql; vercel.json (crons block); lib/navigation.ts (kampaniya gate)

**Əhatə boşluqları:** features/kampaniyalar/broadcast-limit.ts (rate-limit internals) — opened only by reference, not line-audited.; features/kampaniyalar/templates.ts — scanned for discount_type values only, not each template fully validated.; features/kampaniyalar/components/* (campaign-status-buttons, coupon-bulk-dialog, telegram-broadcast-button, campaign-discount-fix, pos-coupon-input) — only pos-coupon-input partially read; the dialog/button client components' loading/disabled/idempotency states not line-audited.; app/(dashboard)/kampaniyalar/{analitika,ayarlar,sablonlar,loyalty,giftcards,kuponlar,yeni}/page.tsx — confirmed which queries/guards they call but did not fully read each page body.; Gift card print and loyalty card print routes (giftcards/[id]/print, kart/[id]/print) — not opened.; broadcast_kampaniyalari / broadcast_log models and any older broadcast feature — out of the campaigns-pricing scope and not audited.

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ Mutations (create/edit/status/archive/coupon/loyalty/gift) are guarded server-side via requireKampaniyaActionPerm with correct codes; direct server-action calls cannot bypass permission — _features/kampaniyalar/actions.ts:34,84,157,401,443,493,574,611,658,690,725,774,829,869,895,922,987,1013,1065_
- ✅ Soft-delete is implemented as status='archived' (no hard delete); deleteCampaign sets status archived and audits — _features/kampaniyalar/actions.ts:689-718 (deleteCampaign)_
- ✅ Status transitions are a finite-state machine; archived cannot be reactivated — _features/kampaniyalar/actions.ts:649-655 VALID_TRANSITIONS, enforced at 664-667_
- ✅ Audit log written on every mutation (yarat/yenile/status/arxiv/balans_deyis/serf/broadcast/bulk) — _features/kampaniyalar/actions.ts:43,132,213,425,458,553,594,635,672,706,752,813,843,881,908,949_
- ✅ Loyalty bonus redemption uses atomic conditional updateMany (balans gte mebleg) inside $transaction, preventing negative balance / race — _features/kampaniyalar/actions.ts:344-372 (applyBonusToSale)_
- ✅ Manual loyalty balance adjust wraps card update + loyalty_tx ledger row in one $transaction — _features/kampaniyalar/actions.ts:788-809 (adjustLoyaltyBalans)_
- ✅ Bulk coupon and gift codes use crypto randomBytes (not Math.random) with collision handling — _features/kampaniyalar/actions.ts:104-118, 22-30, 930-964_
- ✅ createCampaign validates discount params: requires discount_type+value>0 for discount types and rejects percent>100; date logic bitme>=baslama — _features/kampaniyalar/actions.ts:506-530_
- ✅ Matcher read-query filters campaigns by status='active' AND baslama<=now AND (bitme null OR bitme>now), so expired-by-date campaigns are excluded from pricing even without the cron running — _features/kampaniyalar/matcher.ts:53-60_
- ✅ applyCoupon validates aktiv, expiry, current_uses<max_uses, and customer-scoped coupon ownership — _features/kampaniyalar/matcher.ts:272-284_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🔴 **[critical · data-flow] Campaign/coupon usage is NEVER committed on a real sale — commitCampaignApplications and findApplicableCampaigns are orphaned (dead code)**
  - Detal: matcher.ts exports findApplicableCampaigns() (auto-apply engine) and commitCampaignApplications() (writes campaign_usage, increments campaigns.current_uses, accrues loyalty bonus). A repo-wide grep shows NO caller of either function outside matcher.ts itself. The POS sale flow (sale-action.ts) has zero campaign integration — no campaign_usage row is ever created and campaigns.current_uses / coupons.current_uses are never incremented from a completed sale. Consequences: (1) max_uses, max_per_user (campaigns) and coupon max_uses limits are dead — counters stay at 0 so applyCoupon's 'limit dolub' check (matcher.ts:281) and matcher.ts:91-96 limit gates never trigger; coupons are effectively infinitely reusable. (2) Every analytics/ROI surface reads empty data: getCampaignStats (endirimCemi/bonusVerildi/istifade30g), getCampaignROI, getCampaignRoi, and the [id] detail 'İstifadə' card all show 0 forever. (3) Loyalty bonus *accrual* (earning points on purchases, matcher.ts:228-255) never happens through the sale.
  - Sübut: matcher.ts:47 (findApplicableCampaigns) & 201 (commitCampaignApplications) have no external callers (grep: only self-references); sale-action.ts has no campaign/kupon/loyalty/campaign_usage reference (grep returned nothing); queries.ts:46-49 & 279-284 aggregate campaign_usage that is never written
  - Fayllar: features/kampaniyalar/matcher.ts, features/pos/sale-action.ts, features/kampaniyalar/queries.ts

- 🔴 **[critical · data-flow] POS coupon discount is shown to cashier but DROPPED from the persisted sale — son_mebleg overstated, cash/finance mismatch**
  - Detal: In pos-client.tsx the displayed total subtracts the coupon: sonMebleg = umumi - endirimEffectiveMebleg - couponEndirim - bonusAfterDiscount (line 447), and the UI even renders '(kupon −X)' (lines 1749-1751). But the createSale payload only sends endirim_mebleg: endirimEffectiveMebleg (line 903) — couponEndirim is NOT added. createSale then computes son_mebleg = umumi - data.endirim_mebleg (sale-action.ts:118) and sets odenilmis = sonMebleg for non-credit (sale-action.ts:193). Result: the recorded sale total and the cash/card/finance amount are HIGHER than what the cashier collected from the customer (who paid the coupon-reduced price). The coupon discount is silently lost in the books.
  - Sübut: features/pos/components/pos-client.tsx:439-447 (couponEndirim affects displayed sonMebleg) vs :903 (payload sends only endirimEffectiveMebleg); features/pos/sale-action.ts:118,191-193
  - Fayllar: features/pos/components/pos-client.tsx, features/pos/sale-action.ts

- 🔴 **[critical · data-flow] Loyalty bonus redemption double-counts — bonus deducted from card but NOT subtracted from recorded sale total**
  - Detal: POS computes sonMebleg minus bonusAfterDiscount (pos-client.tsx:447) so the customer hands over less cash. But the createSale payload omits bonusAfterDiscount (only endirimEffectiveMebleg is sent, :903), so the sale records son_mebleg without the bonus reduction and marks odenilmis = full sonMebleg (sale-action.ts:193). Separately, after the sale, applyBonusToSale decrements the customer's loyalty balance by bonusAfterDiscount (pos-client.tsx:978-981). Net effect: the bonus is consumed from the card AND the invoice still shows the full amount as paid in cash — overstating revenue/cash and effectively charging the bonus twice (once from card balance, once still owed by the recorded invoice).
  - Sübut: features/pos/components/pos-client.tsx:443-447 (bonusAfterDiscount reduces sonMebleg) & 978-981 (applyBonusToSale decrements card) vs :903 (bonus not in payload); sale-action.ts:118,193
  - Fayllar: features/pos/components/pos-client.tsx, features/pos/sale-action.ts, features/kampaniyalar/actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · permission] Route-gate for /kampaniyalar requires permission codes that are never seeded (kampaniya.view / marketing.view) — locks out legit read users**
  - Detal: lib/auth/route-gate.ts:58 gates /kampaniyalar on anyOf ['kampaniya.view','marketing.view']. Neither code exists: both migrations seed kampaniya.oxu as the read permission (2026-06-01-kampaniyalar.sql:147, 2026-06-02-kampaniya-permissions.sql:11) and navigation gates on kampaniya.oxu (lib/navigation.ts:99). The layout enforces gateRoute and redirects to /icaze-yox when no matching code is held (app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx:120-121). So a non-admin user granted kampaniya.oxu (e.g. manecer/muhasib/baxan/kassir per migration grants) is DENIED access to the whole module despite having read permission. Only sahibkar/admin/owner (who bypass the gate, route-gate.ts:95) can open it.
