The action layer still independently guards mutations with the correct codes, so this is an over-restriction, not a bypass.
  - Sübut: lib/auth/route-gate.ts:58 vs scripts/migrations/2026-06-01-kampaniyalar.sql:147 & 2026-06-02-kampaniya-permissions.sql:11 (grep confirms kampaniya.view/marketing.view appear ONLY in route-gate.ts); app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx:120-121
  - Fayllar: lib/auth/route-gate.ts, scripts/migrations/2026-06-02-kampaniya-permissions.sql, app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Gift card balance (qaliq) is never decremented — gift cards can be sold but cannot be spent**
  - Detal: createGiftCard mints a card with qaliq = nominal (actions.ts:935-944) and checkGiftCardBalance reads it (986-1006), but the only writes to gift_cards are assignGiftCardToCustomer (875, sets owner) and deactivateGiftCard (902, sets aktiv=false). A grep for any qaliq decrement / gift_cards.update tied to a sale returns nothing. There is no redemption path that reduces a gift card's balance when used as payment, and createSale has no gift-card payment handling. Customers' prepaid value is taken in but cannot be drawn down through the implemented flow — a pre-paid liability that never settles.
  - Sübut: features/kampaniyalar/actions.ts:875,902 (only assign/deactivate update gift_cards); grep for 'qaliq decrement' / gift_cards.update outside these two returns nothing; features/pos/sale-action.ts has no gift-card logic
  - Fayllar: features/kampaniyalar/actions.ts, features/pos/sale-action.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] getCampaigns / getCampaignStats include archived (soft-deleted) campaigns by default**
  - Detal: getCampaigns (queries.ts:6-18) builds where from optional status/tip/q only — when no status filter is passed (the default list call from the page) archived campaigns ARE returned. getCampaignStats.total counts all campaigns including archived (queries.ts:39). The audit expectation is that soft-deleted records are excluded from default lists. Compounded by the list page not reading searchParams (see next bug), archived campaigns are always visible in the main grid and inflate the 'Cəm kampaniya' KPI.
  - Sübut: features/kampaniyalar/queries.ts:6-18 (no status<>archived default exclusion); :39 prisma.campaigns.count({}) total; app/(dashboard)/kampaniyalar/page.tsx:30 calls getCampaigns() with no filter
  - Fayllar: features/kampaniyalar/queries.ts, app/(dashboard)/kampaniyalar/page.tsx

- 🟡 **[medium · ux] Campaign list filter links (Aktiv/Qaralama/Bitənlər, ?status=...) are no-ops — page ignores searchParams**
  - Detal: page.tsx renders filter links to /kampaniyalar?status=active|draft|expired (lines 105-109) but KampaniyalarPage takes no searchParams prop and always calls getCampaigns() with no argument (line 30). Clicking the filters changes the URL but the list never filters. getCampaigns DOES support a status filter, so the wiring is simply missing.
  - Sübut: app/(dashboard)/kampaniyalar/page.tsx:27-31 (no searchParams param), :105-109 (filter links); grep for 'searchParams' in page.tsx returns nothing
  - Fayllar: app/(dashboard)/kampaniyalar/page.tsx

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] commitCampaignApplications loops mutations WITHOUT a $transaction (non-atomic) — even if it were wired up**
  - Detal: Should the dead commitCampaignApplications ever be invoked, it performs sequential prisma calls (campaign_usage.create, campaigns.update increment, loyalty_cards.update, loyalty_tx.create) per applied campaign with NO surrounding prisma.$transaction (matcher.ts:207-256). A mid-loop failure would leave usage recorded but counters/bonus inconsistent, and bonus accrual is not idempotent against double-submit. This violates the atomicity expectation for sale-linked mutations.
  - Sübut: features/kampaniyalar/matcher.ts:207-256 — bare awaits, no $transaction wrapper
  - Fayllar: features/kampaniyalar/matcher.ts

- ⚪ **[low · functional] expire-campaigns cron route is not registered in vercel.json — never runs automatically**
  - Detal: app/api/cron/expire-campaigns/route.ts exists and is documented as the auto-expiry job, but vercel.json crons block lists only daily-email-summary, run-automation-rules, compute-reorder-recommendations, check-overdue-tasks — expire-campaigns is absent. So campaigns are never auto-flipped to status='expired' on schedule; expiry happens only via the manual expireOldCampaigns action. Functional impact is contained because the matcher and getCampaignStats already filter by bitme>now at read time (matcher.ts:57; queries.ts:40-42), so expired campaigns do not price or count as active regardless — but the persisted status stays stale ('active'/'paused') indefinitely, which is misleading in lists/detail and ROI status badges.
  - Sübut: app/api/cron/expire-campaigns/route.ts:10-31 exists; vercel.json crons paths (grep) do not include expire-campaigns
  - Fayllar: app/api/cron/expire-campaigns/route.ts, vercel.json

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] Coupon discount sent to POS is computed off pre-discount cemi and is not validated against per-line min price / discount-approval**
  - Detal: applyCoupon computes endirimMebleg on cart.cemi/value (matcher.ts:305-311) and applyCouponAction passes lines:[] (actions.ts:66), so the coupon engine never sees line items. Even if the coupon discount were correctly forwarded to createSale, it would bypass createSale's discount-approval / maya_alti / min_satis_qiymeti checks since those operate on line endirim_faiz and the scalar endirim_mebleg, not on a coupon channel. Low severity only because the coupon currently never reaches the sale (see critical bug above).
  - Sübut: features/kampaniyalar/matcher.ts:305-311; features/kampaniyalar/actions.ts:66 (lines: []); features/pos/sale-action.ts:62-70,148-156 (discount checks operate on lines/endirim_mebleg)
  - Fayllar: features/kampaniyalar/matcher.ts, features/kampaniyalar/actions.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 /kampaniyalar route-gate: Gate requires kampaniya.view/marketing.view which are never seeded; the real read code is kampaniya.oxu. Non-admin users with kampaniya.oxu are redirected to /icaze-yox and cannot open the module at all. — _lib/auth/route-gate.ts:58 vs scripts/migrations/2026-06-02-kampaniya-permissions.sql:11 + lib/navigation.ts:99; enforced app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx:120-121_
- 🔐 Page-level guards on loyalty/giftcards/ayarlar/kuponlar pages: These pages have no per-page requireKampaniyaActionPerm/permission check; they rely entirely on the (mis-coded) route-gate. Read-data queries run without their own permission assertion. Mutations are still safe (server actions guard correctly), but read access control is fully delegated to the broken gate. — _grep for requireKampaniya/getRequestPermissions/notFound/redirect in app/(dashboard)/kampaniyalar/{loyalty,giftcards,ayarlar}/page.tsx returned nothing_
- 🔐 expire-campaigns cron auth: Auth only enforced when CRON_SECRET is set: 'if (process.env.CRON_SECRET && auth !== ...)'. If CRON_SECRET is unset, the endpoint is public, and POST=GET makes it POST-callable; it runs prismaUnscoped.updateMany across ALL tenants. Impact is low (only flips already-past-bitme campaigns to 'expired'; idempotent; no data read/exposure), but it is an unauthenticated cross-tenant write when the secret is absent. — _app/api/cron/expire-campaigns/route.ts:13 (conditional auth), :19 prismaUnscoped, :33 POST=GET_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** features/pos/sale-action.ts (accept campaign/coupon/bonus context, fold coupon+bonus into son_mebleg, write campaign_usage + increment current_uses, all inside the existing $transaction), features/pos/components/pos-client.tsx (include couponEndirim and bonusAfterDiscount in the createSale payload; pass applied campaign/coupon ids), features/kampaniyalar/matcher.ts (wrap commitCampaignApplications mutations in $transaction; make it idempotent; have createSale call it within the sale transaction; increment coupons.current_uses), lib/auth/route-gate.ts (fix /kampaniyalar gate codes to kampaniya.oxu / marketing.broadcast), features/kampaniyalar/queries.ts (exclude status='archived' by default in getCampaigns and getCampaignStats.total), app/(dashboard)/kampaniyalar/page.tsx (read searchParams and pass status filter; or remove dead filter links), vercel.json (register /api/cron/expire-campaigns in crons) — optional given read-time filtering, features/kampaniyalar/actions.ts + sale-action.ts (add gift-card redemption path that decrements gift_cards.qaliq within the sale transaction)

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** No schema migration strictly required for the data-flow bugs (campaigns/coupons/campaign_usage/loyalty/gift models already have the needed columns: current_uses, campaign_usage, gift_cards.qaliq).; Permission data: seed kampaniya.view & marketing.view OR (preferred) change route-gate.ts:58 to use the existing kampaniya.oxu / marketing.broadcast codes — code change, not DB, unless aligning on new codes.

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** "High. The campaigns module is designed to feed POS/ticaret sales (discounts, coupon redemption, loyalty earn/burn) and finance/customer balances, but the integration is broken at the seam: the auto-apply engine (findApplicableCampaigns) and the usage-commit (commitCampaignApplications) are never called by sale-action.ts, and POS-computed coupon/bonus reductions are stripped from the createSale payload. This corrupts downstream modules: (1) finance_operations / cash drawer overstates collected cash because son_mebleg/odenilmis are recorded higher than what the customer paid after coupon+bonus; (2) customer balance for nisye sales would record more debt than the discounted price; (3) loyalty balance is decremented (applyBonusToSale) against an invoice that didn't credit it — double charge; (4) all campaign analytics/ROI (analitika page, getCampaignStats, getCampaignROI) read campaign_usage that is never written, so reporting is uniformly zero; (5) gift cards are a prepaid liability with no redemption path. Loyalty *accrual* on purchases also never fires, so tiers never advance from real sales."

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** "1) Wire campaigns into the sale transaction: extend CreateSaleSchema to accept applied campaign ids, coupon code, and bonus amount; in createSale compute son_mebleg subtracting coupon + bonus; within the SAME $transaction write campaign_usage rows, increment campaigns.current_uses and coupons.current_uses, and accrue/burn loyalty (move applyBonusToSale's decrement and bonus accrual into the sale transaction instead of a separate post-sale call). Make it idempotent on satis_id (campaign_usage has idx on satis_id) to survive double-click/refresh. 2) In pos-client.tsx, include couponEndirim and bonusAfterDiscount in the payload and stop calling applyBonusToSale separately (avoid the double-charge). 3) Either delete the orphaned findApplicableCampaigns/commitCampaignApplications or properly invoke them from the sale flow wrapped in $transaction. 4) Fix route-gate.ts:58 to ['kampaniya.oxu','marketing.broadcast'] (or seed the .view codes) so kampaniya.oxu holders can open the module. 5) Default-exclude status='archived' in getCampaigns/getCampaignStats total. 6) Make the list page read searchParams and forward the status filter. 7) Add a gift-card redemption path decrementing qaliq inside the sale transaction. 8) Register expire-campaigns in vercel.json (or rely on read-time filtering and drop the route). Treat #1-#3 as critical/blocking; verify with a POS coupon+bonus sale that son_mebleg, cash, customer balance, campaign_usage, current_uses, and loyalty balance all reconcile."

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- In POS: add items, apply a valid coupon, complete a cash sale. Verify the persisted sale's son_mebleg equals what the cashier collected (after coupon), the cash/finance movement matches, AND a campaign_usage row exists with current_uses incremented. Currently expected to FAIL (coupon dropped, no usage row).
- Apply the same coupon repeatedly across many sales — verify the coupon's max_uses limit eventually blocks it. Currently expected to FAIL (current_uses never increments, coupon reusable forever).
- Redeem loyalty bonus in a POS sale, then re-open the sale and the customer's loyalty card: verify the invoice total reflects the bonus reduction and the card balance is reduced exactly once. Currently expected to FAIL (invoice unreduced + balance reduced = double charge).
- Open /kampaniyalar/analitika and the campaign detail İstifadə/endirim KPIs after several campaign-driven sales — verify non-zero usage/discount totals. Currently expected to show 0.
- Log in as a non-admin user with only kampaniya.oxu granted and open /kampaniyalar — verify access is allowed. Currently expected to redirect to /icaze-yox.
- Archive a campaign, then open /kampaniyalar without a filter — verify the archived campaign is hidden from the default list and not counted in 'Cəm kampaniya'. Currently expected to still appear.
- Click the Aktiv/Qaralama/Bitənlər filter links on /kampaniyalar — verify the list actually filters. Currently expected to be a no-op.
- Sell a gift card, then attempt to pay for a later sale with it — verify the gift card balance decreases. Currently expected: no redemption path exists.
- With CRON_SECRET unset, hit GET and POST /api/cron/expire-campaigns without auth — verify whether it executes (it will). Confirm whether CRON_SECRET is set in the deployed env.

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Whether CRON_SECRET is actually configured in the deployed environment (determines if the expire-campaigns endpoint is currently public). Code shows conditional auth only.
- Confirm in a running POS that applying a coupon and then completing a sale persists a sale whose son_mebleg ignores the coupon (and that no campaign_usage row appears) — strongly implied by code but not runtime-confirmed.
- Confirm that a non-admin user holding only kampaniya.oxu is redirected to /icaze-yox when opening /kampaniyalar (depends on live permission resolution in getRequestPermissions).

---

## 14. Hesabatlar

**Xülasə:** The Hesabatlar module is feature-rich and mostly correct on tenant scoping, date filtering, exports, and AI quota, and the 5 sensitive sub-reports have page-level permission guards behind a master layout gate. However there are serious data-integrity and permission gaps. CRITICAL: every hesabatlar export/drilldown API route (musteri PII, maliyye P&L, satis, marketplace, mehsul, ai) checks only auth() and is not under the dashboard layout, so it bypasses both the layout gate and report permissions — any logged-in user can download customer PII and full financials. HIGH: no report query filters deleted_at IS NULL (the Prisma extension only injects sahibkar_id), so soft-deleted records can leak; this is masked for sales today only because cancelSale also sets status='legv', but it breaks for restored records and for the generic soft-delete helper, and it already fully breaks the idxal (purchases) report which doesn't even filter status='legv' and counts cancelled purchases. Returns and expenses are summed with no status/deleted_at filter, distorting P&L. The Pul report computes cash from a different ledger (kassalar/kassa_emeliyyatlari) than the canonical finance_operations/maliye_hesablari used by the maliyye module, so balances won't reconcile. MEDIUM: P&L revenue vs COGS use inconsistent qaralama filters; stealth scaling is applied only in getSalesKpi (real numbers leak through every other report and the satis-page share% math is wrong); requireHesabatPagePerm treats hesabat.view as satisfying maliye.view/musteri.oxu so the fine-grained gating is a no-op; customer debt is read from the cached kontragentler.borc instead of the shared balance service. Most numeric findings need a live DB to quantify divergence, but the code paths are unambiguous.

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** app/(dashboard)/hesabatlar/page.tsx (hub, lines 1-60); app/(dashboard)/hesabatlar/satis/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/hesabatlar/maliyye/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/hesabatlar/marja/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/hesabatlar/musteri/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/hesabatlar/pul/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/hesabatlar/emekdas/page.tsx; features/hesabatlar/access-guard.ts; features/hesabatlar/shared.ts; features/hesabatlar/satis-queries.ts; features/hesabatlar/maliyye-queries.ts; features/hesabatlar/marja-queries.ts; features/hesabatlar/musteri-queries.ts; features/hesabatlar/pul-queries.ts; features/hesabatlar/emekdas-queries.ts; features/hesabatlar/idxal-queries.ts; features/hesabatlar/marketplace-queries.ts (lines 1-95); features/hesabatlar/stok-queries.ts (grep); features/hesabatlar/mehsul-queries.ts (grep); features/hesabatlar/ai-insights.ts (grep for quota); app/api/hesabatlar/musteri/export/route.ts; app/api/hesabatlar/maliyye/export/route.ts; app/api/hesabatlar/satis/export/route.ts; app/api/hesabatlar/satis/csv/route.ts; app/api/hesabatlar/marketplace/drilldown/route.ts; app/api/hesabatlar/mehsul/reorder-pdf/route.ts; app/api/hesabatlar/ai/generate/route.ts; lib/db/prisma.ts; lib/db/with-tenant.ts; lib/auth/route-gate.ts; lib/soft-delete/actions.ts; lib/balance/account-balance.ts; features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts (cancel path 200-329); features/ticaret/alis-actions.ts (cancel path grep); prisma/schema.prisma (satis_sifarisleri, alis_sifarisleri, qaytarma_sifarisleri, xerclər, kassa_emeliyyatlari, kassalar, maliye_hesablari models)

**Əhatə boşluqları:** app/(dashboard)/hesabatlar/mehsul/page.tsx, stok/page.tsx, idxal/page.tsx, marketplace/page.tsx, servis/page.tsx, ai/page.tsx — page bodies not fully read (only checked for permission guards via grep and read their backing query files for stok/mehsul/idxal/marketplace).; features/hesabatlar/stok-queries.ts and mehsul-queries.ts — only grepped for status/deleted_at filters, not fully read for correctness of ABC analysis, reorder math, dead-stock logic.; features/hesabat/queries.ts (legacy/separate dir) — not opened; unclear if still used by any page.; features/hesabatlar/components/* (charts, date-range-filter, view-toggle, brick-cards, pl-waterfall, yoy-strip, platform-drilldown-modal) — client rendering correctness not audited beyond how pages pass data.; marketplace-queries.ts lines 96+ (getPlatformDrilldown tail and any further functions) not read.; ai-insights.ts full body (only grepped for quota/permission) — prompt construction and any data leakage not reviewed.; servis report query source not located/verified for soft-delete/cancelled exclusion.

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ Page-level fine-grained permission guards exist on the 5 sensitive sub-reports (maliyye/marja -> maliye.view, musteri -> musteri.oxu, pul -> maliye.view, emekdas -> isci.view/maas.view) via requireHesabatPagePerm, and a layout-level gateRoute requires hesabat.view/hesabat.gor for ALL /hesabatlar/* paths. — _app/(dashboard)/hesabatlar/maliyye/page.tsx:19, musteri/page.tsx:36, emekdas/page.tsx:18, pul/page.tsx:24; lib/auth/route-gate.ts:32; app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx:120_
- ✅ Multi-tenant scoping is enforced: every query runs inside withTenant() and the Prisma $extends injects sahibkar_id; raw SQL queries also pass sahibkarId explicitly as a parameter. — _lib/db/prisma.ts:62-65; features/hesabatlar/satis-queries.ts:44,67 (sahibkar_id = ${sahibkarId})_
- ✅ Sales/POS reports correctly net out cancelled sales for cash flow: cancelSale writes a reversing 'qaytarma' kassa_emeliyyatlari row rather than deleting, and kassa_emeliyyatlari has no deleted_at, so the Pul report nets correctly for cancellations. — _features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts:272-284; pul-queries.ts:14-23 (qaytarma counted as xaric)_
- ✅ AI insights generation enforces a per-tenant hourly/daily quota and logs usage; quota check runs inside buildAiInsights. — _features/hesabatlar/ai-insights.ts:318 (checkAiReportQuota), :342 (ai_sohbet_loq.create kanal: hesabat_ai); access-guard.ts:95-134_
- ✅ Date-range presets (bu-ay, kecen-ay, bu-il, bu-rub, son-7/30 etc.) and YoY/previous-period helpers are implemented and shared across pages. — _features/hesabatlar/shared.ts:21-102; used in maliyye/page.tsx:21, satis/page.tsx:64_
- ✅ Export routes (CSV/XLSX/PDF) are wired to real query functions and produce correct sheet structure; CSV escaping and BOM present. — _app/api/hesabatlar/satis/export/route.ts:36-46; musteri/export/route.ts:10-29_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🔴 **[critical · permission] Export API routes do NOT enforce report-level permissions (backend bypass of PII and financial data)**
  - Detal: All hesabatlar API routes check only auth() (logged in) and never call requireHesabatActionPerm or check the per-report permission. API routes are NOT under (dashboard)/layout.tsx, so they bypass even the layout gateRoute(hesabat.view) guard. A user with NO hesabat/maliye/musteri permission can: (1) GET /api/hesabatlar/musteri/export and download the full customer list with PII (ad, telefon, email, sheher, LTV, borc); (2) GET /api/hesabatlar/maliyye/export and download full P&L (revenue, COGS, OPEX, net profit); (3) GET /api/hesabatlar/satis/export, /satis/csv, /mehsul/reorder-pdf, and POST /ai/generate. The pages enforce permission but the data endpoints behind their buttons do not.
  - Sübut: app/api/hesabatlar/musteri/export/route.ts:18-24 (only auth, then withTenant->getCustomersForExport); maliyye/export/route.ts:12-19; satis/export/route.ts:19-21; satis/csv/route.ts:19-20; marketplace/drilldown/route.ts:10-11; mehsul/reorder-pdf/route.ts:13-14; ai/generate/route.ts:8-9
  - Fayllar: app/api/hesabatlar/musteri/export/route.ts, app/api/hesabatlar/maliyye/export/route.ts, app/api/hesabatlar/satis/export/route.ts, app/api/hesabatlar/satis/csv/route.ts, app/api/hesabatlar/marketplace/drilldown/route.ts, app/api/hesabatlar/mehsul/reorder-pdf/route.ts, app/api/hesabatlar/ai/generate/route.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Reports do not exclude soft-deleted (deleted_at) records — only status='legv' is filtered**
  - Detal: The Prisma $extends tenant filter injects ONLY sahibkar_id; it does NOT add deleted_at IS NULL (unlike many module pages). Every hesabatlar query filters status != 'legv' (and qaralama) but NONE filter deleted_at IS NULL, while satis_sifarisleri/alis_sifarisleri/qaytarma_sifarisleri/xerclər all carry deleted_at. Today this is masked for SALES because cancelSale sets BOTH status='legv' AND deleted_at. But the generic restoreRecord sets deleted_at=NULL WITHOUT resetting status (it explicitly comments status may stay 'legv'), and the generic softDeleteRecord helper sets ONLY deleted_at (no status change) and is whitelisted for satis_sifarisleri/alis_sifarisleri/qaytarma_sifarisleri/finance_operations. If that generic soft-delete path is ever invoked on a sale/purchase/return (the helper exists and supports those tables), or a record is restored to a non-legv status, the reports will silently include it. The active sales list and dashboard, by contrast, DO filter deleted_at IS NULL, so reports can diverge from those module pages.
  - Sübut: lib/db/prisma.ts:62-65 (only sahibkar_id injected, no deleted_at); lib/soft-delete/actions.ts:48-56 (softDeleteRecord sets only deleted_at) and :108-114 (restoreRecord sets deleted_at=NULL, no status reset; comment lines 105-107); contrast features/ticaret/satis-queries.ts:104 (AND deleted_at IS NULL) and dashboard-queries.ts:73; reports e.g. satis-queries.ts:50,108-114; maliyye-queries.ts:23-35
  - Fayllar: features/hesabatlar/satis-queries.ts, features/hesabatlar/maliyye-queries.ts, features/hesabatlar/marja-queries.ts, features/hesabatlar/musteri-queries.ts, features/hesabatlar/marketplace-queries.ts, lib/db/prisma.ts, lib/soft-delete/actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Idxal (purchases) report counts cancelled AND soft-deleted purchases in totals**
  - Detal: getImportStats, getSupplierBreakdown and getImportStatusBreakdown filter NEITHER status != 'legv' NOR deleted_at IS NULL on alis_sifarisleri. cancelPurchase sets status='legv' + deleted_at, so cancelled purchases (and their supplier debt umumi_mebleg - odenilmis) are still summed into total_amount, supplier 'cemi'/'borc', and the status breakdown. This makes the idxal report's totals diverge from the active purchases list (features/ticaret/alis-queries.ts which filters deleted_at) and overstates supplier liabilities.
  - Sübut: features/hesabatlar/idxal-queries.ts:24-28 (aggregate where only tarix), :62-64 (only sahibkar_id+tarix), :90-92 (only sahibkar_id+tarix); cancel sets legv+deleted_at at features/ticaret/alis-actions.ts:522-524; schema deleted_at at prisma/schema.prisma:301
  - Fayllar: features/hesabatlar/idxal-queries.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Returns (qaytarma) summed without status/deleted_at filter inflate P&L returns and return-rate**
  - Detal: qaytarma_sifarisleri has both status (default 'tesdiqlenmemis') and deleted_at columns. The P&L returns line (getPlSummary) and the sales-KPI return_count/return_amount sum ALL qaytarma rows in the range with NO status filter and NO deleted_at filter. So unapproved/draft returns (status='tesdiqlenmemis') and soft-deleted returns still subtract from net revenue and inflate the return rate, diverging from the returns module which tracks status.
  - Sübut: features/hesabatlar/maliyye-queries.ts:42-47 (SUM umumi_mebleg FROM qaytarma_sifarisleri, only sahibkar_id+tarix); satis-queries.ts:71-77 (same); schema status line 4181, deleted_at line 4190
  - Fayllar: features/hesabatlar/maliyye-queries.ts, features/hesabatlar/satis-queries.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Expenses (OPEX) summed without deleted_at filter overstate costs and understate net profit**
  - Detal: xerclər has a deleted_at column, but every expense aggregation in reports sums mebleg_azn with no deleted_at IS NULL guard: getPlSummary OPEX, getFixedVariableBreak, getMonthlyPl12 opex CTE, getExpenseCategories, and the idxal vergi/gomruk subquery. A soft-deleted expense still reduces net profit in the P&L, pul report and the maliyye Excel export, diverging from the expenses module which filters deleted_at.
  - Sübut: features/hesabatlar/maliyye-queries.ts:36-41 (OPEX), :103-113 (fixed/variable), :170-176 (monthly opex); pul-queries.ts:147-157 (getExpenseCategories); idxal-queries.ts:29-36; schema xerclər deleted_at at prisma/schema.prisma:6233
  - Fayllar: features/hesabatlar/maliyye-queries.ts, features/hesabatlar/pul-queries.ts, features/hesabatlar/idxal-queries.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Pul report cash/account balances come from kassalar/kassa_emeliyyatlari, NOT the canonical finance_operations/maliye_hesablari ledger**
  - Detal: The maliyye module's single source of truth for account balances is finance_operations + maliye_hesablari via lib/balance/account-balance.ts (respects deleted_at IS NULL AND status='aktiv', handles transfers bidirectionally). The Pul report instead reads a DIFFERENT ledger (kassalar + kassa_emeliyyatlari) with ad-hoc emeliyyat_nov IN ('satis','medaxil') / ('qaytarma','mexaric') math and no transfer handling. kassalar and maliye_hesablari are distinct tables (schema lines 2646 vs 3512). The 'Hesab balansları' table and opening/closing/runway KPIs on the Pul report will not reconcile with the maliyye module account balances; transfers and finance-only operations (e.g. supplier payments booked only to finance_operations) are missing.
  - Sübut: features/hesabatlar/pul-queries.ts:54-68 (kassalar + kassa_emeliyyatlari), :93-101 (opening from kassa_emeliyyatlari); canonical lib/balance/account-balance.ts:48-101 (finance_operations, deleted_at+status, transfers); features/maliyye/account-queries.ts:172-187 (finance_operations); schema kassalar:2646, maliye_hesablari:3512
  - Fayllar: features/hesabatlar/pul-queries.ts, lib/balance/account-balance.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] P&L revenue and COGS use inconsistent draft (qaralama) filters**
  - Detal: In getPlSummary the revenue aggregate excludes drafts (qaralama: { not: true }) but the COGS raw subquery filters only ss.status != 'legv' and omits the qaralama check. getMonthlyPl12 applies qaralama IS NOT TRUE to revenue but NOT to its COGS CTE either. A sale flagged qaralama=true would contribute COGS without contributing revenue, distorting gross profit and gross margin for that period.
  - Sübut: features/hesabatlar/maliyye-queries.ts:24 (revenue: qaralama not true) vs :27-35 (COGS raw: only status != 'legv'); :151-157 (rev qaralama) vs :159-168 (cogs CTE no qaralama)
  - Fayllar: features/hesabatlar/maliyye-queries.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Stealth scaling applied only in getSalesKpi — other reports leak real amounts and break share % math**
  - Detal: getStealthState scaling (multiply by stealth.scale) is applied ONLY in getSalesKpi total_amount/discount/avg/max/return_amount. Every other query (getDailySales, getTopProductsExt, getTopCustomersExt, getSalesByUser, getSalesPivot, getSalesByWarehouse, getHourlyPerf, P&L, marja, marketplace, pul, emekdas) returns UNSCALED amounts. Result: (1) under stealth mode the satis-page KPI strip shows scaled totals while charts/tables/pivot and all other report pages show the real numbers (stealth defeated); (2) on the satis page the per-row 'Pay %' = p.cemi / kpi.total_amount mixes an unscaled numerator with a scaled denominator, producing wrong percentages.
  - Sübut: features/hesabatlar/satis-queries.ts:84-96 (scale applied) vs getDailySales:116-120, getTopProductsExt:235-239, getSalesByUser:213 (no scale); satis/page.tsx:453,532,604 (share = cemi / kpi.total_amount)
  - Fayllar: features/hesabatlar/satis-queries.ts, app/(dashboard)/hesabatlar/satis/page.tsx

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] requireHesabatPagePerm treats master hesabat.view as satisfying every fine-grained report permission**
  - Detal: requireHesabatPagePerm grants access if the user has the requested perm OR has hesabat.view (the master). Since the layout gateRoute already requires hesabat.view/hesabat.gor to enter ANY /hesabatlar/* path, the master perm is effectively always present, so the per-page maliye.view / musteri.oxu / isci.view checks are no-ops: a user with hesabat.view but WITHOUT maliye.view can still open /hesabatlar/maliyye (P&L), /marja (shows maya/cost), /pul, and /musteri (PII). The intended financial/PII gating does not actually restrict.
  - Sübut: features/hesabatlar/access-guard.ts:58 (ok = perms.some(...) || icazeler.includes('hesabat.view')); layout enforces hesabat.view at lib/auth/route-gate.ts:32
  - Fayllar: features/hesabatlar/access-guard.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Customer debt report reads kontragentler.borc cache directly instead of the shared customer-balance service**
  - Detal: getDebtBuckets, getTopCustomersLtv and the customer CSV export read kontragentler.borc (a cached column) directly rather than computing via lib/balance/customer-balance. If the cached borc drifts from the canonical finance/sale-derived balance (the recalc isn't run), the Müştəri report's debt buckets and 'Cəm borc' total will not reconcile with the maliyye debitor module which uses the shared service. Also the debt aging buckets use GREATEST(son_temas, yenilendi, yaradildi) as the age basis — not invoice/sale due dates — so the aging is heuristic and can misclassify buckets.
  - Sübut: features/hesabatlar/musteri-queries.ts:24 (COALESCE(k.borc,0)), :218-224 (k.borc, age via GREATEST(son_temas,yenilendi,yaradildi)); CSV export getCustomersForExport:86 (k.borc)
  - Fayllar: features/hesabatlar/musteri-queries.ts

- ⚪ **[low · permission] Sales report page (and hub, mehsul, stok, idxal, marketplace, servis, ai) have no page-level permission guard; rely solely on the master layout gate**
  - Detal: Unlike the 5 sensitive pages, SatisReportPage and the hub/mehsul/stok/idxal/marketplace/servis/ai pages call no requireHesabatPagePerm. They depend entirely on the layout gateRoute requiring hesabat.view. That is acceptable for the master-gated reports, but the satis report exposes top-customer PII (names) and per-seller revenue with only the broad hesabat.view, and there is no defense-in-depth if the layout gate is ever bypassed (e.g., the export API routes already are). Idxal/anbar/marketplace/servis reports would ideally also require their domain perms (satinalma.view/anbar.view/marketplace.oxu/servis.view) per the access-guard's own documented map, but those finer perms are never enforced on the page.
  - Sübut: app/(dashboard)/hesabatlar/satis/page.tsx:62-64 (no guard); grep across page.tsx/satis/mehsul/stok/idxal/marketplace/servis/ai shows no requireHesabatPagePerm; intended map documented at features/hesabatlar/access-guard.ts:12-25
  - Fayllar: app/(dashboard)/hesabatlar/satis/page.tsx, app/(dashboard)/hesabatlar/page.tsx

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] Naive Date string parsing in parseDateRange uses local-time strings while DB columns are @db.Date — off-by-one day risk**
  - Detal: parseDateRange builds from/to via new Date(sp.from + 'T00:00:00') and new Date(sp.to + 'T23:59:59') (no timezone), and presets use new Date(year, month, day) — both local-time. satis_sifarisleri.tarix etc. are @db.Date (date-only). Depending on server timezone vs user timezone, BETWEEN comparisons can include/exclude boundary-day rows inconsistently, so a report for 'today' may miss or double-count edge-of-day sales relative to the module list pages.
  - Sübut: features/hesabatlar/shared.ts:64-66; schema satis_sifarisleri.tarix @db.Date at prisma/schema.prisma:5173
  - Fayllar: features/hesabatlar/shared.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 All /api/hesabatlar/* route handlers: Only auth() is checked; no requireHesabatActionPerm / report permission. API routes are outside (dashboard)/layout.tsx so the gateRoute(hesabat.view) guard does not apply. Direct GET/POST bypasses all report gating and leaks customer PII and full financials. — _app/api/hesabatlar/musteri/export/route.ts:18-24; maliyye/export/route.ts:12-19; satis/export/route.ts:19-21; satis/csv/route.ts:19-20; marketplace/drilldown/route.ts:10-11; mehsul/reorder-pdf/route.ts:13-14; ai/generate/route.ts:8-9_
- 🔐 requireHesabatPagePerm fine-grained enforcement: ok = perms.some(...) || icazeler.includes('hesabat.view'); since the layout already requires hesabat.view to reach any /hesabatlar path, the maliye.view/musteri.oxu/isci.view checks never actually deny — a user with only hesabat.view can open P&L, marja (cost data), pul, and customer PII. — _features/hesabatlar/access-guard.ts:58; lib/auth/route-gate.ts:32_
- 🔐 Page guards on satis/hub/mehsul/stok/idxal/marketplace/servis/ai reports: No requireHesabatPagePerm on these pages; they rely only on the master layout gate (hesabat.view). No domain-specific gating (satinalma.view/anbar.view/marketplace.oxu/servis.view) despite being documented in the access-guard map; no defense-in-depth. — _app/(dashboard)/hesabatlar/satis/page.tsx:62-64; features/hesabatlar/access-guard.ts:12-25_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** app/api/hesabatlar/musteri/export/route.ts, app/api/hesabatlar/maliyye/export/route.ts, app/api/hesabatlar/satis/export/route.ts, app/api/hesabatlar/satis/csv/route.ts, app/api/hesabatlar/marketplace/drilldown/route.ts, app/api/hesabatlar/mehsul/reorder-pdf/route.ts, app/api/hesabatlar/ai/generate/route.ts, features/hesabatlar/satis-queries.ts, features/hesabatlar/maliyye-queries.ts, features/hesabatlar/marja-queries.ts, features/hesabatlar/musteri-queries.ts, features/hesabatlar/pul-queries.ts, features/hesabatlar/idxal-queries.ts, features/hesabatlar/marketplace-queries.ts, features/hesabatlar/access-guard.ts, features/hesabatlar/shared.ts

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** Xeyr

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** Reports are read-only so they do not corrupt other modules, but they are the consumer end of the ERP data flow and currently DIVERGE from their source modules in several places: (1) deleted_at not filtered means reports can show records the ticaret/maliyye/expenses module pages have already removed; (2) the Pul report's cash ledger (kassalar/kassa_emeliyyatlari) is a different table set than the canonical finance_operations/maliye_hesablari that the maliyye module and lib/balance treat as source of truth, so account balances, runway and net cash will not match the maliyye account pages; (3) customer debt reads the cached kontragentler.borc rather than lib/balance/customer-balance, so it can drift from the debitor module; (4) idxal counts cancelled purchases, overstating supplier liabilities relative to the purchases module. The most dangerous cross-cutting issue is the unguarded export API routes, which expose customer PII and full financials to any authenticated user regardless of module permissions.

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) Permission (critical): add requireHesabatActionPerm(<matching perm>) to every /api/hesabatlar/* route handler before running the query (musteri.oxu for customer export, maliye.view for maliyye export, hesabat.view for satis export/csv, marketplace.oxu for drilldown, anbar.view for reorder-pdf, ai.istifade/hesabat.view for ai/generate), returning 403 on failure. 2) Permission gating: change access-guard.ts:58 so the master hesabat.view does NOT auto-satisfy the finer perms (drop the '|| icazeler.includes(hesabat.view)' for pages that pass a specific perm, or split into a separate masterOnly helper), and add requireHesabatPagePerm to satis/idxal/marketplace/servis pages with their domain perms. 3) Soft-delete: add 'AND deleted_at IS NULL' to every raw SQL over satis_sifarisleri/alis_sifarisleri/qaytarma_sifarisleri/xerclər and add deleted_at: null to the corresponding Prisma .aggregate/.groupBy where-clauses; alternatively centralize via a shared helper. 4) Idxal: add status != 'legv' AND deleted_at IS NULL to getImportStats/getSupplierBreakdown/getImportStatusBreakdown. 5) Returns/expenses: add the appropriate status (e.g. approved-only) and deleted_at filters to the qaytarma and xerclər sums. 6) P&L consistency: add qaralama IS NOT TRUE to the COGS raw subquery and monthly COGS CTE. 7) Pul report: switch cash-flow/account-balance queries to finance_operations/maliye_hesablari via lib/balance/account-balance.ts so it reconciles with the maliyye module, or document why two ledgers exist. 8) Stealth: apply the stealth scale uniformly across all amount-returning queries (or remove it from getSalesKpi) and fix the share% denominator. 9) Customer debt: compute via lib/balance/customer-balance instead of kontragentler.borc, and base aging on invoice/sale due dates. 10) Date parsing: normalize from/to to UTC day boundaries consistent with @db.Date semantics.

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- As a user whose role has ONLY hesabat.view (no maliye.view/musteri.oxu): open /hesabatlar/maliyye, /marja, /pul, /musteri — confirm whether they are wrongly accessible (expected to be blocked after fix).
- As a logged-in user with NO hesabatlar permissions at all: directly GET /api/hesabatlar/musteri/export?segment=all and /api/hesabatlar/maliyye/export — confirm 403 after fix (currently returns full data).
- Create a sale, then soft-delete/restore it via the generic soft-delete path (or restore a cancelled sale to a non-legv status) and confirm it does NOT reappear in satis/marja/maliyye reports.
- Cancel a purchase (alis) and verify it disappears from the Idxal report totals, supplier breakdown debt, and status breakdown.
- Create and then soft-delete an expense; verify P&L OPEX, pul report expense pie, and maliyye Excel export all exclude it.
- Create an unapproved/draft return (status tesdiqlenmemis); verify it does NOT subtract from P&L returns or inflate the sales return rate until approved.
- Compare the Pul report 'Hesab balansları' and closing balance against the maliyye module account balances for the same accounts (including a transfer between accounts) and confirm they match.
- Enable stealth mode and verify every report page (not just the satis KPI strip) shows consistently scaled numbers, and that the satis 'Pay %' columns are correct.
- Run a report for a single day spanning the local/UTC midnight boundary and confirm the day's sales match the satis module list count exactly.

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Whether kassa_emeliyyatlari and finance_operations are kept perfectly in sync for every cash/card/bank sale, payment, refund and transfer (determines the exact magnitude of Pul-vs-maliyye balance divergence).
- Whether kontragentler.borc is reliably recalculated after every sale/payment (determines if Müştəri debt buckets match the debitor module).
- Whether any production code path actually invokes the generic softDeleteRecord on satis/alis/qaytarma, or restores a record to a non-legv status (determines if the deleted_at gap is currently exploitable vs latent).
- Actual numeric reconciliation between each report KPI and the corresponding module page (satis total vs ticaret list, P&L net vs maliyye, marja revenue vs satis revenue) requires a populated DB.
- Whether qaralama=true sales ever have satis_sifaris_satirlari rows (determines real impact of the COGS qaralama inconsistency).

---

## 15. Sahibkar gizli bölməsi

**Xülasə:** The hidden Sahibkar section is well-architected on the read side: every owner page calls a server-side guard, sensitive profit/maya/salary/balance queries are only reachable from gated pages, PIN login is bcrypt-hashed and rate-limited, the secret-code command-palette unlock is server-gated to the owner role, and stealth mode is correctly display-only (never persisted, never applied to backups/exports), so it poses no data-integrity risk. However the access-control layer has serious flaws. CRITICAL: the primary owner gate checks numeric rol_id===9 (guard.ts, setup, verify, backup API, sened-upload API, senedler actions, stealth action), but signup clones the system sahibkar role into a per-tenant row with a different auto-increment id and assigns that to the owner — so for essentially every real tenant the genuine owner fails the gate and is locked out of the entire section, while the actions that use rolAd===\"sahibkar\" still work, leaving a half-broken state. The codebase itself documents that rol_ad (not rol_id) must be used. HIGH: all 8 mutations in owner-actions.ts have NO role/PIN check, letting any authenticated tenant user write owner notes/tasks/branches/snapshots/partiya/cron by calling the server action directly; and owner \"secret\" documents are served from /public/uploads with no auth or tenant scoping (UUID-only obscurity). MEDIUM/LOW: the secret-code unlock path is not rate-limited and the default code is 4 digits; sahibkar mutations check role at most but never an active PIN session (so the auto-lock is bypassable via direct action calls, including changing the secret code/recovery email); and those security-setting changes write no audit log and use a weaker bcrypt cost. Fixes are code-only (swap numeric checks for the existing rol_ad/isSahibkar checks, add a shared owner+PIN assertion to mutations, move document serving behind an authenticated route, rate-limit the code path) — no schema migration needed.

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** lib/sahibkar/guard.ts; lib/sahibkar/session.ts; lib/sahibkar/visibility.ts; lib/sahibkar/rate-limit.ts; features/sahibkar/actions.ts; features/sahibkar/secret-code.ts; features/sahibkar/unified-verify.ts; features/sahibkar/owner-actions.ts; features/sahibkar/settings-actions.ts; features/sahibkar/mobile-widget-actions.ts; features/sahibkar/gizli-elaqe-actions.ts; features/sahibkar/maya/actions.ts; features/sahibkar/owner-queries.ts (head); features/sahibkar/senedler/actions.ts (head); features/sahibkar/senedler/queries.ts (grep); features/sahibkar/constants.ts; lib/stealth/actions.ts; lib/stealth/server.ts; lib/auth/route-gate.ts; lib/auth/role-check.ts; lib/db/tenant-context.ts; lib/db/with-tenant.ts; app/(dashboard)/sahibkar/layout.tsx; app/(dashboard)/sahibkar/setup/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/sahibkar/verify/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/sahibkar/senedler/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/sahibkar/backup/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/sahibkar/aktivlik/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/sahibkar/gizli-mod/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/sahibkar/maya/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/sahibkar/mobile/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/sahibkar/page.tsx; app/api/sahibkar/backup/route.ts; app/api/sahibkar/maya/template/route.ts; app/api/sahibkar/sened/upload/route.ts; auth.ts (role population); features/auth/signup-action.ts (role clone); prisma/schema.prisma (model roles, model sahibkar_ayar); components/layout/command-palette-body.tsx (verifySearchCode caller); app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx (sidebar gating grep); features/maliyye/queries.ts (stealth application sample)

**Əhatə boşluqları:** features/sahibkar/components/* (client dialogs/forms) not opened in full — UI-level disabled/dedupe state for double-submit on owner mutations not verified (idempotency of takeSnapshot/saveNot on double-click).; features/sahibkar/owner-queries.ts read only first ~40 lines; remaining query bodies (lines ~278-460) not fully audited for correct stealth/realScale handling and tenant scoping.; features/sahibkar/queries.ts, today-pulse-queries.ts, cash-runway-queries.ts stealth scaling read only via grep — did not verify each numeric field is consistently scaled vs left raw.; features/sahibkar/senedler/queries.ts (getSenedTree/saveSenedTree) only grepped — JSON tree tenant scoping and delete-file path traversal safety not fully read.; features/sahibkar/maya/excel-parser.ts and cost-calculator.ts not read — maya cost computation correctness not verified.; features/sahibkar/audit-outbox-queries.ts and the audit page rendering not deeply reviewed.; lib/sahibkar/backup subfolder (features/sahibkar/backup/queries.ts) tenant scoping of getBackupTables not opened.; auth.ts JWT/session callback full flow (whether rol_id could be remapped) only partially read (lines 60-90, 146-178).

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ Every hidden sahibkar page calls a server-side guard before rendering (no page renders without auth+role+PIN). Pages using getSahibkarState() correctly redirect on every non-ready state. — _grep over app/(dashboard)/sahibkar/*/page.tsx: requireSahibkarSession at page.tsx:33, maya:23, hesabat:14, snapshot:16, magaza:13, not:13, tapshiriq:38, filiallar:15, filiallar/[id]:17, ayarlar:34, gizli-elaqe:40, partiya:14, audit:69, qeyd:13, mobile:12, isci:14, ai:14, filial-muqayise:28; getSahibkarState + explicit redirects in senedler/page.tsx:17-20, backup:23-26, aktivlik:24-27, gizli-mod:13-16_
- ✅ PIN verification is rate-limited (5 attempts -> 5 min lockout) and rate-check runs BEFORE bcrypt; PIN stored as bcrypt hash (cost 12). — _features/sahibkar/actions.ts:84-89 (getAttemptStatus before compare), :100 recordFailure, :34 bcrypt.hash(pin,12); lib/sahibkar/rate-limit.ts:58-67_
- ✅ PIN session cookie is HMAC-signed (AUTH_SECRET), httpOnly, secure in prod, with expiry verified via timingSafeEqual. — _lib/sahibkar/session.ts:20-38 sign/verify, :50-55 cookie flags httpOnly+secure_
- ✅ Secret search-code unlock is server-gated: non-sahibkar role is rejected even though the command-palette triggers it for all users. — _features/sahibkar/secret-code.ts:27 returns not-owner if rolAd!==sahibkar; caller components/layout/command-palette-body.tsx:135_
- ✅ settings-actions, mobile-widget-actions, gizli-elaqe-actions, and stealth action all re-check role server-side (defense beyond the page gate). — _settings-actions.ts:32,60,95,125,162 rolAd!==sahibkar; mobile-widget-actions.ts:22; gizli-elaqe-actions.ts:75,100; lib/stealth/actions.ts:24 rol_id!==9 + PIN re-check at :31_
- ✅ Backup API enforces auth + owner role + active PIN session and exports real data with password hashes excluded. — _app/api/sahibkar/backup/route.ts:18-28 (auth, rol_id, touchPinSession), :54-63 istifadeciler select omits sifre_hash, :74 stealth_mode:false_
- ✅ maya/template API and maya parse action use requireSahibkarSession() (full PIN gate). — _app/api/sahibkar/maya/template/route.ts:8; features/sahibkar/maya/actions.ts:12_
- ✅ Stealth mode is display-only: it scales a freshly read raw value at render time and is never persisted; exports/backup/API routes do not apply stealth. — _lib/stealth/server.ts:18-45 applyStealth pure fn; features/maliyye/queries.ts:70-80 scales raw read result only; grep getStealthState in app/api/ returns no hits; backup route sets stealth_mode:false_
- ✅ Security events (PIN setup/change-via-setup, PIN login, lockout, secret-code/unified-verify login, lock/logout) write audit via safeAuditLog (with outbox fallback), gated by cfg.audit_log. — _features/sahibkar/actions.ts:66-74,103-113,126-136,147-157; secret-code.ts:58-68; unified-verify.ts:59-69_
- ✅ Sensitive owner queries (profit/maya/salary/cash) are only imported by gated /sahibkar/* pages or by widgets used solely inside the gated hub page. — _grep importers: owner-queries/queries/aktivlik-queries all under app/(dashboard)/sahibkar/*; today-pulse-widget & cash-runway-widget imported only by app/(dashboard)/sahibkar/page.tsx_
- ✅ Sidebar visibility honors owner's hide setting and only shows the section to sahibkar role. — _lib/sahibkar/visibility.ts:15 (false for non-sahibkar), :24-26 sidebar_gorunsun!==false; app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx:38 getSahibkarSidebarVisible(user.rol_ad)_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🔴 **[critical · permission] Owner gate uses numeric rol_id===9, but signup assigns the owner a CLONED role with an auto-increment id != 9 — real owners are locked out of the entire hidden section**
  - Detal: The sahibkar section's primary gate checks session.user.rol_id===9 (lib/sahibkar/guard.ts:19). But signup clones the system 'sahibkar' role (template id 9) into a NEW roles row per tenant and assigns the owner rol_id = cloned.id (features/auth/signup-action.ts:100-128). roles.id is a GLOBAL @default(autoincrement()) (prisma schema model roles), so the cloned id is virtually never 9 (9 stays the system template). The session carries that cloned id (auth.ts:177 rol_id: rolId). Consequence: for every tenant created via signup, getSahibkarState() returns 'wrong-role' and requireSahibkarSession() redirects the genuine owner to /dashboard; setup/verify pages redirect them away; the backup API, sened upload API, senedler actions, and stealth action all 403/deny the real owner. The codebase itself documents that rolAd (string) must be used instead of rol_id (lib/db/tenant-context.ts:8-12) and provides lib/auth/role-check.ts isSahibkar(); these gates ignore it. Note the server actions that gate on rolAd==="sahibkar" WOULD work, producing a half-broken, inconsistent state.
  - Sübut: lib/sahibkar/guard.ts:19 (rol_id!==9); features/auth/signup-action.ts:12,100-110,118-128; prisma/schema.prisma model roles id @default(autoincrement()); contradicted by lib/db/tenant-context.ts:8-12 and lib/auth/role-check.ts:21
  - Fayllar: lib/sahibkar/guard.ts, features/auth/signup-action.ts, app/(dashboard)/sahibkar/setup/page.tsx, app/(dashboard)/sahibkar/verify/page.tsx, app/api/sahibkar/backup/route.ts, app/api/sahibkar/sened/upload/route.ts, features/sahibkar/senedler/actions.ts, lib/stealth/actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · permission] owner-actions.ts mutations have NO owner-role check — any authenticated tenant user can write owner notes/tasks/branches/snapshots/partiya/cron**
  - Detal: Every mutation in features/sahibkar/owner-actions.ts only calls requireTenant() (which via withTenant merely requires ANY authenticated session — lib/db/with-tenant.ts:27-49 does not enforce role or PIN). Unlike settings-actions/gizli-elaqe-actions/mobile-widget-actions which check rolAd!=="sahibkar", these 8 "use server" exports omit any role/PIN check: saveNot (:22), deleteNot (:55), saveTapshiriq (:80), updateTaskStatus (:113), saveFilial (:133), takeSnapshot (:161), savePartiya (:204), setSnapshotCron (:240), addTaskComment (:258). A non-owner (e.g. kassir) can invoke these server actions directly (POST to the action endpoint) to create/delete owner notes, manipulate owner tasks, create branches, force snapshots, and toggle the snapshot cron — bypassing the page gate entirely. deleteNot/updateTaskStatus also take a raw id with no tenant ownership re-check beyond the prisma tenant filter.
  - Sübut: features/sahibkar/owner-actions.ts:27,87,140,163,211,242,263 destructure only sahibkarId/istifadeciId (no rolAd); contrast settings-actions.ts:32 etc.; with-tenant.ts:27-49 only requires auth
  - Fayllar: features/sahibkar/owner-actions.ts, lib/db/with-tenant.ts

- 🟠 **[high · permission] Owner secret documents are served from /public/uploads with no authentication or tenant scoping**
  - Detal: The sened upload route writes confidential owner documents to public/uploads/sahibkar-senedler/<uuid><ext> and stores url=/uploads/sahibkar-senedler/<name> (app/api/sahibkar/sened/upload/route.ts:50-55). There is no middleware.ts and no protected serving route (only reference to sahibkar-senedler is the upload route). These files are therefore served as plain static assets with NO auth check and NO tenant isolation — protection is only the unguessable UUID (security-by-obscurity). Anyone who obtains/leaks a URL (logs, referer, browser history, shared link) can fetch an owner-only document cross-tenant. Upload is gated (rol_id===9) but retrieval is not. (Secondary: persisting under public/ on serverless/Vercel uses ephemeral FS, so files may also be lost across deploys.)
  - Sübut: app/api/sahibkar/sened/upload/route.ts:50-55; no middleware.ts (ls); grep sahibkar-senedler in app/ returns only the upload route; next.config.ts has no headers/rewrites for uploads
  - Fayllar: app/api/sahibkar/sened/upload/route.ts, features/sahibkar/senedler/queries.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] verifySearchCode (command-palette secret-code unlock) has no rate limiting**
  - Detal: features/sahibkar/secret-code.ts:verifySearchCode does bcrypt.compare against gizli_kod_hash (or string-compares the default '7733') with NO getAttemptStatus/recordFailure rate limiting, unlike verifyPin and verifyPinOrCode which are rate-limited (5/lockout). It is invoked from the global command palette for any 4-8 digit input (command-palette-body.tsx:131-135) and on success establishes a full PIN session (secret-code.ts:54). A sahibkar-role user (or anyone who has gained a sahibkar session but not the PIN) can brute-force the secret code unthrottled; the default code is only 4 digits ('7733', constants.ts:6). Role gating limits exposure to sahibkar-role accounts, but the bypass of the PIN via an unthrottled short code weakens the lock.
  - Sübut: features/sahibkar/secret-code.ts:22-71 (no rate-limit calls); contrast unified-verify.ts:22-28,74; constants.ts:6 DEFAULT_SECRET_CODE='7733'; command-palette-body.tsx:131-135
  - Fayllar: features/sahibkar/secret-code.ts, features/sahibkar/constants.ts, components/layout/command-palette-body.tsx

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] Sahibkar mutations gate only on role, never on an active PIN session — defeats the lock when PIN session is expired/absent**
  - Detal: All sahibkar server actions that DO check authorization check only rolAd==="sahibkar" (or rol_id===9), not an active PIN session (getPinSession/touchPinSession). So a logged-in owner whose PIN session has expired or who never verified can still mutate owner data and even change security settings (changePin requires old PIN, but updateSecretCode, updateRecoveryEmail, updateSessionTimeout, toggleSahibkarSetting do not) by calling the action directly. The PIN gate is the section's core protection (e.g. against someone using the owner's unlocked browser after the 15-min auto-lock); skipping it server-side narrows that protection to a UI-only redirect. The backup API is the lone mutation/read path that correctly requires touchPinSession (backup/route.ts:25-28).
  - Sübut: features/sahibkar/settings-actions.ts:88-112 updateSecretCode (only rolAd check, no PIN session), :118-142 updateRecoveryEmail, :53-76 updateSessionTimeout, :18-47 toggleSahibkarSetting; owner-actions.ts no PIN check; only backup/route.ts:25-28 checks PIN
  - Fayllar: features/sahibkar/settings-actions.ts, features/sahibkar/owner-actions.ts

- ⚪ **[low · functional] Security-sensitive settings changes write no audit log**
  - Detal: Changing the secret unlock code (updateSecretCode), recovery email (updateRecoveryEmail), session timeout, audit-log toggle, and protection toggle (toggleSahibkarSetting) — all in settings-actions.ts — do not call safeAuditLog, even though PIN setup/login/lockout do (actions.ts). An attacker who gains a sahibkar session could disable audit_log and/or change the recovery email / secret code with no audit trail. Note toggling audit_log off itself isn't logged.
  - Sübut: features/sahibkar/settings-actions.ts:18-177 (no safeAuditLog calls anywhere); contrast features/sahibkar/actions.ts:66-74,126-136
  - Fayllar: features/sahibkar/settings-actions.ts

- ⚪ **[low · functional] updateSecretCode hashes the secret code with bcrypt cost 10 while PIN uses cost 12 (and the code can be as short as 3 digits)**
  - Detal: settings-actions.ts:97 bcrypt.hash(yeni_kod, 10) vs actions.ts:34/169 bcrypt.hash(pin, 12). The secret code also allows 3 digits (SecretSchema /^\d{3,12}$/, settings-actions.ts:80) — a 3-digit unlock that, combined with the missing rate-limit on verifySearchCode, is trivially brute-forceable for a sahibkar-role account.
  - Sübut: features/sahibkar/settings-actions.ts:80,97; features/sahibkar/actions.ts:34,169
  - Fayllar: features/sahibkar/settings-actions.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 lib/sahibkar/guard.ts:19 getSahibkarState — rol_id===9: Numeric system-role id used as owner gate; signup assigns owner a cloned role with a different auto-increment id, so the real owner fails the gate and is redirected out of the whole section. Should use rolAd==="sahibkar" / lib/auth/role-check.ts isSahibkar(). — _lib/sahibkar/guard.ts:19 vs features/auth/signup-action.ts:100-128 and prisma model roles autoincrement_
- 🔐 features/sahibkar/owner-actions.ts (all 8 mutations): No owner-role check and no PIN-session check; any authenticated tenant user can invoke them directly to mutate owner-only data. requireTenant()+withTenant only require a logged-in session. — _owner-actions.ts:27,87,140,163,211,242,263 (no rolAd); lib/db/with-tenant.ts:27-49_
- 🔐 Static serving of /public/uploads/sahibkar-senedler/*: Owner-only documents are publicly fetchable with no auth/tenant check; only an unguessable UUID protects them. No middleware or protected route exists. — _app/api/sahibkar/sened/upload/route.ts:50-55; no middleware.ts; grep finds no protected serving route_
- 🔐 features/sahibkar/secret-code.ts verifySearchCode: No rate limiting on the secret-code unlock path (PIN path is rate-limited); default code is 4 digits — brute-forceable for a sahibkar-role session. — _secret-code.ts:22-71; constants.ts:6_
- 🔐 Sahibkar settings/owner actions vs PIN session: Mutations check role at most, never an active PIN session, so the auto-lock can be bypassed by directly invoking actions; security settings (secret code, recovery email, audit toggle) are changeable without a PIN session and without audit logging. — _settings-actions.ts:88-142 (no PIN, no audit); only backup/route.ts:25-28 enforces PIN session_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** lib/sahibkar/guard.ts, app/(dashboard)/sahibkar/setup/page.tsx, app/(dashboard)/sahibkar/verify/page.tsx, app/api/sahibkar/backup/route.ts, app/api/sahibkar/sened/upload/route.ts, features/sahibkar/senedler/actions.ts, lib/stealth/actions.ts, features/sahibkar/owner-actions.ts, features/sahibkar/secret-code.ts, features/sahibkar/settings-actions.ts

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** Xeyr

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** The hidden section reads from core ERP tables (satis_sifarisleri, finance_operations, istifadeciler salaries, kontragentler) for profit/maya/salary/balance views; these are read-only aggregations and do not write back, so they do not corrupt balances/stock. Stealth mode is the main cross-module touchpoint: it is correctly display-only (scales freshly-read raw values, never persists) and is intentionally NOT applied in backup/export/API routes, so it poses no data-integrity risk to finance/tax/bank reconciliation. The real cross-module risk is the inverse: the rol_id===9 mis-gate means stealth-mode toggling and the entire owner control surface are unreachable by real owners, and the unguarded owner-actions let non-owners write owner-scoped rows (notes/tasks/branches/snapshots) — branch (filiallar) creation in particular could surface in other modules that consume filiallar.">No new DB schema changes are required; the critical fix is swapping numeric role checks for role-name checks already supported by the existing rol_ad field and lib/auth/role-check.ts.

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) CRITICAL owner-gate fix: replace every rol_id===9 owner check with the role-NAME check (session.user.rol_ad===\"sahibkar\" in route/page contexts, or lib/auth/role-check.ts isSahibkar() inside tenant scope) so genuine owners with cloned role ids pass. Affected: lib/sahibkar/guard.ts:19, setup/page.tsx:17, verify/page.tsx:15, api/sahibkar/backup/route.ts:22, api/sahibkar/sened/upload/route.ts:26, features/sahibkar/senedler/actions.ts:16, lib/stealth/actions.ts:24. (Verify against live signup data that owner sessions carry rol_ad=\"sahibkar\".) 2) Add an owner-role + PIN-session guard to all owner-actions.ts mutations (and ideally to settings-actions): introduce a shared assertSahibkarSession() that checks isSahibkar() AND getPinSession()!=null, and call it at the top of saveNot/deleteNot/saveTapshiriq/updateTaskStatus/saveFilial/takeSnapshot/savePartiya/setSnapshotCron/addTaskComment and the settings mutations. 3) Serve sahibkar documents via an authenticated route handler (e.g. app/api/sahibkar/sened/[id]/route.ts) that enforces owner+PIN+tenant and streams from a non-public dir; stop writing to public/ (also fixes serverless ephemerality). 4) Rate-limit verifySearchCode using the same getAttemptStatus/recordFailure flow as unified-verify; raise default/min code length and hash cost (12) and consider deprecating the 4-digit default. 5) Add safeAuditLog to updateSecretCode/updateRecoveryEmail/updateSessionTimeout/toggleSahibkarSetting (especially when audit_log is toggled off).

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- Sign up a brand-new tenant, log in as the owner, and try to open /sahibkar (or type the secret code in ⌘K search): expected to reach setup/verify; ACTUAL likely redirect to /dashboard because rol_id!==9 — confirms the lockout bug.
- As a non-owner user (kassir/satıcı) in the same tenant, invoke a sahibkar owner action directly (e.g. create an owner note or toggle snapshot cron) and verify it succeeds — confirms missing role guard on owner-actions.ts.
- Upload a document in /sahibkar/senedler as owner, copy the resulting /uploads/sahibkar-senedler/<uuid> URL, open it in a logged-out incognito window — verify it downloads without auth.
- In ⌘K, repeatedly type wrong 4-digit codes as a sahibkar-role user — verify no lockout occurs on the secret-code path.
- Set a 3-digit secret code via /sahibkar/ayarlar, then verify it is accepted by the unlock flow (weak code allowed).

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Confirm at runtime that a freshly signed-up tenant owner's session.user.rol_id is NOT 9 (it should be the cloned roles.id) and that rol_ad is "sahibkar" — this proves the rol_id===9 gate locks real owners out. Reproduce by signing up a new tenant and hitting /sahibkar.
- Confirm a non-owner (e.g. kassir) session can successfully invoke the owner-actions server actions directly (e.g. saveNot/deleteNot/setSnapshotCron) and persist data, since no role check exists.
- Confirm /uploads/sahibkar-senedler/<uuid>.<ext> is fetchable in the browser without an authenticated session (static asset, no guard).
- Confirm verifySearchCode can be called repeatedly without lockout (no rate limit) for a sahibkar-role account.
- Confirm whether the very first/seed tenant happens to have cloned sahibkar role id == 9 (would mask the bug only for that one tenant).

## 16. Ayarlar

**Xülasə:** The Ayarlar module is split between a newer, well-secured layer (features/ayarlar/* — most actions call requireAyarActionPerm and write critical audit, encrypt secrets, mask sensitive export/import, and use $transaction for plan changes) and an older, UNGUARDED layer (features/ayar/actions.ts and a handful of features/ayarlar action files) that relies solely on tenant scoping plus the page-level route gate. The central, systemic finding: the /ayarlar route gate only protects PAGE navigation (it keys on the x-pathname header set by proxy.ts), NOT the server-action endpoints, which are globally invokable from any page the user can reach. Therefore the unguarded mutations — most critically saveRolePerms (privilege escalation), resetUserPassword/changeUserRole/createUser/deleteUser (account takeover/role abuse), the maliye-hesabı, warehouse, discount-threshold, risk-rule, POS-pricing, qiymet-qayda/icaze, and subscription-plan saves — can be triggered without a per-action authorization check. Tenant scoping (lib/db/prisma.ts $extends) correctly blocks ALL cross-tenant tampering, and system roles are protected in depth, so the exposure is intra-tenant privilege/integrity rather than data isolation. Additional concrete bugs: an account holding a non-zero balance can be deactivated through the edit dialog, bypassing the deleteMaliyyeHesab balance blocker (which is itself not wired to any UI); previewSubscriptionDowngrade reads non-existent plan fields (max_isci/max_anbar) so it never reports user/warehouse overages; discount thresholds aren't validated for monotonic ordering (free_max=100 silently disables approval); changeSubscriptionPlan lacks audit and uses a meblegh that diverges from the displayed yearly price; and the ayar.* permission codes the design depends on (except ayar.qiymet) are never seeded, making the intended delegation non-functional and the route gate effectively admin-only. Strongest fix priorities: (1) add requireAyarActionPerm guards to every unguarded mutating action, (2) seed the missing ayar.* permissions, (3) enforce the balance check on account deactivation, (4) fix the downgrade-preview field names and add validation to thresholds. Live verification is needed to demonstrate the direct-action bypass and the balance-deactivation effect on downstream reports.
