**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ Multi-tenant scoping is enforced on every ayarlar mutation via withTenant() + the Prisma $extends tenant-filter, which injects sahibkar_id into where for read/update/delete/upsert and into data for create. Cross-tenant tampering is blocked even on actions that pass raw ids (e.g. anbarlar.update({where:{id}})). — _lib/db/prisma.ts:61-93; every action file wraps DB work in withTenant() (lib/db/with-tenant.ts:17)_
- ✅ Newer features/ayarlar/* actions correctly guard with requireAyarActionPerm and write critical audit entries: api-keys (generate/revoke/delete), backup (request/restore), mp-komisyon, qiymet-tipi, quick-create-price-type, xerc-kateqoriya, borc-avto, telegram, and the actions.ts export/import/timeline/diff/dashboard/rotateAllApiKeys/previewSubscriptionDowngrade. — _features/ayarlar/api-keys-actions.ts:37,87,107; backup-actions.ts:19,64; mp-komisyon-actions.ts:23; qiymet-tipi-actions.ts:29; borc-avto-actions.ts:21; features/telegram/actions.ts:51_
- ✅ Maliyyə hesabı edit/create writes audit and validates ownership before update (before-row fetch scoped to sahibkar_id); opening balance only editable on create, preventing balance desync. — _features/ayarlar/hesab-actions.ts:38-66 (audit) and components/hesab-dialog.tsx:168-176 (acilis_qaligi only when !hesab)_
- ✅ Role permission editing (saveRolePerms/saveRole/deleteRole/cloneRole) re-validates that the target role belongs to the tenant and is non-system (sistem:false), so system role templates cannot be mutated even by direct call; writes detailed audit with added/removed diff and invalidates role-perms cache. — _features/ayar/actions.ts:152-184 (saveRolePerms), 219-241 (saveRole), 392-398 (deleteRole)_
- ✅ Telegram chat_id is encrypted at rest (encryptString) and masked in audit + export; sensitive groups (telegram/email/smtp/bank/marketplace) are masked on exportSettings and skipped on importSettings. — _features/telegram/actions.ts:58-68; features/ayarlar/actions.ts:91-101,152,170-176,203_
- ✅ computeFormulaPrice guards against null/zero/non-finite base and zero divisor in rounding, returning null+explain rather than NaN/Infinity. — _features/ayarlar/qiymet-formula.ts:28-30,52-54_
- ✅ deleteUser blocks self-deletion and soft-deletes (aktiv:false) preserving audit; deleteRole blocks deletion when users still attached; deleteFilial/deleteFilialAnbar block on dependent anbarlar/stok and soft-delete when history exists. — _features/ayar/actions.ts:922,927-934 (deleteUser); 396-398 (deleteRole); 485,1073-1082 (filial/anbar blockers)_
- ✅ Bank accounts settings page handles empty state and null balances, computes active totals only from aktiv accounts, and uses shared formatMoney; account balance is read directly from maliye_hesablari.qaliq (settings list, not a recomputed report). — _app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/bank/page.tsx:52,58-60,111-123_
- ✅ changeSubscriptionPlan deactivates the prior active subscription and creates the new one inside one prisma.$transaction (atomic). — _features/ayarlar/abune-actions.ts:26-42_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🔴 **[critical · permission] saveRolePerms has no caller permission check — privilege escalation via direct server-action invocation**
  - Detal: saveRolePerms (assign arbitrary permissions to a custom role) only wraps work in withTenant() and validates the target role belongs to the tenant and is non-system. It does NOT call requireAyarActionPerm/requireAyarOwner. The /ayarlar route gate in app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx only protects PAGE navigation keyed on x-pathname (set to req.nextUrl.pathname in proxy.ts:11). A Next.js server action POSTs to the URL of whatever page invoked it, so a non-admin user on an allowed page (e.g. /dashboard, a BYPASS_PREFIXES path) who issues a direct request to this action's endpoint executes it with no authorization check. Because the action can grant ANY permission id to the user's own custom role, this is a privilege-escalation path to admin-equivalent rights. The whole features/ayar/actions.ts file (the older 'ayar' feature) is unguarded, unlike the newer features/ayarlar/* files.
  - Sübut: features/ayar/actions.ts:137-193 (no requireAyar* call); contrast features/ayarlar/api-keys-actions.ts:37; gate is page-only at app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx:116-122 + proxy.ts:11
  - Fayllar: features/ayar/actions.ts, app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx, proxy.ts, lib/auth/route-gate.ts

- 🟠 **[high · permission] User-management server actions (createUser, resetUserPassword, changeUserRole, deleteUser, toggleUserField, setUserSessionLimit, updateUserIpRestriction) have no caller permission guard**
  - Detal: All user-account mutations in features/ayar/actions.ts rely solely on withTenant() + tenant scope; none call requireAyarActionPerm. resetUserPassword can set any tenant user's password (account takeover), changeUserRole can move a user to a higher-privilege role, createUser can mint a new admin-role user. Same direct-action-invocation bypass as saveRolePerms: the route gate does not cover the action endpoint, only the page path. Tenant scope prevents cross-tenant abuse but not intra-tenant privilege abuse by a non-admin.
  - Sübut: features/ayar/actions.ts:721 (createUser), 856 (changeUserRole), 887 (resetUserPassword), 916 (deleteUser), 821 (toggleUserField) — none call requireAyar*
  - Fayllar: features/ayar/actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Account can be deactivated with a non-zero balance via the edit dialog, bypassing the balance blocker**
  - Detal: deleteMaliyyeHesab enforces that an account with |qaliq|>0.01 cannot be deactivated (returns blockers via findAccountBlockers). But that action is NOT wired to any UI component (grep finds no .tsx caller). The only reachable deactivation path is HesabDialog -> saveMaliyyeHesab, whose update branch sets aktiv directly from the checkbox with NO balance check (hesab-actions.ts:43-58). So a user can untick 'Aktivdir' on an account holding money. The bank settings page totals exclude inactive accounts (bank/page.tsx:52), so the balance silently disappears from active cash/bank totals while the row and its finance_operations still exist — an integrity/reporting hazard the deleteMaliyyeHesab guard was meant to prevent.
  - Sübut: features/ayarlar/hesab-actions.ts:43-58 (no balance guard on update); 124-135 (guard exists only in deleteMaliyyeHesab); grep: deleteMaliyyeHesab has no .tsx caller; bank/page.tsx:52
  - Fayllar: features/ayarlar/hesab-actions.ts, features/ayarlar/components/hesab-dialog.tsx, app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/bank/page.tsx

- 🟡 **[medium · functional] previewSubscriptionDowngrade reads non-existent plan fields max_isci/max_anbar — user and warehouse overages never reported**
  - Detal: previewSubscriptionDowngrade casts the plan as {max_isci?, max_mehsul?, max_anbar?} but the abune_planlari model defines max_istifadeci, max_mehsul, max_mesaj_aylıq — there is NO max_isci or max_anbar column. So isciLimit and anbarLimit are always undefined→0, their 'limit>0' guards never fire, and the function silently reports zero user/warehouse overage on any downgrade (only max_mehsul works). The `as unknown as {...}` cast suppresses the TS error that would otherwise catch this. Currently the function is also not wired into any UI (dead but broken).
  - Sübut: features/ayarlar/actions.ts:352-363 vs prisma/schema.prisma abune_planlari (max_istifadeci/max_mehsul/max_mesaj_ayl_q only)
  - Fayllar: features/ayarlar/actions.ts, prisma/schema.prisma

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] changeSubscriptionPlan: no permission guard, no audit, and ayar.abune is unused while previewSubscriptionDowngrade is guarded by it**
  - Detal: The actual mutation changeSubscriptionPlan (abune-actions.ts) has neither requireAyarActionPerm nor an audit() write, even though access-guard defines ayar.abune and the read-only previewSubscriptionDowngrade IS guarded with it (actions.ts:340). Inconsistent: the preview is more protected than the binding plan change. Reachable via PlanChangeDialog and via direct action call by non-admin. No audit means plan switches are untracked.
  - Sübut: features/ayarlar/abune-actions.ts:14-50 (no perm/audit) vs features/ayarlar/actions.ts:340 (preview guarded by ayar.abune)
  - Fayllar: features/ayarlar/abune-actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] Discount-threshold save (satis-tesdiq) and risk-rules/pos-qiymet/qiymet-icaze/qiymet-qayda saves lack permission guards**
  - Detal: These actions change settings that directly steer the sales/approval and pricing data flow but have no requireAyarActionPerm: satis-tesdiq page inline `save`->setDiscountThresholds (page.tsx:14-32, satis-tesdiq.ts:61 no guard); saveRiskRules (risk-rules-actions.ts:22, calls setRiskRules with no guard — controls maya-altı/borc-limit approve/block behavior); savePosPriceSettings (pos-qiymet-actions.ts:11); saveRoleAllowedTiers (qiymet-icaze-actions.ts:8); saveQiymetQayda/deleteQiymetQayda/bulkApplyQiymetQaydalari (qiymet-qayda-actions.ts:67,112,133). Note: setDiscountThresholds and saveRiskRules DO write audit, but the price ones (qiymet-qayda, pos-qiymet, qiymet-icaze) write none. Same route-gate-only protection; direct action invocation bypasses it.
  - Sübut: app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/satis-tesdiq/page.tsx:14-29; features/ayarlar/risk-rules-actions.ts:22; pos-qiymet-actions.ts:11; qiymet-icaze-actions.ts:8; qiymet-qayda-actions.ts:67,112,133
  - Fayllar: app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/satis-tesdiq/page.tsx, features/ayarlar/risk-rules-actions.ts, features/ayarlar/pos-qiymet-actions.ts, features/ayarlar/qiymet-icaze-actions.ts, features/ayarlar/qiymet-qayda-actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Discount thresholds are not validated for monotonic ordering — free_max can exceed all tiers, disabling approval**
  - Detal: setDiscountThresholds clamps each value to 0..100 independently but never checks free_max <= medium_max <= high_max <= admin_max. An admin (or, per the guard bug above, any user reaching the action) can set free_max=100, which makes every discount fall in the 'sərbəst — no approval' band, silently neutralising the entire discount-approval workflow that downstream sales rely on.
  - Sübut: features/ayarlar/satis-tesdiq.ts:61-91 (no ordering validation); page schema only does min(0).max(100) per field (satis-tesdiq/page.tsx:16-21)
  - Fayllar: features/ayarlar/satis-tesdiq.ts, app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/satis-tesdiq/page.tsx

- ⚪ **[low · permission] saveWarehouse (ayarlar/anbar) writes no audit and has no permission guard**
  - Detal: saveWarehouse creates/updates anbarlar (a stock-bearing entity) with only withTenant scoping — no requireAyarActionPerm and no audit() call, unlike the parallel createFilialAnbar/updateFilialAnbar in features/ayar/actions.ts. Tenant scope is intact (update where:{id} gets sahibkar_id injected), so it is not a cross-tenant issue, but warehouse changes affecting stock visibility go untracked and ungated.
  - Sübut: features/ayarlar/anbar-actions.ts:22-52 (no requireAyar*, no audit)
  - Fayllar: features/ayarlar/anbar-actions.ts

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] changeSubscriptionPlan stored meblegh for illik differs from the price shown in the dialog**
  - Detal: For an illik (yearly) plan with null illik_qiymet, changeSubscriptionPlan stores meblegh = ayl_q_qiymet (one month), while the PlanChangeDialog displays the yearly price as ayl_q_qiymet * 10 (plan-change-dialog.tsx:78). The amount the user sees and the amount persisted to abuneler.meblegh diverge when illik_qiymet is unset.
  - Sübut: features/ayarlar/abune-actions.ts:39 (meblegh = plan.illik_qiymet ?? plan.ayl_q_qiymet) vs features/ayarlar/components/plan-change-dialog.tsx:78 (ayl_q_qiymet * 10)
  - Fayllar: features/ayarlar/abune-actions.ts, features/ayarlar/components/plan-change-dialog.tsx

- ⚪ **[low · permission] Defined ayar permission codes (ayar.idare/api_key/abune/backup/kanal/rol_idare/view) are never seeded; only ayar.qiymet exists**
  - Detal: access-guard.ts defines an AyarPerm union and route-gate keys /ayarlar on anyOf:['ayar.view','ayar.idare'], but no seed/migration creates icazeler rows for ayar.idare, ayar.api_key, ayar.abune, ayar.backup, ayar.kanal, ayar.rol_idare, or ayar.view — only ayar.qiymet is seeded (2026-06-03 migration). Consequence: non-admin custom roles can never be granted ayar.* (except qiymet), so the route gate is effectively admin-only (fails closed — good), AND the guarded requireAyarActionPerm checks for those codes can only ever be satisfied by the sahibkar/admin/owner bypass, making the intended delegation (e.g. give backup to a non-admin) impossible. requireAyarOwner is also defined but never called anywhere.
  - Sübut: features/ayarlar/access-guard.ts:24-49; scripts/migrations/2026-06-03-quick-create-permissions.sql:18 (only ayar.qiymet); lib/permission-info.ts:469 (only ayar.idare description, not a seed); grep finds no seed for other ayar.* codes
  - Fayllar: features/ayarlar/access-guard.ts, scripts/migrations/2026-06-03-quick-create-permissions.sql, lib/auth/route-gate.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 Can a non-admin tenant user invoke ayarlar mutations directly (bypassing the page route gate)?: The /ayarlar route gate in app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx checks gateRoute(x-pathname,...) — x-pathname is the page path set by proxy.ts. Server actions are global POST endpoints invoked under whatever page path the user is on, so the gate does not protect the action itself. Unguarded actions in features/ayar/actions.ts (saveRolePerms, createUser, resetUserPassword, changeUserRole, deleteUser, saveCompany, saveFilial, etc.), features/ayar/messages-actions.ts, hesab-actions.ts, anbar-actions.ts, qiymet-qayda-actions.ts, qiymet-icaze-actions.ts, pos-qiymet-actions.ts, risk-rules-actions.ts, abune-actions.ts, and the satis-tesdiq inline save run with no per-action authorization. saveRolePerms is privilege escalation; resetUserPassword/changeUserRole are account takeover. (Partial mitigation: tenant scope blocks cross-tenant; and since ayar.view/idare are not seeded, a non-admin can't reach the pages — but the action endpoints remain callable.) — _app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx:116-122; proxy.ts:11; features/ayar/actions.ts:137 (saveRolePerms, no guard) vs features/ayarlar/api-keys-actions.ts:37 (guarded)_
- 🔐 Are the intended ayar.* delegation permissions grantable to non-admin roles?: Only ayar.qiymet is seeded as an icazeler row. ayar.idare/api_key/abune/backup/kanal/rol_idare/view have no seed, so requireAyarActionPerm for those is satisfiable only by the sahibkar/admin/owner role-name bypass. Backend fails closed (secure) but the delegation model in access-guard.ts is non-functional, and the route gate's ayar.view/ayar.idare allow-list can never match a non-admin. — _scripts/migrations/2026-06-03-quick-create-permissions.sql:18; features/ayarlar/access-guard.ts:54-63; lib/auth/route-gate.ts:56_
- 🔐 Are system role templates protected from modification?: No problem — defense in depth is correct here. RolePermissionsMatrix gates editing on isSystem in the UI (role-permissions-matrix.tsx:85,116,491,506) and saveRolePerms/saveRole re-validate sistem:false on the backend (ayar/actions.ts:152-155,219-222), so system roles cannot be mutated even via direct call. — _features/ayar/actions.ts:152-155; features/ayar/components/role-permissions-matrix.tsx:85,506_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** features/ayar/actions.ts, features/ayar/messages-actions.ts, features/ayarlar/hesab-actions.ts, features/ayarlar/anbar-actions.ts, features/ayarlar/qiymet-qayda-actions.ts, features/ayarlar/qiymet-icaze-actions.ts, features/ayarlar/pos-qiymet-actions.ts, features/ayarlar/risk-rules-actions.ts, features/ayarlar/abune-actions.ts, features/ayarlar/satis-tesdiq.ts, app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/satis-tesdiq/page.tsx, features/ayarlar/actions.ts, features/ayarlar/components/hesab-dialog.tsx, scripts/migrations/ (new ayar permission seed)

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** Seed icazeler rows for the ayar.* permission codes referenced by access-guard.ts and route-gate.ts (ayar.idare, ayar.api_key, ayar.abune, ayar.backup, ayar.kanal, ayar.rol_idare, and ayar.view if intended) so the route gate and requireAyarActionPerm delegation can actually function for non-admin roles; or remove ayar.view from the route gate allow-list if read delegation is not wanted.; No schema change strictly required for the bugs, but note abune_planlari has no max_isci/max_anbar columns — previewSubscriptionDowngrade must read max_istifadeci (and there is no per-warehouse limit field to compare against).

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** Ayarlar is a config hub whose values feed many modules, so the unguarded mutations have outsized blast radius. (1) saveRolePerms feeds lib/auth permissions for the whole app — escalation here grants access to maliyye/anbar/pos/etc. (2) setDiscountThresholds (ticaret qrup) and setRiskRules (maya-altı/borc-limit approve|block) directly steer the sales-approval and tesdiq_telep workflow in features/ticaret — neutralising them weakens financial controls org-wide. (3) saveMarketplaceCommission (mp_komisyon) and marketplace defaults feed marketplace payout/margin math (finance_marketplace_payments) — though that action IS guarded. (4) qiymet-tipi/qiymet-qayda/pos-qiymet/qiymet-icaze formulas drive product pricing surfaced in POS, new-sale, product card, and marketplace — wrong/ungated edits propagate to every sale price. (5) Deactivating a maliye_hesabi with a balance (via the unguarded update path) desyncs cash/bank balances consumed by lib/balance/account-balance and maliyye reports. The tenant extension prevents cross-tenant leakage, so the risk is intra-tenant privilege/integrity, not data isolation.

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) Authorization: add `const p = await requireAyarActionPerm(...); if(!p.ok) return {ok:false,error:p.error}` at the top of every mutating server action in features/ayar/actions.ts (use ayar.rol_idare for role/user actions, ayar.idare otherwise), features/ayar/messages-actions.ts, hesab-actions.ts (ayar.idare), anbar-actions.ts (ayar.idare), qiymet-qayda-actions.ts + qiymet-icaze-actions.ts + pos-qiymet-actions.ts (ayar.qiymet/ayar.idare), risk-rules-actions.ts (ayar.idare), abune-actions.ts changeSubscriptionPlan (ayar.abune), and the satis-tesdiq page inline save (ayar.idare). 2) Seed the missing ayar.* permission codes (migration) so these guards and the route gate are grantable; otherwise they remain admin-only. 3) hesab-actions.ts: in the saveMaliyyeHesab update branch, when aktiv is being set false, run the same |qaliq|>0.01 balance check (or reuse findAccountBlockers) used in deleteMaliyyeHesab, and/or wire deleteMaliyyeHesab into HesabDialog as the deactivation path. 4) satis-tesdiq.ts: validate free_max<=medium_max<=high_max<=admin_max and reject otherwise. 5) actions.ts previewSubscriptionDowngrade: read plan.max_istifadeci (drop the broken max_isci/max_anbar casts; there is no per-warehouse plan limit). 6) abune-actions.ts: add audit() on changeSubscriptionPlan and reconcile the illik meblegh with the dialog's displayed yearly price. 7) Add audit() to saveWarehouse, qiymet-qayda, pos-qiymet, qiymet-icaze saves for parity with the rest of the module.

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- As a non-admin custom role (e.g. 'menecer'), from an allowed page (/dashboard), craft a direct POST to the saveRolePerms server action granting your own rol_id all permission ids; reload and confirm whether you now see admin-only modules — expected (after fix): rejected with permission error.
- As a non-admin, invoke resetUserPassword for another tenant user's id directly; confirm whether the password changes — expected (after fix): blocked.
- Create a bank account with opening balance 500 AZN, post a movement so qaliq>0, then open the edit dialog and untick 'Aktivdir' and save; confirm the account deactivates with a non-zero balance and disappears from bank/page totals (current bug) vs. is blocked (after fix).
- On satis-tesdiq, set free_max=100 (others lower) and save; create a sale with a 40% discount and confirm whether it goes through with no approval (current) vs. is rejected by ordering validation (after fix).
- Wire/observe previewSubscriptionDowngrade for a tenant with more active users than the target plan's max_istifadeci; confirm it currently reports zero user overage (bug) and reports correctly after reading max_istifadeci.
- Change subscription plan and check /ayarlar audit timeline — confirm a plan-change audit entry appears (currently missing).
- Attempt to edit a system role's permission matrix via direct saveRolePerms call — confirm it is rejected (sistem:false guard working).

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Confirm a non-admin authenticated user can actually invoke an unguarded ayarlar server action (e.g. saveRolePerms or resetUserPassword) by replaying its POST from a permitted page — this proves the route-gate-only protection is bypassable end-to-end (static analysis strongly indicates yes, but requires a live request with a forged/copied action id).
- Verify whether deactivating an account with a non-zero balance via HesabDialog actually removes its balance from downstream balance services / reports (lib/balance/account-balance) — bank/page totals exclude it; need to confirm other modules' active-account filters behave the same.
- Confirm at runtime that prisma.findUnique({where:{id}}) on tenant models with sahibkar_id injected does not throw under Prisma 6.19 (pattern is used widely, e.g. qiymet-tipi-actions.ts:54, xerc-kateqoriya-actions.ts:41 — appears supported, but execute once to be sure).
- Confirm the seed/DB state in the live environment: whether ayar.idare/api_key/abune/backup/kanal/rol_idare/view exist as icazeler rows (repo has no seed for them) — if absent, non-admin delegation is impossible and the route gate is admin-only.

---

## 17. İcazələr / Rollar / İstifadəçilər

**Xülasə:** Scope 17 (İcazələr/Rollar/İstifadəçilər) has solid tenant scoping and a clean permission catalog, but the enforcement model has two critical holes. (1) The entire role/user management action file features/ayar/actions.ts (saveRolePerms, saveRole, createUser, changeUserRole, resetUserPassword, deleteUser, deleteRole, toggleUserField, etc.) performs NO permission check — only session+tenant. The central route-gate in app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx only gates page navigation, not server-action execution, so any authenticated low-privilege user can invoke these actions directly to grant themselves permissions, create admins, reset passwords, or take over accounts. The repo even ships the correct guard (requireAyarActionPerm, used everywhere in features/ayarlar/*) but this file never imports it. (2) Privileged access is decided by substring-matching the user-controlled role NAME (rolAd.includes('admin'|'owner'|'sahibkar')) across route-gate, all access-guards, and the maya/profit helpers; since role names have no blocklist (saveRole), a custom role named '…admin…' bypasses every gate in every module. Secondary issues: the qiymet-icaze price-tier action is also unguarded (high), most user/role admin mutations are not audited (high), and the /ayarlar pages rely solely on the layout gate without calling the available requireAyarOwner() (medium). What works well: queries are correctly tenant-scoped, system/template roles are read-only and ownership-checked, role-perm writes are atomic with cache busting, deactivated users are blocked at login, and the sibling vezife/team settings actions ARE properly permission-guarded. The fix is mechanical and pattern-aligned: add requireAyarActionPerm to each ayar action, switch privileged detection to an explicit flag/exact-name set, and add audit calls. Two assertions (action reachability and name-bypass) should be confirmed with a live non-admin session.

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** lib/auth/route-gate.ts; lib/auth/types.ts; lib/auth/permissions.ts; lib/auth/get-permissions.ts; lib/auth/role-check.ts; lib/auth/finance-permissions.ts; lib/permission-modules.ts; lib/permission-info.ts (header + sensitive-code scan); app/api/refresh-perms/route.ts; app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx; app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/layout.tsx; app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/rollar/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/rollar/[id]/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/istifadeci/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/vezife/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/team/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/qiymet-icaze/page.tsx; features/ayar/actions.ts (full); features/ayar/queries.ts (roles/users sections); features/ayar/components/role-editor.tsx; features/ayar/components/role-permissions-matrix.tsx (wiring grep); features/ayar/components/user-detail-panel.tsx (wiring grep); features/ayar/components/user-action-dialogs.tsx (wiring grep); features/ayarlar/access-guard.ts; features/ayarlar/qiymet-icaze.ts; features/ayarlar/qiymet-icaze-actions.ts; features/iscilier/access-guard.ts (requireHrActionPerm); features/iscilier/vezife-actions.ts; features/team/actions.ts (guard grep); lib/db/tenant-context.ts; lib/db/with-tenant.ts; proxy.ts; auth.ts (session rol_ad + login aktiv filter); lib/audit/log.ts (signature)

**Əhatə boşluqları:** lib/permission-info.ts read only header + sensitive-code grep (file is ~510 lines); did not enumerate every code's text — it is descriptive UI metadata, not an enforcement path.; Did not deep-read filial cross-visibility enforcement consumers (saveFilialUserPerm/toggleFilialGorunush write the flags but where those flags are READ/enforced across modules was out of this scope).; features/ayar/components/user-action-dialogs.tsx and role-permissions-matrix.tsx reviewed via grep for action wiring only, not full UI logic.; app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/team/log/page.tsx (team message/audit log visibility) not opened.; Did not verify the rol_icazeleri / roles / istifadeciler models are in TENANT_MODELS (lib/db/tenant-models.ts) — tenant scoping for these actions relies on explicit sahibkar_id where-clauses in code, which were present, but the automatic Prisma-extension filter coverage for these specific models was not separately confirmed.; auth.ts JWT/session callbacks read only around rol_ad/aktiv lines; full session-refresh-on-role-change behavior not traced.

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ Central route-gate is wired into the dashboard layout on every render and feeds pathname via middleware — _app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx:116-122 calls gateRoute(pathname, ...) and redirect('/icaze-yox'); proxy.ts:11 sets x-pathname header consumed at layout.tsx:118_
- ✅ Role/user queries are correctly tenant-scoped — no cross-tenant leak of roles or users — _features/ayar/queries.ts:313-322 getRoles where sahibkar_id+sistem:false; :324-335 getRoleDetail where {id, sahibkar_id}; :292-305 getIstifadeciDetail where {id, sahibkar_id}_
- ✅ saveRolePerms / saveRole / cloneRole / deleteRole correctly reject system (template) roles and enforce tenant ownership — _features/ayar/actions.ts:152-155 (sistem:false + sahibkar_id), :219-222, :356-360, :392-395_
- ✅ Role permission write is atomic (delete+create in one $transaction) and busts the per-role cache so changes apply immediately — _features/ayar/actions.ts:164-170 prisma.$transaction([deleteMany,createMany]); :184 revalidateTag(`role-perms:${rolId}`,'max')_
- ✅ Permission codes are NOT stored in the session cookie (size-safe); loaded per-request from DB with React cache + 5-min unstable_cache — _lib/auth/types.ts:4-8 comment; lib/auth/get-permissions.ts:13-17; lib/auth/permissions.ts:45-60_
- ✅ deleteUser is a soft-delete (aktiv:false) and deactivated users are blocked at login — _features/ayar/actions.ts:927-935 sets aktiv:false/deaktiv_tarix; auth.ts:67 authorize where {email, aktiv:true}_
- ✅ Vezife (job-title) mutations under /ayarlar/vezife ARE permission-guarded — _features/iscilier/vezife-actions.ts:20-21,67-68,104-105 requireHrActionPerm('isci.idare') before each mutation_
- ✅ Team-settings mutations under /ayarlar/team ARE permission-guarded — _features/team/actions.ts:642 toggleTeamAyar -> requireTeamOrgAdmin(); :37 createChannel -> requireTeamActionPerm('team.kanal_yarat')_
- ✅ A proper, reusable action-level guard exists and is applied across most /ayarlar/* settings actions — _features/ayarlar/access-guard.ts:54-63 requireAyarActionPerm; used in features/ayarlar/actions.ts:32,81,139,240,273,303,340,395 and api-keys/backup/telegram/etc._
- ✅ changeUserRole validates the target role belongs to the tenant (or is a system template) before assigning — _features/ayar/actions.ts:863-870 roles.findFirst where {id, OR:[sahibkar_id, sistem:true]}_
- ✅ saveRolePerms and saveRole/create write audit entries — _features/ayar/actions.ts:171-180 audit('icaze_dəyişdir','rol',...); :225-229 and :237-240 audit('yenile'/'yarat','rol',...)_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🔴 **[critical · permission] Role & user management server actions have NO permission guard — any authenticated tenant user can manage roles/users (backend bypass)**
  - Detal: Every mutation in features/ayar/actions.ts (saveRolePerms, saveRole, createRoleFromTemplate, cloneRole, deleteRole, createUser, updateUserProfile, toggleUserField [incl. aktiv deactivation], changeUserRole, resetUserPassword, deleteUser, updateUserIpRestriction, setUserSessionLimit, filial isolation/visibility/user-perm toggles) only wraps work in withTenant()/requireTenant(), which require an authenticated session but check ZERO permission. The file imports no guard (no requireAyarActionPerm, no isAyarPrivileged, no rol check) — confirmed by grep of imports (only prisma, withTenant, requireTenant, password, audit). The ONLY protection is the route-gate in app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx, which gates page NAVIGATION (it reads x-pathname and redirects). Server actions are POST requests whose body executes independently of the layout render, so a low-privilege user (e.g. Kassir) can invoke these actions directly (e.g. via a crafted request or from any page their UI is on) and: create a new admin user, reset any user's password, change their own rol_id to a higher role, grant their role every permission via saveRolePerms, or deactivate the owner. This is a full privilege-escalation / account-takeover path. Contrast with the SAME repo's correct pattern: features/ayarlar/actions.ts and features/iscilier/vezife-actions.ts call requireAyarActionPerm/requireHrActionPerm at the top of every action.
  - Sübut: features/ayar/actions.ts:137 saveRolePerms, :202 saveRole, :290 createRoleFromTemplate, :347 cloneRole, :386 deleteRole, :721 createUser, :821 toggleUserField, :856 changeUserRole, :887 resetUserPassword, :916 deleteUser — none call a permission guard; imports at :3-9 contain no access-guard. Correct pattern shown at features/ayarlar/actions.ts:32 and features/iscilier/vezife-actions.ts:20.
  - Fayllar: features/ayar/actions.ts, features/ayarlar/access-guard.ts, app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx

- 🔴 **[critical · permission] Privilege escalation via custom role name containing 'admin'/'owner'/'sahibkar' (substring bypass)**
  - Detal: Route-gate and all *-privileged checks decide full-access by substring-matching the role NAME: gateRoute lowercases rolAd and returns allowed:true if it includes 'sahibkar'|'admin'|'owner'. The same substring test backs isAyarPrivileged (sahibkar/admin/owner/direktor), isHrPrivileged, canViewCost/Profit (PRIVILEGED_ROLES regex /sahibkar|owner|admin|direktor|muhasib/i), and role-check.ts. Role names are fully user-controlled: saveRole RoleSchema only enforces ad length 2-50 (no blocklist), and rol_ad on the session is taken verbatim from roles.ad (auth.ts:178). Therefore a tenant can create a custom role literally named e.g. 'Anbar admin' or 'owner-köməkçi', assign a low-trust employee to it, and that employee bypasses EVERY route gate and every privileged-helper check (sees maya/profit, full /ayarlar, /maliyye, audit, etc.). Combined with bug #1, this is also reachable by self-service. Matching should be by exact canonical role identity (e.g. rol_id===1 / a flag / exact name set), not substring.
  - Sübut: lib/auth/route-gate.ts:93-96 r.includes('sahibkar')||r.includes('admin')||r.includes('owner'); features/ayarlar/access-guard.ts:35-37; lib/auth/finance-permissions.ts:12 PRIVILEGED_ROLES; features/ayar/actions.ts:195-200 RoleSchema (no name blocklist); auth.ts:178 rol_ad:user.roles?.ad
  - Fayllar: lib/auth/route-gate.ts, features/ayarlar/access-guard.ts, lib/auth/finance-permissions.ts, features/iscilier/access-guard.ts, features/ayar/actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · permission] qiymet-icaze action (which price tiers a role sees in POS) has no permission guard**
  - Detal: saveRoleAllowedTiers (the only mutation for /ayarlar/qiymet-icaze) validates rolId and tier whitelist, then calls setRoleAllowedTiers which only does withTenant/requireTenant. No requireAyarActionPerm / privileged check. Any authenticated tenant user can change which price tiers (cost-adjacent: topdan/partnyor/vip/min_satis) any role is allowed to view in POS — re-enabling sensitive pricing visibility for low-trust roles. setRoleAllowedTiers is tenant-scoped so it cannot cross tenants, but within the tenant it is an unprotected config-mutation and an info-exposure lever.
  - Sübut: features/ayarlar/qiymet-icaze-actions.ts:8-20 saveRoleAllowedTiers — no guard; features/ayarlar/qiymet-icaze.ts:89-114 setRoleAllowedTiers only withTenant/requireTenant. Page: app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/qiymet-icaze/page.tsx
  - Fayllar: features/ayarlar/qiymet-icaze-actions.ts, features/ayarlar/qiymet-icaze.ts, app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/qiymet-icaze/page.tsx

- 🟠 **[high · permission] Most user/role mutations are not written to the audit log**
  - Detal: Only password change (line 125), role-permission change (171), and role create/update (225/237) write audit entries. The following high-sensitivity admin mutations write NO audit record: createUser, changeUserRole (privilege grant), resetUserPassword (admin resetting another user's password), deleteUser (deactivation), toggleUserField (incl. aktiv on/off, 2FA, butun_filiallari_gor), updateUserProfile, createRoleFromTemplate, cloneRole, deleteRole, updateUserIpRestriction, setUserSessionLimit, and all filial isolation/visibility/user-perm toggles. For an ERP with a security/audit module, 'who created/disabled which user and who changed whose role' must be traceable. Confirmed by grep: audit() appears only 4 times in the file.
  - Sübut: features/ayar/actions.ts: audit() only at :125,:171,:225,:237 — absent from createUser(721), changeUserRole(856), resetUserPassword(887), deleteUser(916), toggleUserField(821), deleteRole(386), cloneRole(347), createRoleFromTemplate(290)
  - Fayllar: features/ayar/actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] /ayarlar pages have no page-level owner guard — protection relies solely on the layout route-gate**
  - Detal: The codebase provides requireAyarOwner() (features/ayarlar/access-guard.ts:42-49) for exactly this, but the ayarlar layout (app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/layout.tsx) and the role/user/qiymet-icaze pages do not call it. They depend entirely on app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx route-gate, whose /ayarlar rule only requires anyOf ['ayar.view','ayar.idare'] (plus the substring sahibkar/admin/owner bypass). Consequences: (a) a role granted just 'ayar.view' passes the gate for the whole /ayarlar tree including /ayarlar/rollar, /ayarlar/istifadeci, /ayarlar/qiymet-icaze — there is no finer page-level gate distinguishing view from rol/user management; (b) the gate is defense for navigation only and (per bug #1) does nothing for direct action calls. Adding requireAyarOwner()/requireAyarActionPerm at page top would close the UI-render gap and is the established pattern.
  - Sübut: app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/layout.tsx:8-15 (no guard); app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/rollar/page.tsx:10-15, app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/rollar/[id]/page.tsx:35-50, app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/istifadeci/page.tsx:20-27 (no requireAyarOwner); guard exists unused at features/ayarlar/access-guard.ts:42; route rule lib/auth/route-gate.ts:56
  - Fayllar: app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/layout.tsx, app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/rollar/page.tsx, app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/rollar/[id]/page.tsx, app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/istifadeci/page.tsx, features/ayarlar/access-guard.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] Route-gate default-allows unknown prefixes and any /ayarlar subpath lacking its own guard**
  - Detal: gateRoute returns allowed:true for any pathname that matches no ROUTE_RULES prefix (line 112 'Tanınmayan prefix — default-allow'). This is documented as intentional ('page gates itself'), but combined with bug #5 (settings pages don't self-gate) it means the security model leans on a per-page habit that several in-scope pages don't follow. Any new /ayarlar/<x> route automatically inherits only the broad ayar.view/idare rule. Lower-risk than #1/#2 but it widens the blast radius: forgetting to add a rule or a page guard silently grants access rather than denying.
  - Sübut: lib/auth/route-gate.ts:111-112 'return { allowed: true }' default; BYPASS_PREFIXES :69-78 also blanket-allow /elaqe,/ai,/team,/tapshiriqlar without permission codes
  - Fayllar: lib/auth/route-gate.ts

- ⚪ **[low · permission] changeUserRole and toggleUserField allow promoting/deactivating without 'last admin' or self-protection safeguards (except deleteUser self-check)**
  - Detal: deleteUser blocks deleting yourself (id===istifadeciId, line 922), but changeUserRole has no guard against demoting yourself or the last privileged user, and toggleUserField('aktiv', false) can deactivate the only/last admin/owner of a tenant, potentially locking the tenant out of admin functions. No 'cannot remove the last owner' invariant exists. Given bugs #1-#2 this is secondary, but even for a legitimate admin it is an availability/foot-gun risk.
  - Sübut: features/ayar/actions.ts:856-879 changeUserRole (no self/last-admin check); :821-849 toggleUserField (only validates tenant ownership); compare self-check only at :922 in deleteUser
  - Fayllar: features/ayar/actions.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 Backend permission guard on role/user management server actions: None of the mutations in features/ayar/actions.ts check any permission — only session+tenant. A Kassir/Satıcı can call saveRolePerms/createUser/changeUserRole/resetUserPassword/deleteUser directly and escalate to full admin or take over accounts. Route-gate (UI navigation) does not protect server-action bodies. — _features/ayar/actions.ts:137,202,290,347,386,721,821,856,887,916 — no requireAyarActionPerm/privileged check; imports :3-9 contain no guard_
- 🔐 Privileged-access decided by exact role identity vs substring of user-controlled role name: gateRoute and all isPrivileged/isAyarPrivileged/isHrPrivileged/canViewCost/Profit checks use rolAd.includes('admin'|'owner'|'sahibkar'). Role names are user-set with no blocklist (saveRole RoleSchema, name 2-50 chars). A custom role named '...admin...' bypasses every gate and every cost/profit guard. — _lib/auth/route-gate.ts:93-96; features/ayarlar/access-guard.ts:35-37; lib/auth/finance-permissions.ts:12; features/ayar/actions.ts:195-200 (no name validation); auth.ts:178_
- 🔐 Permission guard on POS price-tier visibility config: saveRoleAllowedTiers is callable by any authenticated user; lets a non-admin grant low-trust roles visibility of cost-adjacent price tiers (topdan/partnyor/vip/min_satis) in POS. — _features/ayarlar/qiymet-icaze-actions.ts:8-20; features/ayarlar/qiymet-icaze.ts:89-114_
- 🔐 Page-level guard on /ayarlar role/user/price pages: ayarlar layout and rollar/istifadeci/qiymet-icaze pages do not call the existing requireAyarOwner(); the only barrier is the broad layout route-gate which equates 'ayar.view' with access to user/role management. — _app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/layout.tsx:8-15; app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/rollar/page.tsx:10; requireAyarOwner exists unused at features/ayarlar/access-guard.ts:42_
- 🔐 Audit trail on user/role admin actions: createUser, changeUserRole, resetUserPassword, deleteUser, toggleUserField, deleteRole, cloneRole, createRoleFromTemplate, IP/session changes are not audited — security-critical changes are untraceable. — _features/ayar/actions.ts: audit() only at :125,:171,:225,:237_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** features/ayar/actions.ts, features/ayarlar/qiymet-icaze-actions.ts, lib/auth/route-gate.ts, features/ayarlar/access-guard.ts, features/iscilier/access-guard.ts, lib/auth/finance-permissions.ts, app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/layout.tsx, app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/rollar/[id]/page.tsx

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** No schema change strictly required for the fixes. Recommended (optional) hardening: add a boolean/role-class column (e.g. roles.privileged or a canonical roles.sistem_kod) so privileged access can be decided by an explicit flag instead of substring-matching roles.ad, eliminating bug #2's user-controlled-name vector.

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** The substring-based privileged-role detection (bug #2) is the highest cross-module risk: lib/auth/route-gate.ts (all modules' navigation), lib/auth/finance-permissions.ts (maya/profit visibility used by anbar product cards, POS, dashboard, hesabatlar), features/iscilier/access-guard.ts (HR/salary), features/ayarlar/access-guard.ts (all settings), and lib/auth/role-check.ts all key off rolAd substrings. A single user-named custom role propagates full access into every one of those modules at once. Bug #1 compounds this because saveRolePerms/changeUserRole are themselves unguarded, so a low-privilege user can self-escalate and then ride the substring bypass everywhere. Conversely the rest of the settings surface (features/ayarlar/*, features/team/*, features/iscilier/vezife-actions.ts) already uses the correct requireXActionPerm pattern, so the inconsistency is concentrated in the features/ayar/* (roles/users/filiallar) action file.

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) (Critical, bug #1) Add an action-level guard to EVERY mutation in features/ayar/actions.ts. Reuse the existing requireAyarActionPerm: role mutations -> requireAyarActionPerm('ayar.rol_idare' or 'ayar.idare'); user mutations -> requireAyarActionPerm(['ayar.idare']) or a dedicated 'istifadeci.idare' code; return its error if !ok BEFORE any prisma write. This mirrors features/ayarlar/actions.ts. 2) (Critical, bug #2) Replace substring privileged detection with exact identity. Define one helper (e.g. isPrivilegedRole) keyed on an explicit flag/canonical name set and use it in route-gate.ts:95, access-guard.ts:35-37, finance-permissions.ts:12, iscilier/access-guard.ts, role-check.ts. Until a DB flag exists, at minimum block custom role names that contain admin/owner/sahibkar/direktor in saveRole/cloneRole/createRoleFromTemplate validation. 3) (High, bug #3) Add requireAyarActionPerm(['ayar.qiymet','ayar.idare']) at the top of saveRoleAllowedTiers. 4) (High, bug #4) Add audit() calls to createUser/changeUserRole/resetUserPassword/deleteUser/toggleUserField/deleteRole/cloneRole/createRoleFromTemplate/IP+session changes. 5) (Medium, bug #5/#6) Call requireAyarOwner() in app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/layout.tsx (or each role/user page) and consider stricter route rules (rol/user subpaths require ayar.rol_idare, not just ayar.view). 6) (Low, bug #7) Add last-privileged-user / self-demotion guards in changeUserRole and toggleUserField('aktiv'). After fixes, re-confirm system roles remain read-only (already enforced) and that legitimate owner/admin still passes.

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- As a Kassir/Satıcı (low role): from the browser devtools, invoke the changeUserRole server action (or saveRolePerms granting your role rol.idare) and confirm whether the change succeeds despite /ayarlar being navigation-blocked — should be DENIED after fix, currently expected to SUCCEED.
- As an owner: create a custom role literally named 'satis admin', assign a test employee, log in as that employee and try to open /maliyye, /ayarlar/rollar, and view product maya in POS — currently expected to be ALLOWED (bypass); after fix should be DENIED.
- As owner: open /ayarlar/qiymet-icaze, then as a low role attempt saveRoleAllowedTiers for the Kassir role enabling topdan/vip tiers — currently expected to SUCCEED; after fix DENIED.
- As owner: change a user's role, reset their password, deactivate a user — then open the audit log and confirm each action appears with actor/target — currently most will be MISSING.
- As owner: try to deactivate (toggleUserField aktiv=false) or demote (changeUserRole) the only owner account — confirm whether the tenant can lock itself out.
- Regression: as legitimate owner/admin, confirm full role/user management still works after guards are added (role perms save, user create, password reset).

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Confirm at runtime that invoking saveRolePerms/createUser/changeUserRole/resetUserPassword/deleteUser as a low-privilege user (e.g. Kassir) actually executes (bug #1). Static reading shows no guard, but a live test posting the server action with a non-admin session is the definitive proof that the layout route-gate does not block server-action execution.
- Confirm a custom role whose name contains 'admin'/'owner' (e.g. 'satis admin') causes gateRoute and isAyarPrivileged to return full access for a user assigned that role (bug #2).
- Confirm whether an active session/JWT for a user deactivated via deleteUser/toggleUserField continues to pass requests until expiry (no session revocation observed in static code) — secondary to bug #1.
- Verify the actual icaze.kod values present in the DB icazeler table (ayar.rol_idare, istifadeci.idare, etc.) so the added guards reference real codes; permission-info.ts lists codes but the live catalog determines what roles can actually be granted.

## 18. Audit log

**Xülasə:** The audit-log module is feature-rich and largely well-built: the page is permission-gated (audit.view via route-gate in the layout), audit_log is tenant-scoped, the central audit() helper is broadly adopted (87 files) with a safeAuditLog outbox fallback, and sampled critical mutations (POS sale, ticaret sale, soft-delete restore/hard-delete) do write audit entries. However there are serious integrity/permission gaps. HIGH: (1) audit_log is not immutable — deleteProductHistoryEntry can permanently delete audit rows and the deletion is itself un-audited; (2) the outbox-retry cron is NOT registered in vercel.json (and Vercel cron would invoke GET while the retry logic lives in POST), so failed audit writes are never recovered; (3) the CSV export API has no audit.view check, letting any authenticated user download the full tenant audit log. MEDIUM: the detail API likewise lacks an audit.view check (in-tenant read bypass). LOW: CSV export ignores the module filter, the date 'to' filter excludes the end day's records, and softDeleteRecord's docstring falsely claims it audits. Tenant isolation itself appears sound; the bypasses are within-tenant permission and immutability problems plus a non-functional outbox. All findings need a quick live confirmation of the API permission behavior and cron registration.

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** app/(dashboard)/audit-log/page.tsx; features/audit-log/queries.ts; features/audit-log/security-queries.ts; features/audit-log/active-devices-queries.ts; features/audit-log/auto-refresh-action.ts; features/audit-log/labels.ts (grepped); features/audit-log/components/audit-detail-modal.tsx; features/audit-log/components/audit-feed-list.tsx (partial); lib/audit/log.ts; lib/audit/safe-log.ts; app/api/audit-log/detail/route.ts; app/api/audit-log/export/route.ts; app/api/cron/audit-outbox-retry/route.ts; lib/auth/route-gate.ts; lib/db/with-tenant.ts; lib/db/tenant-context.ts; lib/db/tenant-models.ts; prisma/schema.prisma (audit_log, audit_log_outbox models); lib/soft-delete/actions.ts; features/pos/sale-action.ts (audit region); features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts (grepped); features/anbar/product-history-actions.ts; vercel.json; lib/permission-modules.ts (audit refs); lib/auth/permissions.ts (AUDIT_VIEW)

**Əhatə boşluqları:** features/audit-log/components/ip-block-manager.tsx, login-hours-manager.tsx, active-devices-panel.tsx, auto-refresh.tsx — UI components not deeply read (security sub-tabs adjacent to but partly outside pure 'audit log' scope).; features/audit-log/security-actions.ts — IP block / login-rule mutations not audited for their own permission guards (adjacent security scope).; Full enumeration of which of the 87 audit() call sites place the audit inside vs after the $transaction was not exhaustively traced; only POS sale, ticaret sale, and soft-delete helpers were sampled. Most observed audits run AFTER transaction commit (e.g. pos/sale-action.ts:335 is outside the $transaction block ending line 323), so a crash between commit and audit() loses the entry with no outbox coverage — noted but not exhaustively quantified.; scripts/reset-transactions.ts deletes audit_log (dev/maintenance) — not analyzed in depth.; app/(dashboard)/sahibkar/audit and app/(dashboard)/platform-admin/audit pages — separate audit views, outside the core module scope, not examined.

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ Page route /audit-log is permission-gated by route-gate (audit.view) enforced in dashboard layout — _lib/auth/route-gate.ts:53 { prefix: '/audit-log', anyOf: ['audit.view'] }; enforced in app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx:120 gateRoute(...) then redirect('/icaze-yox')_
- ✅ audit_log is tenant-scoped: getAuditLog uses scoped prisma inside withTenant so cross-tenant reads are blocked — _lib/db/tenant-models.ts includes 'audit_log'; features/audit-log/queries.ts:35-67 wraps findMany in withTenant() using `prisma`_
- ✅ Detail API explicitly re-scopes by sahibkar_id, blocking cross-tenant row reads — _features/audit-log/security-queries.ts:43 prisma.audit_log.findFirst({ where: { id, sahibkar_id: sahibkarId } })_
- ✅ Central audit() helper auto-captures tenant, user, IP, UA, device fingerprint; toJsonSafe handles Decimal/Date/BigInt; used by 87 files — _lib/audit/log.ts:91-122; grep counted 87 files importing lib/audit/log_
- ✅ safeAuditLog wraps create in try/catch with audit_log_outbox fallback so a failed audit write does not throw and abort the mutation — _lib/audit/safe-log.ts:17-38_
- ✅ Critical mutations sampled DO write audit entries: POS sale logs yarat/pos_satis; ticaret sale has multiple safeAuditLog calls; soft-delete restore/hard-delete call audit() — _features/pos/sale-action.ts:335; features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts:129/177/314; lib/soft-delete/actions.ts:117,160_
- ✅ CSV export writes an audit-of-audit entry (export of the log itself is logged) — _app/api/audit-log/export/route.ts:29 audit('export','audit_log_export',...)_
- ✅ View/filter/search/pagination implemented: where-builder supports emeliyyat, resurs_nov, resurs_nov_in (module), status, istifadeci_id, date range, and q across multiple columns; Pagination wired — _features/audit-log/queries.ts:37-67; app/(dashboard)/audit-log/page.tsx:74-83,505_
- ✅ Outbox retry POST handler is auth-guarded by CRON_SECRET (Bearer or X-Cron-Secret) and implements backoff + abandon at MAX_RETRY_COUNT — _app/api/cron/audit-outbox-retry/route.ts:26-34,64-90_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] audit_log rows can be permanently DELETED — log is not immutable, and the deletion itself is not audited**
  - Detal: deleteProductHistoryEntry performs prisma.audit_log.deleteMany on a single audit row (scoped to resurs_nov='mehsul'). This makes the audit log mutable/tamper-able. It is gated to admin/sahibkar/'audit_log.sil' holders, but (a) an audit log that can be deleted at all defeats tamper-evidence, and (b) the deletion writes NO audit entry of its own, so removing history leaves no trace. A privileged user can erase product price/stock-change history with zero record. Expectation per scope is append-only/immutable audit with tamper resistance.
  - Sübut: features/anbar/product-history-actions.ts:29 prisma.audit_log.deleteMany(...); no audit() call before return on line 38
  - Fayllar: features/anbar/product-history-actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · functional] Outbox retry cron is NOT registered in vercel.json — failed audit writes accumulate and are never retried**
  - Detal: safeAuditLog routes failed audit_log.create attempts into audit_log_outbox (status='pending') expecting a cron to drain them. The route comment claims 'Vercel Cron: vercel.json-da her 15 deq tetiklenir', but vercel.json crons array contains only daily-email-summary, run-automation-rules, compute-reorder-recommendations, check-overdue-tasks — NOT /api/cron/audit-outbox-retry. So outbox rows stay 'pending' forever unless someone manually POSTs with the secret. The self-healing audit guarantee is effectively dead, meaning audit entries lost on transient DB errors are silently never recovered.
  - Sübut: vercel.json:3-20 (no audit-outbox-retry path); contradicted by app/api/cron/audit-outbox-retry/route.ts:13-14 comment
  - Fayllar: vercel.json, app/api/cron/audit-outbox-retry/route.ts

- 🟠 **[high · permission] Audit export API has no audit.view permission check — any authenticated user can export the full tenant audit log**
  - Detal: GET /api/audit-log/export performs getAuditLog(filter, 1, 5000) and streams a CSV of the entire tenant's audit log. The only protection is the implicit withTenant() inside getAuditLog (which requires a logged-in session) — there is NO check for the 'audit.view' permission that gates the page UI. A logged-in low-privilege user (e.g. cashier without audit.view) who is blocked from /audit-log in the UI can still hit /api/audit-log/export?format=csv directly and download all audit data (IPs, user activity, before/after payloads). Cross-tenant is prevented, but in-tenant permission bypass is open.
  - Sübut: app/api/audit-log/export/route.ts:14-26 — no permission gate; contrast lib/auth/route-gate.ts:53 requiring audit.view for the page
  - Fayllar: app/api/audit-log/export/route.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] Audit detail API checks only auth, not audit.view — in-tenant permission bypass to read any audit row**
  - Detal: GET /api/audit-log/detail validates session?.user only. Any authenticated user can fetch full detail (evvelki_data/yeni_data payloads, IP, user-agent, session id) for any audit row in their tenant by guessing/iterating the bigint id, regardless of whether they hold audit.view. Cross-tenant is blocked (getAuditRowDetail re-scopes by sahibkar_id), but a user denied audit.view in the UI can still read individual entries via the API.
  - Sübut: app/api/audit-log/detail/route.ts:8-9 only checks session?.user; getAuditRowDetail (security-queries.ts:43) scopes by sahibkar_id but has no permission check
  - Fayllar: app/api/audit-log/detail/route.ts, features/audit-log/security-queries.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · functional] Outbox retry likely unreachable via Vercel cron even if registered (cron invokes GET, retry logic is in POST)**
  - Detal: Vercel Cron invokes the configured path with a GET request carrying Authorization: Bearer <CRON_SECRET>. In this route, GET only returns pending/abandoned counts; the actual outbox draining (audit_log.create retries) is in the POST handler. So even if /api/cron/audit-outbox-retry were added to vercel.json, the scheduled GET would never process the outbox. Combined with the missing cron registration, the outbox is doubly non-functional in production. Needs runtime confirmation of how this path is intended to be triggered.
  - Sübut: app/api/cron/audit-outbox-retry/route.ts:23 POST does the work; GET (line 103-114) only reports counts; Vercel cron uses GET
  - Fayllar: app/api/cron/audit-outbox-retry/route.ts

- ⚪ **[low · functional] CSV export ignores the module filter (modul / resurs_nov_in) used by the page**
  - Detal: The page builds filter.resurs_nov_in from the selected module (page.tsx:73-79) and the export link is built from filter including it. But the export route only reads q, emeliyyat, resurs_nov, status, istifadeci_id, from, to — it never reads the modul param nor sets resurs_nov_in. So when a user has a module selected and clicks CSV, the export returns ALL resource types, not the filtered module. The CSV does not match the on-screen filtered view.
  - Sübut: app/api/audit-log/export/route.ts:16-24 (no modul/resurs_nov_in); page passes modul via csvParams in page.tsx:95-98
  - Fayllar: app/api/audit-log/export/route.ts, app/(dashboard)/audit-log/page.tsx

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] Date 'to' filter excludes the selected end day's records (midnight boundary)**
  - Detal: getAuditLog parses filter.to with new Date(filter.to). A date-only string like '2026-06-08' parses to 2026-06-08T00:00:00Z, and the filter uses yaradildi.lte = that. So records created during the chosen 'to' day (after midnight) are excluded. A single-day filter (from=to=today) returns almost nothing. The export route shares getAuditLog so the CSV has the same off-by-one-day exclusion. The DatePreset 'Bu gun' (days=0) sets from=to=today, which would return only records exactly at 00:00:00.
  - Sübut: features/audit-log/queries.ts:47-48 where.yaradildi.lte = new Date(filter.to); page.tsx:516 DatePreset 'Bu gun' days=0 sets from===to
  - Fayllar: features/audit-log/queries.ts, app/(dashboard)/audit-log/page.tsx

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] softDeleteRecord docstring claims an audit log is created but the function does not write one**
  - Detal: The generic softDeleteRecord helper's header comment (line 20) states 'Audit log yaranir' (an audit log is created), but the function body (lines 46-65) only runs the UPDATE ... SET deleted_at and returns; there is no audit() call. Callers must log separately. This is only the generic helper (grep shows it is referenced solely within lib/soft-delete/actions.ts), so module cancel actions that inline their own soft-delete SQL may still audit — but any future caller trusting the docstring will produce un-audited deletions. Misleading contract.
  - Sübut: lib/soft-delete/actions.ts:20 comment vs body lines 46-66 (no audit call); restoreRecord (line 117) and hardDeleteRecord (line 160) DO audit
  - Fayllar: lib/soft-delete/actions.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 audit.view on /api/audit-log/export: Export route has no audit.view permission check; only implicit auth via withTenant. A logged-in user without audit.view (blocked from the page) can download the full tenant audit log CSV directly. — _app/api/audit-log/export/route.ts:14-33 — no PERMISSIONS.AUDIT_VIEW / requirePermission gate_
- 🔐 audit.view on /api/audit-log/detail: Detail route checks only session?.user (auth), not audit.view. Any authenticated tenant user can read any audit row's full payload by id. — _app/api/audit-log/detail/route.ts:8-9 if (!session?.user) ... — no permission check_
- 🔐 Immutability / tamper control on audit_log: audit_log rows are deletable via deleteProductHistoryEntry (admin/sahibkar/audit_log.sil). Audit log is not append-only and deletions are not themselves audited. — _features/anbar/product-history-actions.ts:29 prisma.audit_log.deleteMany_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** app/api/audit-log/export/route.ts, app/api/audit-log/detail/route.ts, features/anbar/product-history-actions.ts, vercel.json, app/api/cron/audit-outbox-retry/route.ts, features/audit-log/queries.ts, lib/soft-delete/actions.ts

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** Consider a DB-level guard (trigger or revoked UPDATE/DELETE grants) on audit_log to enforce append-only immutability, since application code (deleteProductHistoryEntry, scripts/reset-transactions.ts) can currently delete rows.

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** Audit logging is cross-cutting: the audit() helper is imported by 87 feature/lib files and underpins not just the audit-log page but the Active Devices panel, anomaly alerts, new-device detection, and login security (login-guard.ts queries audit_log for prior-device checks). Therefore lost or never-retried outbox entries degrade security signals (active devices, new-device alerts, failed-login counts) across modules, not just the audit page. The export/detail permission gaps expose data from every module that logs (sales payloads, customer borc changes, payroll, secret-code access) to under-privileged users. The immutable-log gap undermines forensic trust for all modules.

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) Add an audit.view permission gate to both /api/audit-log/export and /api/audit-log/detail (load session permissions and require PERMISSIONS.AUDIT_VIEW, mirroring the route-gate rule). 2) For audit immutability: either remove deleteProductHistoryEntry's ability to delete audit_log rows, or at minimum write an audit('sil','audit_log', id, ...) entry before deletion and enforce a DB-level append-only constraint. 3) Register /api/cron/audit-outbox-retry in vercel.json (respecting Hobby-plan limits, e.g. daily) AND move the retry logic so the scheduled GET actually drains the outbox, since Vercel cron invokes GET, not POST. 4) Fix the date 'to' boundary in getAuditLog by treating a date-only 'to' as end-of-day (set lte to to+1 day or 23:59:59). 5) Pass the module filter (resurs_nov_in / modul) through to the export route so CSV matches the on-screen filter. 6) Correct or implement softDeleteRecord's docstring claim about audit logging.

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- As a user WITHOUT audit.view, log in, then directly request /api/audit-log/export?format=csv and /api/audit-log/detail?id=<n>. Expected: 403. Likely actual: 200 with data (permission bypass).
- As admin/sahibkar, delete a product-history entry from a product card, then check whether any audit entry records that deletion. Expected: a 'sil' audit row. Actual: none (untracked deletion).
- Force a transient audit_log write failure (or inspect existing audit_log_outbox rows) and confirm whether any cron drains status='pending' rows over time. Expected: drained within ~15 min. Actual: stays pending (cron not registered).
- On /audit-log, select a single module in the filter and click CSV. Compare CSV resource types against the on-screen list. Expected: only that module. Actual: all modules.
- On /audit-log, click 'Bu gun' date preset. Expected: today's records. Actual: near-empty list due to to-date midnight boundary.
- Perform a POS sale and a sale via ticaret, then confirm corresponding 'yarat'/'pos_satis' and sale audit entries appear in the feed (positive path).

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Whether a real low-privilege user (no audit.view) actually receives a CSV from /api/audit-log/export and JSON from /api/audit-log/detail (confirm withTenant only enforces auth, not permission, at runtime).
- Whether deleteProductHistoryEntry is reachable from the product card UI and what role triggers it in practice.
- Confirm at runtime that no Vercel cron triggers /api/cron/audit-outbox-retry and that audit_log_outbox rows remain 'pending' (the route was likely dropped in commit 73721ff 'cron schedule Hobby plan ile uygunlasdir').
- Confirm whether Vercel cron's GET-only invocation means the POST-based retry logic never runs even if re-registered.
- Verify the single-day date filter ('Bu gun') returns near-zero rows due to the midnight lte boundary, against real data.
- Confirm that module-level cancel/delete actions that bypass the generic softDeleteRecord still emit their own audit entries (sampled POS/ticaret do log creates; cancellation/reversal paths not fully traced in this scope).

## 19. Import / Export

**Xülasə:** The Import/Export module is functionally rich (templates, fuzzy header mapping, validation, preview, per-row error isolation, multi-tenant scoping that holds via the Prisma extension) but has several critical defects. Authorization is the biggest hole: NO import or export API route enforces a permission — they only check that someone is logged in, and the central route-gate/ROUTE_RULES is page-level only (no middleware), so any low-privilege user can directly POST to overwrite catalog/stock/customers/suppliers/kassa balances, create employee accounts (with a known temp-password pattern), and GET the full audit log (with IPs) and all employees' salary/KPI data via /api/audit-log/export and /api/iscilier/kpi-export. Data-integrity is the second major problem: importers write opening balances/debts to maliye_hesablari.qaliq and kontragentler.borc, but the balance services compute exclusively from finance_operations and sales — so imported opening data is silently invisible everywhere; no import runs in a $transaction (partial-failure leaves products without stock, stock without movement records, and stuck 'icra_edilir' batches with no idempotency); bank reconciliation increments odenilmis (reducing computed receivables) without creating any account inflow and with no oldest-invoice allocation; and the advertised 48-hour 'Geri qaytar' rollback simply does not exist (no endpoint, no per-row import_satirlari trail). Lower-severity issues include the mehsul stock set-vs-increment contradiction against the UI promise, swallowed anbar_hereketleri write errors, the rol_id===9 backup gate that the codebase itself warns against, and cleartext temp passwords surfaced in the import result log. Most findings are code-verified; the exact exploitability of the unguarded routes and the rol_id===9 mapping are flagged for live confirmation.

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts; features/inteqrasiya/templates.ts; features/inteqrasiya/excel-parser.ts; features/inteqrasiya/components/import-uploader.tsx; app/api/inteqrasiya/import/[key]/route.ts; app/api/inteqrasiya/parse/[key]/route.ts; app/api/inteqrasiya/template/[key]/route.ts; app/api/inteqrasiya/history/route.ts; app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/inteqrasiya/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/idxal-tarixce/page.tsx; app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/backup/page.tsx; app/api/anbar/mehsul-import/sablon/route.ts; features/anbar/import/import-actions.ts; app/api/bank/template/route.ts; features/bank/excel.ts; features/bank/recon-actions.ts; app/api/maliyye/bank/[id]/items/route.ts; app/api/audit-log/export/route.ts; app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts; app/api/sahibkar/backup/route.ts; app/api/anbar/stok/export/route.ts (auth-grep); app/api/anbar/mehsullar/export/route.ts (auth-grep); app/api/hesabatlar/{maliyye,satis,musteri}/export/route.ts (auth-grep); app/api/ticaret/{satis,alis,qaytarma}-export/route.ts (auth-grep); lib/auth/route-gate.ts; lib/db/with-tenant.ts; lib/db/tenant-context.ts; lib/db/prisma.ts (extension); lib/db/tenant-models.ts; lib/balance/account-balance.ts; lib/balance/customer-balance.ts; prisma/schema.prisma (import_partiyalari, anbar_hereketleri, mehsul_barkodlar)
