**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ Multi-tenant isolation holds for all importers and import history: every importer runs inside withTenant() and the Prisma $extends client auto-injects sahibkar_id on reads/writes/upserts for TENANT_MODELS (which includes mehsullar, kontragentler, stok, maliye_hesablari, istifadeciler, anbar_hereketleri, import_partiyalari, bank_cixarisi_satirlari). — _features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts:34 withTenant; lib/db/prisma.ts:53-92 READ_OPS/WRITE_OPS injection; lib/db/tenant-models.ts:16,72,87,93,126,140,194_
- ✅ Excel parser does required-field validation, type coercion, and template-specific sanity checks (negative-number guards, email format, account-type whitelist), and rows with errors are excluded from import (importByKey filters r.errors.length===0). — _features/inteqrasiya/excel-parser.ts:218-227 required+runTemplateChecks; features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts:36 validRows filter_
- ✅ Per-row error isolation: each importer wraps row processing in try/catch and increments xeta without aborting the whole file; counts are written back to import_partiyalari and an audit('import',...) entry is written with status ugur/qismen/ugursuz. — _features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts:207-210, 56-80, 69_
- ✅ Import preview (anbar wizard) dedupes within-file by kod/barkod/ad and resolves create-vs-update against existing products before execution; uses race-safe stok.upsert with the mehsul_id_anbar_id compound key. — _features/anbar/import/import-actions.ts:214-231, 473-488_
- ✅ Template download, parse, and import API routes all require an authenticated session (auth()) and return 401 otherwise; parse route enforces a 20MB upload cap, anbar analyzeImport enforces 10MB, bank recon enforces 10MB. — _app/api/inteqrasiya/import/[key]/route.ts:11-12; parse/[key]/route.ts:24-25; features/anbar/import/import-actions.ts:153; features/bank/recon-actions.ts:32_
- ✅ Sahibkar full-tenant JSON backup export is gated by rol_id===9 AND an active PIN session, and explicitly omits password hashes from the istifadeciler export. — _app/api/sahibkar/backup/route.ts:22-28, 54-63_
- ✅ Most data-export routes (anbar stok/mehsullar, hesabatlar maliyye/satis/musteri, ticaret satis/alis/qaytarma, musteri/techizatci CSV) check auth() session and run inside withTenant for tenant scoping. — _app/api/anbar/stok/export/route.ts:9-10; app/api/hesabatlar/maliyye/export/route.ts:12-13; app/api/ticaret/satis-export/route.ts:16-17_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🔴 **[critical · data-flow] Imported opening account balances and customer/supplier debts are written to dead columns the balance services ignore**
  - Detal: importHesab writes the opening balance to maliye_hesablari.qaliq and importMusteri/importTechizatci write opening debt to kontragentler.borc. But the single-source-of-truth balance services compute balances ENTIRELY from finance_operations (account) and from satis_sifarisleri nisye rows (customer) — they never read the .qaliq / .borc columns. No importer creates a finance_operations row or an offsetting sales/opening-balance record. Result: every imported kassa/bank opening balance and every imported customer/supplier opening debt is silently invisible in dashboards, POS, finance reports and balance modals. The kassa-baslangic template even promises 'ilkin balans daxili maliyyə əməliyyatı kimi yazılır' (recorded as a finance operation) — that write does not exist. This breaks the primary purpose of the migration/onboarding import.
  - Sübut: features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts:339-359 (importHesab sets qaliq, no finance_operations); importer.ts:249,300 (borc column); lib/balance/account-balance.ts:4-22 'qaliq = SUM(finance_operations)'; lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:45,72 'aggregate from satis_sifarisleri', templates.ts:475
  - Fayllar: features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts, features/inteqrasiya/templates.ts, lib/balance/account-balance.ts, lib/balance/customer-balance.ts

- 🔴 **[critical · data-flow] Bank reconciliation allocates payments but creates NO cash/bank movement and never updates customer balance**
  - Detal: processBankStatement matches each statement line to a sales order and increments satis_sifarisleri.odenilmis (and may flip status to 'tesdiq'), but it creates NO finance_operations row for the incoming money and does not touch any maliye_hesablari / customer balance. Since account balance = SUM(finance_operations) and customer debt = aggregate over sales, marking odenilmis higher reduces the customer's computed debt WITHOUT any matching cash/bank inflow being recorded — money 'arrives' in the ledger sense (debt cleared) but never lands in any account. This diverges from the documented rule that a payment must credit the matching account. It also blindly adds r.mebleg to odenilmis with no allocate-to-oldest-open-invoice logic and no overpayment/advance handling: a single statement line is force-applied to one matched order and can push odenilmis far past son_mebleg.
  - Sübut: features/bank/recon-actions.ts:119-135 (updates odenilmis only, no finance_operations.create anywhere in file); lib/balance/account-balance.ts:4-22; lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:72
  - Fayllar: features/bank/recon-actions.ts

- 🔴 **[critical · permission] audit-log CSV export route has NO authentication or permission guard**
  - Detal: app/api/audit-log/export/route.ts GET handler does not call auth() and does no permission check. getAuditLog() internally calls withTenant()->auth() so an unauthenticated request 500s rather than leaking, but ANY authenticated user of any role (e.g. a 'baxan' viewer with no audit.view permission) can GET this endpoint and download the full tenant audit log including user names, IP addresses, URLs and methods. The corresponding dashboard page /audit-log IS gated (route-gate anyOf ['audit.view']) but that gate is page-level only and never applies to /api routes, so a low-privilege user bypasses the UI restriction by hitting the API directly.
  - Sübut: app/api/audit-log/export/route.ts:14-33 (no auth() / no permission check); lib/auth/route-gate.ts:53 (/audit-log requires audit.view, page-level only); route-gate used only in app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx
  - Fayllar: app/api/audit-log/export/route.ts

- 🔴 **[critical · permission] Employee KPI/salary CSV export route has NO authentication or permission guard**
  - Detal: app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts GET handler has no auth() and no permission check. It calls getKpiDashboard({ rawData: true }) which explicitly bypasses stealth/gizli-mod, then streams every employee's salary, earned bonus, bonus pool, fines, net pay, attendance %, sales figures and performance score as CSV. getKpiDashboard wraps in withTenant so anonymous access 500s, but any authenticated user — including roles without isci.view/hr.view — can export all colleagues' confidential payroll data, fully bypassing the page-level gate on /iscilier.
  - Sübut: app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts:16-27 (no auth, rawData:true); lib/auth/route-gate.ts:34 (/iscilier requires isci.view/hr.view, page-level only)
  - Fayllar: app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] No import runs inside a $transaction — partial failure leaves half-written, non-atomic state**
  - Detal: None of the three import paths (features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts, features/anbar/import/import-actions.ts executeImport, features/bank/recon-actions.ts) use prisma.$transaction. Within a single row, importMehsul does separate writes for mehsullar, mehsul_barkodlar and stok; if the stok write throws after the product is created, the product exists with no stock and the batch counters drift. The import batch status is also updated outside any transaction, so a crash mid-file leaves status='icra_edilir' forever (no recovery/idempotency). Re-submitting the same file (double-click / refresh) re-runs the whole import: the uploader resends the file on handleImport with no client disable-after-submit guard beyond a step change and no server-side dedupe key, so duplicate batches and duplicate updates can occur.
  - Sübut: features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts (no $transaction; row writes at 151-205); features/anbar/import/import-actions.ts:395-512 (loop of independent awaits, no tx); features/bank/recon-actions.ts:68-170 (no tx); features/inteqrasiya/components/import-uploader.tsx:97-121 (resends file, no idempotency token)
  - Fayllar: features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts, features/anbar/import/import-actions.ts, features/bank/recon-actions.ts, features/inteqrasiya/components/import-uploader.tsx

- 🟠 **[high · permission] All import/export API routes lack permission gating — login-only check lets any role import/export everything**
  - Detal: Every import route (inteqrasiya import/parse/template, anbar/mehsul-import) and every checked export route guards only with `if (!session?.user) 401` — none verify a permission code (e.g. anbar.idare, ayar.idare, hesabat.view). The central gateRoute/ROUTE_RULES map is invoked only in app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx for dashboard page paths and there is no middleware.ts, so API routes are entirely outside the permission layer. Consequence: a minimal 'baxan' (viewer) user can POST /api/inteqrasiya/import/mehsul to overwrite the product catalog and stock, import/overwrite customers and suppliers, set kassa balances, and — most dangerously — import employees (importEmekdas creates istifadeciler with a known temp password pattern 'temp'+random and rol defaulted to baxan/id 3), and can GET any data-export CSV. Frontend hides the pages but provides zero backend authorization.
  - Sübut: app/api/inteqrasiya/import/[key]/route.ts:11-12 (auth only); parse/[key]/route.ts:11; template/[key]/route.ts:9-10; features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts:370-425 (creates users, rol_id default 3); lib/auth/route-gate.ts:88-113 (gateRoute is path/UI only); no middleware.ts found
  - Fayllar: app/api/inteqrasiya/import/[key]/route.ts, app/api/inteqrasiya/parse/[key]/route.ts, app/api/inteqrasiya/template/[key]/route.ts, app/api/anbar/mehsul-import/sablon/route.ts, features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts

- 🟠 **[high · ux] Idxal-tarixce page advertises a 'Geri qaytar' (rollback) feature that does not exist**
  - Detal: The import-history page states a '«Geri qaytar»' button deletes batch records, reverses stock movements, and that successful imports can be rolled back within 48 hours. There is no such button rendered on the page and no rollback endpoint/server action anywhere (grep for geri-qaytar/rollback/revert finds only this static text). The schema even has import_partiyalari.rollback_de and a per-row import_satirlari relation intended for this, but no importer ever writes import_satirlari rows (it is referenced only in tenant-models.ts), so even a future rollback has no per-row trail to reverse. Users are told a safety net exists that does not.
  - Sübut: app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/idxal-tarixce/page.tsx:153-159 (claims); grep rollback/geri-qaytar across app/api & features finds no handler; prisma import_partiyalari.rollback_de + import_satirlari relation; import_satirlari never written (only tenant-models.ts:73)
  - Fayllar: app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/idxal-tarixce/page.tsx, features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Product import set-vs-add contradiction: UI/template promise stock is INCREASED, code OVERWRITES it**
  - Detal: The inteqrasiya detail page and template instructions tell users 'Eyni barkod varsa stok artırılır (yeni məhsul yox)' (existing barcode => stock is increased). But importMehsul SETS stok.miqdar = say (overwrite, not increment) for an existing product+anbar row. The anbar wizard executeImport likewise SETS miqdar = r.say. Re-importing the same catalog therefore silently resets on-hand stock to the spreadsheet value instead of adding, which can wipe real inventory while the UI promised an additive merge. (The stok-sayim template is correctly documented as set-mode; the mehsul template is the one whose documented behavior contradicts code.)
  - Sübut: features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts:191-205 (data:{miqdar:say} overwrite); app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/inteqrasiya/page.tsx:362-364 ('Eyni barkod varsa stok artırılır'); templates.ts:78 vs features/anbar/import/import-actions.ts:477 (miqdar:yeniMiqdar set)
  - Fayllar: features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts, app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/inteqrasiya/page.tsx, features/inteqrasiya/templates.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · functional] satis/alis/servis templates are downloadable and previewable but have no importer — silently no-op risk and stale 'stok azaldılır' promise**
  - Detal: TEMPLATES defines satis, alis, servis with target tables and instructions like 'Stok avtomatik azaldılır' / 'Status: tesdiq', but the importers map only registers mehsul, musteri, techizatci, kassa-baslangic, emekdas, crm, stok. The import API returns 501 for unregistered keys and the uploader disables the Import button when !importerAvailable, so users cannot actually import sales/purchase/service history despite the prominent template cards. This is gated correctly (no data corruption) but is a dead/misleading feature: the migration of historical sales (which the templates promise will decrement stock and create finance) is impossible, and any reliance on it for opening data is unmet.
  - Sübut: features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts:19-27 (importers map omits satis/alis/servis); importer.ts:29-33 throws for unknown; templates.ts:206-326 (satis/alis/servis defined with stock/finance promises); import/[key]/route.ts:19-26 returns 501
  - Fayllar: features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts, features/inteqrasiya/templates.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Stock movement audit (anbar_hereketleri) failures are silently swallowed during import**
  - Detal: In importStok the anbar_hereketleri.create for inventory-correction and initial-stock movements is suffixed with .catch(()=>{}), and in anbar executeImport the same write is wrapped in try{}catch{/*non-fatal*/}. Because the stok.miqdar update is committed separately (no transaction), a failed movement write leaves stock changed with NO corresponding movement record. Since warehouse reports/movement history derive from anbar_hereketleri, imported stock changes can be untraceable and reconciliation between stok and its movement ledger silently breaks.
  - Sübut: features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts:541-555 and 568-580 (.catch(()=>{})); features/anbar/import/import-actions.ts:492-506 (catch non-fatal)
  - Fayllar: features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts, features/anbar/import/import-actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] sahibkar/backup gate uses numeric rol_id===9, which the codebase explicitly warns is wrong under multi-tenant**
  - Detal: The full-tenant JSON backup export gates on session.user.rol_id !== 9. tenant-context.ts documents that after the multi-tenant migration rol_id numbers differ per sahibkar (roles are unique by sahibkar_id+ad) and that checks must use rol_ad ('sahibkar'), not the numeric id. So the magic number 9 may not correspond to 'sahibkar' for every tenant: it can wrongly deny a legitimate owner, or (worse) match whatever role happens to have id 9 in another tenant's numbering, exposing a complete tenant data dump (sales, finance_operations, salaries, audit) to a non-owner. Needs confirmation against the actual roles seeding.
  - Sübut: app/api/sahibkar/backup/route.ts:22-23 (rol_id !== 9); lib/db/tenant-context.ts:13-19 ('rolId nömrəsi hər sahibkar üçün fərqli olur ... ad istifadə edilməlidir, rolId yox')
  - Fayllar: app/api/sahibkar/backup/route.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] Bank statement-items API route has no auth() and no permission gate**
  - Detal: app/api/maliyye/bank/[id]/items/route.ts GET has no auth() call and no permission check; it relies solely on withTenant() (which throws on no session, preventing anonymous access) and the tenant filter on bank_cixarisi_satirlari. So it is tenant-isolated and not anonymously accessible, but any authenticated low-privilege user can read any bank statement line items for their tenant by id, with no maliye.* permission requirement — inconsistent with the gating on the /maliyye pages.
  - Sübut: app/api/maliyye/bank/[id]/items/route.ts:7-13 (no auth/permission, withTenant only); lib/auth/route-gate.ts:30 (/maliyye requires maliye.* but page-level only)
  - Fayllar: app/api/maliyye/bank/[id]/items/route.ts

- ⚪ **[low · ux] importEmekdas returns generated temp passwords in the import result log shown in the browser and stored in import audit/batch**
  - Detal: When creating new users, importEmekdas pushes the cleartext temporary password into the result log ('müvəqqəti şifrə: ...'). This log is returned to the client (DoneView renders it) and r.log entries are also persisted; the xeta filter only strips error entries from xeta_log, so the success message with the password is shown to whoever runs the import. Combined with the no-permission-gate finding, a low-privilege importer could create accounts and read their initial passwords. Mecburi-sifre-deyis mitigates persistence risk but the credential is still exposed in transit/UI.
  - Sübut: features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts:408-424 (tempPassword in log.mesaj); components/import-uploader.tsx:438-466 (renders result.log)
  - Fayllar: features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts, features/inteqrasiya/components/import-uploader.tsx

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 Permission/role gate on import API routes (inteqrasiya import/parse/template, anbar mehsul-import): Only an auth() login check; no permission verification. A viewer-level user can overwrite product catalog/stock, customers, suppliers, kassa balances and even create employee accounts via direct POST. gateRoute is page-level only and there is no middleware covering /api. — _app/api/inteqrasiya/import/[key]/route.ts:11-12; features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts:370-425; lib/auth/route-gate.ts:88-113 used only in app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx; no middleware.ts_
- 🔐 Auth + permission on /api/audit-log/export: No auth() and no audit.view permission check. Any authenticated user (any role) can download the full audit log with IPs; the /audit-log page gate is bypassed. — _app/api/audit-log/export/route.ts:14-33; lib/auth/route-gate.ts:53_
- 🔐 Auth + permission on /api/iscilier/kpi-export: No auth() and no isci/hr permission check; streams all employees' salary, bonus, fine, net-pay and performance data (rawData:true bypasses stealth). — _app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts:16-27; lib/auth/route-gate.ts:34_
- 🔐 Owner gate on /api/sahibkar/backup full-tenant dump: Uses numeric rol_id===9; tenant-context.ts states rol ids are per-tenant and checks must use rol_ad. Magic number may misclassify owner across tenants (false deny or, worst case, wrong-role match exposing entire tenant dump). — _app/api/sahibkar/backup/route.ts:22-23; lib/db/tenant-context.ts:13-19_
- 🔐 Permission gate on data-export routes (anbar/hesabat/ticaret/musteri/techizatci): All use auth()-login-only with no module permission (e.g. hesabat.view, anbar.view). Tenant-scoped and not anonymous, but any logged-in role can export sensitive reports, customer statements and supplier statements; UI page gating is bypassable via direct URL. — _app/api/hesabatlar/maliyye/export/route.ts:12-13; app/api/ticaret/satis-export/route.ts:16-17; app/api/musteri/[id]/hesab-cixaris.csv/route.ts:15-16_
- 🔐 Permission gate on /api/maliyye/bank/[id]/items: No auth() and no maliye permission; relies only on withTenant + tenant filter. Authenticated low-privilege users can read bank statement line items. — _app/api/maliyye/bank/[id]/items/route.ts:7-13_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** app/api/inteqrasiya/import/[key]/route.ts, app/api/inteqrasiya/parse/[key]/route.ts, app/api/inteqrasiya/template/[key]/route.ts, app/api/anbar/mehsul-import/sablon/route.ts, app/api/audit-log/export/route.ts, app/api/iscilier/kpi-export/route.ts, app/api/sahibkar/backup/route.ts, app/api/maliyye/bank/[id]/items/route.ts, features/inteqrasiya/importer.ts, features/anbar/import/import-actions.ts, features/bank/recon-actions.ts, features/inteqrasiya/templates.ts, app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/idxal-tarixce/page.tsx, app/(dashboard)/ayarlar/inteqrasiya/page.tsx

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** No new columns strictly required, but importHesab must INSERT a finance_operations opening-balance row (yon='daxil', status='aktiv') instead of/ in addition to writing maliye_hesablari.qaliq, so account-balance.ts picks it up; importMusteri/importTechizatci opening debt (kontragentler.borc) must be materialized as a record the customer/supplier-balance services aggregate over (e.g. an opening-balance sales/finance row), since the .borc column is ignored; If rollback is to be supported, importers must write per-row import_satirlari records (table+relation already exist in schema) and a rollback action must populate import_partiyalari.rollback_de

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** High. Import is an onboarding/migration entry point feeding nearly every module. Because balance is computed from finance_operations and sales (not the .qaliq/.borc columns the importers write), imported opening balances/debts never propagate to dashboard, POS available-credit, finance reports, debitor/kreditor, or customer/supplier statements — silent data divergence across the ERP. The bank-recon path mutates satis_sifarisleri.odenilmis (consumed by customer-balance and sales reports) without a matching account inflow, desynchronizing cash/bank vs receivables. Non-atomic imports can leave products without stock or stock without movement records, corrupting warehouse reports and inventory counts. The unguarded API routes also create cross-module privilege-escalation risk (any role can mass-edit catalog/customers, create users, and exfiltrate payroll/audit data)."

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) Add a server-side permission helper (reuse gateRoute logic or a requirePermission util that reads session perms) and call it at the top of every import/export API route and server action: imports => ayar.idare/anbar.idare as appropriate; audit-log/export => audit.view; kpi-export => isci.view/hr.view; bank items + maliyye exports => maliye.view; data exports => matching hesabat/anbar/ticaret view perms. 2) Wrap each import per-row (and the batch-status finalize) in prisma.$transaction so a row's multi-table writes (mehsullar+barkodlar+stok, or stok+anbar_hereketleri) are atomic; remove the .catch(()=>{}) swallowing on anbar_hereketleri so movement failures roll the row back. 3) Make importHesab create a finance_operations opening-balance entry (and importMusteri/importTechizatci materialize opening debt) so the balance services reflect imported opening data; or, if .qaliq/.borc are meant to be authoritative, change the balance services to seed from them — pick ONE source of truth. 4) Rework bank reconciliation to create a finance_operations inflow crediting the matched account and to allocate to oldest open invoice with proper overpayment->advance handling, mirroring the standard payment path. 5) Either implement the rollback action (write import_satirlari per row + a guarded rollback endpoint honoring 48h/rollback_de) or remove the misleading 'Geri qaytar' copy from idxal-tarixce. 6) Fix the mehsul-import stock copy to match documented behavior (decide set vs increment) and align template/instruction text. 7) Replace rol_id===9 backup gate with a rol_ad-based 'sahibkar' check. 8) Stop returning cleartext temp passwords in the import log; deliver them out-of-band or only as a one-time download to the importer.

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- As a 'baxan'/viewer user: open the inteqrasiya page (likely hidden), then directly POST a crafted .xlsx to /api/inteqrasiya/import/mehsul — expect it to be blocked but currently it imports. Same for /api/inteqrasiya/import/emekdas to attempt creating a user.
- As any non-owner user, hit /api/audit-log/export and /api/iscilier/kpi-export in the browser — confirm whether a CSV downloads (should be 403).
- Onboard a new tenant: import kassa-baslangic (one cash 5000, one bank 25000) and a customer with borc 250; then check dashboard cash total, /maliyye account list, and the customer's debt — they should reflect 5000/25000/250 but currently won't.
- Import the product catalog once, then import the same file again — verify stock is added (per UI text) vs reset to the sheet value (actual). Then re-import with one row's stok write forced to fail to observe a product created with missing stock and no movement record.
- Upload a bank statement matching one open invoice — verify a cash/bank inflow appears in the account ledger and the customer's receivable drops consistently; currently only odenilmis changes.
- On the idxal-tarixce page, look for the advertised 'Geri qaytar' button on a completed batch — confirm it is absent / non-functional.
- Double-click the 'İdxal et' button (or refresh during the importing step) and check idxal-tarixce for duplicate batches / doubled row updates.

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Confirm a low-privilege ('baxan') session can actually POST /api/inteqrasiya/import/mehsul and the import succeeds (no other guard) — verifies the permission-bypass severity.
- Confirm an authenticated non-owner can GET /api/audit-log/export and /api/iscilier/kpi-export and receive a 200 with data (not 403) — verifies these two routes leak to any role.
- Confirm via DB whether the 'sahibkar' role's rol_id is actually 9 for all tenants, and whether any other tenant has a non-owner role with id 9 — verifies the backup gate severity.
- After importing kassa-baslangic with a non-zero qaliq, confirm the account balance shown in /maliyye and dashboard remains unchanged (proving .qaliq is ignored by account-balance.ts).
- After importing a customer with borc>0, confirm debitor report / customer-balance shows 0 (proving .borc is ignored).
- Reproduce a double-submit (click Import twice / refresh during importing) to confirm duplicate import_partiyalari batches and duplicate row updates are created (no idempotency).
- Run bank recon against a matched order and confirm no finance_operations row is created while odenilmis increases (account stays flat, receivable drops).

## 20. SaaS / Tenant / Multi-company logic

**Xülasə:** The core multi-tenant mechanism (Prisma $extends auto-filter keyed off the TENANT_MODELS Set + AsyncLocalStorage tenant context via withTenant/runWithTenant) is well-designed and fail-closed: it throws when no tenant is in scope, injects sahibkar_id on reads and writes, and blocks tenant changes during update. Platform-admin cross-tenant access is properly gated (requirePlatformAdmin, rol_id===1, global role PK), signup provisions per-tenant roles, and the external marketplace API derives the tenant from the API key with defense-in-depth filtering. The prismaUnscoped + unstable_cache + manual-scoping pattern across dashboard/CRM/finance queries is intentional and consistently scoped (including parameterized raw SQL). HOWEVER the safety relies on a hand-maintained model list that has drifted: 16 sahibkar_id-bearing models are missing from TENANT_MODELS. For the campaigns/coupons/loyalty/gift-card cluster this is an exploitable CRITICAL cross-tenant leak — both reads (lists, stats) and sale-time writes (matcher applies foreign-tenant campaigns, coupon codes redeem cross-tenant, loyalty balances can be drained by UUID) cross tenant boundaries because the feature code assumed the extension would filter them. Separately, verifyApiKey/getKanalExtraServer use the scoped client against the tenant-scoped `ayarlar` model OUTSIDE any tenant context, which will throw tenant-guard and break the public marketplace API (HIGH, functional). Lower-severity: filial_gorunush read not tenant-filtered (medium), expire-campaigns cron skips auth when CRON_SECRET unset (low). Recommend: (1) code-generate TENANT_MODELS from the schema so every sahibkar_id column is auto-covered; (2) add the 6 marketing models immediately; (3) switch verifyApiKey/getKanalExtraServer to prismaUnscoped with manual sahibkar filter.

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ Core tenant filter via Prisma $extends injects sahibkar_id on reads, updates, deletes, creates, createMany, upsert for any model in TENANT_MODELS, and throws tenant-guard if no tenant in context — _lib/db/prisma.ts:41-99 (READ_OPS/WRITE_OPS sets lines 14-34, injection lines 62-93, fail-closed throw line 53-56)_
- ✅ withTenant() correctly resolves sahibkarId from the authenticated NextAuth session and binds AsyncLocalStorage; awaits fn() inside runWithTenant so PrismaPromise resolves while context is active — _lib/db/with-tenant.ts:17-49 (session.user.sahibkar_id line 41, await note lines 34-38)_
- ✅ upsert injection never allows changing sahibkar_id during update (deletes it from update payload) — _lib/db/prisma.ts:89-92_
- ✅ Platform-admin (cross-tenant) pages and all platform-admin server actions are guarded by requirePlatformAdmin() which redirects unless rol_id===1; roles.id is a global autoincrement PK so new tenants never receive id 1 — _lib/platform-admin/guard.ts:9-14; all 6 app/(dashboard)/platform-admin/*/page.tsx call it; features/platform-admin/actions.ts:11,28,60,89,103; prisma/schema.prisma:4453-4465 (roles.id autoincrement, only (sahibkar_id,ad) unique)_
- ✅ External marketplace API resolves sahibkarId FROM the API key (not client input) then binds runWithTenant and additionally filters sahibkar_id on every query (defense in depth) — _app/api/v1/marketplace/products/route.ts:53 (verifyApiKey), :82 runWithTenant, :92/96/103/110/138 redundant sahibkar_id filters_
- ✅ Signup creates a new tenant with prismaUnscoped inside a $transaction, clones the system role per-tenant, and assigns the owner the newly-cloned role id (not 1) — _features/auth/signup-action.ts:51 $transaction, :100-106 cloned role with sahibkar_id, :128 rol_id: sahibkarRolId_
- ✅ prismaUnscoped + unstable_cache pattern in dashboard/nezaret/elaqe/tapshiriqlar queries consistently applies manual sahibkar_id filtering (including raw $queryRaw parameterized by sahibkarId) — _features/elaqe/queries.ts:252,776-819 (raw query sahibkar_id ::uuid line 796,812); features/dashboard/queries.ts:788 scope obj; features/tapshiriqlar/queries.ts:334-341,374-378_
- ✅ defekt_qeydleri, vezifeler, satinalma_teklif, team_* and filial_mesaj features (NOT in TENANT_MODELS) are defensively hand-scoped with explicit sahibkar_id on reads and writes — _features/anbar/defekt-actions.ts:174,271; features/iscilier/vezife-actions.ts:33,77,82,109,114; features/satinalma/queries.ts:10,37,56; features/team/queries.ts:9,91,121; features/ayar/queries.ts:64,75,123; filial_gorunush writes validate both filial owners ayar/actions.ts:561-565_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🔴 **[critical · permission] Cross-tenant data leak: campaigns/coupons/loyalty_cards/loyalty_tx/gift_cards/campaign_usage are NOT in TENANT_MODELS and their queries pass NO sahibkar_id — every tenant reads/affects every other tenant's marketing data**
  - Detal: campaigns, coupons, gift_cards, loyalty_cards, loyalty_tx, campaign_usage all declare a non-nullable sahibkar_id in the schema but are absent from TENANT_MODELS (Set in lib/db/tenant-models.ts). Because the Prisma $extends filter only acts on models in that Set (prisma.ts:46 `if (!isTenantModel(model)) return query(args)`), these models receive NO automatic sahibkar_id filter. The kampaniyalar feature relies on the extension and passes no manual filter: getCampaigns (queries.ts:12-16), getCampaignStats counts (queries.ts:39-42), getAllCoupons (queries.ts:111), getLoyaltyMembers (queries.ts:76), getLoyaltyOverview (queries.ts:249), getGiftCards (queries.ts:329) all return ALL tenants' rows. The comment at queries.ts:56 ('kept silently in case Prisma extension fails') shows the developer assumed the extension covers these, but it does not. Cross-tenant exposure of every business's promotions, coupon codes, loyalty balances and gift-card balances.
  - Sübut: lib/db/tenant-models.ts:6-220 (campaigns/coupons/loyalty_* absent) vs prisma/schema.prisma:6378-6493; features/kampaniyalar/queries.ts:12,39-42,76,111,249,329
  - Fayllar: lib/db/tenant-models.ts, features/kampaniyalar/queries.ts, prisma/schema.prisma

- 🔴 **[critical · data-flow] Cross-tenant write/lookup at POS: campaign matcher, coupon redemption, loyalty bonus apply/accrue act on other tenants' rows by id**
  - Detal: Because the same 6 models bypass the tenant filter, sale-time logic crosses tenants: findApplicableCampaigns loads ALL active campaigns across ALL tenants (matcher.ts:53-58, no sahibkar_id even though sahibkarId is read at line 49) and applies them to the cart, corrupting discounts/bonuses/free-shipping. applyCoupon finds a coupon by code globally (matcher.ts:272) so a coupon from tenant B is redeemable in tenant A. commitCampaignApplications updates campaigns.current_uses and loyalty_cards balance by id without tenant scope (matcher.ts:223-226,230,233). applyBonusToSale decrements loyalty_cards by kartId with no sahibkar_id (actions.ts:345-353) — a user in tenant A can drain a loyalty balance belonging to tenant B by supplying that card UUID. bulkGenerateCoupons/broadcast look up a parent campaign by id alone (actions.ts:93,170), letting tenant A generate coupons / broadcast against tenant B's campaign.
  - Sübut: features/kampaniyalar/matcher.ts:53-58,212-226,230-252,272; features/kampaniyalar/actions.ts:93,170,345-353
  - Fayllar: features/kampaniyalar/matcher.ts, features/kampaniyalar/actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · functional] External marketplace API broken: verifyApiKey and getKanalExtraServer call scoped prisma on tenant-scoped `ayarlar` outside any tenant context → tenant-guard throws**
  - Detal: In app/api/v1/marketplace/products/route.ts the route calls verifyApiKey(key,kanal) at line 53 and getKanalExtraServer(sahibkarId,kanal) at line 57 — BEFORE entering runWithTenant() at line 82. proxy.ts does NOT establish a tenant AsyncLocalStorage context. verifyApiKey uses the scoped client prisma.ayarlar.findMany (api-key-actions.ts:125) and getKanalExtraServer uses prisma.ayarlar.findUnique (kanal-extra.ts:44). `ayarlar` IS a TENANT_MODEL, so with no tenant in context the extension throws `[tenant-guard] No tenant in context for ayarlar.findMany` (prisma.ts:48-56). verifyApiKey also intends a deliberate cross-tenant scan (comment line 123) which the scoped client would never permit. Net effect: the public marketplace products/orders API (and any caller of these two helpers outside a session) errors at runtime. They should use prismaUnscoped (consistent with the cross-tenant-scan intent).
  - Sübut: app/api/v1/marketplace/products/route.ts:53,57,82; features/qiymet-kanal/api-key-actions.ts:125-128; features/qiymet-kanal/kanal-extra.ts:44; lib/db/prisma.ts:48-56; proxy.ts:9-13
  - Fayllar: features/qiymet-kanal/api-key-actions.ts, features/qiymet-kanal/kanal-extra.ts, app/api/v1/marketplace/products/route.ts, app/api/v1/marketplace/orders/[kanal]/route.ts

- 🟠 **[high · design] TENANT_MODELS list is hand-maintained and already out of sync — 16 models with sahibkar_id are missing; future schema additions will silently leak**
  - Detal: tenant-models.ts:1-2 acknowledges the Set is 'Auto-extracted ... (TODO)' but no generator exists, so it drifts from the schema. A full cross-reference of prisma/schema.prisma shows 16 models declaring sahibkar_id but absent from the Set: audit_log_outbox, campaign_usage, campaigns, coupons, defekt_qeydleri, filial_gorunush, filial_mesaj, gift_cards, loyalty_cards, loyalty_tx, satinalma_teklif, sened_nomre_counter, team_ayar, team_kanal, team_mesaj_log, vezifeler. Several (defekt, vezife, satinalma, team, filial_mesaj) happen to be hand-scoped today, but the campaigns/loyalty cluster is NOT — and any future code touching the still-safe ones without manual scoping will silently leak because the safety net does not cover them. The list should be code-generated from the schema (every model with a sahibkar_id column) at build time.
  - Sübut: lib/db/tenant-models.ts:1-2 (TODO generator) ; schema vs set diff: audit_log_outbox, campaign_usage, campaigns, coupons, defekt_qeydleri, filial_gorunush, filial_mesaj, gift_cards, loyalty_cards, loyalty_tx, satinalma_teklif, sened_nomre_counter, team_ayar, team_kanal, team_mesaj_log, vezifeler
  - Fayllar: lib/db/tenant-models.ts, prisma/schema.prisma

- 🟡 **[medium · permission] filial_gorunush read matrix not tenant-filtered (scopes only by source_filial_id)**
  - Detal: getFilialGorunushMatrix queries prisma.filial_gorunush.findMany({ where: { source_filial_id: sourceFilialId } }) with NO sahibkar_id (queries.ts:147-149). filial_gorunush is not in TENANT_MODELS, so no auto-filter. If sourceFilialId could be supplied as a foreign tenant's filial id, the inter-branch visibility rules of that tenant would be returned. Lower severity because the data is branch-visibility configuration (not financial) and the value normally originates from a validated page context; the write paths (toggleFilialGorunush/bulkSetFilialGorunush) DO validate both filial owners (ayar/actions.ts:561-565). Should still add sahibkar_id to the read where clause.
  - Sübut: features/ayar/queries.ts:147-149 ; (writes safe) features/ayar/actions.ts:561-565
  - Fayllar: features/ayar/queries.ts

- ⚪ **[low · permission] expire-campaigns cron skips auth entirely when CRON_SECRET env is unset**
  - Detal: The cron guard is `if (process.env.CRON_SECRET && auth !== 'Bearer '+CRON_SECRET) return 401` (route.ts:13). When CRON_SECRET is not configured the entire check is skipped, allowing any unauthenticated caller to trigger the global cross-tenant campaign-expiry sweep via prismaUnscoped.campaigns.updateMany. The operation is idempotent (only flips expired statuses) so impact is limited, but the auth should fail-closed if the secret is missing.
  - Sübut: app/api/cron/expire-campaigns/route.ts:12-15,19-25
  - Fayllar: app/api/cron/expire-campaigns/route.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 Can tenant A read/modify tenant B's campaigns, coupons, loyalty cards, gift cards via the kampaniyalar module?: Yes. These models are not in TENANT_MODELS so the $extends filter is skipped, and the feature code passes no manual sahibkar_id. Lists/stats return all tenants; sale-time matcher/coupon/bonus paths read and write other tenants' rows by id/code. — _lib/db/tenant-models.ts:6-220 (absent) vs prisma/schema.prisma:6378-6493; features/kampaniyalar/queries.ts:12,39,76,111,249,329; matcher.ts:53,272,223,345_
- 🔐 Can a non-platform-admin reach /platform-admin/* cross-tenant data?: No — guarded. requirePlatformAdmin() on every page and action redirects unless rol_id===1, and roles.id is a global autoincrement so new tenants get higher ids. — _lib/platform-admin/guard.ts:9-14; prisma/schema.prisma:4453-4465; features/platform-admin/actions.ts:11,28,60,89,103_
- 🔐 Does the external marketplace API trust client-supplied tenant id?: No — tenant is resolved from the API key via verifyApiKey, not from input, and queries redundantly filter sahibkar_id. (But verifyApiKey itself is broken, see bug list.) — _app/api/v1/marketplace/products/route.ts:53,82,92-138_
- 🔐 expire-campaigns cron authorization: Auth check is bypassed entirely if CRON_SECRET env is unset (not fail-closed); allows anonymous trigger of the global expiry sweep. — _app/api/cron/expire-campaigns/route.ts:13_

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** The campaigns/loyalty leak bleeds directly into the sales (POS) flow: findApplicableCampaigns and applyCoupon are invoked during checkout, so cross-tenant campaigns and coupon codes corrupt discount/bonus/free-shipping calculations on real sales, and commitCampaignApplications / applyBonusToSale mutate other tenants' campaign usage counters and loyalty balances inside sale-time logic. campaign_usage also joins satis_sifarisleri for ROI (queries.ts:294-301) — but satis_sifarisleri IS tenant-scoped, so a cross-tenant campaign_usage row would dangle/mismatch revenue attribution. The broken verifyApiKey/getKanalExtraServer would take down the entire marketplace product/order feed integration (Wolt/Birmarket/Tap.az/site adapters).

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) Make tenant isolation fail-safe by construction: implement the missing scripts/extract-tenant-models.ts to generate TENANT_MODELS from prisma/schema.prisma (every model declaring a sahibkar_id column), run it in the build/prisma-generate step, and commit the generated set. Immediately add the 6 leaking marketing models (campaigns, coupons, gift_cards, loyalty_cards, loyalty_tx, campaign_usage) — plus the other 10 currently-only-hand-scoped models — to TENANT_MODELS so the $extends filter covers them. After adding, audit kampaniyalar/queries.ts, matcher.ts and actions.ts: the existing by-id lookups (e.g. campaigns.findFirst({where:{id}}), loyalty_cards.updateMany({where:{id:kartId}})) will then auto-scope and correctly return null/zero-count for foreign-tenant ids; verify no write path double-injects a conflicting sahibkar_id. 2) Fix the marketplace API: change verifyApiKey (api-key-actions.ts:125) and getKanalExtraServer (kanal-extra.ts:44) to use prismaUnscoped (their cross-tenant scan / pre-context call is intentional) — verifyApiKey already returns the owning sahibkar_id, and getKanalExtraServer already takes sahibkarId, so add explicit where filters there for getKanalExtraServer. 3) Add sahibkar_id to getFilialGorunushMatrix's filial_gorunush.findMany where clause (ayar/queries.ts:147). 4) Make expire-campaigns (and any cron with the same pattern) fail-closed: return 401 when CRON_SECRET is unset. Re-run the schema-vs-set diff in CI to prevent future drift.

## CROSS: Satış data axını (bütün satış növləri → stok/maliyyə/müştəri/qaimə)

**Xülasə:** Traced all sale types from server action to stock/finance/customer/qaimə. The two high-volume canonical paths are solid and atomic: POS createSale and the manual marketplace createMarketSatis correctly bundle stock decrement + warehouse movement + finance_operations (or pending payout) + account recalc + customer recalc in one $transaction, correctly distinguishing cash/card/bank (credits the account, no debt) from nisyə (no cash, registers receivable). FIFO payment allocation in receivePartialPayment is correct (oldest invoices first, only surplus to avans — the 2+100/10 AZN case is handled). cancelSale properly reverses stock/cash/balance and soft-deletes. Kredit-şirkəti sale correctly creates no customer debt. However I found several real cross-module data-integrity holes: (1) CRITICAL — the webhook marketplace order API route (/api/v1/marketplace/orders/[kanal]) is a parallel, non-transactional implementation that decrements the wrong stock field (mehsullar.stok_cemi, not the per-warehouse `stok` table used everywhere else), writes no anbar_hereketleri, and creates no finance_operations or customer balance — diverging stock, ledger, and finance. (2) HIGH — approval-gated sales (satis_qaime / maya-altı) are never truly finalized: after approval the sale just flips to 'yeni' and no wired action decrements stock or records finance/balance. (3) HIGH — recordSalePayment and applySplitPayment write only kassa_emeliyyatlari (no finance_operations), so collected follow-up/split money never reaches the finance_operations-derived account balance. Medium issues: teklif→sale conversion leaves money in limbo (negd + odenilmis 0, no finance, not a receivable); recordKreditPayment uses a manual qaliq increment violating the source-of-truth pattern; satis-yeni's finance leg is best-effort try/catch so a sale can commit without its finance_operations. These are code-grounded; the dynamic balance/report consequences (e.g. exact drift after a recalc cascade) need live DB confirmation.

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts; features/pos/sale-action.ts; features/ticaret/market-satis-action.ts; features/ticaret/kredit-yeni-actions.ts; features/ticaret/kredit-actions.ts (acceptKreditPayment, recordKreditPayment); features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts (recordSalePayment, changeSaleStatus, cancelSale, bulkChangeSaleStatus); features/ticaret/split-payment-actions.ts; features/ticaret/teklif-actions.ts (offer→sale conversion, lines 270-369); features/ticaret/access-guard.ts; features/maliyye/actions.ts (receivePartialPayment FIFO, lines 1060-1326); features/tesdiq/actions.ts (approveRequest + propagateDocumentApproval, lines 39-220); features/tesdiq/settings.ts (satis_qaime_aktiv default); lib/balance/customer-balance.ts; lib/balance/account-balance.ts; lib/db/stock-guards.ts; lib/db/sened-nomre.ts; lib/balance/product-stock.ts; features/maliyye/cashflow-queries.ts (data source); app/api/v1/marketplace/orders/[kanal]/route.ts; prisma/schema.prisma (finance_operations, satis_sifarisleri, mehsullar.stok_cemi)

**Əhatə boşluqları:** /marketplace/webhook-orders accept/finalize server action (linked from the webhook route notification) was not opened — it may complete the real stock/finance posting for webhook orders.; qaytarma (return) reversal flows (qaytarma-tez-actions.ts) were only grepped, not fully read — return→stock/finance/customer reversal correctness not deeply audited (adjacent scope).; operation-detail-action.ts and cancel-operation-action.ts reversal of finance_payment_allocations was only partially read (lines 84-122) — full revert-on-cancel correctness not verified.; POS offline-queue replay (lib/pos/offline-queue.ts) idempotency on reconnect not examined.; marketplace payout acceptance (acceptMarketplacePayout / markPayoutReceived bank-credit leg) read only partially (lines 1339-1381).; Front-end UI gating (which roles see which sale buttons) was confirmed only for status/payment buttons; full UI permission gating per sale type not exhaustively mapped.

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ POS sale (createSale) is fully atomic: stock decrement + anbar_hereketleri + kassa_emeliyyatlari + finance_operations + account recalc + customer balance recalc all inside one $transaction with FOR UPDATE stock lock. — _features/pos/sale-action.ts:106-323 ($transaction), :219-238 (safeStockDecrement+anbar_hereketleri), :242-308 (finance_operations + recalculateAccountBalance), :317-319 (recalculateCustomerBalance)_
- ✅ Credit (nisyə) sale correctly creates NO cash inflow and registers a receivable: POS nisye sets odenilmis=0 and skips kassa/finance_operations; customer-balance counts only odenis_nov IN ('nisye','borc'). — _features/pos/sale-action.ts:193 (odenilmis 0 for nisye), :242 (if odenis_nov !== 'nisye' for kassa/finance), lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:72_
- ✅ Cash/card/bank POS sale credits the matching account by nov fallback and creates no customer debt (odenis_nov negd/kart/kecirme not in nisye/borc filter). — _features/pos/sale-action.ts:275-283 (hesab fallback by nov negd), lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:72_
- ✅ FIFO payment allocation is correct: open invoices processed oldest-first, remaining applied per invoice, only surplus after ALL invoices becomes avans (matches the 2+100/10 AZN expectation). — _features/maliyye/actions.ts:1106-1156 (orderBy tarix asc, apply=min(remain,qalig), toAdvance=max(0,remain)), :1237-1245 (avans only when toAdvance>0)_
- ✅ Sale cancellation restores stock + reverses cash (kassa qaytarma) + recalculates customer balance + soft-deletes (deleted_at/legv), inside one $transaction; blocked if payments attached unless acknowledged. — _features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts:213-306 ($transaction), :247-268 (stock restore + medaxil movement), :271-285 (cash reversal), :288-305 (legv+soft-delete+recalc)_
- ✅ Soft-deleted / legv sales excluded from customer balance and credit status (deleted_at IS NULL, status NOT IN legv/qaytarilib). — _lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:69-71, features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts:748 (status<>'legv')_
- ✅ Account balance is single-source-of-truth from finance_operations (status='aktiv', deleted_at IS NULL); sale paths call recalculateAccountBalance after writing the op rather than ad-hoc math. — _lib/balance/account-balance.ts:44-122, called at features/pos/sale-action.ts:305 and satis-yeni-actions.ts:450_
- ✅ Marketplace manual sale (createMarketSatis) is atomic and correctly does NOT credit bank immediately — only a finance_marketplace_payments 'gozlenir' row is created; bank credited later on payout acceptance. — _features/ticaret/market-satis-action.ts:114-269 ($transaction), :236-255 (pending payout, no finance_operations), features/maliyye/actions.ts:1339+ (markPayoutReceived)_
- ✅ Kredit-şirkəti sale (createKreditSatis) is atomic, decrements stock, and correctly creates NO customer debt (odenis_nov='kredit' excluded from balance filter); bank money recorded later via recordKreditPayment. — _features/ticaret/kredit-yeni-actions.ts:78-207 ($transaction), :108 (odenis_nov kredit), :198-204 (recalc), lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:72 (kredit not in filter)_
- ✅ Every sale-creating/mutating server action enforces a backend permission guard before any DB work (defense against direct server-action invocation). — _satis-yeni-actions.ts:103, pos/sale-action.ts:55-57, market-satis-action.ts:74, kredit-yeni-actions.ts:62, satis-actions.ts:34/155/204, split-payment-actions.ts:32_
- ✅ Doc numbers are race-safe via PG next_sened_nomre / counter-table UPSERT inside the tx, reducing duplicate-number risk on double submit. — _lib/db/sened-nomre.ts:56-98; POS also idempotent dup-check exists for payments (maliyye/actions.ts:1078-1092)_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🔴 **[critical · data-flow] Webhook marketplace order route bypasses the entire canonical sale flow (wrong stock table, no movement, no finance, no transaction, no balance recalc)**
  - Detal: POST /api/v1/marketplace/orders/[kanal] creates a satis_sifarisleri with status 'tesdiqlendi' but: (1) decrements mehsullar.stok_cemi directly instead of the per-warehouse `stok` table that lib/balance/product-stock.ts treats as source-of-truth, so POS/new-sale/reports see a DIFFERENT available quantity; (2) writes NO anbar_hereketleri movement (warehouse ledger silently diverges); (3) creates NO finance_operations and does NOT touch customer balance even when a customer is upserted — revenue/receivable is invisible to account balance & finance reports; (4) the sale header sets no anbar_id and the line writes have no stock guard; (5) the stock update and sale create are NOT wrapped in a $transaction (sequential prisma calls), so a mid-stream failure leaves stok decremented with no sale or a partial sale. Duplicate-guard relies on audit_log lookup, not a unique constraint.
  - Sübut: app/api/v1/marketplace/orders/[kanal]/route.ts:129-135 (mutates mehsullar.stok_cemi), :178-205 (create sale+lines, no anbar_hereketleri, no finance), no $transaction wrapping :131/:178; contrast lib/balance/product-stock.ts:43 (reads `stok` table) and market-satis-action.ts:212-232 (canonical path uses safeStockDecrement+anbar_hereketleri)
  - Fayllar: app/api/v1/marketplace/orders/[kanal]/route.ts, lib/balance/product-stock.ts, features/ticaret/market-satis-action.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Approval-gated sale is never finalized: after satis_qaime approval the sale loses stock decrement, finance movement, and customer balance impact**
  - Detal: createOrUpdateSatisYeni skips the whole FINALIZE SYNC block when effectiveNeedsApproval is true (sale saved as 'tesdiq_gozleyir', no stock/finance/balance). On approval, propagateDocumentApproval only flips status 'tesdiq_gozleyir'→'yeni' — it does NOT decrement stock, create finance_operations, or recalc customer balance. The code comment says the salesperson must press 'Tamamla', but the only Tamamla/status actions wired in the UI are changeSaleStatus (status string only — no stock/finance/balance) and recordSalePayment (kassa + odenilmis only — also no stock decrement and no finance_operations). There is NO finalize path that decrements stock for an already-created 'yeni' sale. Net effect when satis_qaime approval (or maya-altı approval) is enabled: an approved sale ships nothing from stock, records no revenue, and (if nisyə) records no receivable. Default satis_qaime_aktiv=false makes this dormant until an owner enables document approval, but maya-altı 'approval' action arms it independently.
  - Sübut: features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts:314 (if !data.qaralama && !effectiveNeedsApproval gates ALL stock/finance/balance), :143-168 (maya-altı sets needs-approval), features/tesdiq/actions.ts:133-149 (approval only sets status='yeni'), features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts:154-197 (changeSaleStatus updates status only), :28-148 (recordSalePayment: no safeStockDecrement, no finance_operations)
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts, features/tesdiq/actions.ts, features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Follow-up sale payments (recordSalePayment, applySplitPayment) write kassa_emeliyyatlari but no finance_operations → cash/bank account balance under-reports collected money**
  - Detal: maliye_hesablari.qaliq is recomputed exclusively from finance_operations (account-balance.ts reads only finance_operations, never kassa_emeliyyatlari). recordSalePayment and applySplitPayment create a kassa_emeliyyatlari row and bump satis_sifarisleri.odenilmis (so customer balance and the odenilmis-based cashflow report stay correct) but create NO finance_operations row and never call recalculateAccountBalance. Consequently money collected on a credit sale's follow-up/split payment is NOT reflected in the bank/cash account ledger qaliq; worse, the next recalculateAccountBalance triggered by any other op on that account reads finance_operations only and will permanently exclude this payment, causing a real account-balance shortfall vs. money actually in the drawer.
  - Sübut: lib/balance/account-balance.ts:48-102 (FROM finance_operations only); features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts:90-126 (recordSalePayment: kassa_emeliyyatlari + odenilmis, no finance_operations); features/ticaret/split-payment-actions.ts:74-96 (applySplitPayment: kassa_emeliyyatlari only); contrast POS sale-action.ts:284-307 which DOES write finance_operations + recalc
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts, features/ticaret/split-payment-actions.ts, lib/balance/account-balance.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Teklif→satış conversion decrements stock but records neither cash receipt nor receivable (money in limbo)**
  - Detal: convertTeklifToSale creates the sale with odenis_nov='negd' and odenilmis=0, decrements stock and writes anbar_hereketleri, but creates NO kassa_emeliyyatlari / finance_operations. Because odenis_nov='negd' is NOT in the customer-balance filter ('nisye','borc'), the unpaid amount is also NOT counted as a receivable. So after conversion the goods have left stock while the amount appears neither as collected cash (account balance) nor as customer debt — invisible to both finance and debitor reports until someone manually runs recordSalePayment (which itself has the no-finance_operations gap above).
  - Sübut: features/ticaret/teklif-actions.ts:284-296 (odenis_nov negd, odenilmis 0), :300-336 (stock decremented), :343-349 (only balance recalc, no finance/kassa); lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:72 (negd excluded)
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/teklif-actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] recordKreditPayment uses manual maliye_hesablari.qaliq increment instead of source-of-truth recalc (drift / pattern violation)**
  - Detal: recordKreditPayment creates a finance_operations 'qaime' row (status default 'aktiv') AND additionally does a manual `qaliq: { increment: tutulan }` on maliye_hesablari. account-balance.ts documentation explicitly warns 'manual delta YOX'. Since calculateAccountBalance already counts the new aktiv finance_operation, the manual increment is redundant; the moment any later recalculateAccountBalance runs for that account it overwrites qaliq with the absolute (already-correct) figure — so the manual bump is silently discarded, but in the meantime two competing update mechanisms touch the same field. Unlike every other path it never calls recalculateAccountBalance, so the documented single-source-of-truth invariant is violated for this flow.
  - Sübut: features/ticaret/kredit-actions.ts:137-162 (finance_operations create + manual `qaliq: { increment }`, no recalculateAccountBalance), contrast lib/balance/account-balance.ts:5-25/128-161 and pos/sale-action.ts:304-307
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/kredit-actions.ts, lib/balance/account-balance.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · error] satis-yeni finance_operations & kassa writes are best-effort try/catch — finance can silently fail while the sale commits**
  - Detal: In createOrUpdateSatisYeni the kassa_emeliyyatlari and finance_operations writes are wrapped in try/catch that only console.warn and continue (no rollback, and unlike POS sale-action.ts no compensating audit 'finance_operation_skip' row). So a B2B new-sale can decrement stock and report itself paid (odenilmis set) while no finance_operations row exists — the account balance and finance reports under-report the revenue with no surfaced error. The atomicity guarantee ('all in one transaction, partial failure rolls back') is therefore not honored for the finance leg of this path.
  - Sübut: features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts:372-391 (kassa try/catch warn-only), :394-456 (finance_operations try/catch warn-only, no audit-skip); contrast pos/sale-action.ts:309-313/352-363 (records financeOpSkipped audit)
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts

- ⚪ **[low · functional] Qarışıq (mixed) payment at sale-creation time is not supported; only single odenis_nov captured, mixed split is a separate post-sale action with the finance gap**
  - Detal: Both createSale (POS) and createOrUpdateSatisYeni accept a single odenis_nov enum; there is no atomic mixed cash+card+nisye capture inside sale creation. Mixed payment is only achievable post-hoc via applySplitPayment, which inherits the 'no finance_operations' gap (bug above). createOrUpdateSatisYeni's partial-payment hybrid does handle one paid portion + remainder to nisyə correctly, but cannot record two simultaneous tender types (e.g. 50 cash + 50 card) on the originating sale.
  - Sübut: features/pos/sale-action.ts:29 (single enum), features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts:64 (single enum) + :348-457 (single paid portion), features/ticaret/split-payment-actions.ts:74-96 (post-sale split, no finance_operations)
  - Fayllar: features/pos/sale-action.ts, features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts, features/ticaret/split-payment-actions.ts

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] getCustomerCreditStatus credit-limit gate omits 'qaytarilib' from exclusion, slightly inconsistent with customer-balance source-of-truth**
  - Detal: The new-sale credit panel/limit query in getCustomerCreditStatus filters status <> 'legv' only, while the canonical lib/balance/customer-balance.ts excludes both 'legv' AND 'qaytarilib'. A fully-returned (qaytarilib) credit sale would still count toward the credit-limit pre-check here but not toward the canonical alacaq, a minor divergence that can over-block a new nisyə sale.
  - Sübut: features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts:748 (status <> 'legv' only) vs lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:70 (status NOT IN ('legv','qaytarilib'))
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts, lib/balance/customer-balance.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 Webhook marketplace order route auth: The order-creating route authenticates only via webhook HMAC signature and then runs with a synthetic system identity (istifadeciId all-zero, icazeler: []). This is acceptable for a signed webhook, but combined with the fact that it bypasses all stock/finance integrity, a leaked/weak per-sahibkar secret allows unauthenticated creation of revenue-affecting sale records (and stock mutation) with no per-user permission gating and no $transaction. — _app/api/v1/marketplace/orders/[kanal]/route.ts:44-56 (signature-only auth), :80-86 (runWithTenant with empty icazeler)_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** app/api/v1/marketplace/orders/[kanal]/route.ts, features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts, features/ticaret/split-payment-actions.ts, features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts, features/tesdiq/actions.ts, features/ticaret/teklif-actions.ts, features/ticaret/kredit-actions.ts

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** Consider a UNIQUE constraint on (sahibkar_id, marketplace_platform, external_id) for webhook orders so duplicate prevention does not rely solely on an audit_log lookup.

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** Highest cross-module risk is stock-availability divergence: lib/balance/product-stock.ts and all canonical sale paths use the per-warehouse `stok` table + anbar_hereketleri, but the webhook marketplace route mutates the denormalized mehsullar.stok_cemi aggregate instead — so POS, new-sale, product card, warehouse and reports will show inconsistent available quantities for any SKU sold via webhook, and the movement ledger will not reconcile. Second is finance divergence: account balance (maliye_hesablari.qaliq) is derived solely from finance_operations, yet several collection paths (recordSalePayment, applySplitPayment, the webhook route) record money only in kassa_emeliyyatlari/odenilmis or nowhere, so the bank/cash ledger and P&L drift from cash actually collected, while customer-debt (driven by odenilmis recalc) stays correct — meaning debitor reports and account balances can disagree about the same payment.

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) Webhook route: rewrite to reuse the canonical createMarketSatis/safeStockDecrement path inside a single $transaction — decrement the `stok` table per anbar (require/resolve an anbar_id), write anbar_hereketleri, create finance_operations (or pending payout) and recalc account+customer balance; replace the audit_log-based dedupe with a DB unique constraint. 2) Approval finalize: add a real finalizeSale server action (or extend changeSaleStatus→'tamamlandi') that performs the stock decrement + finance_operations + balance recalc for a 'yeni'/approved sale exactly once (guard against double-finalize via a state flag), and wire the UI 'Tamamla' button to it. 3) recordSalePayment & applySplitPayment: after writing kassa_emeliyyatlari, also create a finance_operations 'qaime' daxil row tied to satis_id and call recalculateAccountBalance (mirroring POS sale-action.ts). 4) teklif→sale: either create the matching finance/kassa entry on conversion or set odenis_nov='nisye' so the amount registers as a receivable. 5) recordKreditPayment: drop the manual `qaliq: { increment }` and call recalculateAccountBalance instead. 6) satis-yeni: on finance try/catch failure, write a compensating 'finance_operation_skip' audit row like POS does (or move finance write to be non-swallowed so the tx rolls back). Re-verify each with the affected report/balance query after fixes.

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- Enable document approval for sales (Ayarlar→təsdiq satis_qaime_aktiv=true), create a new-sale qaimə that needs approval, approve it, then check whether stock actually decreased, a finance_operations row exists, and (if nisyə) the customer debt appears — expected to FAIL today (none happen).
- Create a credit (nisyə) sale, then collect a partial follow-up payment via the sale row 'Ödəniş' dialog; verify the cash/bank account qaliq increases by the paid amount (expected to FAIL — only kassa_emeliyyatlari + odenilmis change, account qaliq unchanged).
- POST a signed order to /api/v1/marketplace/orders/[kanal] and then open the product in POS/anbar — verify available stock matches; expected mismatch because the route changes mehsullar.stok_cemi only, not the `stok` table.
- Convert a kommersial təklif to a sale, then check debitor report and account balance — the amount should appear somewhere; expected it appears in neither until a manual payment is recorded.
- Make a payment of 10 AZN to a customer who has a 2 AZN and a 100 AZN open nisyə invoice; confirm 2 AZN invoice fully closed and 8 AZN applied to the 100 AZN invoice with 0 avans (expected PASS per code).
- Apply a split payment (e.g. 50 cash + 50 card) to an existing sale and verify the bank/cash account balances reflect 100 AZN (expected FAIL — no finance_operations written).

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Exact account-balance drift magnitude after recordSalePayment/applySplitPayment plus a subsequent recalculateAccountBalance cascade (code shows the payment is excluded from finance_operations; confirm the resulting numeric shortfall in maliye_hesablari.qaliq on a live DB).
- Whether any cron/nightly job (e.g. recalculateAllAccountBalances) or another action reconciles kassa_emeliyyatlari into finance_operations — none found in scope, but a DB trigger could exist outside the files reviewed.
- Whether the webhook marketplace orders are surfaced/finalized by a separate /marketplace/webhook-orders accept flow that later writes the real `stok`/finance entries (the route links to that page but its accept action was not in scope).
- Real-world frequency of the approval-gated finalize gap depends on whether owners enable satis_qaime_aktiv and on maya-altı 'approval' action config — verify against live tenant settings.
