## CROSS: Ödəniş allocation (qaimələrə bağlanma, avans məntiqi)

**Xülasə:** The core payment-allocation algorithm is CORRECT and the user's Test 3 PASSES in code: receivePartialPayment (features/maliyye/actions.ts L1106-1156) loads active open nisye invoices ordered by tarix asc, applies apply=min(remain, qalig) per invoice in FIFO order, decrements remain, and only the leftover AFTER all invoices are covered becomes advance (toAdvance=Math.max(0,remain), L1156). For 2+100 invoices and a 10 payment, invoice#1 is closed (2), invoice#100 gets 8 (qalig 92), advance=0 — not the buggy '8 advance' behavior. The whole thing runs in one $transaction (L1159-1285) with idempotency, allocation records, status auto-close, balance + account recalc, and audit. The same cascade is reused identically across payAllOpenInvoices, paySupplierAllOpen/paySupplierInvoice, and elaqe recordContactPayment, and the UI preview mirrors it. Debt everywhere is derived from SUM(son_mebleg - odenilmis), so allocation flows correctly into debtor/customer/dashboard. THREE issues found: (1) HIGH — cancelling a payment never reverses the avans surplus it created (no allocation row for the advance portion; cancel-operation-action.ts only reverses odenilmis), leaving orphaned/phantom advance; (2) MEDIUM — calculateCustomerBalance hardcodes avans=0 (customer-balance.ts L95), so existing advance is never netted into reported debt and is not auto-applied to new invoices, overstating receivables; (3) permission — elaqe recordContactPayment performs the same debt-clearing allocation but is gated on 'musteri.duzelt' instead of 'odenis.qebul', a bypass of the payment-acceptance gate.

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** features/maliyye/actions.ts (receivePartialPayment L1061-1326; payAllOpenInvoices L1459-1620; paySupplierAllOpen L1632-1793; paySupplierInvoice L1804-1996; applyAdvanceToInvoice L2007-2069); lib/balance/customer-balance.ts (calculateCustomerBalance L50-108; recalculateCustomerBalance L118-148; recalculateAllCustomerBalances L155-228); features/maliyye/customer-invoices-action.ts (getCustomerOpenInvoicesAction L27-86); features/maliyye/components/nisye-payment-quick.tsx (full, incl. preview L260-322 + overpay confirm L128-138); features/maliyye/queries.ts (getDebtors L639-752); features/maliyye/cancel-operation-action.ts (reversal L60-129); features/elaqe/actions.ts (recordContactPayment L365-469); features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts (sonradan ödəniş L70-148); features/ticaret/kredit-actions.ts (acceptKreditPayment L18-64; recordKreditPayment L72-208); app/(dashboard)/maliyye/debitor/page.tsx (L80-99); app/(dashboard)/elaqe/musteriler/[id]/page.tsx (unpaid/borc/avans L118-127, NisyePaymentQuick wiring L290-535); features/maliyye/components/debtor-row-expanded.tsx (borc/NisyePaymentQuick wiring L83-181); prisma/schema.prisma (model finance_payment_allocations L1734; kontragentler.avans L2809; model satis_sifarisleri L5168)

**Əhatə boşluqları:** lib/pos/* and ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts / market-satis-action.ts / split-payment-actions.ts — POS/new-sale immediate-payment paths were not opened; whether they ever route surplus to advance or auto-consume existing avans is unverified (split-payment-actions.ts:93 increments odenilmis for the immediately-paid part but its advance handling was not read).; qaytarma-tez-actions.ts L234/L523 increment odenilmis (return-driven) — return→allocation interaction not examined in this payment-allocation scope.; Secondary sort/tie-break determinism for same-date invoices at the DB level (only tarix asc specified) — flagged for live verification.; supplier-balance.ts internals (paySupplierAllOpen/paySupplierInvoice cascade verified at action level, but supplier balance derivation not opened).

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ Test 3 PASSES in code: a payment is applied to OPEN invoices FIRST in FIFO order (tarix asc), surplus → advance only after ALL invoices are covered. For 2+100 invoices with a 10 payment: invoice#1 gets 2 (closed), invoice#100 gets 8 (partial, qalig 92), toAdvance=0. — _features/maliyye/actions.ts receivePartialPayment — openSales ordered by tarix asc (L1115), filter qalig>0.01 (L1127), loop apply=Math.min(remain, inv.qalig) then remain-=apply (L1142-1154), toAdvance=Math.max(0,remain) computed ONLY after loop (L1156)_
- ✅ Selected invoice (qaime_id) is moved to front of the cascade, remaining open invoices still processed in FIFO order — so explicit selection does not strand other open invoices as advance. — _features/maliyye/actions.ts L1129-1135: processOrder = [selected, ...others]_
- ✅ Per-invoice odenilmis is incremented inside a single prisma.$transaction together with status auto-set to 'tamamlandi' when fully paid, the finance_operations row, finance_payment_allocations rows, customer balance recalc and account balance recalc — atomic. — _features/maliyye/actions.ts L1159-1285 (single $transaction wrapping steps 1-7); odenilmis increment L1227; status close L1228; recalculateCustomerBalance L1253; recalculateAccountBalance L1258_
- ✅ Customer debt (alacaq / debtor borc) is derived from SUM(son_mebleg - odenilmis) over active nisye sales — incrementing odenilmis on allocation directly reduces displayed debt; no divergent recompute per page. — _lib/balance/customer-balance.ts L61-73 (calculate); features/maliyye/queries.ts getDebtors open_sales CTE L663-678 uses SUM(s.son_mebleg - COALESCE(s.odenilmis,0)); elaqe detail unpaid=sales_total-sales_paid page.tsx L118_
- ✅ All five maliyye payment entry points (receivePartialPayment, payAllOpenInvoices, paySupplierAllOpen, paySupplierInvoice) and elaqe recordContactPayment use the identical FIFO cascade algorithm — no divergent ad-hoc allocation. — _actions.ts L1142-1156, L1507-1520, L1676-1688, L1850-1864; elaqe/actions.ts L410-421_
- ✅ Idempotency guard prevents double-submit duplicates: same idempotency_key+customer+amount within 5 minutes returns the existing op id; UI auto-generates a per-session key and disables the submit button while pending. — _actions.ts L1079-1093 dedupe via [IDEM:key] in finance_operations.qeyd; nisye-payment-quick.tsx L149-151 key gen, L358 disabled={pending}_
- ✅ UI preview pane reproduces the server cascade math exactly (same Math.min/remain loop, same advance=Math.max(0,remain)), so the user sees the correct split before submitting; overpay beyond TOTAL debt requires explicit window.confirm. — _nisye-payment-quick.tsx preview L272-286; overpay confirm only when meblegNum > borc (total) +0.01 L130-138_
- ✅ applyAdvanceToInvoice lets existing avans be applied to a specific open invoice: caps at min(amount, invoice qalig), increments odenilmis, decrements avans, recalcs balance — informational finance_op with no cash movement. — _features/maliyye/actions.ts L2041-2065_
- ✅ Cancelling a payment operation reverses invoice allocations: deletes finance_payment_allocations rows and decrements satis/alis odenilmis by the allocated amount, then recalcs account + customer + supplier balances inside one transaction. — _features/maliyye/cancel-operation-action.ts L84-129 (alloc delete L92, odenilmis decrement L96/L103)_
- ✅ Audit log written on payment with full distribution detail (per-invoice applied/old_qalig/new_qalig/closed, total_applied, to_advance). — _features/maliyye/actions.ts safeAuditLog L1262-1284, emeliyyat 'nisye_odenish'_
- ✅ Soft-deleted / cancelled sales excluded from allocation candidate set and from debt aggregation (status NOT IN legv/qaytarilib, deleted_at IS NULL, qaralama=false). — _openSales where status:{not:'legv'},qaralama:{not:true} actions.ts L1110-1112; calculateCustomerBalance deleted_at IS NULL AND status NOT IN ('legv','qaytarilib') customer-balance.ts L69-72_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Cancelling a payment does NOT reverse the advance (avans) surplus — orphaned advance left on customer**
  - Detal: receivePartialPayment puts the post-invoice surplus into kontragentler.avans via `avans: { increment: toAdvance }` (actions.ts L1237-1245) but creates finance_payment_allocations rows ONLY for the invoice-applied portion (distribution entries, L1206-1219) — never for the advance portion. cancelFinanceOperation reverses a payment purely by iterating finance_payment_allocations and decrementing odenilmis (cancel-operation-action.ts L85-107); it never decrements kontragentler.avans (grep for 'avans' in that file returns nothing). recalculateCustomerBalance also cannot fix it because it hardcodes avans=0 (customer-balance.ts L95) and only derives alacaq from sales, leaving the avans column untouched. Net effect: if a payment had any surplus that became advance, cancelling that payment reopens the invoices correctly but leaves the customer's avans permanently inflated by toAdvance — phantom advance balance that getDebtors/customer-detail will display (queries.ts L717) and applyAdvanceToInvoice will let the user spend against non-existent money. Same gap applies to payAllOpenInvoices (avans increment L1539) and elaqe recordContactPayment (avans increment L442).
  - Sübut: features/maliyye/actions.ts L1237-1245 (avans increment, no allocation row for it) vs features/maliyye/cancel-operation-action.ts L84-129 (only decrements odenilmis via allocations, never touches avans)
  - Fayllar: features/maliyye/cancel-operation-action.ts, features/maliyye/actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Advance (avans) is never netted into displayed debt — customer with open invoice AND advance shows full debt while holding offsetting advance**
  - Detal: calculateCustomerBalance hard-codes `const avans = 0` (customer-balance.ts L94-95) and computes alacaq = max(0, satisCemi - odenilmis + servisBorc - 0). The real kontragentler.avans column is ignored in the debt math. Scenario: customer pays 10 with no open invoices → 10 goes entirely to avans (distribution empty, toAdvance=10, actions.ts L1300-1301). Later a 6 AZN nisye sale is created. The debtor list / customer card now show borc=6 (queries.ts open_sales SUM L665) even though the customer is holding a 10 AZN advance that fully covers it. The advance is shown separately (queries.ts L717, debtor-row-expanded.tsx L147-149) and only consumed if a user manually runs applyAdvanceToInvoice against that specific invoice. Advance is NOT auto-applied to newly created invoices, so reported debt overstates the true net receivable. The code comments themselves acknowledge avans is 'manual' (actions.ts L1236) and 'gələcəkdə' / future (customer-balance.ts L94). This is a design limitation that produces inflated debt figures in reports.
  - Sübut: lib/balance/customer-balance.ts L94-99 (avans=0 hardcoded, not derived from kontragentler.avans); features/maliyye/actions.ts L1300-1301 (empty-distribution payment dumps full amount to advance)
  - Fayllar: lib/balance/customer-balance.ts, features/maliyye/actions.ts

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] recalculateCustomerBalance can never lower alacaq below 0 nor reflect advance, so a true credit (overpaid) customer shows 0 debt but their advance is disconnected from the receivable**
  - Detal: alacaq = Math.max(0, satisCemi - odenilmis + servisBorc - avans) with avans=0 (customer-balance.ts L99). Because the cap is 0 and avans is excluded, the canonical balance field can never represent a net credit position, and the two halves (sales-derived alacaq vs the standalone avans column) are maintained by independent code paths. Combined with bug #1 (cancel never adjusts avans) this makes drift between kontragentler.avans and reality undetectable by the recalc routines — recalculateAllCustomerBalances (L155-228) also only recomputes alacaq from sales+service, never reconciles avans.
  - Sübut: lib/balance/customer-balance.ts L99 Math.max(0,...) with avans=0; recalculateAllCustomerBalances L191-208 never selects/updates avans
  - Fayllar: lib/balance/customer-balance.ts

**4. İcazə problemləri:**
- 🔐 odenis.qebul guard on all customer payment actions: None — guard present and correct on the maliyye payment surface — _receivePartialPayment L1062, payAllOpenInvoices L1462, paySupplierAllOpen L1635, paySupplierInvoice L1807, applyAdvanceToInvoice L2008 all call requireMaliyyeActionPerm('odenis.qebul')_
- 🔐 elaqe recordContactPayment permission alignment: recordContactPayment performs a debt-clearing allocation (mutates satis_sifarisleri.odenilmis + creates finance_operations) but is gated on 'musteri.duzelt' (contact edit) instead of 'odenis.qebul'. A user with only contact-edit rights but NOT payment-acceptance rights can clear customer debt and record an incoming finance operation through this path, bypassing the odenis.qebul gate enforced everywhere else. — _features/elaqe/actions.ts recordContactPayment L367 requireElaqeActionPerm('musteri.duzelt'); allocation+finance_op at L424-469 — contrast with maliyye paths requiring 'odenis.qebul'_

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** features/maliyye/cancel-operation-action.ts, lib/balance/customer-balance.ts, features/maliyye/actions.ts, features/elaqe/actions.ts

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** Xeyr

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** Allocation correctly cascades into the debt source-of-truth (satis_sifarisleri.odenilmis), which feeds debtor list, customer 360, dashboard, and ticaret reports consistently. The cross-module risk is the avans column: it is written by 3 payment paths (maliyye, payAllOpen, elaqe) and applyAdvanceToInvoice but is (a) ignored by every balance/debt calculator and (b) not reversed on cancel — so the advance figure shown on debtor list (queries.ts L717), customer detail (page.tsx L127), and the new-operation/quick-op dialogs (operation-form.tsx L150, quick-op-dialog.tsx L131) can drift positive over time and be spent against in applyAdvanceToInvoice even after the originating payment was cancelled. There is no nightly job that reconciles avans (recalculateAllCustomerBalances ignores it).

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) Cancel reversal (bug #1): in cancel-operation-action.ts, when reversing a 'qaime'/payment op, compute the advance portion that this op created (= op.meblegh - SUM(allocations.mebleg)) and decrement kontragentler.avans by that amount (clamped at >=0) before recalc. Alternatively, persist the advance portion as its own finance_payment_allocations row (satis_id NULL, a dedicated marker) at payment time so cancel can reverse it uniformly. 2) Advance netting (bugs #2/#3): decide policy — either (a) auto-apply available avans to newly created nisye invoices at sale time, or (b) net avans into the canonical balance: change calculateCustomerBalance to read kontragentler.avans and return alacaq = max(0, sales+service - avans) AND surface a net 'we owe customer' figure when avans exceeds debt; ensure recalculateAllCustomerBalances mirrors it. 3) Permission (elaqe): change recordContactPayment guard from 'musteri.duzelt' to 'odenis.qebul' (or require both) to match the rest of the payment surface.

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- Test 3 (the user's): create customer with two nisye sales 2 AZN and 100 AZN, receive 10 AZN payment → expect 2-invoice closed, 100-invoice qalig=92, advance=0, debtor borc=92.
- Pay exactly the total open debt (102) → both invoices closed, advance=0, customer disappears from debtor list (getDebtors filters open_total>0).
- Overpay total debt (e.g. 110 against 102) → UI window.confirm shown; on accept both invoices closed and 8 AZN → avans; verify avans displayed on debtor row and customer card.
- Pay with an explicitly selected newer invoice (qaime_id = the 100 one) while an older 2 invoice is open and amount=10 → selected (100) gets 8, then cascade fills the 2 → verify both touched (selected-first then FIFO).
- Cancel a payment that produced advance surplus → verify invoices reopen AND advance is removed (currently expected to FAIL: advance stays inflated).
- Create a new nisye invoice for a customer who already holds an advance → check whether reported debt is net of advance (currently expected: debt shown gross, advance shown separately, not auto-applied).

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Confirm at runtime that paying 10 against open invoices 2 + 100 yields invoice#1 closed (odenilmis=2,status=tamamlandi), invoice#100 odenilmis=8/qalig=92, and kontragentler.avans unchanged (toAdvance=0). Static read confirms this is what the code does.
- Confirm the orphaned-advance bug live: receivePartialPayment with surplus (e.g. pay 12 against a single 2 invoice → 2 applied, 10 to avans), then cancelOperation on that finance_op, and check kontragentler.avans still shows +10 (predicted bug). DB write paths verified statically.
- Confirm whether avans is auto-consumed anywhere on new sale creation (ticaret satis-yeni-actions) — not examined in this scope; if it is, bug #2 severity drops.
- Confirm exact ORDER BY tie-break behavior when two open invoices share identical tarix (only tarix asc is specified, no secondary key like nomre/id) — could make allocation order between same-date invoices non-deterministic; needs DB-level confirmation.

## CROSS: Soft-delete bütövlüyü

**Xülasə:** Soft-delete READING relies entirely on each query opting in to deleted_at IS NULL — the Prisma extension (lib/db/prisma.ts) injects only sahibkar_id, NOT deleted_at, so there is no global safety net. The core balance/stock services are correct and consistent: customer-balance, account-balance, supplier-balance all filter deleted_at + status, and the three module cancel actions (cancelSale, cancelPurchase, cancelFinanceOperation) correctly reverse stock+finance+balance atomically inside a single $transaction and set deleted_at. Stock is unaffected by soft-delete because anbar_hereketleri is append-only and cancel appends offsetting movement rows. The main LIST queries for sales/purchases/finance-ops/expenses/contacts correctly default to the active set. HOWEVER, a recurring class of bug exists in AGGREGATE/REPORT queries that bypass the active-filter: (1) every expense KPI/P&L/cashflow function that hits xercl_r directly omits the legv_de filter, so cancelled expenses inflate financial reports across maliyye and hesabatlar; (2) getOperationsSummary counts soft-deleted finance operations while the list it sits above hides them; (3) returns list+stats have NO deleted_at filter at all; (4) several sales/purchase analytics + CSV export omit the filter. Separately, restore is effectively unimplemented (restoreRecord is dead code; no UI), making cancel one-way. Two important invariants need confirmation: expenses use legv_de (not deleted_at) and deleteExpense does NOT reverse any linked finance_operation; and the 'status=legv ⇔ deleted_at set' coupling for sales/purchases/ops holds only because the module cancel actions set both — there is no DB constraint enforcing it.

Pick one (legv_de) and ensure all expense aggregates filter it, or backfill deleted_at = legv_de.

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** The single biggest cross-module risk is the expense soft-delete marker mismatch: xercl_r is cancelled via legv_de, but financial reporting across TWO modules (maliyye KPIs/P&L/cashflow and hesabatlar P&L) sums xercl_r without that filter, so a cancelled expense simultaneously corrupts the dashboard, the operations summary context, the P&L, and the cashflow chart — while the expense LIST shows it as cancelled. This produces a visible discrepancy (list total != report total) that erodes trust in all finance numbers. Secondary risk: report queries that exclude deleted rows by filtering status='legv' alone (customer-statement, hesabat, receivable-aging) depend on an application-enforced (not DB-enforced) coupling between deleted_at and status; any row with deleted_at set but status unchanged would silently leak into balances/reports. The balance CACHE columns (kontragentler.alacaq/borc, maliye_hesablari.qaliq, stok.miqdar) are themselves correct because the recalc services filter properly, so account/customer/supplier balances and stock are NOT corrupted by soft-deletes — the damage is confined to the report/aggregate read layer plus the missing restore capability.

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) Expenses: add 'AND legv_de IS NULL' to every xercl_r aggregate in features/maliyye/queries.ts (fetchFinanceKpisRaw, fetchMaliyyeDashboardKpisRaw both today+month, getDailyFlow, getMonthlyFlow, getTopExpenseCategories, getPLReport xercl_r subquery, getExpenseCategoryUsage), features/maliyye/cashflow-queries.ts (all 3 bucket queries), and features/hesabat/queries.ts getProfitLoss opex subquery — or refactor these to call getUnifiedExpenseTotal which already filters correctly. 2) getOperationsSummary: read filter.recordStatus and add the same deleted_at branch used in getOperations (default 'aktiv' → deleted_at: null). 3) Returns: add recordStatus support + default deleted_at IS NULL to getReturns and a status NOT IN ('legv') / deleted_at IS NULL filter to getReturnStats. 4) Sales stats: add status NOT IN ('legv','qaytarilib') AND deleted_at IS NULL to the bugun/hefte/ay aggregates in fetchSaleStatsRaw. 5) exportSalesCsv: default-exclude deleted_at IS NOT NULL (add deleted_at: null to where unless an explicit include-deleted flag is passed). 6) extra-actions analytics: add status!=legv (or deleted_at: null) to getPurchaseAnalytics aggregates, getReturnReasonReport, and the getCustomerSalesHistory son_satislar list. 7) getRecentPurchases: add deleted_at: null. 8) Restore: either implement a proper module-level restoreSale/restorePurchase that resets status and re-applies side-effects inside a $transaction, or remove the qeyd.berpa permission + dead restoreRecord helper to avoid advertising a non-functional feature.

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Confirm at runtime that no code path sets satis_sifarisleri/alis_sifarisleri/finance_operations.deleted_at WITHOUT also setting status='legv' (verified true for the cancel actions read; a DB-level audit would confirm no historical/migration drift). If such rows exist, the report queries that filter on status alone (customer-statement.ts, hesabat reports, getReceivableAging) would leak deleted rows.
- Confirm deleteExpense leaving any linked finance_operation un-reversed is intended — if saving an expense creates a finance_operation cash movement, cancelling the expense (legv_de) without cancelling that op would leave the account balance still reduced (outside soft-delete-filter scope but adjacent). Needs tracing saveExpense → finance_operations creation.
- Verify the magnitude of impact for expense-report bugs depends on whether tenants actually cancel expenses; with live data, compare getExpenses total (legv_de filtered) vs getPLReport opex to measure drift.

## CROSS: Hesablama service-lərinin vahidliyi

**Xülasə:** Four balance services exist (customer-balance, supplier-balance, account-balance, product-stock). Three of them (customer, supplier, account) ARE genuinely single-sourced for WRITES: every sale/payment/purchase/return/cancel path calls the matching recalculate* inside its $transaction, and reads use the cached alacaq/borc/qaliq fields. Payment allocation (receivePartialPayment) correctly implements oldest-first FIFO with surplus-only-to-advance, satisfying the critical 2+100/10 AZN requirement. However, unification breaks down in several places. (1) AVAILABLE STOCK has no single source: lib/balance/product-stock.ts (calculateProductStock) is dead code — never imported — and the three places that DO compute availability disagree, because POS/marketplace/new-sale and validateCartStock check raw stok.miqdar and ignore active stok_bron reservations, while the product card subtracts them; reserved units can be oversold via POS. (2) CUSTOMER DEBT is computed three divergent ways: the canonical calculateCustomerBalance includes unpaid service (servis_qeydleri) debt, but getDebtors() (debitor list) and getKontragentBorc() recompute sales-only debt — and contact-context hedges with Math.max(alacaq, openTotal), proving the divergence is known. (3) getReceivableAging filters sales on statuses ('tesdiq','gonderildi') that no flow writes and excludes 'tamamlandi'/'yeni', so the aging report disagrees with both getDebtors and the cached balance. (4) PAYROLL net and the AZ tax/social rates are duplicated/hardcoded across 5 sites (maas-actions x3, maas-queries, hr-actions) with no calculatePayrollNet helper and at least one behavioural difference. (5) The FIFO allocation loop and the invoice qaliq formula are copy-pasted (3 and ~10 sites respectively) with no allocatePaymentToInvoices / calculateInvoiceBalance helper. (6) COGS/margin uses live mehsullar.alish_qiymeti with no line-level cost snapshot, duplicated across 5 report files, so historical margins drift on cost changes. None of these were confirmed at runtime (static read-only audit).

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** lib/balance/customer-balance.ts; lib/balance/supplier-balance.ts; lib/balance/account-balance.ts; lib/balance/product-stock.ts; lib/db/stock-guards.ts; features/anbar/product-context-action.ts; features/maliyye/actions.ts (receivePartialPayment, payAllOpenInvoices, paySupplierAllOpen, paySupplierInvoice, closeGunSonu, markPayoutReceived); features/maliyye/queries.ts (getDebtors, getReceivableAging, getFinanceKpis); features/maliyye/marketplace-queries.ts; features/elaqe/kontragent-borc.ts; features/elaqe/contact-context-action.ts; features/elaqe/queries.ts; features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts (calculateBordro, adjustBordro, bonus/cerime block); features/iscilier/maas-queries.ts; features/iscilier/hr-actions.ts (cerime block); features/iscilier/bonus-calc.ts; features/hesabatlar/marja-queries.ts; features/hesabatlar/maliyye-queries.ts; features/hesabatlar/mehsul-queries.ts; features/hesabatlar/satis-queries.ts; features/hesabatlar/stok-queries.ts; features/pos/sale-action.ts; features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts (validateCartStock + sale create); features/ticaret/market-satis-action.ts; prisma/schema.prisma (satis_sifaris_satirlari, satis_sifarisleri status)

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ calculateCustomerBalance exists as a single canonical source-of-truth for customer debt (sales nisyə qaliq + servis qaliq), and recalculateCustomerBalance is consistently called inside the $transaction of every mutation path (POS, satis-yeni, kredit, market, split-payment, teklif, qaytarma, qaytarma-tez, servis, maliyye payment/cancel). 30+ call sites all import from lib/balance/customer-balance. — _lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:50-108; recalculateCustomerBalance imported in features/pos/sale-action.ts:318, features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts:464, features/maliyye/actions.ts:1252, features/servis/actions.ts:279 etc._
- ✅ calculateAccountBalance / recalculateAccountBalance / checkAccountSufficient form a single canonical cash/bank balance source; checkAccountSufficient is reused for negative-balance guards (supplier payment). — _lib/balance/account-balance.ts:44-216; checkAccountSufficient used in features/maliyye/actions.ts:1717_
- ✅ calculateSupplierBalance / recalculateSupplierBalance exist as the canonical supplier (borc) source, mirroring the customer pattern; invoked from purchase/payment paths. — _lib/balance/supplier-balance.ts:33-101; recalculateSupplierBalance called in features/maliyye/actions.ts:1713_
- ✅ Payment allocation in receivePartialPayment correctly allocates to OPEN invoices oldest-first (orderBy tarix asc) and only the surplus becomes avans — matching the required 2+100 / 10 AZN -> 2 fully paid + 8 to the 100 invoice behaviour. — _features/maliyye/actions.ts:1106-1156 (openSales orderBy tarix asc; loop apply=Math.min(remain,inv.qalig); toAdvance=remain only after all invoices processed)_
- ✅ safeStockDecrement is the single race-safe stock decrement helper (atomic UPDATE ... WHERE miqdar >= n) and is reused by POS, market and new-sale flows for the actual mutation. — _lib/db/stock-guards.ts:21-53; used in features/pos/sale-action.ts:219, features/ticaret/market-satis-action.ts:212_
- ✅ Sales/payments/marketplace mutations are wrapped in prisma.$transaction with idempotency dedupe on receivePartialPayment. — _features/maliyye/actions.ts:1079-1093 (idem dedupe), :1159 ($transaction); features/pos/sale-action.ts uses $transaction with FOR UPDATE row lock at :128_
- ✅ Marketplace net/komissiya is single-sourced via stored columns komisyon_meblegh + xalis_meblegh written at sale time; read queries fall back to son_mebleg - komisyon_meblegh consistently. — _features/ticaret/market-satis-action.ts:122-124,193-194; features/maliyye/marketplace-queries.ts:108; features/maliyye/actions.ts:776-777_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Available-stock formula diverges: POS/market/new-sale ignore active reservations (stok_bron); product card & product-stock service subtract them**
  - Detal: There is NO shared calculateAvailableStock. The 'satıla bilən' number is computed three different ways: (1) lib/balance/product-stock.ts:100 satilabilen = cache - rezerv (subtracts active stok_bron); (2) features/anbar/product-context-action.ts:166 satilabilen = toplam - rezerv (also subtracts rezerv); (3) the actual sell-time availability checks in POS (features/pos/sale-action.ts:135 row.miqdar < line.miqdar), market (features/ticaret/market-satis-action.ts:147), and the client validator validateCartStock (features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts:669-686) all compare against raw stok.miqdar with NO subtraction of stok_bron reservations. Consequence: a unit that is reserved (stok_bron status='aktiv') is shown as unavailable on the product card but can still be sold via POS/market/new-sale, overselling reserved stock and oversubscribing a reservation. The product-stock.ts service (the supposed single source) is never imported anywhere, so it does not unify anything.
  - Sübut: lib/balance/product-stock.ts:100 vs features/anbar/product-context-action.ts:166 vs features/pos/sale-action.ts:135 / features/ticaret/market-satis-action.ts:147 / features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts:669-686 (no stok_bron); confirmed POS has no stok_bron reference at all

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] getReceivableAging filters on sale statuses that no sale flow ever writes — aging report excludes all real receivables**
  - Detal: getReceivableAging selects sales WHERE status IN ('yeni','tesdiq','gonderildi'). But the actual written statuses are: POS & marketplace nisyə sales -> 'tamamlandi' (features/pos/sale-action.ts:188, market-satis-action.ts:182), credit sale -> 'yeni' (kredit-yeni-actions.ts:107), new-sale -> 'yeni'/'tesdiq_gozleyir'/'qaralama' (satis-yeni-actions.ts:194). The strings 'tesdiq' and 'gonderildi' are not in the sales status vocabulary, and completed/POS receivables ('tamamlandi') are explicitly excluded. It also omits odenis_nov IN ('nisye','borc'), deleted_at IS NULL and qaralama filters that getDebtors and calculateCustomerBalance apply. The aging buckets on the maliyye dashboard therefore disagree with both getDebtors() and the cached kontragentler.alacaq.
  - Sübut: features/maliyye/queries.ts:1052 status:{in:['yeni','tesdiq','gonderildi']} vs actual values (pos/sale-action.ts:188 'tamamlandi'); status vocabulary grep shows 'tesdiq_gozleyir' not 'tesdiq', no 'gonderildi'; rendered at app/(dashboard)/maliyye/page.tsx:55

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Customer debt computed three divergent ways; getDebtors list and getKontragentBorc exclude unpaid service (servis_qeydleri) debt that the canonical calculateCustomerBalance includes**
  - Detal: calculateCustomerBalance (the canonical writer of kontragentler.alacaq) sums BOTH nisyə sales qaliq AND open servis_qeydleri repair debt (lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:62-99). But getDebtors() (the debitor list) recomputes borc as SUM(son_mebleg - odenilmis) over sales only, with no service component (features/maliyye/queries.ts:665). getKontragentBorc() independently re-implements its own raw SQL SUM(son_mebleg - odenilmis), also sales-only, no service (features/elaqe/kontragent-borc.ts:42-51). contact-context-action.ts:130 even hedges with borc = Math.max(alacaq, openTotal), proving the two sources are known to disagree. A customer with an unpaid service repair shows different debt on the debitor list vs the customer card vs the cached alacaq.
  - Sübut: lib/balance/customer-balance.ts:75-99 (servis included) vs features/maliyye/queries.ts:665 (sales-only) vs features/elaqe/kontragent-borc.ts:42-51 (sales-only) vs features/elaqe/contact-context-action.ts:130 Math.max(alacaq,openTotal)

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Payroll net (son_meblegh) formula and AZ tax/social rates duplicated across 5 sites with at least one behavioural divergence**
  - Detal: There is no calculatePayrollNet/calculatePayrollBalance single source. The net-salary formula gross = prorata + kpi_bonus + manual_bonus + komisyon; son = gross - cerime - avans - vergi - sosial is re-implemented in: maas-actions.ts:192-195 (calculateBordro — NOTE: omits kpi/manual/cerime/avans terms because they are 0 at creation, so the expression differs), maas-actions.ts:314-317 (bonus/cerime block, uses literal 0.14/0.03 instead of the file's TAX_RATE/SOCIAL_RATE consts), maas-actions.ts:540-543 (adjustBordro), maas-queries.ts:89-98 (read-side, redefines its OWN TAX_RATE/SOCIAL_RATE/COMMISSION_RATE consts at :70-72), and hr-actions.ts:419-422 (cerime, literal 0.14/0.03). The tax/social/commission rates exist as named constants in maas-actions.ts:100-102 AND as duplicate constants in maas-queries.ts:70-72 AND as inline literals — changing the gelir vergisi rate requires editing 5 places. Risk: read-side maas-queries net can disagree with the stored son_meblegh if the formulas drift.
  - Sübut: features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts:100-102 (consts) + :192-195 + :314-317 (literals) + :540-543; features/iscilier/maas-queries.ts:70-72 (dup consts) + :89-98; features/iscilier/hr-actions.ts:419-422 (literals)

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] calculateProductStock / product-stock.ts is dead code — never imported, so it is NOT the single source of stock truth**
  - Detal: lib/balance/product-stock.ts (calculateProductStock, scanStockDrift) is documented as the 'VAHİD HƏQİQƏT MƏNBƏYİ' for product stock and the only place that reconciles the stok cache against the anbar_hereketleri ledger and computes satilabilen = cache - rezerv. A repo-wide grep shows zero imports of '@/lib/balance/product-stock', calculateProductStock, or scanStockDrift outside the file itself. Stock availability everywhere else reads stok.miqdar directly. So the intended single source exists but is unused, and no ledger-vs-cache drift detection is wired in.
  - Sübut: grep for 'balance/product-stock'|calculateProductStock|scanStockDrift across features/app/lib returns only the definition file lib/balance/product-stock.ts; no consumers

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] No allocatePaymentToInvoices helper — the FIFO allocation loop is copy-pasted in 3 functions, risking drift**
  - Detal: The oldest-first allocation algorithm (filter qaliq>0, sort by tarix asc, loop apply=Math.min(remain,qalig), surplus->avans) is independently re-implemented inline in receivePartialPayment (features/maliyye/actions.ts:1117-1156), payAllOpenInvoices (:1492-1520) and paySupplierAllOpen (:1661-1688). They are currently equivalent but already diverge in detail: receivePartialPayment supports an explicit qaime_id pinned first (:1131-1135) while payAllOpenInvoices does not; payAllOpenInvoices performs NO checkAccountSufficient guard, whereas paySupplierAllOpen calls checkAccountSufficient (:1717) and paySupplierInvoice presumably similar. Any future fix to the allocation/rounding rule must be made in 3 places, a classic divergence hazard.
  - Sübut: features/maliyye/actions.ts:1117-1156 vs :1492-1520 vs :1661-1688; sufficiency guard present at :1717 (supplier) but absent in payAllOpenInvoices

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] COGS / margin uses current mehsullar.alish_qiymeti (no line-level cost snapshot) — historical profit drifts when cost changes; formula duplicated in 5 report files**
  - Detal: There is no calculateSaleProfit helper and no cost-at-sale-time column. satis_sifaris_satirlari (prisma/schema.prisma:5147) stores only vahid_qiymet and a generated cemi — no maya/alish snapshot. Every margin/COGS report therefore computes profit as SUM(sls.cemi) - SUM(sls.miqdar * COALESCE(m.alish_qiymeti,0)) using the CURRENT product cost, duplicated identically across features/hesabatlar/marja-queries.ts (lines 23,80-83,137-140,188-191,226-228), maliyye-queries.ts:28,161, mehsul-queries.ts:117-120,144-147, satis-queries.ts:461-464, and stok-queries.ts:14,36. The same maya check is also done ad-hoc in features/pos/sale-action.ts:152 (maya_alti). Because cost is read live, a cost change retroactively rewrites the margin of all past sales, and any divergence in COGS join logic between these 5 files would silently produce different profit numbers per report.
  - Sübut: prisma/schema.prisma:5147-5150 (no cost column); features/hesabatlar/marja-queries.ts:23,80-83; maliyye-queries.ts:28; mehsul-queries.ts:117-120; satis-queries.ts:461-464; pos/sale-action.ts:152

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] No calculateInvoiceBalance helper — invoice qaliq = son_mebleg/umumi_mebleg - odenilmis is hand-coded in ~10 files**
  - Detal: Open-invoice balance (son_mebleg - COALESCE(odenilmis,0) for sales, umumi_mebleg - odenilmis for purchases) is recomputed ad-hoc in features/ticaret/kredit-queries.ts, dashboard-queries.ts, satis-queries.ts, satis-yeni-actions.ts, operation-options-actions.ts, features/pos/sale-queries.ts, features/elaqe/kontragent-borc.ts, features/maliyye/queries.ts (getDebtors, getReceivableAging), features/maliyye/actions.ts, plus supplier-balance.ts. There is no shared calculateInvoiceBalance, so the threshold for 'open' (>0 vs >0.01) and rounding vary slightly between sites.
  - Sübut: grep 'son_mebleg - COALESCE(odenilmis' / 'umumi_mebleg - COALESCE(odenilmis' across kredit-queries.ts, dashboard-queries.ts, satis-queries.ts, pos/sale-queries.ts, kontragent-borc.ts, maliyye/queries.ts:665,1067, supplier-balance.ts:48

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** lib/balance/product-stock.ts, lib/db/stock-guards.ts, features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts, features/pos/sale-action.ts, features/ticaret/market-satis-action.ts, features/anbar/product-context-action.ts, features/maliyye/queries.ts, features/elaqe/kontragent-borc.ts, features/maliyye/actions.ts, features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts, features/iscilier/maas-queries.ts, features/iscilier/hr-actions.ts, features/hesabatlar/marja-queries.ts, features/hesabatlar/maliyye-queries.ts, features/hesabatlar/mehsul-queries.ts, features/hesabatlar/satis-queries.ts

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** Add a line-level cost snapshot column (e.g. satis_sifaris_satirlari.maya_qiymet / alish_qiymeti_at_sale) so calculateSaleProfit can compute margin against cost-at-sale instead of current mehsullar.alish_qiymeti (prevents retroactive margin drift)

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** The biggest cross-module risk is that the 'available stock' and 'customer debt' numbers a user sees depend on WHICH module renders them. Stock: product card (anbar) subtracts reservations; POS/marketplace/ticaret-satis (sales) do not — so reserved goods sold via sales silently bypass the reservation made in ticaret/anbar. Debt: the cached kontragentler.alacaq (written by the servis-inclusive calculateCustomerBalance and consumed by elaqe lists/dashboard/customer card) is larger than the sales-only borc shown by maliyye getDebtors and elaqe getKontragentBorc whenever a service repair is unpaid, and getReceivableAging (maliyye dashboard) is on a third, broken status filter. Payroll net flows from iscilier into maliyye via maas payment finance_operations; the net is recomputed on the read side (maas-queries) with its own constants, so a rate change applied only to maas-actions would make the displayed bordro disagree with paid amounts. COGS/profit flows into hesabatlar (marja, maliyye P&L, mehsul, satis) all from live alish_qiymeti, so a single product cost edit retroactively shifts every historical profit report at once.

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) AVAILABLE STOCK: make lib/balance/product-stock.ts the real single source. Add a calculateAvailableStock(mehsul_id, anbar_id) = stok.miqdar - active stok_bron, and route POS sale-action, market-satis-action, satis-yeni create, and validateCartStock through it so reservations are honoured at sell time (or explicitly document that POS intentionally ignores reservations and exclude POS-bound stock from reservation). 2) CUSTOMER DEBT: delete the ad-hoc SQL in getDebtors() and getKontragentBorc(); have both read kontragentler.alacaq (servis-inclusive, kept current by recalculateCustomerBalance) or call a shared read helper, and drop the Math.max hedge in contact-context-action.ts. 3) AGING: fix getReceivableAging to filter the same way as getDebtors (status NOT IN ('legv','qaytarilib'), qaralama=false, deleted_at IS NULL, odenis_nov IN ('nisye','borc'), qaliq>0) instead of the non-existent 'tesdiq'/'gonderildi' set. 4) PAYROLL: extract one calculatePayrollNet({prorata,kpi_bonus,manual_bonus,komisyon,cerime,avans}) and one PAYROLL_RATES constant; call it from calculateBordro, the bonus/cerime blocks, adjustBordro, hr-actions, and maas-queries. 5) ALLOCATION: extract allocatePaymentToInvoices(openInvoices, amount, {pinnedId?}) returning {distribution, toAdvance} and reuse it in receivePartialPayment, payAllOpenInvoices, paySupplierAllOpen; add a shared calculateInvoiceBalance. 6) PROFIT: add a line-level cost snapshot column and a calculateSaleProfit helper, then point all 5 hesabatlar COGS queries at it. Each change is read/logic-only except the profit snapshot, which needs a migration + backfill.

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- Reserve N units of a product via ticaret new-sale draft (reserve_stock), then sell the same units via POS in the same anbar — verify whether POS blocks or oversells the reserved units
- Create a customer with one unpaid nisyə sale AND one open service repair (temir_xerci > musteriden_alinan); compare the debt shown on the debitor list (maliyye/debitor), the customer card (elaqe), and the maliyye dashboard aging — they should match but are expected to differ
- Make a 10 AZN payment to a customer with a 2 AZN and a 100 AZN open invoice and verify 2 fully paid + 8 applied to the 100 invoice + 0 advance (this path is correct in receivePartialPayment)
- Complete a POS nisyə sale (status tamamlandi) and confirm it appears in the receivable aging buckets on maliyye/page (expected to be MISSING due to status filter)
- Change a product's alish_qiymeti and re-open last month's marja report — verify whether historical margin changes (expected yes, since cost is read live)
- Edit cerime on a draft bordro, then open the maas table read view — verify son_meblegh matches between the stored value and the recomputed read-side value

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Confirm at runtime that a stok_bron-reserved unit can actually be sold through POS/marketplace (overselling reservations) since these flows never read stok_bron
- Confirm getReceivableAging returns empty/wrong buckets on the live maliyye dashboard given no sales carry status 'tesdiq'/'gonderildi'
- Confirm a customer with an open servis_qeydleri repair shows a HIGHER alacaq (cached, via calculateCustomerBalance) than the borc shown in the debitor list (getDebtors, sales-only)
- Confirm that maas-queries.ts read-side net matches the stored son_meblegh after a tax-rate change (formula duplicated)
- Confirm whether the dead product-stock.ts drift scan is exposed via any admin/cron endpoint not found by grep

## CROSS: Stok hesablamasının ekranlar arası uyğunluğu

**Xülasə:** There is no single shared 'available stock' computation across screens — the documented single-source-of-truth helper (lib/balance/product-stock.ts: calculateProductStock) is dead code with zero callers. Instead at least four divergent formulas exist: (1) warehouse stok page = miqdar − rezerv (bron active+unexpired); (2) mehsullar list = stock-in-standart/servis-anbars − rezerv (active, NO expiry filter); (3) product-card context = toplam-all-anbars − rezerv (active, NO expiry filter); (4) ALL sale paths (POS, yeni-satış, kredit, market, təklif) plus transfer modal and marketplace sync = RAW stok.miqdar with NO bron subtraction at all. The most serious consequence is functional, not cosmetic: validateCartStock and every sale commit (sale-action.ts:135, market-satis-action.ts:147, satis-yeni-actions.ts validateCartStock) gate on physical miqdar, so stock reserved by an active bron — created by the very draft flows in this app — is fully sellable, an over-sell/double-allocation risk, and marketplace sync advertises those reserved units externally. Secondary divergences: bron expiry filter present on stok page but missing on list/product-card; defekt/karantın warehouse exclusion applied only on the list; product-card uses a company-wide available while POS uses per-warehouse. Separately, the 30-day stock-movement report (hesabatlar/stok-queries.ts:58-59) filters on nov values the app never writes ('satis','transfer_gelis','transfer_gediss','duzelis_*'), so sales/transfers don't show up — a real report bug. Recommend funneling every screen (read + write guard) through one shared available-stock function with a single canonical bron filter and explicit anbar-type/scope parameters, and wiring it into validateCartStock and the FOR UPDATE sale guards so display and enforcement agree. All findings are read-only and code-grounded; none were verified against live data.

**Əhatə boşluqları:** servis (service module) stock consumption screens — whether servis intake/return reads available consistently was not opened in detail; only the servis_qeydleri count join in stok-queries was seen.; konsiqnasiya screens' available-stock display (features/anbar/konsiqnasiya) — not opened; only confirmed konsiqnasiya_mexaric exists in the canonical ledger.; qaytarma (return) flows' effect on shown available and any return-specific stock lookup UI — not opened in this scope.; dashboard/nezaret-merkezi stock widgets — not examined for which formula they use.; Exact CHECK constraint enumeration of anbar_hereketleri.nov in prisma/schema.prisma was inferred from code comments (sale-action.ts:231) and write sites, not read from the schema directly.; stok-change-emitter / revalidation behavior (lib/stock-change-emitter.ts) — not examined; could affect whether screens show stale vs fresh numbers but not the formula divergence itself.

**2/6. İşləyən (keçən) funksiyalar:**
- ✅ Warehouse stok page (anbar/stok) computes available correctly as movcud = miqdar - rezerv per (mehsul, anbar), and the bron filter is the correct one: stok_bron status='aktiv' AND (bitme_tarixi IS NULL OR bitme_tarixi >= CURRENT_DATE) — _features/anbar/stok-queries.ts:149-155 (rezerv subquery with expiry filter) and :224 movcud = Math.max(0, r.miqdar - rezerv)_
- ✅ Mehsullar LIST page exposes a real available figure satis_acig_miqdari and even excludes defekt/karantın/transit warehouses from sellable stock — _features/anbar/queries.ts:573 SATIS_ACIQ_ANBAR_NOV = {standart, servis}; :628 satis_acig_miqdari = Math.max(0, satis_acig_anbar_stok - bron)_
- ✅ A canonical shared stock service exists and documents the intended single-source-of-truth formula (cache - rezerv) with the correct active+unexpired bron filter and a documented movement-nov ledger — _lib/balance/product-stock.ts:28-103 calculateProductStock; rezerv subquery :83-90 has status='aktiv' AND bitme_tarixi expiry guard_
- ✅ Sales DO create reservations (stok_bron) for drafts in yeni-satış, so bron is a real, populated dimension that the divergent screens then ignore — _features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts:289-300 tx.stok_bron.create for 48h reserve when qaralama+reserve_stock_

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Shared stock service (calculateProductStock) is dead code — no screen uses the documented single source of truth**
  - Detal: lib/balance/product-stock.ts is explicitly documented as 'Məhsul stoku üçün VAHID HƏQİQƏT MƏNBƏYİ' (single source of truth for product stock). A repo-wide grep shows calculateProductStock and scanStockDrift have ZERO callers anywhere in app/ or features/. Every screen instead rolls its own ad-hoc available-stock math, which is the structural root cause of all the divergences below. The intended consistency mechanism is not wired in.
  - Sübut: grep 'calculateProductStock' across repo returns only the definition in lib/balance/product-stock.ts:28; no import in any app/ or features/ file. scanStockDrift (product-stock.ts:109) also has no caller.
  - Fayllar: lib/balance/product-stock.ts, features/anbar/stok-queries.ts, features/anbar/queries.ts, features/anbar/product-context-action.ts, features/pos/sale-queries.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] POS / yeni-satış / kredit-satış / market-satış / təklif all show and validate RAW physical stock, ignoring active bron reservations**
  - Detal: All sale-entry screens read stok_miqdari from features/pos/sale-queries.ts mapProduct, which sums raw stok.miqdar with NO bron subtraction (the function never touches stok_bron). The same physical number is shown in dropdowns ('stok: {p.stok_miqdari}') and is the basis for the front-end 'stok kifayət etmir' guard. Result: a product whose physical 5 units are fully reserved by an active bron (created by exactly these draft flows) shows '5' sellable in POS/new-sale while the warehouse stok page and mehsullar list correctly show 0 available (movcud / satis_acig_miqdari). Two screens display two different numbers for the same product.
  - Sübut: features/pos/sale-queries.ts:56-91 mapProduct sums Number(s.miqdar) only, no stok_bron read; consumed by satis-yeni-client.tsx:844, kredit-yeni-client.tsx:312, market-satis-client.tsx:466, yeni-satis-modal.tsx:824, yeni-teklif-modal.tsx:381, pos-client.tsx:1298. POS cart guard pos-client.tsx:834 filters l.miqdar > l.stok_miqdari (physical).
  - Fayllar: features/pos/sale-queries.ts, features/pos/components/pos-client.tsx, features/ticaret/components/satis-yeni-client.tsx, features/ticaret/components/kredit-yeni-client.tsx, features/ticaret/components/market-satis-client.tsx, features/ticaret/components/yeni-satis-modal.tsx, features/ticaret/components/yeni-teklif-modal.tsx

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Server-side stock validation (validateCartStock + sale actions) checks physical stok.miqdar, not available — bron can be oversold**
  - Detal: The shared real-time validator validateCartStock reads stok.miqdar and labels it 'available' (the comment literally says 'available pool') but never subtracts stok_bron. The atomic sale commit guards likewise compare row.miqdar (raw) < line.miqdar. So reserved-but-not-yet-sold stock is fully sellable through every sale path, and the reservation a draft created provides no real protection — the same physical unit can be sold while it is reserved for another order. This is a genuine over-sell / double-allocation risk, not just a display mismatch.
  - Sübut: features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts:669-678 validateCartStock selects only stok.miqdar; comment :642-645 calls it available. POS commit features/pos/sale-action.ts:133-136 throws only when row.miqdar < line.miqdar. market-satis-action.ts:147-151 same raw check. No path subtracts stok_bron before the FOR UPDATE comparison.
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts, features/pos/sale-action.ts, features/ticaret/market-satis-action.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Stock-movement 30-day report filters on movement type (nov) values that the app never writes — report shows wrong/zero movement**
  - Detal: getStockMovement30 classifies daxil/xaric using nov IN ('medaxil','transfer_gelis','qaytarma','duzelis_artir') and nov IN ('mexaric','satis','transfer_gediss','duzelis_azalt'). The actual anbar_hereketleri.nov values written by the system are 'mexaric' (sales — sale-action.ts:231, market-satis-action.ts:224, satis-yeni-actions.ts:331), 'transfer_giris'/'transfer_cixis' (transfers), 'stok_duzelis' (adjustments). The strings 'transfer_gelis','transfer_gediss','satis','duzelis_artir','duzelis_azalt' are never emitted ('satis' is the emeliyyat_nov, not nov). So sales never count as xaric and transfers/adjustments are mis/under-counted in this report, contradicting the stock numbers shown elsewhere. (Note: the canonical ledger in product-stock.ts uses yet another set of nov values incl. 'transfer_giris','konsiqnasiya_mexaric','servis_mexaric' etc., which is the correct list — confirming the report query is the outlier.)
  - Sübut: features/hesabatlar/stok-queries.ts:58-59 (invalid nov filters). Real writers: features/pos/sale-action.ts:231 nov:'mexaric'; features/anbar/transfer/actions.ts:150,161 'transfer_cixis'/'transfer_giris'; features/anbar/stock-actions.ts:89 'stok_duzelis'. Canonical ledger nov set: lib/balance/product-stock.ts:52-72.
  - Fayllar: features/hesabatlar/stok-queries.ts, lib/balance/product-stock.ts, features/pos/sale-action.ts, features/anbar/transfer/actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Bron expiry filter is inconsistent: some screens count expired reservations, others exclude them**
  - Detal: Available-stock screens disagree on which bron rows count. Warehouse stok page and the shared service exclude expired bron (status='aktiv' AND (bitme_tarixi IS NULL OR bitme_tarixi >= CURRENT_DATE)). But the mehsullar LIST page and the product-card context panel filter only status='aktiv' with NO expiry check, so a stale/expired-but-not-cleaned bron keeps reducing the shown available quantity on those screens while NOT reducing it on the stok page. Same product, same instant, different 'available' number depending on whether the expiry guard is present.
  - Sübut: Correct (with expiry): stok-queries.ts:149-155 and product-stock.ts:83-90. Missing expiry: features/anbar/queries.ts:558-566 groupBy stok_bron where {status:'aktiv'} only; features/anbar/product-context-action.ts:91-98 aggregate stok_bron where {status:'aktiv'} only.

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Warehouse-type exclusion (defekt/karantın/transit) applied only on mehsullar list, not on other available-stock screens**
  - Detal: The mehsullar LIST defines available as stock in SATIS_ACIQ_ANBAR_NOV={standart,servis} minus bron, deliberately excluding defekt/karantın/transit warehouses. No other screen applies this rule: warehouse stok page subtracts bron from miqdar regardless of anbar nov; product-card context panel sums toplam across ALL warehouses; POS getPosAnbarOptions restricts anbar dropdown to nov IN ('standart','servis') but the per-product stok_miqdari/diger_anbarlarda in mapProduct counts every warehouse's stok. So a product sitting in a defekt warehouse is counted as available on POS/product-card but not on the mehsullar list.
  - Sübut: List exclusion: features/anbar/queries.ts:573,578-581,628. Product card sums all anbars: product-context-action.ts:88 toplam = anbarlar.reduce(...) over every stok row; :166 satilabilen = toplam - rezerv. POS mapProduct sums all anbar stok rows: sale-queries.ts:58-73.
  - Fayllar: features/anbar/queries.ts, features/anbar/product-context-action.ts, features/pos/sale-queries.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · ux] Product-card context panel computes available across ALL warehouses while POS/new-sale compute per selected warehouse — different bases**
  - Detal: getProductContext.satilabilen = toplam(all warehouses) - rezerv(all warehouses), a company-wide figure. POS/new-sale stok_miqdari is per-selected-anbar (mapProduct.inSelected). So the 'Satıla bilər' shown on the product detail/context panel can be much larger than what POS will let you sell from the active register's warehouse, with no reconciliation. They are not the same quantity and are not labelled as different scopes consistently.
  - Sübut: features/anbar/product-context-action.ts:88,164-166 (toplam across all anbars, satilabilen company-wide). features/pos/sale-queries.ts:61-62,89 (inSelected = only matching anbarId → stok_miqdari).
  - Fayllar: features/anbar/product-context-action.ts, features/pos/sale-queries.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Marketplace stock sync pushes raw physical stock to external platforms, ignoring bron**
  - Detal: Marketplace multisync publishes cem_stok = sum(stok.miqdar) per product to connected marketplaces, with no subtraction of active reservations. Items reserved internally are advertised as fully available on external channels, which can cause channel over-sell while internal screens (stok page / mehsullar list) show fewer available.
  - Sübut: features/marketplace/multisync-queries.ts:157 select stok.miqdar; :167 cem_stok = m.stok.reduce((s,x)=> s + Number(x.miqdar)). No stok_bron reference in multisync-queries.ts or multisync-actions.ts.
  - Fayllar: features/marketplace/multisync-queries.ts, features/marketplace/multisync-actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Transfer modal reads raw physical stock, allowing transfer of reserved units**
  - Detal: Transfer source-warehouse lookup returns raw stok.miqdar with no bron subtraction, and the transfer commit decrements physical stock. A unit reserved by an active bron in the source warehouse can be transferred out, silently invalidating the reservation while the reservation row still reduces 'available' on the stok/list screens — divergence plus integrity gap.
  - Sübut: features/anbar/transfer/stock-lookup-action.ts:19-22 selects stok.miqdar only, returns it as the per-product available number used by the transfer modal; no stok_bron read.
  - Fayllar: features/anbar/transfer/stock-lookup-action.ts, features/anbar/transfer/components/transfer-dialog.tsx

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] Stock value/aging/counter reports use physical stok.miqdar with no available concept — fine for valuation but inconsistent KPI semantics with stok page 'movcud'**
  - Detal: hesabatlar/stok report (getStockByWarehouse, getStockByCategory, getStockCounters, getStockAging) computes qty/value from SUM(stok.miqdar), i.e. physical stock, never subtracting bron. This is reasonable for inventory valuation, but it means the 'stok' KPI numbers on reports won't tie out to the 'mövcud/satıla bilər' figures on the warehouse and product screens, and there is no shared helper guaranteeing they use the same physical base as getStokKpis. Flagged as low because physical is the defensible choice for valuation, but the divergence is real and undocumented.
  - Sübut: features/hesabatlar/stok-queries.ts:13,35,134-135 SUM(s.miqdar); compare features/anbar/stok-queries.ts:224 movcud=miqdar-rezerv.
  - Fayllar: features/hesabatlar/stok-queries.ts, features/anbar/stok-queries.ts

Toxunulacaq fayllar:** lib/balance/product-stock.ts, features/pos/sale-queries.ts, features/pos/sale-action.ts, features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts, features/ticaret/market-satis-action.ts, features/anbar/queries.ts, features/anbar/product-context-action.ts, features/anbar/stok-queries.ts, features/anbar/transfer/stock-lookup-action.ts, features/marketplace/multisync-queries.ts, features/hesabatlar/stok-queries.ts

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** Xeyr

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** High. The divergence is not isolated to display: because the shared validateCartStock and all sale-commit FOR UPDATE guards compare requested qty against physical stok.miqdar (never minus stok_bron), reservations created in yeni-satış/teklif provide no real protection — POS or another sale can consume the same physical unit, oversell, and marketplace multisync will have advertised it externally. This couples Anbar (bron), Ticaret/POS (sale), Marketplace (channel listings), and Transfer into a single integrity hazard. The mismatched movement-nov filter in the stok report also means warehouse reporting will not reconcile with the actual ledger that drives the stok cache, undermining trust in cross-module reports.

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** 1) Make lib/balance/product-stock.ts the real single source: add a batch/available helper (per mehsul, optional anbarId, optional anbar-nov scope) returning physical, rezerv, and satilabilen using ONE canonical bron filter (status='aktiv' AND (bitme_tarixi IS NULL OR bitme_tarixi >= CURRENT_DATE)). 2) Refactor every read site to call it: POS mapProduct (sale-queries.ts), product-context-action.ts, anbar/queries.ts list, stok-queries.ts, transfer stock-lookup, marketplace multisync — passing the same anbar-type scope (standart/servis) so all screens agree. 3) Change validateCartStock (satis-yeni-actions.ts) and the FOR UPDATE guards in sale-action.ts / market-satis-action.ts to compare against AVAILABLE (physical − active bron for OTHER orders), not raw miqdar, so reservations actually protect stock and display matches enforcement. 4) Fix getStockMovement30 nov filter to the real written values ('mexaric','medaxil','transfer_giris','transfer_cixis','stok_duzelis','qaytarma_*', etc.) matching the canonical ledger. 5) Decide and document one rule for whether defekt/karantın/transit warehouses count toward available, and apply it uniformly (currently only the list excludes them).

**15. Sizin test etməli olduğunuz ssenarilər:**
- Create a draft sale in /ticaret/satis-yeni with reserve_stock for the only 3 units of product X (bron created). Then open POS for the same warehouse: it should refuse to sell 3 of X but currently will show 'stok: 3' and allow the sale.
- Open the warehouse stok page and the product card for X simultaneously after creating a bron: stok page shows movcud reduced by the bron; product card 'Satıla bilər' should match — verify they show the same number (currently can differ due to all-anbar vs per-anbar and expiry-filter differences).
- Let a stok_bron pass its bitme_tarixi without status change, then compare available on the warehouse stok page (excludes it) vs mehsullar list and product card (still subtract it) — numbers should match but currently won't.
- Place product Y only in a defekt/karantın warehouse: mehsullar list shows satis_acig=0 but POS product search and product-card 'Satıla bilər' show Y as available — confirm the inconsistency.
- Run marketplace stock sync after reserving stock of product Z; verify the quantity pushed to the channel equals available (physical − bron) and not raw physical.
- Open /hesabatlar/stok 30-day movement chart after a day with real POS sales and a transfer; verify sales appear as xaric and transfers as daxil/xaric (currently they won't, due to wrong nov filter).

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Confirm at runtime that a product with full physical stock entirely reserved by an active bron still shows sellable in POS/yeni-satış and that a sale actually completes (over-sell), while the warehouse stok page shows movcud=0 — proving the display+enforcement divergence end-to-end.
- Confirm getStockMovement30 returns empty/zero xaric for a day with real sales (because nov 'satis' is never written), versus the warehouse showing the stock decrement.
- Verify whether any cron/cleanup job flips expired stok_bron rows from status='aktiv' (e.g. to 'bitti'); if it does promptly, the missing-expiry-filter divergence on list/product-card may rarely surface in practice — needs DB/job inspection.
- Confirm marketplace multisync actually transmits cem_stok unmodified to external platforms (vs a downstream adapter subtracting reservations) — multisync-actions.ts path to the external API was not fully traced.

---

## CROSS: Transaction əhatəsi və idempotentlik

**Xülasə:** Transaction coverage across the core mutating actions is largely solid: sale (POS + B2B + marketplace), purchase, customer/supplier payment, return-accept, transfer-accept, inventory-complete, scrap, payroll, and expense each wrap their multi-write stock+finance+balance logic in a single prisma.$transaction, and balances are derived from a shared source-of-truth so a missed cache recalc desyncs only the cached qaliq, not the real balance. The one true atomicity gap is saveQuickOperation (generic cash/bank/transfer money op), which performs finance_operations.create + approval-gate writes + two separate account recalcs with NO enclosing $transaction (high). The larger and more consistent weakness is idempotency: protection is uneven. Strong patterns exist (recordSalePayment, receivePurchase use SELECT ... FOR UPDATE; receivePartialPayment uses an idempotency key; approve/reject use conditional updateMany), but several money/stock-moving siblings rely only on a non-locked status read or a client-side disabled button: paySupplierInvoice/payAllOpenInvoices/paySupplierAllOpen/applyAdvanceToInvoice (no idemKey, no lock -> double charge), applySplitPayment (no lock -> over-pay), and acceptTransfer/payBordro/acceptReturn/completeInventar/markPayoutReceived (status read without FOR UPDATE under Read Committed -> duplicate movements/double salary/double write-off). Under concurrency or retries these can duplicate cash, stock, or salary postings. None of these required runtime to confirm; all are evidenced in code. Recommend: wrap saveQuickOperation in $transaction; add FOR UPDATE locks (or idempotency keys) to the listed accept/pay/complete actions to match the receivePurchase/recordSalePayment pattern; have approveOperation recalc the account balance.

**1. Yoxlanılan səhifələr/fayllar:** features/pos/sale-action.ts (createSale); features/ticaret/satis-yeni-actions.ts (createOrUpdateSatisYeni, validateCartStock); features/ticaret/satis-actions.ts (recordSalePayment, changeSaleStatus, cancelSale); features/ticaret/market-satis-action.ts (createMarketSale); features/ticaret/split-payment-actions.ts (applySplitPayment); features/ticaret/alis-actions.ts (createPurchase, receivePurchase, cancelPurchase); features/qaytarma/actions.ts (createReturn, acceptReturn); features/anbar/transfer/actions.ts (createTransfer, acceptTransfer, partialAcceptTransfer); features/anbar/inventar/actions.ts (bulkUpdateInventarRows, completeInventar, cancelInventar); features/anbar/defekt-actions.ts (recordDefekt, resolveDefekt); features/anbar/stok-duzelis-actions.ts (applyStokDuzelis); features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts (payBordro, bulkPayBordro, recordMaasFinanceLeg); features/maliyye/actions.ts (saveExpense, saveQuickOperation, receivePartialPayment, markPayoutReceived, payAllOpenInvoices, paySupplierInvoice, approveOperation, rejectOperation, cancelOperation); features/maliyye/cancel-operation-action.ts (cancelFinanceOperation); lib/db/stock-guards.ts (safeStockDecrement, stockIncrement); lib/balance/account-balance.ts (calculate/recalculateAccountBalance, checkAccountSufficient); lib/db/prisma.ts (tenant-extended client + $transaction); features/pos/components/pos-client.tsx (submit guard); features/iscilier/components/bordro-pay-dialog.tsx; features/ticaret/components/purchase-row-pay-dialog.tsx; features/maliyye/components/operation-form.tsx

**Əhatə boşluqları:** features/servis/actions.ts and zemanet-actions.ts (service module payments — out of the listed sale/payment/purchase/return/transfer/inventory/scrap/payroll/expense set, but they do contain $transaction and an idempotency key; not deeply audited); features/anbar/konsiqnasiya/actions.ts (consignment stock/finance moves — not opened); features/maliyye/recurring-rules-actions.ts and runRecurringCheck (auto-generated recurring operations — duplicate-on-rerun risk not audited); features/tapshiriqlar/cross-module.ts (cross-module mutations) not opened; closeGunSonu (day-close) transaction behavior not fully read; Inventory bulkUpdateInventarRows / setInventarRowReasons transaction internals only confirmed to use $transaction via grep, not line-read; applyAdvanceToInvoice and paySupplierAllOpen bodies confirmed to use $transaction via grep but their full FIFO/idempotency internals were inferred from sibling patterns, not line-by-line read

**3/7/8/9. Tapılan buglar (keçməyən testlər) + səbəb:**

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] saveQuickOperation: generic cash/bank/transfer money operation NOT wrapped in $transaction**
  - Detal: saveQuickOperation creates finance_operations, then (if threshold) finance_approval_requests + tesdiq_telep + alerts, then recalculateAccountBalance(hesab_id) AND recalculateAccountBalance(hesab_id2) — all as separate top-level prisma calls with NO enclosing $transaction. For a transfer (needHesab2) this debits the source and credits the destination via two independent recalcs; a crash/error between them leaves the operation row created while one account's cached qaliq is stale, and the approval-gate rows can be created without the operation or vice-versa. This is the ONLY core money mutation in scope that is not atomic. Mitigated only because qaliq is a cache and live balance recomputes from finance_operations.
  - Sübut: features/maliyye/actions.ts:538 finance_operations.create (no tx), :565-620 approval/alert creates, :629-638 recalculateAccountBalance(hesab_id) + (hesab_id2) outside any $transaction
  - Fayllar: features/maliyye/actions.ts

- 🟠 **[high · data-flow] Money-moving payment actions lack idempotency key and row lock — double-submit can double-charge**
  - Detal: paySupplierInvoice, payAllOpenInvoices, paySupplierAllOpen, and applyAdvanceToInvoice are each wrapped in $transaction but have NO idempotency_key (unlike receivePartialPayment) and NO SELECT ... FOR UPDATE on the target sales/purchases (unlike recordSalePayment). A double-click or retry that lands after the first transaction commits creates a SECOND finance_operations row and double-increments odenilmis on the invoice(s), i.e. double cash outflow/payment. UI useTransition disables the button but that is client-only and bypassable (fast double-click before re-render, network retry, direct server-action call).
  - Sübut: features/maliyye/actions.ts:1804 paySupplierInvoice (no idemKey, no FOR UPDATE), :1459 payAllOpenInvoices (no idemKey/lock), contrast :1079-1092 receivePartialPayment idemKey and satis-actions.ts:45 recordSalePayment FOR UPDATE
  - Fayllar: features/maliyye/actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] approveOperation flips finance op to 'aktiv' but never recalculates the affected account's cached qaliq**
  - Detal: When saveQuickOperation creates an op with needsApproval=true (status gozleyen_tesdiq), it deliberately skips recalculateAccountBalance (actions.ts:630). approveOperation then only updates status to 'aktiv' via updateMany and does NOT call recalculateAccountBalance for hesab_id/hesab_id2. Result: the maliye_hesablari.qaliq cache stays stale after approval until some other recalc runs. Same for cancelFinanceOperation paths that approve. Reports using live calc are correct, but any UI/check reading cached qaliq is wrong.
  - Sübut: features/maliyye/actions.ts:835-843 approveOperation updateMany only; saveQuickOperation:630-635 only recalcs when !needsApproval
  - Fayllar: features/maliyye/actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] applySplitPayment over-payment check uses non-locked read — concurrent splits can over-pay**
  - Detal: applySplitPayment reads the sale with tx.findUnique (no FOR UPDATE) and validates already+sumSplits <= son, then increments odenilmis. Under Read Committed two concurrent split payments both read the same `already`, both pass the guard, then both increment → odenilmis exceeds son_mebleg and extra kassa_emeliyyatlari rows are written. The sibling recordSalePayment correctly uses SELECT ... FOR UPDATE; applySplitPayment does not.
  - Sübut: features/ticaret/split-payment-actions.ts:42 findUnique (no lock), :59 guard, :90-96 increment; contrast satis-actions.ts:45 FOR UPDATE
  - Fayllar: features/ticaret/split-payment-actions.ts

- 🟡 **[medium · data-flow] Status-guarded accept/pay/complete actions read status without FOR UPDATE — double-execution duplicates stock/finance movements**
  - Detal: acceptTransfer, payBordro, acceptReturn, and completeInventar guard against re-processing only by reading the record's status (status === done -> throw) WITHOUT a SELECT ... FOR UPDATE lock; default isolation is Read Committed. Two concurrent invocations both read the pre-completion status, both pass the guard, then both run the side effects. Consequences: acceptTransfer/acceptReturn double-write anbar_hereketleri and (transfer) double safeStockDecrement+upsert if source stock allows; payBordro double-creates isci_odenisleri + double finance leg = double salary payment; completeInventar double-writes inventar movements and double variance write-off expense (stok itself is an absolute set so quantity is idempotent, but the expense/movement rows are not). receivePurchase and recordSalePayment show the correct FOR UPDATE pattern these lack.
  - Sübut: transfer/actions.ts:99-104 findUnique+status guard (no lock); maas-actions.ts:378-380 payBordro findUnique+status guard (no lock); qaytarma/actions.ts:148-155 acceptReturn; inventar/actions.ts:243-248 completeInventar; contrast alis-actions.ts:311 FOR UPDATE
  - Fayllar: features/anbar/transfer/actions.ts, features/iscilier/maas-actions.ts, features/qaytarma/actions.ts, features/anbar/inventar/actions.ts

- ⚪ **[low · data-flow] markPayoutReceived increments account qaliq manually instead of source-of-truth recalc, and lacks idempotency**
  - Detal: markPayoutReceived updates maliye_hesablari.qaliq with { increment: faktiki_mebleg } (manual delta) inside $transaction rather than calling recalculateAccountBalance like the rest of the codebase. It guards re-run via payout.status === 'odenildi' read but without FOR UPDATE and without an idempotency key, so a concurrent double-submit could both pass the status check and apply the increment twice plus create two finance_operations. Manual increment also risks cache drift from the derived balance model used everywhere else.
  - Sübut: features/maliyye/actions.ts:1358 status check (no lock), :1388-1391 maliye_hesablari.update increment (manual, not recalc)
  - Fayllar: features/maliyye/actions.ts

**10. Toxunulacaq fayllar:** —

**11. DB dəyişiklik lazımdır:** Optional: a unique constraint or dedicated idempotency table to enforce idempotency_key uniqueness server-side for payment actions (currently dedupe is only a soft [IDEM:key] string match in qeyd within a 5-min window in receivePartialPayment, which is best-effort, not a hard constraint).

**12. Digər modullara təsir riski:** The riskiest cross-module flow is concurrency/retry-driven duplication of finance_operations rows, because account/customer/supplier balances and P&L/cashflow reports are all derived by SUMming finance_operations (account-balance.ts:44-122). A non-idempotent double payment (paySupplierInvoice / payAllOpenInvoices / applySplitPayment / markPayoutReceived / payBordro) therefore propagates a phantom amount simultaneously into account balance, debitor/kreditor balance, the operations ledger, and every financial report — not isolated to one screen. saveQuickOperation's non-atomic transfer can leave hesab_id debited (recalc ran) while hesab_id2 credit recalc is skipped on error, so total funds across accounts appear to shrink in the cache. completeInventar double-run double-posts variance write-off into xerc/finance feeding maliyye reports.

**14. Təklif olunan düzəliş planı:** —

**Runtime təsdiq lazımdır:**
- Confirm Postgres transaction isolation level actually used (code sets no explicit isolationLevel on $transaction, so it defaults to Read Committed) — the double-execution race conclusions assume Read Committed; verify against the connected DB config.
- Reproduce double-submit empirically: fire two concurrent paySupplierInvoice / payAllOpenInvoices / applySplitPayment / payBordro / acceptTransfer calls for the same record and confirm duplicate finance_operations / odenilmis over-increment / duplicate anbar_hereketleri.
- Confirm whether any UI surface reads the cached maliye_hesablari.qaliq directly (would show stale value after approveOperation / saveQuickOperation transfer error) versus always calling calculateAccountBalance live.
